from pathlib import Path


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent




#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Paired per-task analysis of Polyglot result rows.

Task difficulty dominates the variance at n=40, so outcomes are compared task by
task (McNemar-style discordant pairs), never as two aggregate pass rates. The
noise floor is the same-quant repeat disagreement rate; any cross-quant delta
that does not clear it is not a result.

When both legs solve everything -- which Polyglot reaches on this model -- there
are no discordant pairs and this says nothing. Compare cost instead, with
paired_runs.py.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import math
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path


def load(path: Path) -> dict[tuple[str, int], dict]:
    """Rows written before a schema addition simply lack those keys.

    Normalising here rather than rewriting the file keeps the analysis off the
    runner's append target, which it must never race.
    """

    rows = {}
    raw = []
    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            raw.append(json.loads(line))
    keys = set().union(*(r.keys() for r in raw)) if raw else set()
    for r in raw:
        full = {k: r.get(k) for k in keys}
        rows[(full["quant"], full["run_index"], full["task_id"])] = derive_timed_out(full)
    return rows


TIMEOUT_NOTE = "task timed out after "


def derive_timed_out(row: dict) -> dict:
    """Recover the timeout flag for rows predating the column.

    This re-encodes the note the harness itself wrote, so it adds no information
    that was not already recorded. Everything else absent from an older row --
    transcripts, server counters -- stays null rather than being reconstructed.
    """

    if row.get("timed_out") is None:
        note = row.get("notes")
        row["timed_out"] = isinstance(note, str) and note.startswith(TIMEOUT_NOTE)
    return row


def outcomes(rows, quant, run_index, include_timeouts: bool = False) -> dict[str, bool]:
    """Per-task pass/fail, dropping timed-out tasks by default.

    A wall-clock cut-off scales with decode rate, so a larger quant hits it for
    being larger. Scoring that as a quality failure would let the harness
    parameter masquerade as a quantization effect; the task is treated as
    missing instead, which shrinks n honestly.
    """

    return {
        task: bool(r["task_success"])
        for (q, ri, task), r in rows.items()
        if q == quant and ri == run_index and (include_timeouts or not r.get("timed_out"))
    }


def discordant(a: dict[str, bool], b: dict[str, bool]) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    shared = sorted(set(a) & set(b))
    a_only = sum(1 for t in shared if a[t] and not b[t])
    b_only = sum(1 for t in shared if b[t] and not a[t])
    return len(shared), a_only, b_only


def paired_difference_ci(n: int, n01: int, n10: int, z: float = 1.96) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Two-sided CI on the paired difference in pass rate, discordants only.

    The signed difference is what the decision needs; total disagreement is
    direction-blind and cannot distinguish symmetric churn from a real loss.
    """

    if n == 0:
        return (0.0, 0.0)
    diff = (n10 - n01) / n
    # Agresti-Min style: variance of the paired difference from the discordant counts.
    var = (n01 + n10 - (n10 - n01) ** 2 / n) / n**2
    half = z * math.sqrt(max(var, 0.0))
    return (max(-1.0, diff - half), min(1.0, diff + half))


def exact_mcnemar_p(n01: int, n10: int) -> float:
    """Two-sided exact binomial p on the discordant pairs only."""
    n = n01 + n10
    if n == 0:
        return 1.0
    k = min(n01, n10)
    tail = sum(math.comb(n, i) for i in range(k + 1)) / 2**n
    return min(1.0, 2 * tail)


def main() -> None:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__,
                                 formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    ap.add_argument("results", type=Path, help="polyglot result JSONL")
    ap.add_argument("--floor-quant", required=True,
                    help="quant run twice to establish the noise floor")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    rows = load(args.results)
    quants = sorted({q for (q, _, _) in rows})

    print("== per-run pass counts ==")
    seen = defaultdict(int)
    for (q, ri, _t), r in rows.items():
        seen[(q, ri)] += bool(r["task_success"])
    counts = defaultdict(int)
    for (q, ri, _t) in rows:
        counts[(q, ri)] += 1
    for key in sorted(counts):
        q, ri = key
        print(f"  {q:<12} run {ri}: {seen[key]:2d}/{counts[key]:2d}")

    timeouts = defaultdict(int)
    for (q, ri, _t), r in rows.items():
        if r.get("timed_out"):
            timeouts[(q, ri)] += 1
    if timeouts:
        print("\n== timeouts (a harness cut-off, not a model verdict) ==")
        for key in sorted(timeouts):
            print(f"  {key[0]:<12} run {key[1]}: {timeouts[key]}")

    floor = None
    a, b = outcomes(rows, args.floor_quant, 1), outcomes(rows, args.floor_quant, 2)
    if a and b:
        n, x, y = discordant(a, b)
        floor = (x + y) / n if n else None
        print(f"\n== noise floor ({args.floor_quant}, run 1 vs run 2) ==")
        print(f"  shared tasks {n}, disagreements {x + y} "
              f"({x} run1-only, {y} run2-only) = {floor:.1%}")
        print("  This is how much churn the same model shows against itself. It is NOT"
              " a threshold: total disagreement is direction-blind, so symmetric churn"
              " can exceed it with no capability difference while a small one-sided"
              " loss can matter. Read the signed difference and its interval instead.")

    print("\n== paired cross-quant comparisons (run 1) ==")
    for i, q1 in enumerate(quants):
        for q2 in quants[i + 1:]:
            a, b = outcomes(rows, q1, 1), outcomes(rows, q2, 1)
            if not a or not b:
                continue
            n, x, y = discordant(a, b)
            rate = (x + y) / n if n else 0.0
            p = exact_mcnemar_p(x, y)
            lo, hi = paired_difference_ci(n, x, y)
            print(f"  {q1} vs {q2}: n={n} discordant={x + y} ({rate:.1%}) "
                  f"[{q1}-only {x}, {q2}-only {y}]")
            print(f"      exact McNemar p={p:.3f}; "
                  f"{q2}-{q1} difference {(y - x) / n:+.1%} "
                  f"(95% CI {lo:+.1%} to {hi:+.1%})")
            if floor is not None:
                print(f"      same-quant repeat disagreed on {floor:.1%} of tasks "
                      "-- context for how much churn is normal, not a threshold")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Assemble the cross-engine table from the bench rows and the drivers' FIT lines.

Context ceilings are not in the bench rows: each engine reports its own, in its own words,
in its server log -- so the drivers echo a `FIT <label> ...` line and this reads that back
rather than re-deriving a number no client can see.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import statistics as st
from collections import defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

from scripts import ROOT

SPEED = ROOT / "results" / "session1" / "speed"


def rows() -> dict[tuple, dict[str, list[float]]]:
    """Group by label AND the hardware it ran on.

    Grouping on the label alone merged the same quant benched on a 2x5070 Ti and a
    2x5060 Ti into one row of six samples, with whichever card was read last
    overwriting the other. The power cap joins the key because it is an experiment
    variable: the same card at two caps is two configurations.
    """

    out: dict[tuple, dict[str, list[float]]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
    for line in (SPEED / "engines.jsonl").read_text().splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        r = json.loads(line)
        key = (r["label"], r.get("gpu"), r.get("gpu_count"), r.get("power_limit_w"))
        for field in ("decode_tok_s", "ttft_s", "prefill_tok_s"):
            if r.get(field):
                out[key][field].append(r[field])
        if r.get("completion_tokens") and r.get("total_s"):
            out[key]["overall"].append(r["completion_tokens"] / r["total_s"])
        out[key]["engine"] = r.get("engine")
    return out


def card_name(gpu: str | None, count: int | None) -> str:
    """`NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090` -> `3090`, `RTX 5070 Ti 300W` -> `5070 Ti`.

    Anything without an RTX marker is passed through unchanged so a bad value stays
    visibly bad: 36 rows here carry a rental label in this field, and normalising
    them would hide that rather than fix it.
    """

    if not gpu:
        return "?"
    words = gpu.split()
    if "RTX" in words:
        words = words[words.index("RTX") + 1:]
        words = [w for w in words if not re.fullmatch(r"\d+W", w)]
        gpu = " ".join(words) or gpu
    return f"{count}x{gpu}" if count and count > 1 else gpu


def ceilings() -> dict[str, str]:
    """Every `FIT <label> <free text>` line any driver wrote, last one winning."""
    found: dict[str, str] = {}
    for log in SPEED.glob("*.log"):
        for line in log.read_text(errors="replace").splitlines():
            m = re.search(r"\bFIT\s+(\S+)\s+(.*)", line)
            if not m:
                continue
            # Each engine states its ceiling in its own log line; keep the token count and
            # drop the rest, or ninfer's startup line alone is wider than the table.
            text = m.group(2).strip()
            n = re.search(r"(?:GPU KV cache size:|resolved=)\s*([0-9,]+)\s*tokens", text)
            found[m.group(1)] = f"{n.group(1)} tokens" if n else text
    return found


def main() -> int:
    data, fits = rows(), ceilings()
    width = max((len(k[0]) for k in data), default=10)
    # The card is not decoration: 5090 and 3090 rows sit in the same file, and a table
    # that ranks them together without naming the card invites exactly the wrong reading.
    print(f"{'engine · config':{width}s} {'card':>22s} {'decode':>8s} {'overall':>8s} "
          f"{'ttft':>6s} {'prefill':>8s} {'n':>3s}  context ceiling")
    for (label, gpu, count, watts), v in sorted(
        data.items(), key=lambda kv: -st.mean(kv[1]["decode_tok_s"] or [0])
    ):
        def m(field: str) -> str:
            if not v[field]:
                return "-"
            # Median for the latency columns: the first request against a freshly started
            # vLLM pays graph capture (36 s against 1.4 s for the next one), and a mean
            # over four tasks turns that one-off into the reported prefill rate.
            f = st.median if field in ("ttft_s", "prefill_tok_s") else st.mean
            return f"{f(v[field]):.1f}"
        fit = fits.get(label) or fits.get(label.split("-", 1)[-1], "")
        card = card_name(gpu, count) + (f" {watts:.0f}W" if watts else "")
        print(f"{label:{width}s} {card:>22s} {m('decode_tok_s'):>8s} {m('overall'):>8s} "
              f"{m('ttft_s'):>6s} {m('prefill_tok_s'):>8s} {len(v['overall']):>3d}  {fit}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Attach the evaluation containers' verdict to a SWE-bench leg's rows.

run_swebench.py produces patches and leaves `task_success` null, because a patch is
not a solve. This reads the report the official harness writes and fills that in.

Nothing here decides whether a patch is correct. The harness runs the instance's own
FAIL_TO_PASS and PASS_TO_PASS tests inside the published image and says so; this maps
its answer onto the rows and, where it has no answer, leaves the row ungraded rather
than guessing. That distinction is the point: an image that would not build and a
patch that broke the tests are the same empty result to a caller that only counts
successes, and only one of them is the quant's fault.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
from typing import Any

# The report's own partition of the instances it was given. `unresolved` and
# `error` overlap the two failure-classification lists, so those are checked first.
_RESOLVED = "resolved_ids"
_MODEL_FAILURE = ("unresolved_ids", "empty_patch_ids")
# Only confirmed environment faults. The harness separates these from
# `ambiguous_failure_ids` precisely so the ambiguous ones are "never mistaken for a
# confirmed environment fault" (swebench/harness/infra_failure.py) -- it keeps them in
# unresolved_ids on purpose, and excluding them here would discard failures the patch
# may well have caused.
_NO_VERDICT = ("infra_failure_ids", "error_ids", "incomplete_ids")


def load_report(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    report = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if not isinstance(report, dict) or _RESOLVED not in report:
        raise SystemExit(f"{path} is not a SWE-bench run report")
    return report


def verdicts(report: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, tuple[bool | None, str | None]]:
    """Instance id -> (resolved, reason it has no verdict).

    A no-verdict class is checked before the failure classes it overlaps: the harness
    leaves an infra failure in `unresolved_ids` too, and reading that first would
    charge a container that never built to the model.
    """
    out: dict[str, tuple[bool | None, str | None]] = {}
    for key in _NO_VERDICT:
        for instance_id in report.get(key, []):
            out[instance_id] = (None, key.removesuffix("_ids"))
    for key in _MODEL_FAILURE:
        for instance_id in report.get(key, []):
            out.setdefault(instance_id, (False, None))
    for instance_id in report.get(_RESOLVED, []):
        # Resolved wins outright: the tests passed, whatever else was logged.
        out[instance_id] = (True, None)
    return out


def grade(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], report: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, int]]:
    table = verdicts(report)
    tally = {"resolved": 0, "unresolved": 0, "ungraded": 0}
    graded = []
    for row in rows:
        row = dict(row)
        resolved, reason = table.get(row.get("task_id"), (None, "absent from the report"))
        row["task_success"] = resolved
        row["tests_passed"] = resolved
        if resolved is None:
            note = f"ungraded: {reason}"
            row["notes"] = f"{row['notes']}; {note}" if row.get("notes") else note
            tally["ungraded"] += 1
        else:
            tally["resolved" if resolved else "unresolved"] += 1
        graded.append(row)
    return graded, tally


def run_harness(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Path:
    """Invoke the official harness, which needs docker and the swebench package.

    Shelled out rather than imported so this script stays stdlib-only and the harness
    can live in its own environment; --python names that interpreter.
    """
    # Scoped to what was submitted. The pinned export holds all 300 instances, and
    # without this the harness counts the 248 nobody asked for as incomplete -- which
    # buries a genuinely missing prediction in a pile of expected ones.
    submitted = sorted({
        json.loads(line)["instance_id"]
        for line in Path(args.predictions).read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        if line.strip()
    })
    if not submitted:
        raise SystemExit(f"{args.predictions} holds no predictions")
    command = [
        args.python, "-m", "swebench.harness.run_evaluation",
        "--dataset_name", str(args.dataset),
        "--predictions_path", str(args.predictions),
        "--instance_ids", *submitted,
        "--run_id", args.run_id,
        "--max_workers", str(args.max_workers),
        "--report_dir", str(args.report_dir),
    ]
    print(" ".join(command), file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
    subprocess.run(command, check=True)
    reports = sorted(Path(args.report_dir).glob(f"*.{args.run_id}.json"))
    if len(reports) != 1:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"expected one report matching *.{args.run_id}.json in {args.report_dir}, "
            f"found {[p.name for p in reports]}"
        )
    return reports[0]


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter
    )
    parser.add_argument("--rows", required=True, type=Path, help="leg output from run_swebench.py")
    parser.add_argument("--quant", required=True, help="which leg in --rows to grade")
    parser.add_argument("--run-index", required=True, type=int)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--out", type=Path,
        help="graded copy (default: <rows stem>.graded.jsonl). The input is never "
             "rewritten: it is the append-only record of what the run measured.",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--report", type=Path, help="an existing harness report to ingest")
    parser.add_argument("--run", action="store_true", help="run the harness to produce one")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--dataset", type=Path,
        default=Path(".cache/subsets/SWE-bench--SWE-bench_Multilingual-"
                     "846e647b9f33c0b51b739d005d13d85493c9af09.jsonl"),
        help="full instance records; the subset in subsets/ carries no tests, so the "
             "harness needs the pinned canonical export",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--predictions", type=Path, help="prediction JSONL from the same leg")
    parser.add_argument("--run-id", help="harness run id; defaults to <quant>-r<run-index>")
    parser.add_argument("--report-dir", type=Path, default=Path("./.swebench-reports"))
    parser.add_argument("--python", default=sys.executable, help="interpreter holding swebench")
    parser.add_argument("--max-workers", type=int, default=4)
    return parser


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    if bool(args.report) == bool(args.run):
        raise SystemExit("pass exactly one of --report and --run")
    args.run_id = args.run_id or f"{args.quant}-r{args.run_index}"

    rows = [
        json.loads(line)
        for line in args.rows.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
        if line.strip()
    ]
    leg = [r for r in rows if r.get("quant") == args.quant and r.get("run_index") == args.run_index]
    if not leg:
        raise SystemExit(f"no rows for quant {args.quant!r} at run-index {args.run_index}")

    if args.run:
        if not args.predictions:
            raise SystemExit("--run needs --predictions")
        report_path = run_harness(args)
    else:
        report_path = args.report
    report = load_report(report_path)

    graded, tally = grade(leg, report)
    out = args.out or args.rows.with_suffix(".graded.jsonl")
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with out.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        for row in graded:
            handle.write(json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True,
                                    separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False) + "\n")

    total = tally["resolved"] + tally["unresolved"]
    rate = f"{tally['resolved'] / total:.3f}" if total else "n/a"
    print(f"{out}: {tally['resolved']} resolved, {tally['unresolved']} unresolved, "
          f"{tally['ungraded']} ungraded -- pass rate {rate} over {total} graded")
    if tally["ungraded"]:
        # Loud, because the denominator is now smaller than the subset and a pass rate
        # over a shrunken denominator is not comparable with one over the full subset.
        print(f"WARNING: {tally['ungraded']} instance(s) have no verdict; compare quants "
              f"only on instances graded in every arm", file=sys.stderr)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Progress of the in-flight cost legs, counted by run_index.

Line count is the wrong gauge: an orphaned runner from a killed leg kept appending to the
same file under its own run_index, and `wc -l` read 85/78 on a leg that had done 42.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import collections
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys

from scripts import ROOT

# Void legs stay out: one that can never reach TOTAL holds the completion signal open
# forever. Run 11 is void -- see results/session2/polyglot-cost/void/README.md.
LEGS = {  # run_index -> (experiment dir, label)
    12: ("polyglot-cost-q2", "Q2_K_XL 5070Ti"),
    13: ("polyglot-cost-q4b", "Q4_K_XL 3090 #2"),
}
TOTAL = 78
root = ROOT / "results" / "session2"

parts, done = [], True
for run, (exp, label) in sorted(LEGS.items()):
    counts: collections.Counter = collections.Counter()
    path = root / exp / "polyglot.jsonl"
    if path.exists():
        for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
            if line.strip():
                try:
                    counts[json.loads(line).get("run_index")] += 1
                except ValueError:
                    pass
    n = counts.get(run, 0)
    stray = {k: v for k, v in counts.items() if k != run}
    parts.append(f"{label} r{run} {n}/{TOTAL}" + (f" [STRAY {stray}]" if stray else ""))
    done = done and n >= TOTAL
print(" | ".join(parts))
sys.exit(0 if not done else 3)


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Summarize the local mixed-GPU probe artifacts without pooling instruments."""

import argparse
import csv
from datetime import datetime
import json
import statistics
from pathlib import Path


def rows(path):
    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()] if path.exists() else []


def metrics(path):
    if not path.exists():
        return {}
    return {parts[0]: float(parts[1]) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()
            if not line.startswith('#') and len(parts := line.split()) == 2}


def median(records, field):
    values = [row[field] for row in records if row.get(field) is not None]
    return round(statistics.median(values), 2) if values else None


def summarize(arm):
    shallow = rows(arm / 'engines.jsonl')
    deep = rows(arm / 'depth.jsonl')
    props = json.loads((arm / 'props.json').read_text()) if (arm / 'props.json').exists() else {}
    concurrent_path = arm / 'concurrency/summary.json'
    concurrent = json.loads(concurrent_path.read_text()) if concurrent_path.exists() else {}
    before = metrics(arm / 'metrics-before.txt')
    after = metrics(arm / 'metrics-shallow.txt')
    delta = {key: value - before.get(key, 0) for key, value in after.items()}
    devices = {}
    if (arm / 'gpu.csv').exists():
        with (arm / 'gpu.csv').open() as stream:
            for raw in csv.DictReader(stream, skipinitialspace=True):
                row = {k.strip(): v.strip() for k, v in raw.items() if k and v}
                try:
                    index = row['index']
                    used = float(row['memory.used [MiB]'].split()[0])
                    free = float(row['memory.free [MiB]'].split()[0])
                    temperature = float(row['temperature.gpu'])
                except (KeyError, ValueError):
                    continue
                device = devices.setdefault(index, {
                    'name': row['name'], 'uuid': row['uuid'],
                    'initial_used_mib': used, 'max_used_mib': used, 'min_free_mib': free,
                    'max_temperature_c': temperature, 'max_link_gen': 0, 'link_widths': [],
                    'initial_temperature_c': temperature, 'final_temperature_c': temperature,
                    'max_power_w': 0, 'busy_power_w': [], 'max_fan_percent': None,
                    'samples': [],
                })
                device['max_used_mib'] = max(device['max_used_mib'], used)
                device['min_free_mib'] = min(device['min_free_mib'], free)
                device['max_temperature_c'] = max(device['max_temperature_c'], temperature)
                device['final_temperature_c'] = temperature
                power = float(row['power.draw [W]'].split()[0])
                device['samples'].append((datetime.strptime(row['timestamp'], '%Y/%m/%d %H:%M:%S.%f'),
                                          power, temperature))
                device['max_power_w'] = max(device['max_power_w'], power)
                if float(row['utilization.gpu [%]'].split()[0]) >= 50:
                    device['busy_power_w'].append(power)
                if 'fan.speed [%]' in row:
                    device['max_fan_percent'] = max(device['max_fan_percent'] or 0,
                                                   float(row['fan.speed [%]'].split()[0]))
                device['max_link_gen'] = max(device['max_link_gen'], int(row['pcie.link.gen.current']))
                width = int(row['pcie.link.width.current'])
                if width not in device['link_widths']:
                    device['link_widths'].append(width)
    for device in devices.values():
        busy = device.pop('busy_power_w')
        device['busy_power_median_w'] = round(statistics.median(busy), 2) if busy else None
        samples = device.pop('samples')
        device['observed_seconds'] = round((samples[-1][0] - samples[0][0]).total_seconds(), 2)
        device['sampled_energy_wh'] = round(sum(
            (right[0] - left[0]).total_seconds() * (left[1] + right[1]) / 2
            for left, right in zip(samples, samples[1:])) / 3600, 3)
        buckets = {}
        for timestamp, power, temperature in samples:
            minute = int((timestamp - samples[0][0]).total_seconds() // 60)
            bucket = buckets.setdefault(minute, {'temperature_c': [], 'power_w': []})
            bucket['temperature_c'].append(temperature)
            bucket['power_w'].append(power)
        device['minute_medians'] = {
            minute: {key: round(statistics.median(values), 2) for key, values in bucket.items()}
            for minute, bucket in buckets.items()}
    drafted = delta.get('llamacpp:spec_decode_num_draft_tokens_total', 0)
    accepted = delta.get('llamacpp:spec_decode_num_accepted_tokens_total', 0)
    prompt_seconds = delta.get('llamacpp:prompt_seconds_total', 0)
    return {
        'arm': arm.name,
        'context': props.get('default_generation_settings', {}).get('n_ctx'),
        'slots': props.get('total_slots'),
        'engine_commit': props.get('build_info'),
        'shallow_rows': len(shallow),
        'errors': [row['error'] for row in shallow + deep if row.get('error')],
        'shallow_decode_median': median(shallow, 'decode_tok_s'),
        'shallow_prefill_server': round(delta.get('llamacpp:prompt_tokens_total', 0) / prompt_seconds, 2)
        if prompt_seconds else None,
        'shallow_acceptance': accepted / drafted if drafted else None,
        'depth': [{key: row.get(key) for key in (
            'depth_target', 'depth_padding_tokens', 'server_high_water_tokens',
            'decode_tps', 'prefill_tps', 'draft_accept_rate', 'error')}
            for row in deep],
        'devices': devices,
        'concurrency': {key: concurrent.get(key) for key in (
            'complete', 'mtp_live', 'requested_requests', 'padding_tokens', 'makespan_seconds',
            'completion_tokens', 'output_tokens_per_wall_second',
            'output_tokens_per_first_token_to_last_finish_second',
            'max_observed_busy_slots', 'mtp', 'requests')} if concurrent else None,
    }


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('root', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--expect-rows', type=int,
                        help='validate one finished arm instead of summarizing the experiment')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.expect_rows is not None:
        measured = rows(args.root / 'engines.jsonl') + rows(args.root / 'depth.jsonl')
        if len(measured) != args.expect_rows or any(row.get('error') for row in measured):
            raise SystemExit('probe has missing or failed measurements; inspect JSONL')
        return
    results = [summarize(arm) for arm in sorted(args.root.iterdir())
               if arm.is_dir() and (arm / 'server.log').exists()]
    print(json.dumps(results, indent=2))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


"""Wake periodically to inspect the live Swift tuning state until morning."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from datetime import datetime, time, timedelta
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import time as walltime


def morning_deadline(now: datetime, target: time = time(8, 0)) -> datetime:
    deadline = now.replace(hour=target.hour, minute=target.minute,
                           second=0, microsecond=0)
    if deadline <= now:
        deadline += timedelta(days=1)
    return deadline


def command(args: list[str]) -> str:
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(args, capture_output=True, text=True,
                                timeout=10, check=False)
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
        return f"error:{type(error).__name__}:{error}"
    return result.stdout.strip()


def units() -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    raw = command(["systemctl", "--user", "list-units", "--all", "--no-legend",
                   "qwen-tuning-*.service"])
    rows = []
    for line in raw.splitlines():
        fields = line.split()
        if len(fields) >= 4:
            rows.append({"unit": fields[0], "load": fields[1],
                         "active": fields[2], "substate": fields[3]})
    return rows


def gpu_processes() -> list[str]:
    raw = command(["nvidia-smi", "--query-compute-apps=gpu_uuid,pid,process_name",
                   "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"])
    return [line for line in raw.splitlines() if line]


def campaign_states(root: Path) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
    rows = []
    for state_path in sorted(root.glob("campaign-swift-tuning-*/state.json"),
                             key=lambda path: path.stat().st_mtime):
        try:
            state = json.loads(state_path.read_text())
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            continue
        rows.append({"campaign": state_path.parent.name,
                     "status": state.get("status"),
                     "completed": len(state.get("completed", {})),
                     "mtime": state_path.stat().st_mtime})
    return rows


def snapshot(root: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
    current_units = units()
    active = [row for row in current_units
              if row["active"] in {"active", "activating", "reloading"}]
    states = campaign_states(root)
    running = [row for row in states if row["status"] == "running"]
    if active or running:
        reason = "campaign_running"
    else:
        reason = "nothing_to_do"
    return {"time": walltime.time(), "reason": reason,
            "units": current_units, "campaigns": states[-8:],
            "gpu_compute": gpu_processes()}


def run(args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
    now = datetime.now().astimezone()
    deadline = morning_deadline(now)
    consecutive_nothing = 0
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    while True:
        now = datetime.now().astimezone()
        if now >= deadline:
            row = {"time": walltime.time(), "reason": "deadline_reached",
                   "deadline": deadline.isoformat()}
            with args.out.open("a") as stream:
                stream.write(json.dumps(row) + "\n")
            return 0
        row = snapshot(args.root)
        row["deadline"] = deadline.isoformat()
        with args.out.open("a") as stream:
            stream.write(json.dumps(row) + "\n")
        if row["reason"] == "nothing_to_do":
            consecutive_nothing += 1
            if consecutive_nothing >= args.nothing_threshold:
                stop = {"time": walltime.time(),
                        "reason": "nothing_to_do_threshold",
                        "consecutive": consecutive_nothing,
                        "deadline": deadline.isoformat()}
                with args.out.open("a") as stream:
                    stream.write(json.dumps(stop) + "\n")
                return 0
        else:
            consecutive_nothing = 0
        if args.once:
            return 0
        remaining = max(0, min(args.interval, (deadline - now).total_seconds()))
        walltime.sleep(remaining)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--root", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--interval", type=int, default=28 * 60)
    parser.add_argument("--nothing-threshold", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--once", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.interval < 1 or args.nothing_threshold < 1:
        parser.error("interval and nothing-threshold must be positive")
    return run(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


"""Periodic evidence-only summary of the fixed local overnight campaigns."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import defaultdict
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import re
import statistics
import time

from scripts.instruments.hillclimb import atomic_json, read_sealed


def read_jobs(campaign):
    if not campaign.exists():
        return []
    return [(path.stem[4:], read_sealed(path)) for path in sorted(campaign.glob("job-*.json"))]


def coding_rows(agentic, corrected_bartowski=None):
    original = read_jobs(agentic)
    if corrected_bartowski is None:
        return original
    expected = {name: row['task'] for name, row in original if row.get('model') == 'bartowski-iq4-xs'}
    manifest = read_sealed(corrected_bartowski / 'manifest.json')
    declared = {job['id']: job['task'] for job in manifest['queue']}
    if (not expected or declared != expected or len(declared) != len(manifest['queue']) or
            any(job['model'] != 'bartowski-iq4-xs' for job in manifest['queue'])):
        raise ValueError('corrected campaign must cover exactly the original Bartowski tasks')
    corrected = read_jobs(corrected_bartowski)
    for name, row in corrected:
        if name not in expected or row.get('model') != 'bartowski-iq4-xs' or row.get('task') != expected[name]:
            raise ValueError('corrected result does not match its declared Bartowski job')
    # Replace the entire incompatible arm, never fill pending jobs from the old failures.
    return [(name, row) for name, row in original if row.get('model') != 'bartowski-iq4-xs'] + corrected


def summarize(root, corrected_bartowski=None):
    throughput = root / "campaign-remaining-quants-20260913-r3"
    agentic = root / "campaign-agentic-20260913"
    rows = read_jobs(root / "campaign-20260912-b") + read_jobs(throughput)
    repeated, slots = defaultdict(list), []
    for name, row in rows:
        if name.startswith("repeat-"):
            repeated[row.get("model", "unknown")].append(row)
        if name.startswith("slots-"):
            slots.append(row)
    quant_stats = []
    for model, records in sorted(repeated.items()):
        passed = [r for r in records if r["status"] == "passed"]
        speeds = [r["decode_tps"] for r in passed]
        quant_stats.append({"model": model, "processed_repeats": len(records), "passed_repeats": len(passed),
                           "median_tg": statistics.median(speeds) if speeds else None,
                           "median_pp": statistics.median(r["prefill_tps"] for r in passed) if passed else None,
                           "minimum_tg": min(speeds) if speeds else None, "maximum_tg": max(speeds) if speeds else None,
                           "qualified_repeat_speed": len(passed) >= 12 and statistics.median(speeds) >= 35})
    tasks = defaultdict(list)
    for _, row in coding_rows(agentic, corrected_bartowski):
        tasks[row.get("model", "unknown")].append(row)
    task_stats, paired = [], []
    baseline = {r["task"]: r for r in tasks.get("iq3-control", [])
                if r.get("probe", {}).get("task_success")}
    for model, records in sorted(tasks.items()):
        measured = [r for r in records if r["status"] == "passed" and r.get("probe", {}).get("complete")]
        probes = [r["probe"] for r in measured]
        wall = sum(p["verified_wall_seconds"] for p in probes)
        prefill = sum(p["prefill_seconds"] for p in probes)
        decode = sum(p["decode_seconds"] for p in probes)
        task_stats.append({"model": model, "processed": len(records), "measured_tasks": len(probes),
                           "solved": sum(p["task_success"] for p in probes),
                           "timed_out": sum(p["timed_out"] for p in probes),
                           "wall_seconds": wall, "prefill_seconds": prefill, "decode_seconds": decode,
                           "other_seconds": max(0, wall - prefill - decode),
                           "prefill_fraction": prefill / wall if wall else None,
                           "decode_fraction": decode / wall if wall else None,
                           "output_tokens": sum(p["metrics_delta"].get("decode_tokens", 0) for p in probes),
                           "sampled_board_wh": sum(r.get("sampled_board_energy_wh", 0) for r in measured)})
        comparisons = [(baseline[r["task"]]["probe"]["verified_wall_seconds"], r["probe"]["verified_wall_seconds"])
                       for r in measured if r["probe"]["task_success"] and r["task"] in baseline and model != "iq3-control"]
        if comparisons:
            paired.append({"model": model, "both_solved_pairs": len(comparisons),
                           "candidate_over_baseline_geomean": math.exp(statistics.mean(math.log(b / a) for a, b in comparisons)),
                           "candidate_over_baseline_total": sum(b for a, b in comparisons) / sum(a for a, b in comparisons)})
    states = {}
    campaigns = [throughput, agentic] + ([corrected_bartowski] if corrected_bartowski else [])
    for campaign in campaigns:
        for filename in ("state.json", "stage-status.json"):
            if corrected_bartowski and filename == 'stage-status.json':
                continue
            path = campaign / filename
            if path.exists():
                state = json.loads(path.read_text())
                states[campaign.name + "/" + filename] = {
                    "status": state.get("status", state.get("phase")), "reason": state.get("reason"),
                    "completed": len(state.get("completed", {})),
                    "active": state.get("active", {}).get("id") if state.get("active") else None}
    return {"updated_epoch": time.time(), "states": states, "near_100k_repeats": quant_stats,
            "corrected_bartowski": str(corrected_bartowski) if corrected_bartowski else None,
            "task_details": {model: {r['task']: {
                "status": r['status'], "reason": r.get('reason'),
                "solved": r.get('probe', {}).get('task_success', False),
                "wall_seconds": r.get('probe', {}).get('verified_wall_seconds')}
                for r in records} for model, records in tasks.items()},
            "concurrency_jobs_processed": len(slots),
            "concurrency_jobs_passed": sum(r["status"] == "passed" for r in slots),
            "real_task_totals": task_stats, "paired_both_solved_only": paired}


def render(summary):
    title = "# Coding pilot comparison — corrected Bartowski" if summary.get('corrected_bartowski') else "# Overnight evidence report"
    lines = [title, "", "Updated: " + time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S %Z"), "",
             "Live results are provisional until queues finish. No automatic production-profile promotion.", "",
             "## Queue state", ""]
    for name, state in summary["states"].items():
        lines.append(f"- `{name}`: {state['status']}; {state['completed']} recorded jobs; active `{state['active']}`; reason `{state['reason']}`.")
    if summary.get('corrected_bartowski'):
        lines += ["", "Bartowski coding source: `" + summary['corrected_bartowski'] + "`.",
                  "This replaces the original incompatible-template arm for comparison; original failures remain unchanged on disk.",
                  "The two successful template-validation solves are reused, not repeated. The other arms are the overnight observations.",
                  "Daytime desktop activity and chronological order differ; this is a small paired pilot, not a randomized replicated trial."]
    lines += ["", "## Repeated near-100k throughput", "",
              "Each request uses about 98k input and up to 2,048 output; these are not graded solves.",
              "Desktop and CPU activity varied; compare repeated medians, not a single fastest sample.", "",
              "| Quant | Valid / processed repeats | Median PP | Median TG | TG range | >=35 TG with 12 valid repeats |",
              "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|"]
    for row in summary["near_100k_repeats"]:
        fmt = lambda key: "" if row[key] is None else f"{row[key]:.2f}"
        lines.append(f"| {row['model']} | {row['passed_repeats']} / {row['processed_repeats']} | {fmt('median_pp')} | {fmt('median_tg')} | {fmt('minimum_tg')}–{fmt('maximum_tg')} | {row['qualified_repeat_speed']} |")
    lines += ["", f"Concurrent 100k-per-slot configurations: {summary['concurrency_jobs_passed']} passed out of {summary['concurrency_jobs_processed']} processed.",
              "Failure applies to the tested split/batch/cache settings, not every possible concurrency configuration.", "",
              "## Real coding-task pilot", "",
              "Natural tool-loop history, fresh isolated workspaces, trusted external tests, MTP on, no throughput output cap.",
              "Totals include failed/timed-out measured tasks; fit skips and instrument failures are not coding failures.", "",
              "Totals with different task counts are not directly comparable; use the matched-task ratios below.",
              "The single-IQ3 control has 65,536 configured context and paired candidates have 102,400; this compares complete setups, not the GPU alone.", "",
              "| Quant | Solved / measured / processed | Timeouts | Wall s | Prefill s (%) | Decode s (%) | Other s | Output tokens | GPU-board Wh |",
              "|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|"]
    for row in summary["real_task_totals"]:
        pp = row["prefill_fraction"] or 0
        tg = row["decode_fraction"] or 0
        lines.append(f"| {row['model']} | {row['solved']} / {row['measured_tasks']} / {row['processed']} | {row['timed_out']} | {row['wall_seconds']:.1f} | {row['prefill_seconds']:.1f} ({pp:.1%}) | {row['decode_seconds']:.1f} ({tg:.1%}) | {row['other_seconds']:.1f} | {row['output_tokens']} | {row['sampled_board_wh']:.2f} |")
    if not summary["real_task_totals"]:
        lines += ["", "No real coding-task result has been recorded yet."]
    lines += ["", "Board energy includes model startup and both installed boards, including idle 2060 power in the single-card control; it is not wall-plug energy.",
              "Task wall time includes trusted grading but excludes server startup. Inspect failed/retried attempt logs separately for recovery cost.", "",
              "## Paired time to verified solution", "",
              "Only tasks solved by both arms enter these descriptive ratios (candidate / single-IQ3 baseline; lower is faster).",
              "This is conditional on both solving, not an overall success-adjusted ranking. Small or incomplete pilots do not establish superiority."]
    for row in summary["paired_both_solved_only"]:
        lines.append(f"- {row['model']}: {row['both_solved_pairs']} paired solves; geometric ratio {row['candidate_over_baseline_geomean']:.3f}; ratio of totals {row['candidate_over_baseline_total']:.3f}.")
    if summary.get('corrected_bartowski'):
        columns = [('iq3-control', 'Single IQ3'), ('bartowski-iq4-xs', 'Bartowski IQ4'),
                   ('unsloth-q4-m', 'Q4_K_M'), ('unsloth-iq4-xs', 'Unsloth IQ4'),
                   ('jpetrina-iq4-pure', 'jpetrina IQ4')]
        details = summary['task_details']
        lines += ['', '## Per-task verified wall seconds', '',
                  'Only solved tasks display a time. Pending/failed jobs are not zero-duration solves.', '',
                  '| Task | ' + ' | '.join(label for _, label in columns) + ' |',
                  '|---|' + '---:|' * len(columns)]
        for task in sorted(details.get('iq3-control', {})):
            values = []
            for model, _ in columns:
                row = details.get(model, {}).get(task)
                values.append('pending' if row is None else
                              f"{row['wall_seconds']:.2f}" if row['status'] == 'passed' and row['solved'] else
                              row.get('reason') or ('failed tests' if row['status'] == 'passed' else row['status']))
            lines.append('| ' + ' | '.join([task, *values]) + ' |')
    lines += ["", "## Boundaries", "",
              "The single-card fallback is unchanged. Browser closure, driver/P2P/BIOS changes and lower target KV precision are not deployment assumptions.",
              "The old .meta-exposed 78-task runs are not clean quality controls. This eight-task pilot is not a replacement for the later hardened full 78-task evaluation.",
              "See AUTONOMOUS.md and AVENUES.md for outstanding MTP-confidence/draft-KV, NCCL, placement, context-ceiling and soak experiments.", ""]
    return "\n".join(lines)


def write_report(root, corrected_bartowski=None, stem='OVERNIGHT'):
    summary = summarize(root, corrected_bartowski)
    atomic_json(root / (stem + '-SUMMARY.json'), summary)
    temporary = root / (stem + '-REPORT.md.tmp')
    temporary.write_text(render(summary))
    temporary.replace(root / (stem + '-REPORT.md'))


def campaign_terminal(campaign):
    # An error may still be recovered by the bounded service restart policy.
    return json.loads((campaign / 'state.json').read_text())['status'] in ('complete', 'stopped')


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--root", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--deadline-epoch", type=float)
    parser.add_argument('--corrected-bartowski', type=Path)
    parser.add_argument('--output-stem')
    parser.add_argument('--until-complete', action='store_true')
    args = parser.parse_args()
    stem = args.output_stem or ('PILOT-COMPARISON' if args.corrected_bartowski else 'OVERNIGHT')
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9_-]+', stem) or (args.corrected_bartowski and stem == 'OVERNIGHT'):
        parser.error('use a separate safe filename stem for corrected results')
    if args.until_complete and (not args.corrected_bartowski or not args.deadline_epoch):
        parser.error('--until-complete requires a corrected campaign and a fixed deadline')
    next_report = 0
    while True:
        now = time.time()
        terminal = args.until_complete and campaign_terminal(args.corrected_bartowski)
        if now >= next_report or terminal:
            write_report(args.root, args.corrected_bartowski, stem)
            next_report = now + 1800
        if args.deadline_epoch is None or terminal:
            break
        if now >= args.deadline_epoch + 30:
            write_report(args.root, args.corrected_bartowski, stem)
            break
        time.sleep(min(30, max(0, args.deadline_epoch + 30 - now)))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Paired per-task comparison of two legs on the same task set.

Unpaired means are not comparable here: the tasks differ in difficulty by more
than the quants differ from each other.

Companion to analyze_paired.py, which compares pass/fail via discordant pairs.
Use that one when outcomes differ; this one when they do not -- once both legs
solve everything, McNemar has nothing to work with and the whole signal is in
what each answer cost.

Report the sign test before the ratio. Costs are heavy-tailed (one leg's median
is 5.5K completion tokens against a 41K max), so a ratio built from means is set
by whichever task ran away: the same pair of legs read as 1.39x by mean of totals
and 1.28x paired, and the second is accompanied by 23 of 39 tasks -- a coin flip.
"""
import argparse, json, math, statistics as st, sys

FIELDS = ("decode_tokens", "completion_tokens", "wall_seconds", "decode_tps")


def load(paths, idx, quant=None):
    """Rows for one leg, keyed by task.

    Refuses a task that arrives twice under different quants. Run indices are unique
    within a file but not across quants in it, so keying on the index alone lets a
    second quant's rows overwrite the first's and the comparison then runs on a
    silently mixed leg. Pass `quant` to name which one is wanted.
    """
    rows = {}
    for path in paths:
        with open(path) as handle:
            for line in handle:
                line = line.strip()
                if not line:
                    continue
                d = json.loads(line)
                if d.get("run_index") != idx or (quant is not None and d.get("quant") != quant):
                    continue
                seen = rows.get(d["task_id"])
                if seen is not None and seen.get("quant") != d.get("quant"):
                    sys.exit(f"run {idx} holds {seen.get('quant')} and {d.get('quant')} "
                             f"for {d['task_id']}; name one with --base-quant/--cand-quant")
                rows[d["task_id"]] = d
    return rows


def sign_p(wins: int, n: int) -> float:
    """Two-sided exact sign test. Assumes nothing about the cost distribution."""
    if n == 0:
        return 1.0
    pmf = [math.comb(n, k) * 0.5 ** n for k in range(n + 1)]
    return min(1.0, 2 * min(sum(pmf[: wins + 1]), sum(pmf[wins:])))


def summarise(ratios: list[float]) -> dict:
    logs = [math.log(r) for r in ratios]
    gm = math.exp(st.mean(logs))
    sd = st.stdev(logs) if len(logs) > 1 else 0.0
    se = sd / math.sqrt(len(logs)) if len(logs) > 1 else 0.0
    wins = sum(1 for r in ratios if r > 1)
    return {
        "n": len(ratios), "geometric": gm, "log_sd": sd,
        "lo": math.exp(math.log(gm) - 1.96 * se), "hi": math.exp(math.log(gm) + 1.96 * se),
        "wins": wins, "p": sign_p(wins, len(ratios)),
    }


def tasks_needed(log_sd: float, ratio: float) -> int:
    """Paired tasks for 80% power at alpha 0.05. Why 40 resolves 1.3x and no finer."""
    return math.ceil(((1.96 + 0.84) * log_sd / math.log(ratio)) ** 2)


def main_argv(argv=None):
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--path", action="append", default=None,
                    help="result JSONL; repeat to compare legs written to different files")
    ap.add_argument("--base", type=int, required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--cand", type=int, required=True)
    # Run indices are unique within one file but not across them, and the two legs worth
    # comparing now live in different sessions' files.
    ap.add_argument("--base-quant", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--cand-quant", default=None)
    ap.add_argument("--solved-only", action="store_true",
                    help="pair only tasks both legs solved; a failed task's cost is not a cost")
    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
    paths = a.path or ["results/session1/speed/3090-polyglot.jsonl"]

    base, cand = load(paths, a.base, a.base_quant), load(paths, a.cand, a.cand_quant)
    for rows, idx in ((base, a.base), (cand, a.cand)):
        # load() catches a task claimed by two quants; this catches a leg whose tasks
        # were split between them, which leaves no single task duplicated.
        quants = {r.get("quant") for r in rows.values()}
        if len(quants) > 1:
            sys.exit(f"run {idx} holds more than one quant {sorted(quants)}; not one leg")
    if a.solved_only:
        base = {t: r for t, r in base.items() if r.get("task_success")}
        cand = {t: r for t, r in cand.items() if r.get("task_success")}
    shared = sorted(set(base) & set(cand))
    if not shared:
        sys.exit(f"no shared tasks between run {a.base} and run {a.cand}")

    dropped = (set(base) ^ set(cand))
    print(f"run {a.base} n={len(base)}  run {a.cand} n={len(cand)}  paired={len(shared)}")
    if dropped:
        print(f"unpaired (excluded): {sorted(dropped)}")

    for r, tag in ((base, a.base), (cand, a.cand)):
        ok = sum(1 for t in shared if r[t].get("task_success"))
        e = {r[t].get("engine") for t in shared}
        q = {r[t].get("quant") for t in shared}
        print(f"  run {tag}: {'/'.join(sorted(map(str, e)))} {'/'.join(sorted(map(str, q)))} "
              f"solved {ok}/{len(shared)}")

    print(f"\n{'task':<34}" + "".join(f"{f:>26}" for f in FIELDS))
    ratios = {f: [] for f in FIELDS}
    for t in shared:
        cells = ""
        for f in FIELDS:
            b, c = base[t].get(f), cand[t].get(f)
            if b in (None, 0) or c in (None, 0):
                cells += f"{'-':>26}"
                continue
            ratios[f].append(c / b)
            cells += f"{b:>10.0f} {c:>7.0f} {c/b:>6.2f}x"
        print(f"{t[:33]:<34}{cells}")

    stats = {f: summarise(v) for f, v in ratios.items() if len(v) > 1}
    print(f"\n{'ratio cand/base':<34}" + "".join(f"{f:>26}" for f in FIELDS))

    def row(label, cell):
        print(f"{label:<34}" + "".join(
            f"{cell(stats[f]):>26}" if f in stats else f"{'-':>26}" for f in FIELDS))

    for label, fn in (("median", st.median), ("mean", st.fmean)):
        print(f"{label:<34}" + "".join(
            f"{fn(ratios[f]):>25.2f}x" if len(ratios[f]) > 1 else f"{'-':>26}" for f in FIELDS))
    row("geometric", lambda s: f"{s['geometric']:.2f}x")
    row("95% CI", lambda s: f"[{s['lo']:.2f}, {s['hi']:.2f}]")
    row("tasks cand > base", lambda s: f"{s['wins']}/{s['n']}")
    row("sign test p", lambda s: f"{s['p']:.3f}")

    if "completion_tokens" in stats:
        sd = stats["completion_tokens"]["log_sd"]
        need = "  ".join(f"{r:.2f}x:{tasks_needed(sd, r)}" for r in (1.5, 1.3, 1.2, 1.15, 1.1))
        print(f"\npaired tasks for 80% power on completion_tokens (log sd {sd:.3f}):\n  {need}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main_argv()


"""Post-hoc signatures from sealed coding results, rollouts and server counters."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shlex
import statistics
import time

from scripts.analysis.overnight_summary import campaign_terminal, coding_rows
from scripts.instruments.hillclimb import atomic_json, digest_file, read_sealed


def timestamp(value):
    return datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace('Z', '+00:00')).timestamp() if value else None


def text_size(content):
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return len(content)
    if isinstance(content, list):
        return sum(len(item.get('text', '')) for item in content if isinstance(item, dict))
    return 0


def command_text(arguments):
    command = arguments.get('cmd', arguments.get('command', ''))
    if isinstance(command, list):
        return command[-1] if len(command) >= 3 and command[1] in ('-c', '-lc') else shlex.join(command)
    return command if isinstance(command, str) else ''


def action_tags(name, arguments):
    if name == 'write_stdin':
        return ['poll' if not arguments.get('chars') else 'interactive_input']
    if 'apply_patch' in name:
        return ['edit']
    command = command_text(arguments)
    # Categories describe shell text, not proven intent; mixed calls can have several labels.
    patterns = {
        'edit': r'\b(?:apply_patch|tee)\b|\bcat\s*>|\bsed\s+-i|\.write_(?:text|bytes)\s*\(|open\([^\n]*,[ ]*[\x22\x27][wax]|\bwriteFile',
        'test': r'\bcargo\s+test\b|\b(?:npx\s+(?:--no-install\s+)?)?jest\b|\bnpm\s+(?:run\s+)?test\b|\bpytest\b|\bpython\S*\s+-m\s+unittest\b',
        'lint_format': r'\b(?:eslint|prettier|rustfmt)\b|\bcargo\s+(?:fmt|clippy)\b',
        'build': r'\bcargo\s+(?:build|check)\b|\bnpm\s+run\s+build\b',
        'dependencies': r'\bnpm\s+(?:install|ci)\b|\bpip\s+install\b|\bcargo\s+add\b',
        'inspect': r'\b(?:ls|find|rg|grep|cat|head|tail|wc)\b|\bsed\s+-n\b|\.read_(?:text|bytes)\s*\(',
        'git': r'\bgit\s+(?:diff|status|log|show)\b',
        'cleanup': r'\brm\s',
    }
    # Do not classify embedded source code as shell commands after a heredoc opener.
    shell_head = command.split('\n', 1)[0] if '<<' in command else command
    tags = [tag for tag, pattern in patterns.items() if re.search(pattern, command if tag == 'edit' else shell_head)]
    return tags or ['other']


def interval_union_seconds(intervals):
    total = 0.0
    end = None
    for start, stop in sorted(intervals):
        if stop < start:
            continue
        total += max(0, stop - max(start, end if end is not None else start))
        end = max(stop, end) if end is not None else stop
    return total


def server_requests(log):
    records = {}
    for number, line in enumerate(log.splitlines(), 1):
        task = re.search(r'\| task\s+(\d+)\s*\|', line)
        if not task:
            continue
        key = task[1]
        match = re.search(r'(prompt eval time|(?<!prompt )\beval time)\s*=\s*([\d.]+) ms /\s*(\d+) tokens', line)
        if match:
            row = records.setdefault(key, {'server_task_id': key})
            prefix = 'prefill' if match[1] == 'prompt eval time' else 'decode'
            row[prefix + '_seconds'] = float(match[2]) / 1000
            row[prefix + '_tokens'] = int(match[3])
            row[prefix + '_log_line'] = number
        release = re.search(r'stop processing: n_tokens = (\d+), truncated = (\d+)', line)
        if release and key in records:
            records[key].update(end_context_tokens=int(release[1]), truncated=bool(int(release[2])))
    return [r for r in records.values() if 'prefill_seconds' in r and 'decode_seconds' in r]


def parse_rollouts(paths):
    requests, calls, outputs, command_items, compactions, compact_items = {}, {}, {}, {}, [], {}
    task_starts, task_completions, client_windows, cli_versions = set(), set(), set(), set()
    messages = Counter()
    content_ids = set()
    for path in paths:
        pending = {'reasoning_chars': 0, 'assistant_chars': 0, 'tool_argument_chars': 0, 'call_ids': []}
        thread = str(path)
        for number, line in enumerate(path.read_text().splitlines(), 1):
            row = json.loads(line)
            payload = row.get('payload', {})
            kind = row.get('type')
            now = timestamp(row.get('timestamp'))
            evidence = {'source': str(path), 'line': number}
            if kind == 'session_meta':
                thread = payload.get('id', thread)
                cli_versions.add(payload.get('cli_version', 'unknown'))
            if kind == 'token_usage_record':
                response_id = payload.get('response_id')
                if not response_id:
                    raise ValueError('token usage record has no response ID: ' + str(path))
                usage = payload['usage']
                if response_id in requests:
                    if requests[response_id]['usage'] != usage:
                        raise ValueError('conflicting repeated response usage: ' + response_id)
                    continue
                requests[response_id] = {**evidence, 'response_id': response_id, 'completed_at': now,
                                         'usage': usage, **pending}
                for call_id in pending['call_ids']:
                    calls[call_id]['response_id'] = response_id
                pending = {'reasoning_chars': 0, 'assistant_chars': 0, 'tool_argument_chars': 0, 'call_ids': []}
            elif kind == 'response_item':
                subtype = payload.get('type')
                identity = (thread, subtype, payload.get('id') or number)
                if identity in content_ids:
                    continue
                content_ids.add(identity)
                if subtype in ('function_call', 'custom_tool_call'):
                    call_id = payload['call_id']
                    raw = payload.get('arguments', payload.get('input', ''))
                    try:
                        arguments = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else raw
                    except json.JSONDecodeError:
                        arguments = {}
                    if not isinstance(arguments, dict):
                        arguments = {}
                    command = command_text(arguments)
                    calls[call_id] = {**evidence, 'call_id': call_id, 'name': payload['name'], 'called_at': now,
                                      'actions_inferred': action_tags(payload['name'], arguments),
                                      'process_session_id': str(arguments['session_id']) if 'session_id' in arguments else None,
                                      'command_preview': command[:300], 'command_chars': len(command),
                                      'command_sha256': hashlib.sha256(command.encode()).hexdigest() if command else None}
                    pending['call_ids'].append(call_id)
                    pending['tool_argument_chars'] += len(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else len(json.dumps(raw))
                elif subtype in ('function_call_output', 'custom_tool_call_output'):
                    output = payload.get('output', '')
                    text = output if isinstance(output, str) else json.dumps(output)
                    process = re.search(r'Process running with session ID (\d+)', text)
                    outputs[payload['call_id']] = {'returned_at': now, 'output_chars': len(text),
                        'running_process_session_id': process[1] if process else None,
                        'network_error_in_output': bool(re.search(r'\b(?:ENOTFOUND|EAI_AGAIN|ENETUNREACH|ECONNREFUSED)\b', text)),
                        'rejected_or_tool_error': bool(re.search(r'^(?:\w+ failed:|Error parsing|Error:)', text)),
                        'error_preview': text[:220] if re.search(r'^(?:\w+ failed:|Error parsing|Error:)', text) else None}
                elif subtype == 'reasoning':
                    pending['reasoning_chars'] += text_size(payload.get('content'))
                elif subtype == 'message' and payload.get('role') == 'assistant':
                    pending['assistant_chars'] += text_size(payload.get('content'))
            elif kind == 'compacted':
                compactions.append({**evidence, 'completed_at': now, 'response_id': payload.get('compaction_response_id'),
                                    'summary_chars': len(payload.get('message', ''))})
            elif kind == 'event_msg':
                subtype = payload.get('type')
                if subtype == 'task_started':
                    task_starts.add((thread, payload.get('turn_id')))
                    if payload.get('model_context_window'):
                        client_windows.add(payload['model_context_window'])
                elif subtype == 'task_complete':
                    task_completions.add((thread, payload.get('turn_id')))
                elif subtype == 'item_completed':
                    item = payload.get('item', {})
                    if item.get('type') == 'CommandExecution':
                        command_items[item['id']] = {'exit_code': item.get('exit_code'), 'status': item.get('status')}
                    elif item.get('type') == 'ContextCompaction':
                        compact_items[item['id']] = (payload.get('started_at_ms'), payload.get('completed_at_ms'))
                    elif item.get('type') == 'AgentMessage':
                        messages[item.get('phase') or 'unspecified'] += 1
    compact_ids = {r['response_id'] for r in compactions}
    for request in requests.values():
        request['is_compaction'] = request['response_id'] in compact_ids
    for call in calls.values():
        call.update(outputs.get(call['call_id'], {}))
        call['command_completed_event'] = call['call_id'] in command_items
        call.update(command_items.get(call['call_id'], {}))
    process_origins = {r['running_process_session_id']: r for r in calls.values()
                       if r['name'] == 'exec_command' and r.get('running_process_session_id')}
    for call in calls.values():
        origin = process_origins.get(call.get('process_session_id'))
        if origin:
            call['origin_call_id'] = origin['call_id']
            call['origin_command_preview'] = origin['command_preview']
    return {'requests': list(requests.values()), 'tools': list(calls.values()), 'compactions': compactions,
            'compaction_intervals': [(a / 1000, b / 1000) for a, b in compact_items.values() if a is not None and b is not None],
            'outer_turns_started': len(task_starts), 'outer_turns_completed': len(task_completions),
            'agent_messages': dict(messages), 'client_reported_windows': sorted(client_windows),
            'cli_versions': sorted(cli_versions), 'command_completed_events': len(command_items)}


def session_signature(campaign, row):
    out = campaign / row['attempt']
    probe = row['probe']
    paths = sorted((out / 'codex-state/sessions').rglob('rollout-*.jsonl'))
    if not paths:
        raise ValueError('missing rollout for measured task: ' + str(out))
    parsed = parse_rollouts(paths)
    requests, tools = parsed['requests'], parsed['tools']
    warnings = []
    before = json.loads((out / 'metrics-before.json').read_text())
    after = json.loads((out / 'metrics-after.json').read_text())
    peak = after.get('n_tokens_max')
    if before.get('n_tokens_max') != 0 or peak is None or peak > probe['client_context']:
        warnings.append('fresh-server context high-water could not be validated')
        peak = None
    usage = {key: sum(r['usage'].get(key, 0) for r in requests)
             for key in ('input_tokens', 'cached_input_tokens', 'output_tokens', 'reasoning_output_tokens')}
    delta = probe['metrics_delta']
    if usage['output_tokens'] != delta['decode_tokens'] or usage['input_tokens'] - usage['cached_input_tokens'] != delta['prefill_tokens']:
        warnings.append('rollout/server token totals differ')
    backend = server_requests((out / 'server.log').read_text())
    matched = len(backend) == len(requests) and all(a['usage']['output_tokens'] == b['decode_tokens'] for a, b in zip(requests, backend))
    if matched:
        for request, timing in zip(requests, backend):
            request.update(timing)
    else:
        warnings.append('per-response server timing alignment failed')
    tool_intervals = [(r['called_at'], r['returned_at']) for r in tools if r.get('called_at') is not None and r.get('returned_at') is not None]
    requests_by_id = {r['response_id']: r for r in requests}
    first_edit = next((r for r in tools if 'edit' in r['actions_inferred']), None)
    edit_index = next((i for i, r in enumerate(requests) if first_edit and first_edit.get('response_id') == r['response_id']), None)
    commands = Counter(r['command_sha256'] for r in tools if r['command_sha256'])
    compaction_requests = [r for r in requests if r['is_compaction']]
    compact_before_after = []
    for i, request in enumerate(requests):
        if request['is_compaction']:
            compact_before_after.append({'response_id': request['response_id'],
                'input_tokens': request['usage']['input_tokens'], 'output_tokens': request['usage']['output_tokens'],
                'next_request_input_tokens': requests[i + 1]['usage']['input_tokens'] if i + 1 < len(requests) else None})
    return {'model': row['model'], 'task': row['task'], 'attempt': str(out), 'solved': probe['task_success'],
            'rollout_sessions': len(paths),
            'wall_seconds': probe['verified_wall_seconds'], 'prefill_seconds': probe['prefill_seconds'],
            'decode_seconds': probe['decode_seconds'], 'other_seconds': probe['other_seconds'],
            'outer_turns': parsed['outer_turns_completed'], 'model_responses': len(requests),
            'normal_model_responses': len(requests) - len(compaction_requests),
            'tool_requests': len(tools), 'tool_names': dict(Counter(r['name'] for r in tools)),
            'command_completed_events': parsed['command_completed_events'],
            'actions_inferred': dict(Counter(tag for r in tools for tag in r['actions_inferred'])),
            'action_sequence_inferred': [r['actions_inferred'][0] for r in tools],
            'nonzero_command_exits': sum(r.get('exit_code') not in (None, 0) for r in tools),
            'rejected_or_tool_errors': sum(r.get('rejected_or_tool_error', False) for r in tools),
            'network_error_outputs': sum(r.get('network_error_in_output', False) for r in tools),
            'exact_repeated_commands': sum(n - 1 for n in commands.values()),
            'tool_wait_union_seconds': interval_union_seconds(tool_intervals),
            'generated_tokens': usage['output_tokens'], 'input_tokens_cumulative': usage['input_tokens'],
            'cached_input_tokens_cumulative': usage['cached_input_tokens'],
            'max_response_output_tokens': max((r['usage']['output_tokens'] for r in requests), default=0),
            'max_request_input_tokens': max((r['usage']['input_tokens'] for r in requests), default=0),
            'visible_reasoning_chars': sum(r['reasoning_chars'] for r in requests if not r['is_compaction']),
            'output_tokens_through_first_edit_request': sum(r['usage']['output_tokens'] for r in requests[:edit_index + 1]) if edit_index is not None else None,
            'first_edit_request_response_output': requests_by_id.get(first_edit.get('response_id'), {}).get('usage', {}).get('output_tokens') if first_edit else None,
            'peak_context_tokens': peak, 'configured_context': probe['client_context'],
            'legacy_double_counted_peak': probe.get('legacy_sampled_context_tokens', probe.get('peak_context_tokens')),
            'client_compaction_threshold': probe['client_compaction'],
            'client_reported_windows': parsed['client_reported_windows'],
            'compactions': len(parsed['compactions']), 'compaction_details': compact_before_after,
            'compaction_elapsed_seconds': interval_union_seconds(parsed['compaction_intervals']),
            'compaction_inference_seconds': sum(r['prefill_seconds'] + r['decode_seconds'] for r in compaction_requests) if matched else None,
            'backend_timings_aligned': matched, 'warnings': warnings, 'agent_messages': parsed['agent_messages'],
            'cli_versions': parsed['cli_versions'],
            'sources': [{'path': str(p), 'sha256': digest_file(p)} for p in paths],
            'requests': requests, 'tools': tools, 'compaction_events': parsed['compactions']}


def paired_explanations(signatures):
    baseline = {r['task']: r for r in signatures if r['model'] == 'iq3-control' and r['solved']}
    results = []
    for model in sorted({r['model'] for r in signatures} - {'iq3-control'}):
        pairs = [(baseline[r['task']], r) for r in signatures
                 if r['model'] == model and r['solved'] and r['task'] in baseline]
        totals = dict.fromkeys(['wall_delta', 'prefill_delta', 'decode_token_volume_delta', 'decode_rate_delta', 'other_delta'], 0.0)
        for base, candidate in pairs:
            nb, nc = base['generated_tokens'], candidate['generated_tokens']
            if nb <= 0 or nc <= 0:
                raise ValueError('paired decode decomposition needs positive token counts')
            sb, sc = base['decode_seconds'] / nb, candidate['decode_seconds'] / nc
            # Average both replacement orders so token volume and rate terms sum exactly to decode delta.
            totals['decode_token_volume_delta'] += (nc - nb) * (sb + sc) / 2
            totals['decode_rate_delta'] += (nc + nb) * (sc - sb) / 2
            for target, field in [('wall_delta', 'wall_seconds'), ('prefill_delta', 'prefill_seconds'), ('other_delta', 'other_seconds')]:
                totals[target] += candidate[field] - base[field]
        results.append({'model': model, 'paired_tasks': len(pairs), **totals})
    return results


def analyze(root, corrected):
    original = root / 'campaign-agentic-20260913'
    signatures, excluded = [], []
    for name, row in coding_rows(original, corrected):
        if row['status'] != 'passed':
            excluded.append({'job': name, 'model': row.get('model'), 'reason': row.get('reason')})
            continue
        campaign = corrected if row['model'] == 'bartowski-iq4-xs' else original
        signatures.append(session_signature(campaign, row))
    state = json.loads((corrected / 'state.json').read_text())
    return {'schema_version': 1, 'analyzer_sha256': digest_file(Path(__file__)),
            'updated': datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(), 'bartowski_state': state['status'],
            'sessions': signatures, 'excluded': excluded, 'paired_explanations': paired_explanations(signatures),
            'scope': 'post-hoc eight-task pilot; natural histories; no causal or full-suite claim'}


def analyze_campaign(campaign):
    manifest = read_sealed(campaign / 'manifest.json')
    signatures, excluded = [], []
    for job in manifest['queue']:
        path = campaign / ('job-' + job['id'] + '.json')
        if not path.exists():
            excluded.append({'job': job['id'], 'model': job['model'], 'reason': 'pending'})
            continue
        row = read_sealed(path)
        if row['status'] != 'passed':
            excluded.append({'job': job['id'], 'model': row.get('model'), 'reason': row.get('reason')})
            continue
        signatures.append(session_signature(campaign, row))
    return {'schema_version': 1, 'analyzer_sha256': digest_file(Path(__file__)),
            'updated': datetime.now().astimezone().isoformat(),
            'campaign_state': json.loads((campaign / 'state.json').read_text())['status'],
            'sessions': signatures, 'excluded': excluded, 'paired_explanations': paired_explanations(signatures),
            'scope': 'Post-hoc declared full-suite sessions; natural histories; descriptive, not causal.'}


def render(result):
    rows = result['sessions']
    lines = ['# Coding-session signatures', '', 'Updated: ' + result['updated'], '',
             f"Measured sessions: {len(rows)}; stage: {result.get('campaign_state', result.get('bartowski_state'))}.",
             result['scope'], '',
             '## Aggregate signatures', '',
             '| Model | Sessions | Model responses | Exec / poll requests | Output tokens | Longest response | Largest context | Compactions |',
             '|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|']
    for model in sorted({r['model'] for r in rows}):
        group = [r for r in rows if r['model'] == model]
        lines.append('| ' + ' | '.join(map(str, [model, len(group), sum(r['model_responses'] for r in group),
            f"{sum(r['tool_names'].get('exec_command', 0) for r in group)} / {sum(r['tool_names'].get('write_stdin', 0) for r in group)}",
            sum(r['generated_tokens'] for r in group), max(r['max_response_output_tokens'] for r in group),
            max((r['peak_context_tokens'] for r in group if r['peak_context_tokens'] is not None), default='unknown'),
            sum(r['compactions'] for r in group)])) + ' |')
    lines += ['', '## Output-length distribution by model response', '',
              'Each observation is one server response, including compaction; responses within a task are not independent trials.', '',
              '| Model | Median output | <512 tokens | 512–2,047 | 2,048–8,191 | >=8,192 |',
              '|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|']
    for model in sorted({r['model'] for r in rows}):
        values = [q['usage']['output_tokens'] for r in rows if r['model'] == model for q in r['requests']]
        bins = [sum(n < 512 for n in values), sum(512 <= n < 2048 for n in values),
                sum(2048 <= n < 8192 for n in values), sum(n >= 8192 for n in values)]
        lines.append('| ' + ' | '.join(map(str, [model, statistics.median(values), *bins])) + ' |')
    lines += ['', '## Paired time-difference accounting', '',
              'Candidate minus single IQ3, in seconds, on matching solved tasks; negative is less time.',
              'Decode is split into output-volume and measured-rate terms by averaging both replacement orders. These add to the observed decode difference, not a causal experiment; depth, MTP and content also affect the measured rate.', '',
              '| Model | Pairs | Wall delta | Prefill delta | Decode: token volume | Decode: rate | Other delta |',
              '|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|']
    for r in result['paired_explanations']:
        cells = [f"{r[k]:+.1f}" for k in ['wall_delta', 'prefill_delta', 'decode_token_volume_delta', 'decode_rate_delta', 'other_delta']]
        lines.append('| ' + ' | '.join([r['model'], str(r['paired_tasks']), *cells]) + ' |')
    lines += ['', '## Per-session signature', '',
              '| Model / task | Wall s | Outer / model turns | Tool requests | Output | Max context | Compactions | Tokens through first requested edit |',
              '|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|']
    for r in rows:
        lines.append(f"| {r['model']} / {r['task']} | {r['wall_seconds']:.1f} | {r['outer_turns']} / {r['model_responses']} | {r['tool_requests']} | {r['generated_tokens']} | {r['peak_context_tokens']} | {r['compactions']} | {r['output_tokens_through_first_edit_request']} |")
    lines += ['', '## Inferred tool-action distribution', '',
              'Labels are heuristic and multi-label: a combined edit/test command can count in both columns. Polling is separate from issuing a command.', '',
              '| Model | Inspect | Edit | Test | Lint/format | Poll | Rejected/tool errors | Nonzero exits | Exact repeated commands |',
              '|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|']
    for model in sorted({r['model'] for r in rows}):
        group = [r for r in rows if r['model'] == model]
        counts = [sum(r['actions_inferred'].get(k, 0) for r in group) for k in ['inspect', 'edit', 'test', 'lint_format', 'poll']]
        counts += [sum(r[k] for r in group) for k in ['rejected_or_tool_errors', 'nonzero_command_exits', 'exact_repeated_commands']]
        lines.append('| ' + ' | '.join(map(str, [model, *counts])) + ' |')
    lines += ['', '## Compaction observations', '']
    for r in rows:
        if r['compactions']:
            inference = r['compaction_inference_seconds']
            lines.append(f"- {r['model']} / {r['task']}: {r['compactions']} compaction(s), {r['compaction_elapsed_seconds']:.1f} s elapsed; " +
                         (f"{inference:.1f} s backend inference." if inference is not None else 'backend attribution unavailable.'))
            for c in r['compaction_details']:
                lines.append(f"  Compaction input {c['input_tokens']:,}, output {c['output_tokens']:,}; next request input {c['next_request_input_tokens']}.")
    if not any(r['compactions'] for r in rows):
        lines.append('No persisted compaction events in the measured sessions.')
    lines += ['', '## Tool-wait and environment observations', '',
              'Call-to-result intervals are unioned to avoid double-counting overlap. These include transport and waiting, not just CPU execution.', '',
              '| Model | Observed tool-wait union s | Non-inference wall s | Outputs containing network errors |',
              '|---|---:|---:|---:|']
    for model in sorted({r['model'] for r in rows}):
        group = [r for r in rows if r['model'] == model]
        lines.append(f"| {model} | {sum(r['tool_wait_union_seconds'] for r in group):.1f} | {sum(r['other_seconds'] for r in group):.1f} | {sum(r['network_error_outputs'] for r in group)} |")
