    lines += ['', 'Some optional `npx eslint` invocations tried to fetch an uncached package and failed in the deliberately offline jail. Poll records retain their originating command when its session ID is available. This overhead depends on both model tool choices and the benchmark environment.', '']
    lines += ['', '## Largest single model responses', '',
              '| Model / task | Output tokens | Context entering request | Backend decode s | Compaction |',
              '|---|---:|---:|---:|---|']
    largest = sorted([(r, q) for r in rows for q in r['requests']], key=lambda x: x[1]['usage']['output_tokens'], reverse=True)[:10]
    for r, q in largest:
        value = q.get('decode_seconds')
        seconds = 'unknown' if value is None else f'{value:.1f}'
        lines.append(f"| {r['model']} / {r['task']} | {q['usage']['output_tokens']} | {q['usage']['input_tokens']} | {seconds} | {q['is_compaction']} |")
    lines += ['', '## Measurement contract and caveats', '',
              '- One outer turn can contain many model responses and tool calls. Responses are deduplicated by response ID; compaction requests are included and separately identified.',
              '- Context is the fresh, single-task server high-water counter, checked against its zero starting value and configured capacity. The old sampler added decoded tokens twice; its values are preserved only as excluded legacy data. This does not change task wall time or trusted grades.',
              '- Cumulative input tokens include repeatedly cached prefixes and are not session context size. Client-reported effective windows are stored separately from configured server context and compaction thresholds.',
              '- Compaction evidence comes from persisted compacted records, not keyword searches or guessed context drops. Recorded compaction duration overlaps inference/tool time and must not be added to wall time.',
              '- Tool wait is the union of call-to-result timestamp intervals, including polling/transport. It is not pure CPU time; command exit codes can mask failed tests behind shell pipelines. Trusted grades remain authoritative.',
              '- Visible reasoning is counted in characters, not tokens. The server reports reasoning-token counts as zero despite visible reasoning; output tokens combine reasoning, answers and tool arguments.',
              '- Command categories and the first-edit marker are heuristic. Exact repeated commands need not be wasted work. Examine the linked raw evidence before assigning intent or causality.',
              '- The JSON contains per-request tokens/timings, tool categories/outcomes, timestamps, source lines and rollout hashes. Original results and running snapshots are never edited.',
              '- [Official Codex JSONL event documentation](https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/non-interactive-mode) and [compaction event documentation](https://learn.chatgpt.com/docs/app-server) establish the public event distinction; this parser uses the actual persisted CLI rollout schema.', '']
    warnings = [(r['model'], r['task'], r['warnings']) for r in rows if r['warnings']]
    lines += ['Validation warnings: ' + ('none.' if not warnings else json.dumps(warnings)), '']
    return '\n'.join(lines)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument('--root', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--corrected-bartowski', type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--after-completion', action='store_true')
    parser.add_argument('--deadline-epoch', type=float)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.after_completion and args.deadline_epoch is None:
        parser.error('--after-completion requires a deadline')
    if args.after_completion:
        while not campaign_terminal(args.corrected_bartowski) and time.time() < args.deadline_epoch:
            time.sleep(15)
    result = analyze(args.root, args.corrected_bartowski)
    atomic_json(args.root / 'SESSION-SIGNATURES.json', result)
    temporary = args.root / 'SESSION-SIGNATURES.md.tmp'
    temporary.write_text(render(result))
    temporary.replace(args.root / 'SESSION-SIGNATURES.md')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Regenerate the whole experiment's state into one file.

Written so a cold reader -- a person at breakfast or a fresh agent session -- needs
no context beyond this file. It reads only artifacts on disk, so it stays correct
after the session that produced them is gone.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import statistics
from pathlib import Path

from scripts import ROOT

S1 = ROOT / "results" / "session1"


def rows(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    if not path.exists():
        return []
    out = []
    for line in path.read_text().splitlines():
        if line.strip():
            try:
                out.append(json.loads(line))
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                continue  # torn line from a killed writer
    return out


def rate(rs: list[dict], num: str, den: str) -> float | None:
    n = sum(r.get(num) or 0 for r in rs)
    d = sum(r.get(den) or 0 for r in rs)
    return n / d if d else None


def fmt(x, p=1, dash="--"):
    return f"{x:,.{p}f}" if isinstance(x, (int, float)) else dash


def legs_table(pg: list[dict], lc: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    groups: dict[tuple, list[dict]] = {}
    for r in pg:
        groups.setdefault((r.get("quant"), r.get("run_index"), "polyglot"), []).append(r)
    if lc:
        groups[(lc[0].get("quant"), 64, "longctx-64K")] = lc
    out = ["| leg | n | pass | timeouts | decode tok/s | prefill tok/s | decode tok/task | wall/task |",
           "|---|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|--:|"]
    for (quant, run, kind), rs in sorted(groups.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0])):
        ok = sum(1 for r in rs if r.get("task_success"))
        to = sum(1 for r in rs if r.get("timed_out"))
        dt = statistics.mean([r.get("decode_tokens") or 0 for r in rs]) if rs else 0
        wt = statistics.mean([r.get("wall_seconds") or 0 for r in rs]) if rs else 0
        out.append(
            f"| {quant} {kind} run{run} | {len(rs)} | {ok}/{len(rs)} | {to} | "
            f"{fmt(rate(rs,'decode_tokens','decode_seconds'))} | "
            f"{fmt(rate(rs,'prefill_tokens','prefill_seconds'),0)} | {fmt(dt,0)} | {fmt(wt,0)} s |"
        )
    return out


def paired_tokens(pg: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    """Q4 vs IQ3 on decode tokens to a test-verified solution.

    IQ3 is averaged over its runs so its test-retest noise does not masquerade as a
    quant effect. Timed-out tasks are dropped as missing, never scored as failures.
    """
    q4 = {r["task_id"]: r for r in pg if r.get("quant") == "UD-Q4_K_XL"}
    iq = {}
    for r in pg:
        if r.get("quant") == "UD-IQ3_XXS" and not r.get("timed_out") and r.get("decode_tokens"):
            iq.setdefault(r["task_id"], []).append(r["decode_tokens"])
    pairs = [(t, q4[t]["decode_tokens"], statistics.mean(iq[t]))
             for t in q4 if t in iq and not q4[t].get("timed_out") and q4[t].get("decode_tokens")]
    if len(pairs) < 2:
        return ["_Not enough paired tasks yet._"]
    d = [q - i for _, q, i in pairs]
    n = len(d)
    md, sd = statistics.mean(d), statistics.stdev(d)
    se = sd / (n ** 0.5)
    lo, hi = md - 1.96 * se, md + 1.96 * se
    mq = statistics.mean(q for _, q, _ in pairs)
    mi = statistics.mean(i for _, _, i in pairs)
    return [
        f"- Paired tasks: **{n}**; Q4 lower on **{sum(1 for x in d if x < 0)}/{n}**",
        f"- Q4 {mq:,.0f} vs IQ3 {mi:,.0f} decode tokens/task (**{mi/mq:.2f}x**)",
        f"- Mean paired difference **{md:,.0f}**, 95% CI [{lo:,.0f}, {hi:,.0f}], t={md/se:.2f}"
        f" -> {'**excludes zero**' if lo * hi > 0 else 'spans zero'}",
        "",
        "Correctness is saturated (both quants pass everything scored so far), so this is a",
        "cost difference, not a capability one. Caveat: arXiv:2504.04823 finds quantization",
        "does NOT increase output length -- but it tests W4A16 and above on single-shot",
        "benchmarks, while this is ~3.06-bit in an agentic loop, so the regimes differ.",
    ]


def mtp_table(bench: list[dict]) -> list[str]:
    g: dict[tuple, list[dict]] = {}
    for r in bench:
        g.setdefault((r.get("quant"), r.get("label"), r.get("depth_target")), []).append(r)
    out = ["| quant | MTP | depth | n | tok/s | accept |", "|---|---|--:|--:|--:|--:|"]
    for k, v in sorted(g.items(), key=lambda kv: str(kv[0])):
        tps = [x["decode_tps"] for x in v if x.get("decode_tps")]
        ar = [x["draft_accept_rate"] for x in v if x.get("draft_accept_rate")]
        out.append(f"| {k[0]} | {k[1]} | {k[2]:,} | {len(v)} | "
                   f"{fmt(statistics.mean(tps)) if tps else '--'} | "
                   f"{fmt(statistics.mean(ar),2) if ar else '--'} |")
    return out


def kld_section() -> list[str]:
    logs = sorted((S1 / "rental" / "kld").glob("*.log")) if (S1 / "rental" / "kld").exists() else []
    if not logs:
        return ["_Not run yet._"]
    out = ["| quant | mean KLD | median | 99th pct | same top-1 |", "|---|--:|--:|--:|--:|"]
    for f in logs:
        if f.name in {"base.log", "run.log"}:  # drivers, not measurements
            continue
        text = f.read_text(errors="replace")
        def grab(pat):
            m = re.search(pat, text, re.I)
            return m.group(1).strip() if m else "--"
        out.append(f"| {f.stem} | {grab(r'Mean +KLD *: *([0-9.eE+-]+)')} | "
                   f"{grab(r'Median +KLD *: *([0-9.eE+-]+)')} | "
                   f"{grab(r'99\.0% +KLD *: *([0-9.eE+-]+)')} | "
                   f"{grab(r'Same top p *: *([0-9.]+ *%?)')} |")
    return out


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=S1 / "STATUS.md")
    ap.add_argument("--stamp", default="", help="timestamp string; callers pass date(1)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    pg = rows(S1 / "codegen" / "polyglot.jsonl")
    lc = rows(S1 / "codegen" / "longctx.jsonl")
    bench = rows(S1 / "mtp" / "bench.jsonl")
    env = (S1 / "rental" / "environment.txt")

    doc = [f"# Qwen3.8-27B quantization experiment - status", ""]
    if args.stamp:
        doc += [f"_Generated {args.stamp}._", ""]
    doc += ["Regenerate with `PYTHONPATH=. python3 -m scripts.analysis.status_report`. Reads only on-disk",
            "artifacts, so it is valid after the producing session is gone.", ""]

    doc += ["## Legs", ""] + legs_table(pg, lc) + [""]
    doc += ["## Q4 vs IQ3: decode tokens to a verified solution", ""] + paired_tokens(pg) + [""]
    doc += ["## Speculative decoding (MTP)", ""] + (mtp_table(bench) if bench else ["_Not run._"]) + [""]
    doc += ["## KL-divergence vs Q8_0 reference", ""] + kld_section() + [""]

    if env.exists():
        caps = [l for l in env.read_text().splitlines() if "GeForce" in l and "," in l]
        doc += ["## Environment", "", "```", *caps[:4], "```",
                "",
                "Every tok/s figure above came from a 5090 power-capped by the host, so",
                "absolutes are a floor for an owned card; ratios between quants are sound.",
                ""]

    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.out.write_text("\n".join(doc) + "\n")
    print(f"wrote {args.out} ({len(doc)} lines)")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


"""Read-only, change-only campaign status for a second tmux pane."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import statistics
import sys
import time


WHY = {
    "baseline": "qualify normal-desktop headroom and deep-context decode",
    "split": "compare placement while retaining the 102400-token window",
    "microbatch": "measure prefill workspace at the selected placement",
    "batch": "isolate logical prefill batch size",
    "interaction": "recheck the two best splits with the two best microbatches",
    "layer": "matched layer-split control",
    "mtp": "compare maximum draft lengths by useful throughput",
    "probability": "compare probability floors at the selected draft length",
    "minimum": "isolate minimum draft length",
    "draft-cache": "screen experimental Q8 draft-cache capacity and kernels",
    "swarm": "screen simultaneous slots at the explicitly named per-agent context",
    "solve": "verify a coding task with the trusted grader",
}
EVENTS = {"attempt_start", "attempt_end", "transient_retry", "campaign_stop",
          "campaign_error", "job_end", "job_recovered", "verify_model", "download"}


class EventLog:
    def __init__(self, path):
        self.path, self.offset, self.identity = path, 0, None
        self.rows = []

    def read(self):
        try:
            with self.path.open("rb") as stream:
                stat = os.fstat(stream.fileno())
                identity = (stat.st_dev, stat.st_ino)
                if identity != self.identity or stat.st_size < self.offset:
                    self.rows, self.offset, self.identity = [], 0, identity
                stream.seek(self.offset)
                while line := stream.readline():
                    if not line.endswith(b"\n"):
                        break
                    self.offset = stream.tell()
                    try:
                        row = json.loads(line)
                        if row.get("kind") in EVENTS:
                            self.rows.append(row)
                    except (ValueError, AttributeError):
                        continue
        except FileNotFoundError:
            pass
        return self.rows


def job_for_event(event, queue):
    name = event.get("job", "")
    return next((job for job in queue if name in (job["id"], job["id"] + "-fit")), None)


def signature(job):
    config = job.get("configs", [{}])[0]
    return (tuple(job.get("targets", [])), config.get("context"), config.get("slots"),
            job.get("workload", "throughput"))


def estimate(queue, completed, events, now):
    starts, durations, groups = {}, [], {}
    for event in events:
        job = job_for_event(event, queue)
        if not job:
            continue
        name = job["id"]
        if event["kind"] == "attempt_start":
            starts.setdefault(name, event["time"])
        elif event["kind"] == "job_end" and name in starts and event.get("status") == "passed":
            duration = event["time"] - starts.pop(name)
            if duration > 0:
                durations.append(duration)
                groups.setdefault(signature(job), []).append(duration)
    remaining = [job for job in queue if job["id"] not in completed]
    if not remaining:
        return "ETA: complete"
    if not durations:
        return "ETA: unknown; no completed timed measurements"
    seconds, unmatched = 0, 0
    for job in remaining:
        samples = groups.get(signature(job))
        unmatched += not bool(samples)
        duration = statistics.median(samples or durations)
        if job["id"] in starts:
            elapsed = now - starts[job["id"]]
            if elapsed > duration:
                return "ETA: unknown; active job exceeds its measured estimate"
            duration -= elapsed
        seconds += duration
    end = datetime.fromtimestamp(now) + timedelta(seconds=seconds)
    return f"ETA: {end:%H:%M:%S} (~{seconds / 60:.0f} min)"


def owner_status(state):
    owner = state.get("coordinator", {})
    if state.get("status") != "running" or not owner:
        return None
    try:
        if owner.get("pid_namespace") != os.readlink("/proc/self/ns/pid"):
            return "coordinator liveness unknown across PID namespaces"
        fields = Path(f"/proc/{int(owner['pid'])}/stat").read_text().rsplit(")", 1)[1].split()
        if fields[19] == owner.get("start") and fields[0] != "Z":
            return None
    except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, IndexError):
        pass
    return "coordinator absent; saved running state is stale"


def resolve_campaign(campaign):
    """Follow a newer numbered preparation when the requested path is stale."""
    try:
        state = json.loads((campaign / "state.json").read_text())
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return campaign
    if state.get("status") != "prepared":
        return campaign
    candidates = []
    prefix = campaign.name + "-"
    for sibling in campaign.parent.glob(prefix + "*"):
        if not sibling.is_dir():
            continue
        try:
            sibling_state = json.loads((sibling / "state.json").read_text())
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            continue
        if sibling_state.get("status") in {"running", "complete", "stopped", "error"}:
            candidates.append((sibling_state.get("status") == "running", sibling.stat().st_mtime, sibling))
    if not candidates:
        return campaign
    candidates.sort(key=lambda row: (row[0], row[1]), reverse=True)
    return candidates[0][2]


def render(message, redraw=False, stream=None):
    stream = sys.stdout if stream is None else stream
    if redraw:
        stream.write("\x1b[2J\x1b[H")
    stream.write(message.rstrip() + "\n")
    stream.flush()


def snapshot(campaign, events, now=None, redraw=False, elapsed_since_update=None, display_time=None):
    now = time.time() if now is None else now
    display_time = now if display_time is None else display_time
    manifest = json.loads((campaign / "manifest.json").read_text())
    state = json.loads((campaign / "state.json").read_text())
    queue, completed = manifest["queue"], state.get("completed", {})
    active = state.get("active") or {}
    live = owner_status(state)
    latest = events[-1] if events else {}
    attempt = next((e for e in reversed(events) if e["kind"] == "attempt_start"
                    and active and job_for_event(e, [active])), {})
    config = attempt.get("config") or active.get("configs", [{}])[0]
    status = state["status"]
    # Time and the moving ETA never trigger a new line by themselves.
    key = json.dumps([status, active, completed, state.get("reason"), state.get("resource_wait"),
                      latest, live], sort_keys=True)
    counter = f"{len(completed)}/{len(queue)}"
    if redraw:
        counter = "\x1b[1m" + counter + "\x1b[22m"
    lines = [f"[{datetime.fromtimestamp(display_time):%H:%M:%S}] {campaign.name}: {status}; {counter} jobs"]
    if active and status == "running":
        name = active["id"]
        why = next((text for prefix, text in WHY.items() if name.startswith(prefix + "-")),
                   "execute the next arm in the frozen manifest")
        if "baseline-before" in name or "baseline-after" in name:
            why = "baseline bracket to detect desktop or thermal drift"
        lines.append(f"Arm: {name}. Why: {why}.")
        context, slots = config.get("context"), config.get("slots", 1)
        total = context * slots if context is not None else "unknown"
        lines.append(f"Model: {attempt.get('model', active.get('model', 'unknown'))}; "
                     f"context {context}/agent × {slots} slots = {total} total; "
                     f"{config.get('mode', 'tensor')} split {config.get('split', '69,31')}; "
                     f"batch/ub {config.get('batch', 2048)}/{config.get('ubatch', 128)}; "
                     f"MTP max/min/pmin {config.get('maximum', 3)}/{config.get('minimum', 0)}/{config.get('p_min', 0)}; "
                     f"draft KV {config.get('draft_k', 'f16')}/{config.get('draft_v', 'f16')}.")
        targets = active.get("targets")
        if isinstance(targets, list) and len(targets) == 1:
            targets = targets[0]
        lines.append(f"Task: {active.get('task', 'unknown')}; input target {targets}; "
                     f"seed {active.get('seed', 1729)}.")
    if latest:
        detail = latest.get("reason") or latest.get("status") or latest.get("model") or latest.get("job", "")
        lines.append(f"Update: {latest['kind']} {detail}".rstrip())
    if live or state.get("reason") or state.get("resource_wait"):
        lines.append(f"State detail: {live or state.get('reason') or state.get('resource_wait')}")
    if status == "complete":
        eta = "ETA: complete"
    elif status != "running" or live:
        eta = "ETA: unavailable while the campaign is not running"
    else:
        eta = estimate(queue, completed, events, now)
    if elapsed_since_update is not None:
        lines.append(f"Since update: {max(0, int(elapsed_since_update))}s | {eta}")
    else:
        lines.append(eta)
    return key, "\n".join(lines)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("campaign", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--current-file", type=Path,
                        help="follow a dispatcher-published campaign path")
    parser.add_argument("--interval", type=float, default=10)
    parser.add_argument("--once", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--redraw", action="store_true",
                        help="clear the terminal and redraw the latest changed snapshot")
    parser.add_argument("--timer", action="store_true",
                        help="with --redraw, show seconds since the last state update")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not math.isfinite(args.interval) or args.interval <= 0:
        parser.error("interval must be positive and finite")
    if args.timer and not args.redraw:
        parser.error("--timer requires --redraw")
    log, previous = EventLog(args.campaign / "events.jsonl"), None
    last_change = time.monotonic()
    last_change_wall = time.time()
    last_draw = 0.0
    if args.redraw:
        sys.stdout.write("\x1b[?25l")
        sys.stdout.flush()
    try:
        while True:
            requested_campaign = args.campaign
            if args.current_file:
                try:
                    published = args.current_file.read_text().strip()
                    if published:
                        requested_campaign = Path(published)
                except OSError:
                    pass
            effective_campaign = resolve_campaign(requested_campaign)
            now_monotonic = time.monotonic()
            try:
                if log.path != effective_campaign / "events.jsonl":
                    log = EventLog(effective_campaign / "events.jsonl")
                key, message = snapshot(
                    effective_campaign, log.read(), redraw=args.redraw,
                    elapsed_since_update=(now_monotonic - last_change if args.timer else None),
                    display_time=last_change_wall)
                key = f"{effective_campaign}\0{key}"
                if effective_campaign != requested_campaign:
                    message = f"Following active campaign: {effective_campaign}\n" + message
            except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as error:
                key = message = f"Waiting for readable campaign state: {error}"
            changed = key != previous
            if changed:
                last_change = now_monotonic
                last_change_wall = time.time()
                if args.timer:
                    key, message = snapshot(effective_campaign, log.rows, redraw=args.redraw,
                                            elapsed_since_update=0, display_time=last_change_wall)
                    if effective_campaign != requested_campaign:
                        message = f"Following active campaign: {effective_campaign}\n" + message
                render(message, args.redraw)
                previous = key
                last_draw = now_monotonic
            elif args.timer and now_monotonic - last_draw >= 1:
                _, message = snapshot(effective_campaign, log.rows, now=time.time(), redraw=args.redraw,
                                       elapsed_since_update=now_monotonic - last_change,
                                       display_time=last_change_wall)
                if effective_campaign != requested_campaign:
                    message = f"Following active campaign: {effective_campaign}\n" + message
                render(message, args.redraw)
                last_draw = now_monotonic
            if args.once:
                return
            time.sleep(min(args.interval, 1) if args.timer else args.interval)
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        return
    finally:
        if args.redraw:
            sys.stdout.write("\x1b[?25h")
            sys.stdout.flush()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()




#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build reproducible general, Rust, and JavaScript/TypeScript KLD corpora."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import defaultdict
import dataclasses
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath
import re
import subprocess
import sys

from scripts import ROOT
import tempfile
from typing import Any, Iterable, Iterator
import urllib.request
import zipfile


BUILDER_VERSION = "1"
DEFAULT_TARGET_BYTES = 1_290_590
MIN_BYTES = 512
MAX_BYTES = 262_144
MIN_LINES = 20
MAX_MEAN_LINE_BYTES = 200
MINHASH_PERMUTATIONS = 128
MINHASH_THRESHOLD = 0.80
_U64_MASK = (1 << 64) - 1
_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(rb"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*|[0-9]+|[^\s]")
_EXCLUDED_DIRS = {
    "target",
    "dist",
    "build",
    "out",
    ".next",
    "coverage",
    "node_modules",
    "vendor",
    "third_party",
    "generated",
    "gen",
    "__snapshots__",
    "fixtures",
    "__fixtures__",
    "testdata",
}
_JS_EXTENSIONS = {".ts", ".tsx", ".js", ".mjs", ".cjs"}


@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Source:
    repo: str
    sha: str
    root: Path


@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Candidate:
    repo: str
    sha: str
    path: str
    content: bytes
    sha256: str
    is_test: bool


@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CorpusArtifact:
    data: bytes
    manifest: bytes
    file_count: int


def _run(
    command: list[str],
    *,
    cwd: Path | None,
    timeout: float,
    input_bytes: bytes | None = None,
) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[bytes]:
    return subprocess.run(
        command,
        cwd=cwd,
        input=input_bytes,
        capture_output=True,
        timeout=timeout,
        check=True,
    )


def _safe_cache_name(repo: str, sha: str) -> str:
    return repo.replace("/", "--") + "-" + sha


def _clone_exact(repo: str, sha: str, cache_dir: Path, timeout: float) -> Source:
    root = cache_dir / _safe_cache_name(repo, sha)
    if root.exists():
        head = _run(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=root, timeout=timeout).stdout.strip()
        if head.decode("ascii") != sha:
            raise ValueError(f"cached checkout has wrong revision: {root}")
        return Source(repo, sha, root)

    cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="kld-clone-", dir=cache_dir) as temp_dir:
        staged = Path(temp_dir) / "repo"
        staged.mkdir()
        _run(["git", "init", "--quiet"], cwd=staged, timeout=timeout)
        _run(
            ["git", "remote", "add", "origin", f"https://github.com/{repo}.git"],
            cwd=staged,
            timeout=timeout,
        )
        _run(["git", "fetch", "--depth=1", "origin", sha], cwd=staged, timeout=timeout)
        _run(["git", "checkout", "--quiet", "--detach", "FETCH_HEAD"], cwd=staged, timeout=timeout)
        os.replace(staged, root)
    return Source(repo, sha, root)


def _download_general(config: dict[str, Any], cache_dir: Path, timeout: float) -> bytes:
    expected_archive = str(config["archive_sha256"])
    archive = cache_dir / f"wikitext-2-raw-v1-{expected_archive}.zip"
    cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if not archive.exists():
        request = urllib.request.Request(str(config["url"]), headers={"User-Agent": "kld-corpus-builder/1"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
            data = response.read()
        if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != expected_archive:
            raise ValueError("downloaded Wikitext archive SHA-256 mismatch")
        with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=cache_dir, delete=False) as handle:
            temporary = Path(handle.name)
            handle.write(data)
            handle.flush()
            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
        os.replace(temporary, archive)
    archive_data = archive.read_bytes()
    if hashlib.sha256(archive_data).hexdigest() != expected_archive:
        raise ValueError("cached Wikitext archive SHA-256 mismatch")
    with zipfile.ZipFile(archive) as bundle:
        data = bundle.read(str(config["member"]))
    if hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest() != str(config["file_sha256"]):
        raise ValueError("wiki.test.raw SHA-256 mismatch")
    return data


def _is_excluded(path: str, language: str) -> bool:
    pure = PurePosixPath(path)
    parts = pure.parts
    if any(part in _EXCLUDED_DIRS for part in parts[:-1]):
        return True
    if any(parts[index : index + 2] == ("tests", "baselines") for index in range(len(parts) - 1)):
        return True
    name = pure.name
    if name.endswith((".min.js", ".bundle.js", "_pb.js", ".pb.rs")):
        return True
    if language == "rust" and name.startswith("unicode_tables") and name.endswith(".rs"):
        return True
    if language == "rust":
        return pure.suffix != ".rs"
    return pure.suffix not in _JS_EXTENSIONS or name.endswith(".d.ts")


def _attribute_exclusions(source: Source, paths: list[str], timeout: float) -> set[str]:
    if not paths:
        return set()
    payload = b"\0".join(os.fsencode(path) for path in paths) + b"\0"
    completed = _run(
        [
            "git",
            "check-attr",
            "-z",
            "--stdin",
            "linguist-generated",
            "linguist-vendored",
            "linguist-detectable",
        ],
        cwd=source.root,
        timeout=timeout,
        input_bytes=payload,
    )
    fields = completed.stdout.split(b"\0")
    if fields and not fields[-1]:
        fields.pop()
    if len(fields) % 3:
        raise ValueError(f"unexpected git check-attr output for {source.repo}")
    excluded: set[str] = set()
    for index in range(0, len(fields), 3):
        path = os.fsdecode(fields[index])
        attribute = fields[index + 1].decode("ascii")
        value = fields[index + 2].decode("utf-8", "strict").casefold()
        if attribute in {"linguist-generated", "linguist-vendored"} and value in {"set", "true"}:
            excluded.add(path)
        if attribute == "linguist-detectable" and value == "false":
            excluded.add(path)
    return excluded


def _normalise_content(raw: bytes) -> bytes | None:
    if b"\0" in raw[:8192]:
        return None
    try:
        text = raw.decode("utf-8", "strict")
    except UnicodeDecodeError:
        return None
    return text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace("\r", "\n").encode("utf-8")


def _is_test_file(path: str, content: bytes) -> bool:
    pure = PurePosixPath(path)
    lowered_parts = {part.casefold() for part in pure.parts[:-1]}
    name = pure.name.casefold()
    return bool(
        lowered_parts.intersection({"test", "tests", "__tests__"})
        or ".test." in name
        or ".spec." in name
        or name.startswith(("test_", "tests_"))
        or b"#[test]" in content
        or b"#[cfg(test)]" in content
    )


def collect_candidates(source: Source, language: str, timeout: float) -> list[Candidate]:
    tracked = _run(["git", "ls-files", "-z"], cwd=source.root, timeout=timeout).stdout
    paths = [os.fsdecode(value) for value in tracked.split(b"\0") if value]
    paths = [path for path in paths if not _is_excluded(path, language)]
    excluded = _attribute_exclusions(source, paths, timeout)
    candidates: list[Candidate] = []
    for path in sorted(paths, key=os.fsencode):
        if path in excluded:
            continue
        raw = (source.root / path).read_bytes()
        if not MIN_BYTES <= len(raw) <= MAX_BYTES:
            continue
        normalised = _normalise_content(raw)
        if normalised is None:
            continue
        lines = normalised.splitlines()
        if len(lines) < MIN_LINES:
            continue
        if sum(map(len, lines)) / len(lines) > MAX_MEAN_LINE_BYTES:
            continue
        emitted = normalised.rstrip(b"\n")
        digest = hashlib.sha256(emitted).hexdigest()
        candidates.append(
            Candidate(source.repo, source.sha, path, emitted, digest, _is_test_file(path, emitted))
        )
    return candidates


def _shingle_hashes(content: bytes) -> frozenset[int]:
    tokens = _TOKEN_RE.findall(content)
    if len(tokens) < 5:
        tokens = [content]
    shingles: set[int] = set()
    windows: Iterable[bytes]
    if len(tokens) == 1:
        windows = tokens
    else:
        windows = (b"\0".join(tokens[index : index + 5]) for index in range(len(tokens) - 4))
    for window in windows:
        shingles.add(int.from_bytes(hashlib.blake2b(window, digest_size=8).digest(), "big"))
    return frozenset(shingles)


def minhash_signature(shingles: frozenset[int]) -> tuple[int, ...]:
    minima = [_U64_MASK] * MINHASH_PERMUTATIONS
    for value in shingles:
        second = ((value << 17) | (value >> 47)) & _U64_MASK
        second |= 1
        for permutation in range(MINHASH_PERMUTATIONS):
            permuted = (value + permutation * second + permutation * permutation * 0x9E3779B97F4A7C15) & _U64_MASK
            if permuted < minima[permutation]:
                minima[permutation] = permuted
    return tuple(minima)


def deduplicate(candidates: Iterable[Candidate]) -> list[Candidate]:
    ordered = sorted(candidates, key=lambda item: (os.fsencode(item.path), os.fsencode(item.repo)))
    exact: set[str] = set()
    signatures: list[tuple[int, ...]] = []
    accepted: list[Candidate] = []
    buckets: dict[tuple[int, tuple[int, ...]], list[int]] = defaultdict(list)
    rows_per_band = 4
    for candidate in ordered:
        if candidate.sha256 in exact:
            continue
        shingles = _shingle_hashes(candidate.content)
        signature = minhash_signature(shingles)
        possible: set[int] = set()
        for band in range(MINHASH_PERMUTATIONS // rows_per_band):
            start = band * rows_per_band
            possible.update(buckets[(band, signature[start : start + rows_per_band])])
        duplicate = any(
            sum(left == right for left, right in zip(signature, signatures[index]))
            / MINHASH_PERMUTATIONS
            >= MINHASH_THRESHOLD
            for index in possible
        )
        if duplicate:
            continue
        index = len(accepted)
        accepted.append(candidate)
        signatures.append(signature)
        exact.add(candidate.sha256)
        for band in range(MINHASH_PERMUTATIONS // rows_per_band):
            start = band * rows_per_band
            buckets[(band, signature[start : start + rows_per_band])].append(index)
    return accepted


def round_robin(groups: list[list[Candidate]]) -> Iterator[Candidate]:
    positions = [0] * len(groups)
    while True:
        yielded = False
        for index, group in enumerate(groups):
            if positions[index] < len(group):
                yield group[positions[index]]
                positions[index] += 1
                yielded = True
        if not yielded:
            return


def serialize_candidates(candidates: Iterable[Candidate], target_bytes: int) -> CorpusArtifact:
    data = bytearray()
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    test_bytes = 0
    for candidate in candidates:
        separator_size = 2 if data else 0
        projected_size = len(data) + separator_size + len(candidate.content) + 1
        if projected_size > target_bytes:
            break
        projected_test = test_bytes + (len(candidate.content) if candidate.is_test else 0)
        if candidate.is_test and projected_test * 4 > projected_size:
            continue
        if data:
            data.extend(b"\n\n")
        start = len(data)
        data.extend(candidate.content)
        end = len(data)
        rows.append(
            {
                "repo": candidate.repo,
                "sha": candidate.sha,
                "path": candidate.path,
                "sha256": candidate.sha256,
                "byte_start": start,
                "byte_end": end,
            }
        )
        test_bytes = projected_test
    data.extend(b"\n")
    manifest = b"".join(
        (json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
        for row in rows
    )
    return CorpusArtifact(bytes(data), manifest, len(rows))


def build_code_corpus(
    source_configs: list[dict[str, Any]],
    language: str,
    cache_dir: Path,
    timeout: float,
    target_bytes: int,
) -> CorpusArtifact:
    sources = [
        _clone_exact(str(config["repo"]), str(config["sha"]), cache_dir, timeout)
        for config in source_configs
    ]
    candidates = deduplicate(
        candidate
        for source in sources
        for candidate in collect_candidates(source, language, timeout)
    )
    by_repo: dict[str, list[Candidate]] = defaultdict(list)
    for candidate in candidates:
        by_repo[candidate.repo].append(candidate)
    groups = [sorted(by_repo[source.repo], key=lambda item: os.fsencode(item.path)) for source in sources]
    return serialize_candidates(round_robin(groups), target_bytes)


def _general_artifact(config: dict[str, Any], data: bytes) -> CorpusArtifact:
    row = {
        "repo": str(config["repo"]),
        "sha": str(config["archive_sha256"]),
        "path": str(config["member"]),
        "sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(),
        "byte_start": 0,
        "byte_end": len(data),
    }
    manifest = (json.dumps(row, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n").encode()
    return CorpusArtifact(data, manifest, 1)


def build_all(
    sources: dict[str, Any], cache_dir: Path, timeout: float, target_bytes: int
) -> dict[str, CorpusArtifact]:
    general_data = _download_general(sources["general"], cache_dir, timeout)
    return {
        "general": _general_artifact(sources["general"], general_data),
        "rust": build_code_corpus(sources["rust"], "rust", cache_dir, timeout, target_bytes),
        "js-ts": build_code_corpus(sources["js-ts"], "js-ts", cache_dir, timeout, target_bytes),
    }


def _source_revisions(sources: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "general": {
            "url": sources["general"]["url"],
            "archive_sha256": sources["general"]["archive_sha256"],
            "file_sha256": sources["general"]["file_sha256"],
        },
        "rust": [{"repo": item["repo"], "sha": item["sha"]} for item in sources["rust"]],
        "js-ts": [{"repo": item["repo"], "sha": item["sha"]} for item in sources["js-ts"]],
    }


def build_record(
    sources: dict[str, Any], artifacts: dict[str, CorpusArtifact], timestamp: str
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "builder_version": BUILDER_VERSION,
        "timestamp": timestamp,
        "sources": _source_revisions(sources),
        "corpora": {
            name: {
                "sha256": hashlib.sha256(artifact.data).hexdigest(),
                "byte_count": len(artifact.data),
                "file_count": artifact.file_count,
            }
            for name, artifact in artifacts.items()
        },
    }


def _write_outputs(output_dir: Path, artifacts: dict[str, CorpusArtifact], record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for name, artifact in artifacts.items():
        (output_dir / f"{name}.txt").write_bytes(artifact.data)
        (output_dir / f"{name}.manifest.jsonl").write_bytes(artifact.manifest)
    (output_dir / "BUILD.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8"
    )


def _verify(output_dir: Path, artifacts: dict[str, CorpusArtifact]) -> list[str]:
    with (output_dir / "BUILD.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        recorded = json.load(handle)
    failures: list[str] = []
    for name, artifact in artifacts.items():
        actual = {
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(artifact.data).hexdigest(),
            "byte_count": len(artifact.data),
            "file_count": artifact.file_count,
        }
        if recorded.get("corpora", {}).get(name) != actual:
            failures.append(f"{name}: rebuilt metadata differs from BUILD.json")
        manifest_path = output_dir / f"{name}.manifest.jsonl"
        if not manifest_path.is_file() or manifest_path.read_bytes() != artifact.manifest:
            failures.append(f"{name}: rebuilt manifest differs")
    return failures


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    root = ROOT
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Clone exact revisions, filter and deduplicate source files, interleave repositories, "
            "and build byte-reproducible KLD corpora. Normal builds write ignored .txt bytes plus "
            "committable manifests and BUILD.json; --verify rebuilds without overwriting."
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,
        epilog=(
            "Example: python scripts/build_kld_corpora.py --timestamp "
            "2026-08-20T00:00:00Z; then run the same command with --verify and no timestamp."
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--sources",
        type=Path,
        default=root / "corpora" / "sources.json",
        help="pinned source manifest",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, default=root / "corpora", help="corpus and manifest directory")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--cache-dir",
        type=Path,
        default=root / ".cache" / "kld-corpora",
        help="reusable exact-revision checkouts and downloads",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--target-bytes",
        type=int,
        default=DEFAULT_TARGET_BYTES,
        help="maximum serialized bytes for each code corpus; files are never truncated",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=300, help="seconds allowed for each git or HTTP operation")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--timestamp",
        help="literal reproducible timestamp stored in BUILD.json; required unless --verify",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--verify",
        action="store_true",
        help="rebuild and compare SHA-256 metadata and manifests with existing BUILD.json; write nothing",
    )
    return parser


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    if args.timeout <= 0:
        raise SystemExit("--timeout must be positive")
    if args.target_bytes <= 0:
        raise SystemExit("--target-bytes must be positive")
    if not args.verify and args.timestamp is None:
        raise SystemExit("--timestamp is required for a normal build")
    with args.sources.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        sources = json.load(handle)
    artifacts = build_all(sources, args.cache_dir.resolve(), args.timeout, args.target_bytes)
    if args.verify:
        failures = _verify(args.output_dir, artifacts)
        if failures:
            for failure in failures:
                print(f"VERIFY FAILED: {failure}", file=sys.stderr)
            return 1
        print("KLD corpus verification passed", file=sys.stderr)
        return 0
    record = build_record(sources, artifacts, args.timestamp)
    _write_outputs(args.output_dir, artifacts, record)
    for name, metadata in record["corpora"].items():
        print(f"{name}: {metadata['byte_count']} bytes {metadata['sha256']}", file=sys.stderr)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""Derive frozen Polyglot, Diff-XYZ, and SWE-bench Multilingual subsets."""

from __future__ import annotations

# The dataset names its languages only through the log parser it grades with.
# Grading runs inside the instance's own published image, so admitting a language
# costs nothing here -- the earlier rust/jsts restriction left a clean pool of 14,
# which cannot carry a 50-instance subset.
_PARSER_LANGUAGE = {
    "parse_log_cargo": "rust",

# Per language, then per repo within it. Both caps exist so no single repo's idioms
# decide the score; the language cap is not binding for rust or jsts, whose clean
# pools are smaller than it.
_AGENTIC_PER_LANGUAGE = 10
_AGENTIC_PER_REPO = 3


def _run(command: list[str], *, cwd: Path | None, timeout: float) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[bytes]:
    return subprocess.run(
        command,
        cwd=cwd,
        capture_output=True,
        timeout=timeout,
        check=True,
    )


def _cache_name(label: str, sha: str) -> str:
    return label.replace("/", "--") + "-" + sha


def ensure_repo(
    url: str,
    label: str,
    sha: str,
    cache_dir: Path,
    timeout: float,
    *,
    checkout: bool,
) -> Path:
    root = cache_dir / _cache_name(label, sha)
    if not root.exists():
        cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="subset-source-", dir=cache_dir) as temp_dir:
            staged = Path(temp_dir) / "repo"
            staged.mkdir()
            _run(["git", "init", "--quiet"], cwd=staged, timeout=timeout)
            _run(["git", "remote", "add", "origin", url], cwd=staged, timeout=timeout)
            _run(
                ["git", "fetch", "--filter=blob:none", "--depth=1", "origin", sha],
                cwd=staged,
                timeout=timeout,
            )
            _run(["git", "update-ref", "refs/subsets/pinned", sha], cwd=staged, timeout=timeout)
            os.replace(staged, root)
    _run(["git", "cat-file", "-e", f"{sha}^{{commit}}"], cwd=root, timeout=timeout)
    if checkout:
        _run(["git", "checkout", "--quiet", "--detach", sha], cwd=root, timeout=timeout)
    return root


def _canonical_jsonl(rows: Iterable[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
    return b"".join(
        (json.dumps(row, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
        for row in rows
    )


def _load_jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        for line_number, line in enumerate(handle, 1):
            if not line.strip():
                continue
            row = json.loads(line)
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise ValueError(f"{path}:{line_number}: expected a JSON object")
            rows.append(row)
    return rows


def _validate_dataset(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], config: dict[str, Any], label: str) -> None:
    if len(rows) != int(config["row_count"]):
        raise ValueError(f"{label}: expected {config['row_count']} rows, got {len(rows)}")
    digest = hashlib.sha256(_canonical_jsonl(rows)).hexdigest()
    if digest != str(config["jsonl_sha256"]):
        raise ValueError(f"{label}: canonical JSONL SHA-256 mismatch ({digest})")


def fetch_dataset_rows(
    config: dict[str, Any], cache_dir: Path, timeout: float, supplied: Path | None = None
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    label = str(config["dataset"])
    if supplied is not None:
        rows = _load_jsonl(supplied)
        _validate_dataset(rows, config, label)
        return rows
    cache = cache_dir / (_cache_name(label, str(config["revision"])) + ".jsonl")
    if cache.is_file():
        rows = _load_jsonl(cache)
        _validate_dataset(rows, config, label)
        return rows

    revision_url = "https://huggingface.co/api/datasets/" + label + "/revision/" + str(config["revision"])
    request = urllib.request.Request(revision_url, headers={"User-Agent": "frozen-subset-builder/1"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
        revision = json.load(response)
    if revision.get("sha") != config["revision"]:
        raise ValueError(f"{label}: Hugging Face did not resolve the pinned revision")

    rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    expected_count = int(config["row_count"])
    for offset in range(0, expected_count, 100):
        query = urllib.parse.urlencode(
            {
                "dataset": label,
                "config": "default",
                "split": "test",
                "offset": offset,
                "length": min(100, expected_count - offset),
            }
        )
        page_request = urllib.request.Request(
            "https://datasets-server.huggingface.co/rows?" + query,
            headers={"User-Agent": "frozen-subset-builder/1"},
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(page_request, timeout=timeout) as response:
            page = json.load(response)
        if int(page.get("num_rows_total", -1)) != expected_count:
            raise ValueError(f"{label}: dataset row count changed")
        for expected_index, wrapped in enumerate(page.get("rows", []), offset):
            if wrapped.get("row_idx") != expected_index:
                raise ValueError(f"{label}: non-contiguous row indices")
            if wrapped.get("truncated_cells"):
                raise ValueError(f"{label}: row {expected_index} contains truncated cells")
            row = wrapped.get("row")
            if not isinstance(row, dict):
                raise ValueError(f"{label}: row {expected_index} is not an object")
            rows.append(row)
    _validate_dataset(rows, config, label)
    cache.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    data = _canonical_jsonl(rows)
    with tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(dir=cache.parent, delete=False) as handle:
        temporary = Path(handle.name)
        handle.write(data)
        handle.flush()
        os.fsync(handle.fileno())
    os.replace(temporary, cache)
    return rows


def _rank(namespace: str, task_id: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(f"{SEED_PREFIX}|{namespace}|{task_id}".encode()).hexdigest()


def derive_codegen(
    polyglot_root: Path,
    manifest_path: Path,
    source_repo: str,
    source_sha: str,
    excluded: frozenset[str] = frozenset(),
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Select every *viable* exercise in the two languages, ordered by seeded rank.

    Viability is not a judgement call: some Exercism exercises ship a reference
    example that cannot compile as given (crates used but never declared in
    Cargo.toml), so neither a model nor the reference can pass them. They are
    identified by running selftest_polyglot.py over the full pool and recorded in
    subsets/excluded.json, which keeps the selection deterministic and auditable
    rather than silently scoring unscoreable tasks.

    It took the first 20 per language, which resolves a 1.3x cost difference and
    nothing finer -- the measured per-task log-ratio spread needs ~160 paired tasks
    for 1.15x. Taking the whole pool is the only free way to buy that, and it extends
    rather than resamples: every task the first 40 held is still here, so the legs
    already run against them stay comparable.
    """

    tasks: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for language in ("rust", "javascript"):
        exercise_root = polyglot_root / language / "exercises" / "practice"
        ranked = sorted(
            (
                (_rank("codegen", f"{language}/{path.name}"), f"{language}/{path.name}", path)
                for path in exercise_root.iterdir()
                if path.is_dir()
            ),
            key=lambda item: (item[0], item[1]),
        )
        if len(ranked) != {"rust": 30, "javascript": 49}[language]:
            raise ValueError(f"unexpected {language} Polyglot pool size: {len(ranked)}")
        viable = [item for item in ranked if item[1] not in excluded]
        if len(viable) < 20:
            raise ValueError(
                f"only {len(viable)} viable {language} exercises after exclusions; need 20"
            )
        for rank, task_id, source in viable:
            with (source / ".meta" / "config.json").open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                files = json.load(handle)["files"]
            tasks.append(
                {
                    "task_id": task_id,
                    "language": language,
                    "repo": source_repo,
                    "source_sha": source_sha,
                    "source_dir": os.path.relpath(source, manifest_path.resolve().parent),
                    "solution_path": files["solution"],
                    "example_path": files["example"],
                    "test_cmd": (
                        ["cargo", "test", "--", "--include-ignored"]
                        if language == "rust"
                        else ["npm", "test", "--", "--runInBand"]
                    ),
                    "rank": rank,
                }
            )
    return tasks


def _repo_key(repo: str) -> str:
    return repo.casefold()


def derive_diffxyz(
    rows: Iterable[dict[str, Any]], excluded_repos: set[str]
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    selected: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    excluded = {_repo_key(repo) for repo in excluded_repos}
    for language in ("rust", "javascript"):
        ranked: list[tuple[str, str, str, dict[str, Any]]] = []
        for row in rows:
            if row.get("lang") != language or _repo_key(str(row.get("repo", ""))) in excluded:
                continue
            old_code = row.get("old_code")
            if not isinstance(old_code, str) or not 200 <= len(old_code) <= 12_000:
                continue
            identity = f"{row['repo']}@{row['commit']}:{row['path']}"
            ranked.append((_rank("diffxyz", identity), str(row["repo"]), str(row["path"]), row))
        counts: Counter[str] = Counter()
        language_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        for rank, repo, path, row in sorted(ranked, key=lambda item: (item[0], item[1], item[2])):
            repo_key = _repo_key(repo)
            if counts[repo_key] >= 3:
                continue
            identity = f"{repo}@{row['commit']}:{path}"
            language_rows.append(
                {
                    "task_id": identity,
                    "language": language,
                    "repo": repo,
                    "commit": row["commit"],
                    "path": path,
                    "old_code": row["old_code"],
                    "new_code": row["new_code"],
                    "udiff": row["udiff"],
                    "search-replace": row["search-replace"],
                    "rank": rank,
                }
            )
            counts[repo_key] += 1
            if len(language_rows) == 25:
                break
        if len(language_rows) != 25:
            raise ValueError(f"Diff-XYZ produced only {len(language_rows)} {language} rows")
        selected.extend(language_rows)
    return selected


def multi_swe_repo_set(root: Path, sha: str, timeout: float) -> set[str]:
    # Every language directory. A repo appears under exactly one of them, so naming a
    # subset of the directories silently admits every other language's contaminated repos.
    completed = _run(
        ["git", "ls-tree", "-r", "--name-only", sha],
        cwd=root,
        timeout=timeout,
    )
    repos: set[str] = set()
    for raw_path in completed.stdout.decode("utf-8").splitlines():
        filename = Path(raw_path).name
        suffix = "_dataset.jsonl"
        if filename.endswith(suffix) and "__" in filename:
            owner, repo = filename[: -len(suffix)].split("__", 1)
            repos.add(f"{owner}/{repo}")
    if not repos:
        raise ValueError("Multi-SWE-bench repository set is empty")
    return repos


def load_submission_results(root: Path, expected_count: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    paths = sorted((root / "evaluation" / "multilingual").glob("*/per_instance_details.json"))
    if len(paths) != expected_count:
        raise ValueError(f"expected {expected_count} multilingual submissions, found {len(paths)}")
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for path in paths:
        with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            value = json.load(handle)
        if not isinstance(value, dict):
            raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected a JSON object")
        results.append(value)
    return results


def derive_agentic(
    rows: Iterable[dict[str, Any]],
    submissions: list[dict[str, Any]],
    leakage_repos: set[str],
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Select the instances by seeded rank, capped per language and per repo.

    The 0.25-0.80 solve-rate window is the whole design: an instance every submitted
    system solves and one none of them solve both score the same for every quant, so
    neither can separate anything. It is deliberately unchanged here -- what widened
    is the set of languages, which the window then filters as before.
    """
    leakage = {_repo_key(repo) for repo in leakage_repos}
    pools: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str, dict[str, Any], float]]] = defaultdict(list)
    for row in rows:
        language = _PARSER_LANGUAGE.get(row.get("log_parser"))
        if language is None or _repo_key(str(row.get("repo", ""))) in leakage:
            continue
        instance_id = str(row["instance_id"])
        solve_rate = sum(
            bool(result.get(instance_id, {}).get("resolved")) for result in submissions
        ) / len(submissions)
        if 0.25 <= solve_rate <= 0.80:
            pools[language].append((_rank("agentic", instance_id), instance_id, row, solve_rate))

    chosen: dict[str, tuple[dict[str, Any], float, str, str]] = {}
    for language in sorted(pools):
        counts: Counter[str] = Counter()
        accepted = 0
        for rank, instance_id, row, solve_rate in sorted(pools[language], key=lambda item: (item[0], item[1])):
            repo_key = _repo_key(str(row["repo"]))
            if counts[repo_key] >= _AGENTIC_PER_REPO:
                continue
            chosen[instance_id] = (row, solve_rate, rank, language)
            counts[repo_key] += 1
            accepted += 1
            if accepted == _AGENTIC_PER_LANGUAGE:
                break
    if set(chosen) != set(EXPECTED_AGENTIC_IDS):
        missing = sorted(set(EXPECTED_AGENTIC_IDS) - set(chosen))
        extra = sorted(set(chosen) - set(EXPECTED_AGENTIC_IDS))
        raise AssertionError(f"agentic derivation changed; missing={missing}, extra={extra}")
    # The 10-instance subset must stay a prefix of this one, or the legs already run
    # against it stop being comparable with anything measured from here on.
    if not set(ORIGINAL_AGENTIC_IDS) <= set(chosen):
        raise AssertionError(
            f"lost original instances: {sorted(set(ORIGINAL_AGENTIC_IDS) - set(chosen))}"
        )

    output: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for instance_id in EXPECTED_AGENTIC_IDS:
        row, solve_rate, rank, language = chosen[instance_id]
        output.append(
            {
                "instance_id": instance_id,
                "repo": row["repo"],
                "base_commit": row["base_commit"],
                "problem_statement": row["problem_statement"],
                "image": "swebench/sweb.eval.x86_64."
                + instance_id.replace("__", "_1776_")
                + ":latest",
                "language": language,
                "solve_rate": solve_rate,
                "rank": rank,
            }
        )
    return output


def _write_json(path: Path, value: Any) -> bytes:
    data = (json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n").encode("utf-8")
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_bytes(data)
    return data


def _write_jsonl(path: Path, rows: Iterable[dict[str, Any]]) -> bytes:
    data = _canonical_jsonl(rows)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_bytes(data)
    return data


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    root = ROOT
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Fetch exact source snapshots and deterministically materialize 79 Polyglot tasks, "
            "50 Diff-XYZ rows with both generation targets, and 52 SWE-bench Multilingual instances. "
            "Fetched JSON rows and Git checkouts are cached, so reruns are offline."
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,
        epilog=(
            "Example: python scripts/make_subsets.py. After the first cached run no network is "
            "needed; --diffxyz-jsonl and --swebench-jsonl accept pre-fetched canonical exports."
        ),
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--sources",
        type=Path,
        default=root / "subsets" / "sources.json",
        help="pinned upstream revisions and canonical dataset hashes",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--corpus-sources",
        type=Path,
        default=root / "corpora" / "sources.json",
        help="L1 corpus source manifest used by the Diff-XYZ leakage filter",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output-dir",
        type=Path,
        default=root / "subsets",
        help="destination for polyglot.json, diffxyz.jsonl, swebench.jsonl, and BUILD.json",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--cache-dir",
        type=Path,
        default=root / ".cache" / "subsets",
        help="reusable exact-revision Git and canonical JSONL cache",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--diffxyz-jsonl",
        type=Path,
        help="offline canonical Diff-XYZ JSONL export; its pinned SHA-256 is required",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--swebench-jsonl",
        type=Path,
        help="offline canonical SWE-bench Multilingual JSONL export; its pinned SHA-256 is required",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=300, help="seconds allowed for each Git or HTTP operation")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--exclude",
        help="JSON file with task_ids proven unscoreable by the harness self-test "
             "(default: subsets/excluded.json)",
    )
    return parser


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    if args.timeout <= 0:
        raise SystemExit("--timeout must be positive")
    with args.sources.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        sources = json.load(handle)
    cache = args.cache_dir.resolve()
    output_dir = args.output_dir.resolve()

    poly_config = sources["polyglot"]
    polyglot = ensure_repo(
        poly_config["url"],
        poly_config["repo"],
        poly_config["sha"],
        cache,
        args.timeout,
        checkout=True,
    )
    experiment_config = sources["swebench_experiments"]
    experiments = ensure_repo(
        experiment_config["url"],
        experiment_config["repo"],
        experiment_config["sha"],
        cache,
        args.timeout,
        checkout=True,
    )
    multi_config = sources["multi_swe_bench"]
    multi_swe = ensure_repo(
        multi_config["url"],
        multi_config["dataset"],
        multi_config["sha"],
        cache,
        args.timeout,
        checkout=False,
    )

    diff_rows = fetch_dataset_rows(sources["diffxyz"], cache, args.timeout, args.diffxyz_jsonl)
    swe_rows = fetch_dataset_rows(sources["swebench_multilingual"], cache, args.timeout, args.swebench_jsonl)
    leakage_repos = multi_swe_repo_set(multi_swe, multi_config["sha"], args.timeout)
    submissions = load_submission_results(experiments, int(experiment_config["submission_count"]))

    polyglot_path = output_dir / "polyglot.json"
    excluded_path = Path(args.exclude) if getattr(args, "exclude", None) else (
        ROOT / "subsets" / "excluded.json"
    )
    excluded = frozenset(
        json.loads(excluded_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("task_ids", [])
        if excluded_path.is_file()
        else []
    )
    codegen = derive_codegen(
        polyglot,
        polyglot_path,
        str(poly_config["repo"]),
        str(poly_config["sha"]),
        excluded,
    )
    if excluded:
        print(f"excluded {len(excluded)} unscoreable exercise(s): {sorted(excluded)}", file=sys.stderr)
    agentic = derive_agentic(swe_rows, submissions, leakage_repos)
    with args.corpus_sources.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        corpus_sources = json.load(handle)
    l1_repos = {
        item["repo"]
        for language in ("rust", "js-ts")
        for item in corpus_sources[language]
    }
    diffxyz = derive_diffxyz(diff_rows, l1_repos | {row["repo"] for row in agentic})

    outputs = {
        "polyglot.json": _write_json(polyglot_path, codegen),
        "diffxyz.jsonl": _write_jsonl(output_dir / "diffxyz.jsonl", diffxyz),
        "swebench.jsonl": _write_jsonl(output_dir / "swebench.jsonl", agentic),
    }
    build = {
        "generator_version": GENERATOR_VERSION,
        "seed_prefix": SEED_PREFIX,
        "sources": sources,
        "outputs": {
            name: {"sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), "byte_count": len(data)}
            for name, data in outputs.items()
        },
    }
    _write_json(output_dir / "BUILD.json", build)
    print(f"generated {len(codegen)} codegen tasks, {len(diffxyz)} Diff-XYZ rows, "
          f"and {len(agentic)} agentic instances", file=sys.stderr)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Verify that every Polyglot exercise distinguishes a solution from its stub."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
from typing import Any

from scripts.instruments.run_polyglot import (
    _run_tests,
    load_manifest,
    prepare_test_harness,
    task_file_paths,
    task_test_command,
)


_OUTPUT_LIMIT = 8_000


def _captured_output(completed: subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]) -> str:
    output = completed.stdout + completed.stderr
    if len(output) <= _OUTPUT_LIMIT:
        return output
    return "[output truncated]\n" + output[-_OUTPUT_LIMIT:]


def _source_path(task: dict[str, Any], manifest_dir: Path) -> Path:
    source = Path(task["source_dir"])
    return source if source.is_absolute() else manifest_dir / source


def _overlay_reference(task: dict[str, Any], workdir: Path) -> None:
    examples = task_file_paths(task, workdir, "example_path", "example")
    solutions = task_file_paths(task, workdir, "solution_path", "solution")
    if len(examples) != len(solutions):
        matched: list[Path] = []
        for example in examples:
            same_suffix = [solution for solution in solutions if solution.suffix == example.suffix]
            if len(same_suffix) != 1 or same_suffix[0] in matched:
                raise ValueError(
                    "example_path and solution_path cannot be matched uniquely by suffix"
                )
            matched.append(same_suffix[0])
        solutions = matched
    for example, solution in zip(examples, solutions):
        if not example.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"reference example does not exist: {example}")
        solution.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copy2(example, solution)


def _check(
    task: dict[str, Any], manifest_dir: Path, timeout: float, reference: bool
) -> tuple[bool, dict[str, Any]]:
    try:
        command = task_test_command(task)
        source = _source_path(task, manifest_dir)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="polyglot-selftest-") as temp_dir:
            workdir = Path(temp_dir) / "exercise"
            shutil.copytree(source, workdir)
            if reference:
                _overlay_reference(task, workdir)
            replacements = prepare_test_harness(task, workdir)
            completed = _run_tests(command, workdir, timeout)
        check_passed = completed.returncode == 0 if reference else completed.returncode != 0
        return check_passed, {
            "returncode": completed.returncode,
            "javascript_tests_enabled": replacements,
            "output": _captured_output(completed),
        }
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as exc:
        return False, {
            "returncode": None,
            "javascript_tests_enabled": None,
            "output": f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}",
        }


def check_task(
    task: dict[str, Any], manifest_dir: Path, timeout: float
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    task_id = (
        str(task.get("task_id", "<missing-task-id>"))
        if isinstance(task, dict)
        else "<missing-task-id>"
    )
    if not isinstance(task, dict):
        message = "ValueError: each manifest entry must be a JSON object"
        failed = {"returncode": None, "javascript_tests_enabled": None, "output": message}
        return {
            "task_id": task_id,
            "reference_passes": False,
            "stub_fails": False,
            "broken": True,
            "reference": failed,
            "stub": dict(failed),
        }
    missing = [
        field
        for field in ("task_id", "language", "source_dir", "test_cmd")
        if field not in task
    ]
    if missing or not task_id:
        message = f"ValueError: missing required field(s): {', '.join(missing) or 'task_id'}"
        failed = {"returncode": None, "javascript_tests_enabled": None, "output": message}
        return {
            "task_id": task_id or "<missing-task-id>",
            "reference_passes": False,
            "stub_fails": False,
            "broken": True,
            "reference": failed,
            "stub": dict(failed),
        }

    reference_passes, reference_details = _check(task, manifest_dir, timeout, True)
    stub_fails, stub_details = _check(task, manifest_dir, timeout, False)
    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "reference_passes": reference_passes,
        "stub_fails": stub_fails,
        "broken": not (reference_passes and stub_fails),
        "reference": reference_details,
        "stub": stub_details,
    }


def _diagnostic(result: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    failures: list[str] = []
    if not result["reference_passes"]:
        failures.append(
            f"reference solution did not pass ({result['reference']['output'].strip() or 'no output'})"
        )
    if not result["stub_fails"]:
        returncode = result["stub"]["returncode"]
        if returncode == 0:
            failures.append("untouched stub passed (tests do not reject the stub)")
        else:
            failures.append(
                f"untouched stub check could not run ({result['stub']['output'].strip() or 'no output'})"
            )
    return f"BROKEN {result['task_id']}: " + "; ".join(failures)


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Require the reference to pass and the untouched stub to fail for every task.",
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,
    )
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path, help="JSON self-test report")
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=300, help="seconds allowed per check")
    return parser


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    if args.timeout <= 0:
        raise SystemExit("--timeout must be positive")
    tasks = load_manifest(args.manifest)
    manifest_dir = args.manifest.resolve().parent
    results = [check_task(task, manifest_dir, args.timeout) for task in tasks]
    report = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "manifest": str(args.manifest.resolve()),
        "harness_verified": bool(results) and all(not result["broken"] for result in results),
        "tasks": results,
    }
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    args.out.write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")

    broken = [result for result in results if result["broken"]]
    if broken:
        for result in broken:
            print(_diagnostic(result), file=sys.stderr)
        print(
            f"POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST FAILED: {len(broken)} of {len(results)} task(s) BROKEN",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
        return 1
    print(f"POLYGLOT HARNESS VERIFIED: {len(results)} task(s)", file=sys.stderr)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())




"""Gated, resumable real-task stage behind the overnight throughput queue."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import hillclimb as h
from scripts.instruments.chat_templates import BARTOWSKI_PREFIX


MODEL_ORDER = ["iq3-control", "bartowski-iq4-xs", "unsloth-q4-m", "unsloth-iq4-xs",
               "jpetrina-iq4-pure", "unsloth-q4-xl"]
TASK_ORDER = ["javascript/affine-cipher", "rust/word-count", "javascript/bowling", "rust/forth",
              "javascript/alphametics", "rust/robot-name", "javascript/connect", "rust/doubly-linked-list"]


def task_queue(task_ids=None, model_ids=None):
    return [{"id": "solve-" + model + "-" + task.replace("/", "-"), "kind": "profile",
             "model": model, "task": task, "workload": "agentic", "targets": [0],
             "configs": [{"context": 65536 if model == "iq3-control" else 102400,
                          "slots": 1, "mode": "tensor", "split": "70,30" if model == "unsloth-q4-xl" else "69,31",
                          "ubatch": 512 if model == "iq3-control" else 128, "maximum": 3,
                          **({"chat_template": BARTOWSKI_PREFIX} if model == "bartowski-iq4-xs" else {})}]}
            for task in (TASK_ORDER if task_ids is None else task_ids)
            for model in (MODEL_ORDER if model_ids is None else model_ids)]


class AgenticRunner(h.Runner):
    def execute_job(self, job):
        config = {"devices": h.DEVICES, **job["configs"][0]}
        if job["model"] == "iq3-control":
            config.update(devices=[h.GPU0], mode="layer", split="1")
        model = self.ensure_model(job["model"])
        fit = self.attempt("agentic-fit-" + job["model"], model, config, 0)
        if fit["status"] != "passed":
            return {"status": "skipped", "model": job["model"], "task": job["task"],
                    "reason": "no_safe_model_fit", "fit_reason": fit.get("reason")}
        config["workload"] = "agentic"
        result = self.attempt(job["id"], model, config, 0, task=job["task"])
        return {**result, "workload": "agentic"}

    def run_probe(self, config, target, task, seed, out):
        if config.get("workload") != "agentic":
            return super().run_probe(config, target, task, seed, out)
        command = [sys.executable, "-m", "scripts.instruments.agentic_probe", "probe",
                   "--assets", str(self.snapshot / "agentic-assets"), "--task", task,
                   "--url", self.url, "--context", str(config["context"]),
                   "--timeout", "7200", "--out", str(out)]
        child = self.spawn(command, out / "agentic.log")
        return self.wait_child(child, 7250)

    def report(self):
        super().report()
        full_suite = bool(self.manifest.get("continuation"))
        scope = ("All 78 declared tasks; natural task history, not padded-100k throughput probes."
                 if full_suite else "Natural task history; these are not padded-100k throughput probes or the full 78-task suite.")
        lines = ["# Real coding-task " + ("full suite" if full_suite else "pilot"), "", f"Status: {self.state['status']}",
                 f"Processed: {len(self.state['completed'])}/{len(self.manifest['queue'])}",
                 f"Active: {self.state.get('active')}", "",
                 "Full Codex tool loops, isolated reference-free workspace and pristine grading.",
                 scope,
                 "A valid measurement may contain a failed/timed-out task. No throughput generation cap applies.", "",
                 "| Model | Task | Result | Verified wall s | Prefill s | Decode s | Other s | Output tokens |",
                 "|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|"]
        for path in self.state["completed"].values():
            row = h.read_sealed(self.path / path)
            probe = row.get("probe", {})
            outcome = ("solved" if probe.get("task_success") else
                       "timeout" if probe.get("timed_out") else "failed tests") if row["status"] == "passed" else row.get("reason", row["status"])
            values = [probe.get(key) for key in ("verified_wall_seconds", "prefill_seconds", "decode_seconds", "other_seconds")]
            formatted = ["" if value is None else f"{value:.2f}" for value in values]
            tokens = probe.get("metrics_delta", {}).get("decode_tokens", "")
            lines.append("| " + " | ".join([row.get("model", ""), row.get("task", ""), outcome, *formatted, str(tokens)]) + " |")
        temp = self.path / "REPORT.md.tmp"
        temp.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
        os.replace(temp, self.path / "REPORT.md")


def wait_for_predecessor(campaign, predecessor, deadline):
    error_since = None
    while True:
        if (campaign / "STOP").exists() or time.time() >= deadline:
            h.atomic_json(campaign / "stage-status.json", {"phase": "stopped", "reason": "stop_or_deadline", "updated": time.time()})
            return False
        state = json.loads((predecessor / "state.json").read_text())
        if state["status"] == "complete":
            return True
        error_since = (error_since if error_since is not None else time.monotonic()) if state["status"] == "error" else None
        if state["status"] == "stopped" or (error_since is not None and time.monotonic() - error_since >= 180):
            h.atomic_json(campaign / "stage-status.json", {"phase": "blocked", "reason": "predecessor_not_complete",
                                                         "predecessor_reason": state.get("reason"), "updated": time.time()})
            return False
        h.atomic_json(campaign / "stage-status.json", {"phase": "waiting_for_throughput", "updated": time.time(),
                                                     "predecessor_completed": len(state["completed"]), "deadline_epoch": deadline})
        time.sleep(min(15, max(0, deadline - time.time())))


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("command", choices=["prepare", "resume", "status", "stop"])
    parser.add_argument("--campaign", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--assets", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--after-campaign", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--deadline-epoch", type=float)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    campaign = args.campaign.resolve()
    if args.command == "prepare":
        if args.assets is None:
            parser.error("--assets is required")
        assets = args.assets.resolve()
        if not json.loads((assets / "selftest.json").read_text())["harness_verified"]:
            parser.error("trusted grader has not passed its self-test")
        h.initialize(campaign, ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models", profiles=task_queue(),
                     max_attempts=3, agentic_assets=assets)
        print(campaign)
        return
    if args.command == "stop":
        (campaign / "STOP").touch()
        return
    if args.command == "status":
        for name in ("stage-status.json", "REPORT.md"):
            if (campaign / name).exists():
                print((campaign / name).read_text())
        return
    if args.deadline_epoch is None or args.deadline_epoch <= 0:
        parser.error("--deadline-epoch is required")
    snapshot = campaign / "snapshot"
    if ROOT.resolve() != snapshot.resolve():
        command = [sys.executable, "-m", "scripts.instruments.agentic_campaign", "resume",
