                   "--campaign", str(campaign), "--deadline-epoch", str(args.deadline_epoch)]
        if args.after_campaign:
            command += ["--after-campaign", str(args.after_campaign.resolve())]
        env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(snapshot), PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1")
        os.chdir(snapshot)
        os.execve(sys.executable, command, env)
    if args.after_campaign and not wait_for_predecessor(campaign, args.after_campaign, args.deadline_epoch):
        return
    if (campaign / "STOP").exists():
        return
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "stage-status.json", {"phase": "running_tasks", "updated": time.time(),
                                                 "deadline_epoch": args.deadline_epoch})
    AgenticRunner(campaign, 24, args.deadline_epoch).run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


"""Full tool-loop coding probe with inaccessible oracles and pristine external grading."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import tomllib

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.corpora.selftest_polyglot import _overlay_reference
from scripts.instruments import emit, isolated_exec, run_polyglot as poly
from scripts.instruments.bench_mtp import acceptance
from scripts.instruments.hillclimb_probe import api, digest_file


TASK_IDS = ["javascript/affine-cipher", "javascript/alphametics", "javascript/bowling",
            "javascript/connect", "rust/word-count", "rust/robot-name", "rust/forth",
            "rust/doubly-linked-list"]
TEST_SCOPE = "trusted-tests-v2"


def write_json(path, value):
    Path(path).write_text(json.dumps(value, indent=2, allow_nan=False) + "\n")


def prepare_assets(destination, *, full_suite=False):
    destination.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    node = Path(subprocess.check_output(["node", "-p", "process.execPath"], text=True).strip())
    rust = Path(subprocess.check_output(["rustup", "which", "rustc"], text=True).strip())
    assets = {"node_root": str(node.parent.parent), "rust_root": str(rust.parent.parent),
              "cargo_registry": str(Path.home() / ".cargo/registry"),
              "node_modules": str(ROOT / ".cache/js-deps/node_modules"),
              "codex": str(Path(shutil.which("codex")).resolve())}
    assets["binary_hashes"] = {str(path): digest_file(path)
                                for path in (node, rust, Path(assets["codex"]))}
    tasks = json.loads((ROOT / "subsets/polyglot.json").read_text())
    selected = []
    task_ids = ([task["task_id"] for task in tasks] if full_suite else TASK_IDS)
    if len(task_ids) != len(set(task_ids)) or any(t["language"] not in {"javascript", "rust"} for t in tasks):
        raise ValueError("suite must contain unique supported tasks")
    for task_id in task_ids:
        task = dict(next(t for t in tasks if t["task_id"] == task_id))
        source = (ROOT / "subsets" / task["source_dir"]).resolve()
        task["test_paths"] = [str(p.relative_to(source)) for p in
                              poly.task_file_paths(task, source, "test_path", "test")]
        target = destination / "sources" / task_id
        shutil.copytree(source, target)
        task["source_dir"] = "sources/" + task_id
        selected.append(task)
    shutil.copy2(ROOT / "subsets/rust-deps.json", destination / "rust-deps.json")
    write_json(destination / "tools.json", assets)
    write_json(destination / "tasks.json", selected)
    return assets, selected


def enable_js_tests(text):
    text = re.sub(r"(?m)^([ \t]*)x(test|it|describe)(?=\s*\()", r"\1\2", text)
    return re.sub(r"(?m)^([ \t]*)(test|it|describe)\.skip(?=\s*\(|\.each)", r"\1\2", text)


def materialize(task, asset_dir, destination, *, reference=False):
    source = asset_dir / task["source_dir"]
    shutil.copytree(source, destination)
    if reference:
        _overlay_reference(task, destination)
    if task["language"] == "javascript":
        for relative in task["test_paths"]:
            path = destination / relative
            path.write_text(enable_js_tests(path.read_text()))
        (destination / "node_modules").symlink_to("/node_modules", target_is_directory=True)
    else:
        deps = json.loads((asset_dir / "rust-deps.json").read_text())["deps"].get(task["task_id"], {})
        cargo = destination / "Cargo.toml"
        data = tomllib.loads(cargo.read_text())
        data.setdefault("dependencies", {}).update(deps)
        cargo.write_text(dump_toml(data))
    poly._strip_reference_material(destination)


def toml_value(value):
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return "{ " + ", ".join(json.dumps(k) + " = " + toml_value(v) for k, v in value.items()) + " }"
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return "[" + ", ".join(toml_value(v) for v in value) + "]"
    if isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
        return json.dumps(value, allow_nan=False)
    raise ValueError("unsupported TOML value")


def dump_toml(data):
    return "\n".join(json.dumps(key) + " = " + toml_value(value) for key, value in data.items()) + "\n"


def transfer_solution(task, workdir, grade_dir):
    ignored = []
    for relative in task["solution_path"]:
        source = workdir / relative
        if source.is_symlink() or not source.is_file() or not source.resolve().is_relative_to(workdir.resolve()):
            raise ValueError("solution is missing or escapes the workspace: " + relative)
        if source.stat().st_size > 16 * 1024**2:
            raise ValueError("solution file exceeds 16 MiB: " + relative)
        if relative == "Cargo.toml":
            supplied = tomllib.loads(source.read_text())
            trusted = tomllib.loads((grade_dir / relative).read_text())
            for section in ("dependencies", "dev-dependencies"):
                if section in supplied:
                    for spec in supplied[section].values():
                        if isinstance(spec, dict) and set(spec) & {"path", "git", "registry"}:
                            raise ValueError("grading permits only cached registry dependencies")
                    trusted[section] = supplied[section]
            ignored = [key for key in supplied if key not in ("dependencies", "dev-dependencies")
                       and supplied[key] != trusted.get(key)]
            (grade_dir / relative).write_text(dump_toml(trusted))
        else:
            target = grade_dir / relative
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.copyfile(source, target)
    return ignored


def rust_test_plan(task, asset_dir):
    source = asset_dir / task["source_dir"]
    tests = sorted(str(p.relative_to(source)) for p in (source / "tests").glob("*.rs"))
    if not tests or not set(task["test_paths"]) <= set(tests):
        raise ValueError("trusted Rust integration targets are missing or unsupported")
    starter = (source / "src/lib.rs").read_text()
    docs = "\n".join(re.findall(r"(?m)^\s*//(?:/|!)(?!/)[ ]?(.*)$", starter))
    return tests, docs if "```" in docs else ""


def test_suite(task, workdir, assets, timeout, *, asset_dir):
    started = time.monotonic()
    readonly = task["test_paths"]
    docs = ""
    if task["language"] == "javascript":
        command = ["/node/bin/node", "/node_modules/jest/bin/jest.js", *task["test_paths"],
                   "--runInBand", "--json", "--outputFile=/work/.grader-result.json"]
    else:
        readonly, docs = rust_test_plan(task, asset_dir)
        command = ["/rust/bin/cargo", "test", "--offline"]
        # Only immutable integration targets count, never implementation-owned unit tests.
        for relative in readonly:
            command += ["--test", Path(relative).stem]
        command += ["--", "--include-ignored"]
    result = isolated_exec.run(workdir, assets, command, timeout, readonly=readonly)
    output = result.stdout + result.stderr
    returncode = result.returncode
    if docs and returncode == 0:
        # Starter doctests must survive a candidate replacing all of src/lib.rs.
        doc_path = workdir / ".grader-doctests.md"
        doc_path.write_text(docs + "\n")
        cargo = tomllib.loads((asset_dir / task["source_dir"] / "Cargo.toml").read_text())
        name = cargo.get("lib", {}).get("name", cargo["package"]["name"].replace("-", "_"))
        libraries = list((workdir / "target/debug/deps").glob(f"lib{name}-*.rlib"))
        if len(libraries) != 1:
            raise ValueError("trusted doctests require exactly one freshly built candidate library")
        command = ["/rust/bin/rustdoc", "--test", "/work/" + doc_path.name,
                   "--edition", str(cargo["package"].get("edition", "2015")),
                   "--extern", name + "=/work/" + str(libraries[0].relative_to(workdir)),
                   "-L", "dependency=/work/target/debug/deps", "--test-args", "--include-ignored"]
        result = isolated_exec.run(workdir, assets, command,
                                   poly._remaining(started + timeout), readonly=[*readonly, doc_path.name])
        output += result.stdout + result.stderr
        returncode = result.returncode
    passed = failed = ignored = 0
    if task["language"] == "javascript":
        report_path = workdir / ".grader-result.json"
        if report_path.exists():
            report = json.loads(report_path.read_text())
            passed, failed, ignored = (report.get(key, 0) for key in
                                      ("numPassedTests", "numFailedTests", "numPendingTests"))
    else:
        for match in re.finditer(r"test result: (?:ok|FAILED)\. (\d+) passed; (\d+) failed; (\d+) ignored;", output):
            counts = [int(value) for value in match.groups()]
            passed += counts[0]
            failed += counts[1]
            ignored += counts[2]
    return {"returncode": returncode, "passed": passed, "failed": failed, "ignored": ignored,
            "test_scope": TEST_SCOPE, "test_paths": readonly, "starter_doctests": bool(docs),
            "seconds": time.monotonic() - started, "output": output[-24000:]}


def selftest(asset_dir, assets, tasks):
    rows = []
    for task in tasks:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="qwen-grader-check-") as scratch:
            scratch = Path(scratch)
            stub, reference = scratch / "stub", scratch / "reference"
            materialize(task, asset_dir, stub)
            materialize(task, asset_dir, reference, reference=True)
            reference_result = test_suite(task, reference, assets, 300, asset_dir=asset_dir)
            stub_result = test_suite(task, stub, assets, 300, asset_dir=asset_dir)
            good_reference = (reference_result["returncode"] == 0 and reference_result["passed"] > 0
                              and reference_result["failed"] == reference_result["ignored"] == 0)
            rows.append({"task_id": task["task_id"], "reference_passes": good_reference,
                         "stub_fails": stub_result["returncode"] != 0,
                         "expected_passed_tests": reference_result["passed"],
                         "reference": reference_result, "stub": stub_result})
            print(json.dumps({"task": task["task_id"], "reference_passes": good_reference,
                              "stub_fails": rows[-1]["stub_fails"]}), flush=True)
    report = {"test_scope": TEST_SCOPE,
              "harness_verified": all(r["reference_passes"] and r["stub_fails"] for r in rows), "tasks": rows}
    write_json(asset_dir / "selftest.json", report)
    return report


def client_config(context):
    compact = 58000 if context == 65536 else context - 16384
    return f'''model = "local-benchmark"
model_provider = "benchmark"
model_context_window = {context}
model_auto_compact_token_limit = {compact}
model_reasoning_effort = "xhigh"
approval_policy = "never"
sandbox_mode = "danger-full-access"
web_search = "disabled"
project_doc_max_bytes = 0

[model_providers.benchmark]
name = "Isolated local benchmark"
base_url = "http://127.0.0.1:8081/v1"
wire_api = "responses"
requires_openai_auth = false
supports_websockets = false
request_max_retries = 2
stream_max_retries = 2
stream_idle_timeout_ms = 900000
'''


def fresh_context_peak(before, after, context):
    # This server includes generated tokens in n_tokens_max; adding n_decoded double-counts them.
    if before.get("n_tokens_max") != 0:
        raise ValueError("task context accounting requires a fresh server")
    peak = after.get("n_tokens_max")
    if not isinstance(peak, (int, float)) or not 0 <= peak <= context or int(peak) != peak:
        raise ValueError("invalid per-task context high-water mark")
    return int(peak)


def probe(asset_dir, task_id, url, context, out, timeout, engine=None):
    # engine=None is the single-slot llama-server contract; another engine supplies check(),
    # meter(out) (start/stop/port, stop() returning the task's context peak), context_peak()
    # and peak_source.
    assets = json.loads((asset_dir / "tools.json").read_text())
    tasks = json.loads((asset_dir / "tasks.json").read_text())
    task = next(t for t in tasks if t["task_id"] == task_id)
    checks = json.loads((asset_dir / "selftest.json").read_text())
    if not checks["harness_verified"] or checks.get("test_scope") != TEST_SCOPE:
        raise ValueError("trusted grader self-test has not passed")
    expected_tests = next(r["expected_passed_tests"] for r in checks["tasks"] if r["task_id"] == task_id)
    for path, expected in assets["binary_hashes"].items():
        if digest_file(path) != expected:
            raise ValueError("tool binary changed: " + path)
    if engine is None:
        props = api(url, "/props")
        if props["total_slots"] != 1 or props["default_generation_settings"]["n_ctx"] != context:
            raise ValueError("agentic probe requires the declared single slot")
    else:
        engine.check(url, context)
    started = time.monotonic()
    deadline = started + timeout
    workdir = out / "workspace"
    materialize(task, asset_dir, workdir)
    client_home = out / "codex-state"
    client_home.mkdir()
    (client_home / "config.toml").write_text(client_config(context))
    prompt = (poly._prompt(task) + " Do not modify tests. Dependencies are preinstalled; "
              "external network access is disabled. The supported solution files are: "
              + ", ".join(task["solution_path"]) + ".")
    before = emit.scrape_metrics(url)
    write_json(out / "metrics-before.json", before)
    sampler = emit.ContextSampler(url) if engine is None else engine.meter(out)
    sampler.start()
    rounds, stats = [], []
    success = timed_out = False
    error = None
    try:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="qwen-bridge-") as bridge_dir:
            bridge = Path(bridge_dir) / "model.sock"
            port = int(url.rsplit(":", 1)[-1]) if engine is None else sampler.port
            with isolated_exec.inference_bridge(bridge, port):
                for attempt in range(2):
                    (out / f"prompt-{attempt}.txt").write_text(prompt)
                    transcript = out / f"codex-{attempt}.jsonl"
                    # The outer mount/PID/network jail is the boundary; no user config or credentials enter it.
                    result = isolated_exec.run(workdir, assets,
                        ["/runner/codex", "exec", "--skip-git-repo-check", "--json", "-"],
                        poly._remaining(deadline), bridge=bridge, client_home=client_home,
                        readonly=task["test_paths"], input_text=prompt,
                        stdout_path=transcript, stderr_path=out / f"codex-{attempt}.stderr")
                    stats.append(emit.parse_codex_stats(transcript.read_text()))
                    if result.returncode:
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Codex exited with status {result.returncode}")
                    grade_dir = out / f"grade-{attempt}"
                    materialize(task, asset_dir, grade_dir)
                    try:
                        ignored = transfer_solution(task, workdir, grade_dir)
                        grade = test_suite(task, grade_dir, assets, poly._remaining(deadline), asset_dir=asset_dir)
                    except (ValueError, FileNotFoundError) as invalid_solution:
                        ignored = []
                        grade = {"returncode": 1, "passed": 0, "failed": 0, "ignored": 0,
                                 "seconds": 0, "output": "Invalid solution: " + str(invalid_solution)}
                    grade["ignored_cargo_metadata_changes"] = ignored
                    rounds.append(grade)
                    write_json(out / f"grade-{attempt}.json", grade)
                    success = (grade["returncode"] == 0 and grade["passed"] == expected_tests
                               and grade["failed"] == grade["ignored"] == 0)
                    if success:
                        break
                    prompt = (poly._prompt(task) + "\nTrusted grading failed. Repair the solution without changing tests.\n"
                              + f"Expected {expected_tests} trusted tests; got {grade['passed']} passed, "
                              + f"{grade['failed']} failed, {grade['ignored']} ignored; exit {grade['returncode']}.\n"
                              + grade["output"])
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        timed_out = True
    except Exception as failure:
        error = f"{type(failure).__name__}: {failure}"
    finally:
        wall = time.monotonic() - started
        sampled_peak = sampler.stop()
    after = emit.scrape_metrics(url)
    write_json(out / "metrics-after.json", after)
    peak = (fresh_context_peak(before, after, context) if engine is None
            else engine.context_peak(sampled_peak, context))
    delta = emit.metrics_delta(before, after)
    prefill, decode = delta.get("prefill_seconds"), delta.get("decode_seconds")
    valid_timing = prefill is not None and decode is not None and prefill + decode <= wall + 2
    summary = {"workload": "agentic", "task_id": task_id, "complete": error is None and valid_timing,
               "task_success": success, "timed_out": timed_out, "error": error,
               "verified_wall_seconds": wall, "prefill_seconds": prefill, "decode_seconds": decode,
               "other_seconds": max(0, wall - prefill - decode) if valid_timing else None,
               "grader_seconds": sum(r["seconds"] for r in rounds), "grading_rounds": len(rounds),
               "expected_passed_tests": expected_tests, "harness_verified": True,
               "metrics_scope": "task_single_slot", "metrics_delta": delta, "mtp": acceptance(delta),
               "mtp_live": delta.get("draft_tokens", 0) > 0 and delta.get("draft_accepted_tokens", 0) > 0,
               "depth_verified": True, "overlap_verified": True, "context_occupancy_requirement": "natural task history",
               "peak_context_tokens": peak, "legacy_sampled_context_tokens": sampled_peak,
               "context_peak_source": "fresh-server n_tokens_max" if engine is None else engine.peak_source,
               "client_context": context,
               "client_compaction": 58000 if context == 65536 else context - 16384,
               "agent_stats": poly._merge_stats(stats), "throughput_output_cap_applied": False,
               "external_network": False, "pristine_grader": True, "test_scope": TEST_SCOPE}
    write_json(out / "probe.json", summary)
    return summary


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("command", choices=["prepare", "selftest", "probe"])
    parser.add_argument("--assets", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--task")
    parser.add_argument("--url", default="http://127.0.0.1:8081")
    parser.add_argument("--context", type=int, default=102400)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=7200)
    parser.add_argument("--full-suite", action="store_true")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.command == "prepare":
        prepare_assets(args.assets, full_suite=args.full_suite)
        return 0
    if args.command == "selftest":
        result = selftest(args.assets, json.loads((args.assets / "tools.json").read_text()),
                          json.loads((args.assets / "tasks.json").read_text()))
        return 0 if result["harness_verified"] else 1
    result = probe(args.assets, args.task, args.url, args.context, args.out, args.timeout)
    return 0 if result["complete"] and result["mtp_live"] else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


"""Cold, overlapping requests with per-request timings and observed slot occupancy."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, as_completed
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import threading
import time
import urllib.request

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import bench_endpoint, bench_mtp, emit, longctx


def get_json(base_url, endpoint):
    with urllib.request.urlopen(base_url.rstrip("/").removesuffix("/v1") + endpoint,
                                timeout=10) as response:
        return json.load(response)


def read_stream(lines, started, now=time.monotonic):
    usage, timings, fragments, finish_reasons = {}, {}, [], []
    first_token = None
    for raw in lines:
        line = raw.decode().strip()
        if not line.startswith("data:"):
            continue
        body = line[5:].strip()
        if body == "[DONE]":
            break
        event = json.loads(body)
        if event.get("error"):
            raise ValueError(f"server stream error: {event['error']}")
        usage = event.get("usage") or usage
        timings = event.get("timings") or timings
        for choice in event.get("choices", []):
            delta = choice.get("delta") or {}
            material = (delta.get("reasoning_content") or delta.get("reasoning")
                        or delta.get("content") or delta.get("tool_calls"))
            if material:
                if first_token is None:
                    first_token = now() - started
                fragments.append(delta)
            if choice.get("finish_reason"):
                finish_reasons.append(choice["finish_reason"])
    if not usage.get("completion_tokens") or not timings or first_token is None:
        raise ValueError("missing completion usage, server timings or streamed output")
    return {"usage": usage, "timings": timings, "ttft_seconds": first_token,
            "finish_reasons": finish_reasons, "output_deltas": fragments}


def request_one(base_url, model, prompt, index, max_tokens, timeout, barrier, stagger):
    barrier.wait()
    if stagger:
        time.sleep(index * stagger)
    started = time.monotonic()
    payload = {
        "model": model,
        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        "max_tokens": max_tokens, "temperature": 1.0, "seed": 1729 + index,
        "chat_template_kwargs": {"reasoning_effort": "xhigh"},
        "tools": bench_endpoint.TOOLS, "tool_choice": "auto",
        "stream": True, "stream_options": {"include_usage": True},
        "cache_prompt": False,
    }
    row = {"request_index": index, "started_monotonic": started,
           "prompt_sha256": hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode()).hexdigest(), "error": None}
    request = urllib.request.Request(
        base_url.rstrip("/").removesuffix("/v1") + "/v1/chat/completions",
        json.dumps(payload).encode(), {"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
            row.update(read_stream(response, started))
    except Exception as error:
        row["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
    row["finished_monotonic"] = time.monotonic()
    row["wall_seconds"] = row["finished_monotonic"] - started
    return row


def summarize(requests, slot_samples):
    successful = [row for row in requests if not row["error"]]
    complete = len(successful) == len(requests) and bool(requests)
    total = sum(row["usage"]["completion_tokens"] for row in successful)
    makespan = (max(row["finished_monotonic"] for row in requests)
                - min(row["started_monotonic"] for row in requests)) if requests else 0
    first = min((row["started_monotonic"] + row["ttft_seconds"] for row in successful),
                default=None)
    last = max((row["finished_monotonic"] for row in successful), default=None)
    return {
        "complete": complete, "completed_requests": len(successful),
        "makespan_seconds": makespan, "completion_tokens": total,
        "output_tokens_per_wall_second": total / makespan if complete and makespan else None,
        # This window can contain another request's prefill and unequal decode tails.
        "output_tokens_per_first_token_to_last_finish_second":
            total / (last - first) if complete and first is not None and last > first else None,
        "max_observed_busy_slots": max((sum(bool(slot.get("is_processing"))
                                               for slot in sample.get("slots", []))
                                         for sample in slot_samples), default=0),
    }


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--base-url", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--quant", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--env-json", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--requests", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--expected-slots", type=int, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--expected-context", type=int, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--padding", type=int, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--max-tokens", type=int, default=768)
    parser.add_argument("--stagger-seconds", type=float, default=0)
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=1800)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if min(args.requests, args.expected_slots, args.expected_context, args.max_tokens) < 1:
        parser.error("requests, slots, context and output budget must be positive")
    if args.padding < 0 or args.stagger_seconds < 0:
        parser.error("padding and stagger must be nonnegative")
    props = get_json(args.base_url, "/props")
    if args.quant not in props.get("model_path", ""):
        raise SystemExit("wrong model served")
    if (props["total_slots"] != args.expected_slots
            or props["default_generation_settings"]["n_ctx"] != args.expected_context):
        raise SystemExit("server slot count or per-slot context differs from requested arm")
    model = bench_endpoint.resolve_model(args.base_url, None)
    manifest = ROOT / "subsets/polyglot.json"
    task = bench_mtp.load_tasks(manifest, 1)[0]
    prompt = bench_mtp.bench_prompt(task, manifest.parent)
    actual_padding = 0
    if args.padding:
        padding, actual_padding = longctx.build_padding(task, args.padding, args.base_url)
        prompt = longctx.wrap_prompt(prompt, padding)
    barrier = threading.Barrier(args.requests)
    stopped = threading.Event()
    samples = []

    def sample_slots():
        while not stopped.is_set():
            try:
                samples.append({"monotonic": time.monotonic(),
                                "slots": get_json(args.base_url, "/slots")})
            except Exception as error:
                samples.append({"monotonic": time.monotonic(), "error": str(error)})
            stopped.wait(1)

    if args.out.exists():
        raise SystemExit("output exists; choose a new result path")
    args.out.mkdir(parents=True)
    before = emit.scrape_metrics(args.base_url)
    sampler = threading.Thread(target=sample_slots, daemon=True)
    sampler.start()
    rows = []
    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=args.requests) as executor:
            futures = [executor.submit(
                request_one, args.base_url, model, f"Request {i}.\n{prompt}", i,
                args.max_tokens, args.timeout, barrier, args.stagger_seconds)
                for i in range(args.requests)]
            for future in as_completed(futures):
                row = future.result()
                rows.append(row)
                (args.out / f"request-{row['request_index']}.json").write_text(json.dumps(row, indent=2))
                print(json.dumps({key: row.get(key) for key in
                                  ("request_index", "wall_seconds", "ttft_seconds", "timings", "error")}), flush=True)
    finally:
        stopped.set()
        sampler.join(timeout=12)
    after = emit.scrape_metrics(args.base_url)
    delta = emit.metrics_delta(before, after)
    result = {
        "instrument": "cold-concurrency-v1", "timestamp": emit.utc_timestamp(),
        "quant": args.quant, "task_id": task["task_id"],
        "env": emit.load_env(args.env_json), "props": props,
        "requested_requests": args.requests, "padding_tokens": actual_padding,
        "max_output_tokens": args.max_tokens, "temperature": 1, "seed_base": 1729,
        "stagger_seconds": args.stagger_seconds, "with_tools": True, "cache_prompt": False,
        "metrics_delta": delta, "mtp": bench_mtp.acceptance(delta),
        "slot_samples": samples,
        "requests": [{key: value for key, value in row.items() if key != "output_deltas"}
                     for row in sorted(rows, key=lambda row: row["request_index"])],
        **summarize(rows, samples),
    }
    result["mtp_live"] = delta.get("draft_tokens", 0) > 0 and delta.get("draft_accepted_tokens", 0) > 0
    (args.out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2))
    if not result["complete"] or not result["mtp_live"]:
        raise SystemExit("failed requests or no accepted MTP tokens; inspect raw results")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Engine-agnostic decode/prefill bench over any OpenAI-compatible endpoint.

The point is comparability: llama.cpp, vLLM and ninfer expose different metrics, so
nothing server-side is trusted here. Every number comes from the stream itself --
time to first token, wall time, and the token counts the API reports -- which is the
only instrument all three engines can be held to at once.

Rows are written as plain jsonl on purpose. The shared emit schema rejects unknown
fields at write time, which once killed two legs after their GPU time was already paid.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import bench_mtp, emit



def resolve_model(base_url: str, api_key: str | None, timeout: float = 20.0) -> str:
    """Ask the server what it serves. vLLM rejects a model id it does not recognise,
    and its id is the checkpoint path, which the caller has no reason to know."""

    request = urllib.request.Request(base_url.rstrip("/").removesuffix("/v1") + "/v1/models")
    if api_key:
        request.add_header("Authorization", f"Bearer {api_key}")
    with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
        return json.loads(response.read())["data"][0]["id"]


# A minimal but real tool schema. Attaching tools is what puts a server into constrained
# decoding, and that path is invisible to a bench that never sends any -- which is how a
# 145 tok/s engine came to serve an agent at 8.6.
TOOLS = [{
    "type": "function",
    "function": {
        "name": "write_file",
        "description": "Write text to a file",
        "parameters": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {"path": {"type": "string"}, "contents": {"type": "string"}},
            "required": ["path", "contents"],
        },
    },
}]


def count_tokens(base_url: str, model: str, text: str, timeout: float = 30.0) -> int | None:
    """Token count from the served model's own tokenizer.

    Two wire shapes, because the engines disagree: vLLM takes {model, prompt} and answers
    {count}, llama-server takes {content} and answers {tokens: [...]} -- see
    longctx.py:_tokenize. Speaking only one of them returns None on the other engine, which
    is most of this experiment's legs.

    Returns None when neither shape works, so a missing count never reads as zero
    reasoning; callers should surface the first None rather than record it silently.
    """

    if not text.strip():
        return 0
    url = base_url.rstrip("/").removesuffix("/v1") + "/tokenize"
    for payload, read in (
        ({"model": model, "prompt": text}, lambda d: d.get("count")),
        ({"content": text}, lambda d: len(d["tokens"]) if "tokens" in d else None),
    ):
        request = urllib.request.Request(
            url, data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
        )
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
                n = read(json.load(response))
        except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
            continue
        if isinstance(n, int):
            return n
    return None


def split_thinking(reasoning: str, content: str) -> tuple[str, str, bool]:
    """Return (thinking, answer, truncated).

    With no parser the thinking arrives inline: the template opens <think> and the model
    closes it, so the closing tag is the only delimiter present. When the token budget runs
    out mid-thought no closing tag ever arrives -- and treating that as pure content would
    score it as zero reasoning, the exact opposite of what the same run reports on an engine
    whose parser tags it. Unterminated output is thinking, and says so.
    """

    if reasoning:
        return reasoning, content, False
    head, sep, tail = content.partition("</think>")
    if sep:
        return head, tail, False
    return content, "", True


def stream_once(base_url: str, model: str, prompt: str, max_tokens: int,
                timeout: float, api_key: str | None = None,
                with_tools: bool = False) -> dict:
    payload = {
        "model": model,
        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
        "temperature": 0.0,
        "stream": True,
        "stream_options": {"include_usage": True},
    }
    if with_tools:
        payload["tools"] = TOOLS
        payload["tool_choice"] = "auto"
    request = urllib.request.Request(
        base_url.rstrip("/").removesuffix("/v1") + "/v1/chat/completions",
        data=json.dumps(payload).encode(),
        headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"},
    )
    if api_key:
        request.add_header("Authorization", f"Bearer {api_key}")
    started = time.monotonic()
    ttft = None
    chunks = 0
    usage = {}
    reasoning_text: list[str] = []
    answer_text: list[str] = []
    with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
        for raw in response:
            line = raw.decode(errors="replace").strip()
            if not line.startswith("data:"):
                continue
            body = line[5:].strip()
            if body == "[DONE]":
                break
            try:
                event = json.loads(body)
            except json.JSONDecodeError:
                continue
            if event.get("usage"):
                usage = event["usage"]
            for choice in event.get("choices") or []:
                delta = choice.get("delta") or {}
                # This vLLM streams thinking as `reasoning`; watching only
                # `reasoning_content` misses every one of them, and on a prompt whose
                # whole budget goes to thinking no `content` delta ever arrives -- so the
                # row came back with null ttft and null rates and looked like a server
                # that never streamed.
                think = delta.get("reasoning") or delta.get("reasoning_content") or ""
                said = delta.get("content") or ""
                if think:
                    reasoning_text.append(think)
                if said:
                    answer_text.append(said)
                if think or said:
                    chunks += 1
                    if ttft is None:
                        ttft = time.monotonic() - started
    total = time.monotonic() - started
    out_tokens = usage.get("completion_tokens")
    # Falling back to chunk count would quietly compare tokens against SSE events, so a
    # row without usage is reported as missing rather than estimated.
    #
    # No TTFT means no decode rate either: dividing by total time would put a
    # prefill-inclusive number in the decode column beside engines that do stream.
    # The parser does NOT withhold deltas while the model thinks -- it streams them under
    # the key `reasoning`, which is why watching only `reasoning_content` made a
    # thinking-only response look like a server that never streamed at all.
    decode_s = total - ttft if ttft is not None else None
    return {
        "ttft_s": round(ttft, 3) if ttft is not None else None,
        "total_s": round(total, 3),
        "prompt_tokens": usage.get("prompt_tokens"),
        "completion_tokens": out_tokens,
        "chunks": chunks,
        "decode_tok_s": round(out_tokens / decode_s, 2)
        if out_tokens and decode_s and decode_s > 0 else None,
        "overall_tok_s": round(out_tokens / total, 2) if out_tokens and total > 0 else None,
        "prefill_tok_s": round(usage["prompt_tokens"] / ttft, 1)
        if usage.get("prompt_tokens") and ttft else None,
        "reasoning_text": "".join(reasoning_text),
        "answer_text": "".join(answer_text),
    }


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--base-url", default="http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1")
    parser.add_argument("--label", required=True, help="engine + decoding config")
    parser.add_argument("--engine", required=True, help="llama.cpp | vllm | ninfer")
    # No --gpu: hand-typing the card is how 36 of 124 rows here ended up labelled with
    # a rental label, TTL included, and the table rendered it as the card.
    parser.add_argument("--env-json", required=True,
                        help="scripts/gpuenv.py capture for the box under test")
    parser.add_argument("--experiment-id", required=True,
                        help="without it a later experiment's rows are indistinguishable "
                             "in this sink")
    parser.add_argument("--quant", required=True, help="quantization label recorded verbatim")
    parser.add_argument("--model", default="qwen3.8-27b")
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, default=ROOT / "subsets" / "polyglot.json")
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--tasks", type=int, default=4)
    parser.add_argument("--max-tokens", type=int, default=768)
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=900.0)
    parser.add_argument("--api-key", default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--with-tools", action="store_true",
                        help="attach a tool schema, so the row measures the path an agent uses")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    env = emit.load_env(args.env_json)
    if args.model == "auto":
        args.model = resolve_model(args.base_url, args.api_key)
        print(f"serving model: {args.model}")
    served = emit.probe_server(args.base_url).get("served_model_path")

    tasks = bench_mtp.load_tasks(args.manifest, args.tasks)
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    rows = 0
    tokenize_warned = False
    for task in tasks:
        prompt = bench_mtp.bench_prompt(task, args.manifest.parent)
        row = {
            "schema_version": emit.SCHEMA_VERSION,
            "timestamp": emit.utc_timestamp(),
            "experiment_id": args.experiment_id,
            "label": args.label, "engine": args.engine, "quant": args.quant,
            "served_model_path": served,
            "task_id": task["task_id"], "max_tokens": args.max_tokens,
            "with_tools": args.with_tools,
            **env,
        }
        try:
            row.update(stream_once(args.base_url, args.model, prompt,
                                   args.max_tokens, args.timeout, args.api_key,
                                   args.with_tools))
            # Counted, not stored: the text itself is large and the transcripts already
            # hold it. The split is what the quant comparison needs -- how much of the
            # budget a quant spends thinking before it answers.
            think, said, truncated = split_thinking(
                row.pop("reasoning_text", ""), row.pop("answer_text", "")
            )
            row["reasoning_tokens"] = count_tokens(args.base_url, args.model, think)
            row["content_tokens"] = count_tokens(args.base_url, args.model, said)
            # Also truncated when the budget ran out, which the delimiter cannot show on
            # the parser path: reasoning is tagged, so no missing </think> betrays it. A
            # capped row's reasoning share is a floor, not a measurement.
            row["thinking_truncated"] = bool(
                truncated
                or (row.get("completion_tokens") or 0) >= args.max_tokens
            )
            if row["reasoning_tokens"] is None and not tokenize_warned:
                # Silently recording None makes a broken tokenizer call look like an
                # endpoint that was never there.
                print(f"WARNING: /tokenize did not answer at {args.base_url}; "
                      "the reasoning split will be null for this run", flush=True)
                tokenize_warned = True
        except Exception as error:                       # noqa: BLE001
            row["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
        emit.append_row(args.out, row)
        rows += 1
        print(f"{args.label} {task['task_id']}: "
              f"{row.get('decode_tok_s')} tok/s decode, ttft {row.get('ttft_s')}s")
    return 0 if rows else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Matched MTP-on/off throughput bench.

Speculative decoding changes speed, not answer quality, so it is measured on its
own rather than switched on mid-experiment: a paired quant contrast that mixed two
decoding algorithms would attribute the difference to the quant.

Draft acceptance depends on what is being generated, so the prompts are real task
prompts, and depth is swept because a draft head that pays at 8K need not pay at 64K.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import time
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import emit, longctx



def load_tasks(manifest: Path, count: int) -> list[dict]:
    data = json.loads(manifest.read_text())
    tasks = data["tasks"] if isinstance(data, dict) else data
    return sorted(tasks, key=lambda t: t["task_id"])[:count]


def bench_prompt(task: dict, manifest_dir: Path) -> str:
    """A tool-free restatement of the task. The agentic prompt cannot be reused:
    with no working directory the model would answer that it cannot see the files,
    and short refusals measure nothing. Stub plus tests forces real code generation,
    which is what draft acceptance has to be measured on."""

    base = (manifest_dir / task["source_dir"]).resolve()
    skip = {".meta", "node_modules", "target", ".git"}
    tests = sorted(
        str(path.relative_to(base))
        for path in base.rglob("*")
        if path.is_file()
        and not skip & set(path.relative_to(base).parts)
        and re.search(r"test|spec", str(path.relative_to(base)), re.IGNORECASE)
    )
    parts = [f"Implement this {task['language']} exercise. Reply with the complete solution file."]
    for name in list(task["solution_path"]) + tests:
        path = base / name
        if path.is_file():
            parts.append(f"--- {name} ---\n{path.read_text(errors='replace')}")
    return "\n\n".join(parts)


def generate(base_url: str, prompt: str, max_tokens: int, effort: str, timeout: float) -> dict:
    payload = {
        "model": emit.lane_model("quant"),
        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        "max_tokens": max_tokens,
        "temperature": 1.0,
        "stream": False,
        "chat_template_kwargs": {"reasoning_effort": effort},
    }
    request = urllib.request.Request(
        base_url.rstrip("/").removesuffix("/v1") + "/v1/chat/completions",
        json.dumps(payload).encode(),
        {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
    )
    with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
        return json.loads(response.read())


def acceptance(row: dict) -> dict:
    """Per-draft acceptance is the diagnostic: a high token count with a low
    accepted/draft ratio means the head is drafting but guessing wrong."""
    drafts = row.get("drafts") or 0
    drafted = row.get("draft_tokens") or 0
    accepted = row.get("draft_accepted_tokens") or 0
    out = {}
    if drafted:
        out["draft_accept_rate"] = accepted / drafted
    if drafts:
        out["accepted_per_draft"] = accepted / drafts
    return out


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--base-url", default="http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1")
    parser.add_argument("--label", required=True, help="mtp-off | mtp-on")
    parser.add_argument("--quant", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--env-json", required=True,
                        help="scripts/gpuenv.py capture; without it these rows cannot "
                             "say which card produced the speculation numbers")
    parser.add_argument("--experiment-id", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--manifest", type=Path, default=ROOT / "subsets" / "polyglot.json")
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--tasks", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--depths", default="0,64000")
    parser.add_argument("--max-tokens", type=int, default=768)
    parser.add_argument("--reasoning-effort", default="xhigh")
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=900.0)
    args = parser.parse_args()

    env = emit.load_env(args.env_json)
    probe = emit.probe_server(args.base_url)
    if args.quant not in str(probe.get("served_model_path", "")):
        raise SystemExit(f"server is not serving {args.quant}: {probe.get('served_model_path')}")

    depths = [int(d) for d in args.depths.split(",") if d.strip()]
    tasks = load_tasks(args.manifest, args.tasks)

    for depth in depths:
        for task in tasks:
            prompt = bench_prompt(task, args.manifest.parent)
            actual = 0
            if depth:
                try:
                    padding, actual = longctx.build_padding(task, depth, args.base_url)
                except SystemExit as leak:  # skip this task, not the rest of the sweep
                    print(f"skipping {task['task_id']} at {depth}: {leak}", flush=True)
                    continue
                prompt = longctx.wrap_prompt(prompt, padding)
            before = emit.scrape_metrics(args.base_url)
            started = time.monotonic()
            try:
                generate(args.base_url, prompt, args.max_tokens, args.reasoning_effort, args.timeout)
                failure = None
            except Exception as error:  # a refused request must not look like a slow one
                failure = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
            wall = time.monotonic() - started
            after = emit.scrape_metrics(args.base_url)

            row = {
                "schema_version": emit.SCHEMA_VERSION,
                "timestamp": emit.utc_timestamp(),
                "experiment_id": args.experiment_id,
                "label": args.label,
                "quant": args.quant,
                "served_model_path": probe.get("served_model_path"),
                **env,
                "task_id": task["task_id"],
                "depth_target": depth,
                "depth_padding_tokens": actual,
                "wall_seconds": wall,
                "engine_commit": probe.get("engine_commit"),
                "error": failure,
            }
            row.update(emit.metrics_delta(before, after))
            row.update(acceptance(row))
            emit.append_row(args.out, row)
            print(
                f"{args.label} {task['task_id']} depth={depth} "
                f"tps={row.get('decode_tps')} accept={row.get('draft_accept_rate')}",
                flush=True,
            )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


"""Calibrate qualified Swift context windows with cold/warm per-card telemetry."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import threading
import time

from scripts.instruments import hillclimb_probe as probe


SERVE = Path("/home/xanewok/.codex/skills/local-qwen-server/bin/serve.sh")
QUERY = "uuid,memory.used,memory.free,temperature.gpu,power.draw"


def gpu_sample():
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=" + QUERY, "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=True)
    rows = {}
    for row in csv.reader(result.stdout.splitlines(), skipinitialspace=True):
        if len(row) != 5:
            raise ValueError("invalid GPU telemetry row")
        rows[row[0]] = {
            "memory.used": float(row[1]), "memory.free": float(row[2]),
            "temperature.gpu": float(row[3]), "power.draw": float(row[4]),
        }
    if not rows:
        raise ValueError("nvidia-smi returned no GPUs")
    return {"time": time.time(), "gpus": rows}


class GpuSampler:
    def __init__(self, path, interval=0.5):
        self.path, self.interval = path, interval
        self.rows, self.stop = [], threading.Event()
        self.thread = threading.Thread(target=self._run, daemon=True)

    def _run(self):
        while not self.stop.is_set():
            try:
                self.rows.append(gpu_sample())
            except Exception as error:
                self.rows.append({"time": time.time(), "error": f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"})
            self.stop.wait(self.interval)

    def __enter__(self):
        self.thread.start()
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *_):
        self.stop.set()
        self.thread.join(timeout=5)
        self.path.write_text(json.dumps(self.rows, indent=2) + "\n")


def service(serve, action, context=None):
    env = dict(os.environ, PROFILE="swift-pair")
    if context is not None:
        env["CTX"] = str(context)
    return subprocess.run([str(serve), action], env=env, text=True, check=action == "start")


def phase(url, task, target, seed, output, cache_prompt):
    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    before = gpu_sample()
    with GpuSampler(output / "gpu-telemetry.json") as sampler:
        result = probe.run_probe(url, task, target, 2048, 1, seed, output,
                                 cache_prompt=cache_prompt)
    after = gpu_sample()
    good = [row for row in sampler.rows if "gpus" in row]
    devices = sorted(before["gpus"])
    result = {
        "cache_prompt": cache_prompt, "requested_prompt_tokens": target,
        "max_output_tokens": 2048, "probe": result,
        "before": before, "after": after,
        "minimum_free_mib": {
            device: min((row["gpus"][device]["memory.free"] for row in good
                         if device in row["gpus"]), default=before["gpus"][device]["memory.free"])
            for device in devices
        },
        "peak_used_mib": {
            device: max((row["gpus"][device]["memory.used"] for row in good
                         if device in row["gpus"]), default=before["gpus"][device]["memory.used"])
            for device in devices
        },
        "peak_temperature_c": {
            device: max((row["gpus"][device]["temperature.gpu"] for row in good
                         if device in row["gpus"]), default=before["gpus"][device]["temperature.gpu"])
            for device in devices
        },
    }
    (output / "calibration.json").write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n")
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--tasks", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--serve", type=Path, default=SERVE)
    parser.add_argument("--url", default="http://127.0.0.1:8080")
    parser.add_argument("--windows", nargs="+", type=int, default=[71680, 90112, 102400])
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1729)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if any(window < 1 or window % 2048 for window in args.windows):
        parser.error("windows must be positive multiples of 2048")
    args.out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    task = json.loads(args.tasks.read_text())["javascript/affine-cipher"]
    summary = {"windows": args.windows, "target_rule": "window - 2048", "phases": []}
    try:
        for window in args.windows:
            service(args.serve, "stop")
            service(args.serve, "start", window)
            loaded = gpu_sample()
            window_out = args.out / f"context-{window}"
            window_out.mkdir()
            (window_out / "loaded.json").write_text(json.dumps(loaded, indent=2) + "\n")
            phases = []
            target = window - 2048
            for name, cache_prompt in (("cold", True), ("warm", True)):
                phase_out = window_out / name
                try:
                    phases.append({"name": name, **phase(args.url, task, target, args.seed, phase_out, cache_prompt)})
                except Exception as error:
                    phase_out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
                    failure = {"name": name, "status": "error", "error": f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"}
                    (phase_out / "calibration.json").write_text(json.dumps(failure, indent=2) + "\n")
                    phases.append(failure)
            summary["phases"].append({"context": window, "loaded": loaded, "phases": phases})
            (window_out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary["phases"][-1], indent=2) + "\n")
    finally:
        service(args.serve, "stop")
        service(args.serve, "start", 102400)
    (args.out / "summary.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + "\n")
    print(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


"""Narrow, versioned adapters for pinned embedded chat templates."""

import hashlib


BARTOWSKI_PREFIX = "bartowski-leading-instructions-v1"
BARTOWSKI_TEMPLATE_SHA256 = "c3cf9e34abf4f9e36c2d72165aa9c132d3e2a725b6c2586aaa3a8af9d7a81041"

LEADING_INSTRUCTIONS = r"""{%- set sysns = namespace(count=0, text='') %}
{%- for message in messages %}
    {%- if sysns.count == loop.index0 and (message.role == 'system' or message.role == 'developer') %}
        {%- set sys_content = render_content(message.content, false, true)|trim %}
        {%- if sys_content %}
            {%- set sysns.text = sysns.text + ('\n' if sysns.text else '') + sys_content %}
        {%- endif %}
        {%- set sysns.count = sysns.count + 1 %}
    {%- endif %}
{%- endfor %}
{%- if sysns.count %}
    {%- set messages = [{'role': 'system', 'content': sysns.text}] + messages[sysns.count:] %}
{%- endif %}
"""


def bartowski_template(source):
    if hashlib.sha256(source.encode()).hexdigest() != BARTOWSKI_TEMPLATE_SHA256:
        raise ValueError("Bartowski embedded template differs from the pinned source")
    # Only the leading instruction block is merged; later roles must still fail in place.
    anchor = "{%- set reasoning_instructions = '' %}"
    return source.replace(anchor, LEADING_INSTRUCTIONS + anchor, 1)


SWIFT_DROP_TRACES_PREFIX = "swift-drop-historical-thinking-v1"
SWIFT_TEMPLATE_SHA256 = "4af3dcf0e98bf87adf684a4824683ab15fffd64e93ed1170061ef86a15827ba8"

PRESERVE_THINKING_GUARD = (
    "{%- if preserve_thinking is undefined or preserve_thinking is true"
    " or loop.index0 > ns.last_query_index %}"
)
DROP_THINKING_GUARD = (
    "{%- if (preserve_thinking is defined and preserve_thinking)"
    " or loop.index0 > ns.last_query_index %}"
)


def swift_drop_traces_template(source):
    """Flip the Swift template's `preserve_thinking` default from true to false."""
    if hashlib.sha256(source.encode()).hexdigest() != SWIFT_TEMPLATE_SHA256:
        raise ValueError("Swift embedded template differs from the pinned source")
    # Invert the default rather than delete the branch: assistant turns after the last user
    # message are the in-flight tool-call chain and must keep their traces, or the model
    # loses the reasoning behind a call it is still acting on.
    if source.count(PRESERVE_THINKING_GUARD) != 1:
        raise ValueError("preserve_thinking guard is not unique in the pinned source")
    return source.replace(PRESERVE_THINKING_GUARD, DROP_THINKING_GUARD, 1)


"""Frozen, confirmation-gated full-suite continuation; no automatic production promotion."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import defaultdict
import copy
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import shutil
import statistics
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import time

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import agentic_campaign as a, hillclimb as h
from scripts.instruments.hillclimb_probe import digest_file


MODELS = ["iq3-control", "unsloth-q4-m", "bartowski-iq4-xs"]
CONFIRM_TASKS = ["javascript/affine-cipher", "javascript/bowling", "rust/word-count", "rust/forth"]
SEEDS = [1911, 1912, 1913]
MEMORY_RECOVERY = {"wait_seconds": 900, "stable_seconds": 10, "extra_headroom_mib": 128}
WATCHDOG_RECOVERY = {"max_retries": 3, "backoff_seconds": [20, 40, 60],
                     "wait_seconds": 120, "stable_seconds": 10}


def watchdog_timeout(log):
    lower = log.lower()
    return ("the launch timed out and was terminated" in lower
            and not any(word in lower for word in ("illegal memory access", "device-side assert",
                        "unspecified launch failure", "fallen off the bus"))
            and all(code == "8" for code in re.findall(r"(?i)\bxid\s*(?:\([^)]*\))?\s*:\s*(\d+)", log)))


def watchdog_kernel_check(since):
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(["journalctl", "-k", "--since", "@" + str(int(since)),
                                 "--no-pager", "-o", "cat", "--grep", r"NVRM: Xid"],
                                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
    except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
        raise h.StopCampaign("watchdog_kernel_health_unconfirmed") from error
    codes = re.findall(r"(?i)\bxid\s*(?:\([^)]*\))?\s*:\s*(\d+)", result.stdout)
    if result.returncode not in (0, 1) or not codes or any(code != "8" for code in codes):
        raise h.StopCampaign("watchdog_kernel_health_unconfirmed")
    return result.stdout


def memory_pressure(reason):
    return reason == "oom" or str(reason).startswith("vram_reserve: ")


def recovery_requirements(result, before, reserves=(512, 256), extra_headroom_mib=128):
    required = {}
    for gpu, reserve in zip(result["config"]["devices"], reserves):
        allocated = max(0, before["gpus"][gpu]["memory.free"] - result["minimum_free_mib"][gpu])
        required[gpu] = allocated + reserve + extra_headroom_mib
    return required


def choose_microbatches(rows):
    expected = {(m, t, s, u) for m in MODELS[1:] for t in CONFIRM_TASKS
                for s in SEEDS for u in (128, 512)}
    indexed = {}
    for row in rows:
        config = row.get("config", {})
        key = (row.get("model"), row.get("task"), row.get("seed"), config.get("ubatch"))
        if key not in expected or key in indexed:
            raise ValueError("confirmation must contain each declared case exactly once")
        if any(config.get(k) != v for k, v in
               {"context": 102400, "slots": 1, "maximum": 3, "split": "69,31", "mode": "tensor"}.items()):
            raise ValueError("confirmation settings differ from declared single-agent control")
        indexed[key] = row
    if set(indexed) != expected:
        raise ValueError("confirmation cases are incomplete")

    def valid(row):
        probe = row.get("probe", {})
        return (row["status"] == "passed" and all(probe.get(k) for k in
                ("complete", "mtp_live", "depth_verified", "overlap_verified")) and
                all(isinstance(row.get(k), (int, float)) and math.isfinite(row[k]) and row[k] > 0
                    for k in ("prefill_tps", "decode_tps")))

    selection = {}
    for model in MODELS[1:]:
        arms = {u: [indexed[model, t, s, u] for t in CONFIRM_TASKS for s in SEEDS] for u in (128, 512)}
        good = {u: [r for r in arms[u] if valid(r)] for u in arms}
        choice, reason, ratios = 128, "retain baseline: improvement not established", {}
        if all(len(good[u]) == 12 for u in arms):
            ratios = {metric: math.exp(statistics.mean(math.log(c[metric] / b[metric])
                       for b, c in zip(arms[128], arms[512]))) for metric in ("prefill_tps", "decode_tps")}
            if ((ratios["prefill_tps"] >= 1.05 and ratios["decode_tps"] >= .95) or
                    (ratios["decode_tps"] >= 1.05 and ratios["prefill_tps"] >= .95)):
                choice, reason = 512, "confirmed useful throughput improvement; coding validation pending"
        elif len(good[512]) == 12:
            choice, reason = 512, "candidate has complete safe confirmation; baseline does not"
        elif not good[128]:
            choice, reason = None, "no safe baseline and no complete candidate confirmation"
        else:
            reason = "retain baseline provisionally: incomplete safe confirmation"
        chosen = good.get(choice, [])
        median = statistics.median(r["decode_tps"] for r in chosen) if chosen else None
        selection[model] = {"ubatch": choice, "reason": reason,
                            "valid_repeats": {str(u): len(rs) for u, rs in good.items()},
                            "paired_geometric_ratios": ratios, "median_decode_tps": median,
                            "near_100k_speed_gate": len(chosen) == 12 and median >= 35,
                            "production_qualified": False}
    return selection


def confirmation_observation(job, row):
    if row.get("model") != job["model"]:
        raise ValueError("confirmation model differs from declared job")
    if row.get("job") == job["id"] + "-fit" and row.get("target") == 0 and row.get("status") == "failed":
        # Fit probes use placeholder prompt/seed; attribute rejection to the declared request.
        return {**row, "task": job["task"], "seed": job["seed"]}
    if (row.get("job") != job["id"] or row.get("task") != job["task"]
            or row.get("seed") != job["seed"] or row.get("target") != 98304):
        raise ValueError("confirmation observation differs from declared request")
    return row


def resolve_selection(source, campaign):
    manifest = h.read_sealed(source / "manifest.json")
    rows, evidence = [], {}
    for job in manifest["queue"]:
        path = source / ("job-" + job["id"] + ".json")
        rows.append(confirmation_observation(job, h.read_sealed(path)))
        evidence[str(path)] = digest_file(path)
    result = {"source": str(source), "source_manifest_sha256": digest_file(source / "manifest.json"),
              "evidence_pins": evidence, "models": choose_microbatches(rows)}
    path = campaign / "selection.json"
    if path.exists():
        if h.read_sealed(path) != result:
            raise ValueError("confirmation evidence or selection changed after freezing")
    else:
        h.sealed_json(path, result)
    return result


def tree_fingerprint(directory):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    for path in sorted(directory.rglob("*")):
        if path.is_symlink():
            value = "link:" + os.readlink(path)
        elif path.is_file():
            value = digest_file(path)
        else:
            continue
        digest.update(json.dumps([str(path.relative_to(directory)), value]).encode())
    return digest.hexdigest()


def revalidate_measurement(source, campaign, job, row, fingerprint):
    from scripts.instruments import agentic_probe as probe

    assets_dir = campaign / "snapshot/agentic-assets"
    checks = json.loads((assets_dir / "selftest.json").read_text())
    if checks.get("test_scope") != probe.TEST_SCOPE or not checks["harness_verified"]:
        raise ValueError("grader migration requires current full-suite self-test")
    expected = next(t["expected_passed_tests"] for t in checks["tasks"] if t["task_id"] == job["task"])
    recorded = row["probe"]
    # A false repair changes model history and elapsed time; a later passing solution cannot salvage it.
    eligible = (recorded.get("complete") and recorded.get("task_success")
                and not recorded.get("timed_out") and not recorded.get("error")
                and recorded.get("grading_rounds") == 1 and recorded.get("expected_passed_tests") == expected)
    request = {"source_attempt_sha256": fingerprint, "expected_passed_tests": expected,
               "source_result_sha256": digest_file(source / ("job-" + job["id"] + ".json")),
               "destination_manifest_sha256": digest_file(campaign / "manifest.json")}
    path = campaign / "revalidation" / (job["id"] + ".json")
    if path.exists():
        report = h.read_sealed(path)
        if report["request"] != request:
            raise ValueError("grader revalidation inputs changed")
        return report
    report = {"request": request, "eligible": bool(eligible), "test_scope": probe.TEST_SCOPE}
    if eligible:
        tasks = json.loads((assets_dir / "tasks.json").read_text())
        task = next(t for t in tasks if t["task_id"] == job["task"])
        assets = json.loads((assets_dir / "tools.json").read_text())
        original = json.loads((source / row["attempt"] / "grade-0.json").read_text())
        if (original["returncode"] != 0 or original["passed"] != expected
                or original["failed"] or original["ignored"]):
            raise ValueError("recorded first-round success contradicts saved grading evidence")
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="qwen-regrade-") as scratch:
            work = Path(scratch) / "grade"
            probe.materialize(task, assets_dir, work)
            probe.transfer_solution(task, source / row["attempt"] / "grade-0", work)
            grade = probe.test_suite(task, work, assets, 300, asset_dir=assets_dir)
        report["grade"] = grade
        report["eligible"] = (grade["returncode"] == 0 and grade["passed"] == expected
                              and grade["failed"] == grade["ignored"] == 0)
    path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    h.sealed_json(path, report)
    print(json.dumps({"revalidated": job["id"], "reuse": report["eligible"]}), flush=True)
    return report


def reuse_completed(source, campaign, *, allow_queue_reorder=False, revalidate_grader=False,
                    allow_watchdog_recovery=False):
    source, campaign = source.resolve(), campaign.resolve()
    if source == campaign:
        raise ValueError("recovery requires a new campaign")
    old, new = (h.read_sealed(p / "manifest.json") for p in (source, campaign))
    for key in ("engine_commit", "binary", "binary_pins", "devices", "port", "models", "safety", "max_attempts"):
        if old[key] != new[key]:
            raise ValueError("recovery changed comparison contract: " + key)
    old_jobs, new_jobs = ({job["id"]: job for job in m["queue"]} for m in (old, new))
    if (len(old_jobs) != len(old["queue"]) or len(new_jobs) != len(new["queue"])
            or old_jobs != new_jobs or (not allow_queue_reorder and old["queue"] != new["queue"])):
        raise ValueError("recovery changed comparison contract: queue")
    allowed = {"scripts/instruments/hillclimb.py", "scripts/instruments/confirmed_agentic.py"}
    if revalidate_grader:
        allowed |= {"scripts/instruments/agentic_probe.py", "agentic-assets/selftest.json"}
    changed = {key for key in old["snapshot_pins"].keys() | new["snapshot_pins"].keys()
               if old["snapshot_pins"].get(key) != new["snapshot_pins"].get(key)}
    if changed - allowed:
        raise ValueError("recovery changed inference/grader assets: " + str(sorted(changed - allowed)))
    excluded_policy = {"memory_recovery"} | ({"queue_order"} if allow_queue_reorder else set())
    if allow_watchdog_recovery:
        if new["continuation"].get("watchdog_recovery") != WATCHDOG_RECOVERY:
            raise ValueError("recovery requires the bounded watchdog policy")
        excluded_policy.add("watchdog_recovery")
    old_policy, new_policy = ({k: v for k, v in m["continuation"].items() if k not in excluded_policy}
                              for m in (old, new))
    if new_policy != old_policy:
        raise ValueError("recovery changed selection policy")
    old_state, state = (json.loads((p / "state.json").read_text()) for p in (source, campaign))
    if old_state["status"] not in {"stopped", "complete"} or old_state.get("owned"):
        raise ValueError("source campaign must be inactive and have no owned processes")
    if state["status"] != "prepared" or state.get("owned"):
        raise ValueError("destination campaign must not have started")
    imported, requeued = [], []
    for job in new["queue"]:
        name = old_state["completed"].get(job["id"])
        if not name:
            continue
        row = h.read_sealed(source / name)
        if row["status"] != "passed":
            requeued.append({"job": job["id"], "reason": row.get("reason"), "source_record": str(source / name)})
            continue
        if row.get("model") != job["model"] or row.get("task") != job["task"]:
            raise ValueError("source result does not match queued task")
        relative = Path(row["attempt"])
        if relative.is_absolute() or relative.parts[0] != "attempts" or ".." in relative.parts:
            raise ValueError("invalid source attempt path")
        origin, target = source / relative, campaign / relative
        fingerprint = tree_fingerprint(origin)
        if revalidate_grader:
            report = revalidate_measurement(source, campaign, job, row, fingerprint)
            if not report["eligible"]:
                requeued.append({"job": job["id"], "reason": "grader_revalidation_required_rerun",
                                 "source_record": str(source / name)})
                continue
        if not target.exists():
            scratch = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=".import-", dir=campaign / "attempts"))
            shutil.copytree(origin, scratch, symlinks=True, dirs_exist_ok=True)
            if tree_fingerprint(scratch) != fingerprint:
                raise ValueError("copied attempt verification failed")
            os.rename(scratch, target)
        elif tree_fingerprint(target) != fingerprint:
            raise ValueError("existing imported attempt differs")
        destination = campaign / ("job-" + job["id"] + ".json")
        if destination.exists() and h.read_sealed(destination) != row:
            raise ValueError("existing imported result differs")
        h.sealed_json(destination, row)
        state["completed"][job["id"]] = destination.name
        imported.append({"job": job["id"], "source_result_sha256": digest_file(source / name),
                         "attempt_tree_sha256": fingerprint})
        if revalidate_grader:
            imported[-1]["revalidation_sha256"] = digest_file(campaign / "revalidation" / (job["id"] + ".json"))
    audit = {"source": str(source), "source_manifest_sha256": digest_file(source / "manifest.json"),
             "changed_coordinator_files": sorted(changed & {
                 "scripts/instruments/hillclimb.py", "scripts/instruments/confirmed_agentic.py"}),
             "changed_snapshot_files": sorted(changed), "queue_order_changed": old["queue"] != new["queue"],
             "grader_revalidated": revalidate_grader,
             "watchdog_recovery_changed": old["continuation"].get("watchdog_recovery") != new["continuation"].get("watchdog_recovery"),
             "reused": imported, "requeued": requeued}
    h.sealed_json(campaign / "RECOVERY.json", audit)
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "state.json", state)
    return audit


def suite_queue(task_ids, order="task-first"):
    jobs = a.task_queue(task_ids, MODELS)
    for job in jobs:
        if job["model"] != "iq3-control":
            alternative = dict(job["configs"][0], ubatch=512)
            job["configs"].append(alternative)
    if order == "model-first":
        jobs.sort(key=lambda job: MODELS.index(job["model"]))
    elif order != "task-first":
        raise ValueError("unknown queue order")
    return jobs


def handoff_at_boundary(source, campaign, job_id, deadline):
    source, campaign = source.resolve(), campaign.resolve()
    manifest = h.read_sealed(source / "manifest.json")
    if source == campaign or job_id not in {j["id"] for j in manifest["queue"]}:
        raise ValueError("handoff requires a distinct source and a declared boundary job")
    request = {"source": str(source), "job": job_id,
               "source_manifest_sha256": digest_file(source / "manifest.json"), "deadline_epoch": deadline}
    request_path = campaign / "HANDOFF.json"
    if request_path.exists() and h.read_sealed(request_path) != request:
        raise ValueError("handoff request changed")
    h.sealed_json(request_path, request)
    done_path, stop_path = campaign / "HANDOFF-DONE.json", campaign / "HANDOFF-STOP.json"
    if done_path.exists():
        if h.read_sealed(done_path)["recovery_sha256"] != digest_file(campaign / "RECOVERY.json"):
            raise ValueError("handoff recovery audit changed")
        return True

    def status(phase, **extra):
        h.atomic_json(campaign / "stage-status.json", {"phase": phase, "source": str(source),
                                                     "boundary_job": job_id, "updated": time.time(), **extra})
    status("waiting_for_task_boundary")
    while True:
        if (campaign / "STOP").exists() or time.time() >= deadline:
            status("stopped", reason="stop_or_deadline")
            return False
        state = json.loads((source / "state.json").read_text())
        if (state["status"] in {"stopped", "error"} and not
                (state["status"] == "stopped" and state.get("reason") == "requested_stop" and stop_path.exists())):
            status("blocked", reason="source_stopped", source_reason=state.get("reason"))
            return False
        if (source / "STOP").exists() and not stop_path.exists():
            status("blocked", reason="source_stop_not_owned_by_handoff")
            return False
        boundary = source / ("job-" + job_id + ".json")
        if job_id in state.get("completed", {}):
            if h.read_sealed(boundary)["status"] != "passed":
                status("blocked", reason="boundary_not_valid_measurement")
                return False
            # STOP is first requested after the full logical result is sealed, never mid-task.
            if state["status"] == "running" and not stop_path.exists():
                h.sealed_json(stop_path, {"requested_at": time.time()})
                status("stopping_at_task_boundary")
            if stop_path.exists():
                (source / "STOP").touch()
            coordinator = state.get("coordinator", {})
            alive = (coordinator.get("start") is not None and
                     h.proc_identity(coordinator["pid"]) == coordinator["start"])
            if state["status"] in {"stopped", "complete"} and not state.get("owned") and not alive:
                reuse_completed(source, campaign, allow_queue_reorder=True)
                h.sealed_json(done_path, {"recovery_sha256": digest_file(campaign / "RECOVERY.json")})
                status("handoff_complete")
                return True
        if stop_path.exists() and time.time() >= h.read_sealed(stop_path)["requested_at"] + 180:
            status("blocked", reason="source_shutdown_timeout")
            return False
        time.sleep(.2)


class ConfirmedRunner(a.AgenticRunner):
    def __init__(self, campaign, deadline, selection):
        super().__init__(campaign, max(0, deadline - time.time()) / 3600, deadline)
        self.selection = selection
        self.watchdog_policy = self.manifest.get("continuation", {}).get("watchdog_recovery")
        self.recovery_extra_headroom_mib = self.manifest.get("continuation", {}).get(
            "recovery_extra_headroom_mib", MEMORY_RECOVERY["extra_headroom_mib"])
        if self.watchdog_policy is not None and self.watchdog_policy != WATCHDOG_RECOVERY:
            raise ValueError("unrecognized watchdog recovery policy")

    def retryable_failure(self, reason):
        return (reason == "cuda_watchdog" and self.watchdog_policy is not None
                or memory_pressure(reason) or super().retryable_failure(reason))

    def retryable_gpu_fault(self, log):
        return self.watchdog_policy is not None and watchdog_timeout(log)

    def retry_limit(self, reason):
        if reason == "cuda_watchdog" and self.watchdog_policy is not None:
            return 1 + self.watchdog_policy["max_retries"]
        return super().retry_limit(reason)

    def retry_count(self, failures, started_attempts):
        if failures and failures[-1].get("reason") == "cuda_watchdog" and self.watchdog_policy is not None:
            # A coordinator crash during a retry consumes that attempt, even without result.json.
            return started_attempts
        return super().retry_count(failures, started_attempts)

    def retry_backoff(self, failures, count):
        if failures[-1].get("reason") == "cuda_watchdog" and self.watchdog_policy is not None:
            return self.watchdog_policy["backoff_seconds"][count - 1]
        return super().retry_backoff(failures, count)

    def wait_for_watchdog(self, job_id, result, seconds):
        if self.children or self.state.get("owned"):
            raise h.StopCampaign("watchdog_recovery_requires_stopped_children")
        attempt = self.path / result["attempt"]
        with (attempt / "telemetry.jsonl").open() as stream:
            before = json.loads(stream.readline())
        required = recovery_requirements(result, before, self.vram_reserves, self.recovery_extra_headroom_mib)
        since = result["finished_at"] - result["elapsed_seconds"]
        kernel = watchdog_kernel_check(since)
        h.atomic_json(attempt / "watchdog-kernel.json", {"since_epoch": since, "log": kernel})
        until = result["finished_at"] + self.watchdog_policy["wait_seconds"]
        retry_at = time.monotonic() + seconds
        stable_since = None
        self.state["resource_wait"] = {"job": job_id, "reason": "cuda_watchdog",
                                       "required_free_mib": required, "until_epoch": until}
        self.save()
        self.event("watchdog_wait", **self.state["resource_wait"])
        self.report()
        while True:
            self.poll(reserves=False)
            if time.time() >= until:
                raise h.StopCampaign("watchdog_health_unresolved: " + job_id)
            enough = all(self.samples[-1]["gpus"][gpu]["memory.free"] >= value
                         for gpu, value in required.items())
            stable_since = (stable_since if stable_since is not None else time.monotonic()) if enough else None
