            if (stable_since is not None and time.monotonic() - stable_since >= self.watchdog_policy["stable_seconds"]
                    and time.monotonic() >= retry_at):
                watchdog_kernel_check(since)
                h.endpoint_free(self.manifest["port"])
                self.state.pop("resource_wait", None)
                self.save()
                self.event("watchdog_recovered", job=job_id, required_free_mib=required)
                return
            time.sleep(.5)

    def wait_before_retry(self, job_id, result, seconds):
        if result.get("reason") == "cuda_watchdog" and self.watchdog_policy is not None:
            return self.wait_for_watchdog(job_id, result, seconds)
        if not memory_pressure(result.get("reason")):
            return super().wait_before_retry(job_id, result, seconds)
        if self.children:
            raise h.StopCampaign("memory_recovery_requires_stopped_children")
        with (self.path / result["attempt"] / "telemetry.jsonl").open() as stream:
            before = json.loads(stream.readline())
        required = recovery_requirements(result, before, self.vram_reserves, self.recovery_extra_headroom_mib)
        until = result["finished_at"] + MEMORY_RECOVERY["wait_seconds"]
        retry_at = time.monotonic() + seconds
        stable_since = None
        self.state["resource_wait"] = {"job": job_id, "reason": result["reason"],
                                       "required_free_mib": required, "until_epoch": until}
        self.save()
        self.event("memory_wait", **self.state["resource_wait"])
        self.report()
        while True:
            # No inference process exists while waiting; thermal/RAM/power/STOP guards still apply.
            self.poll(reserves=False)
            if time.time() >= until:
                raise h.StopCampaign("memory_pressure_unresolved: " + job_id)
            sample = self.samples[-1]
            enough = all(sample["gpus"][gpu]["memory.free"] >= value for gpu, value in required.items())
            stable_since = (stable_since if stable_since is not None else time.monotonic()) if enough else None
            if (stable_since is not None and time.monotonic() - stable_since >= MEMORY_RECOVERY["stable_seconds"]
                    and time.monotonic() >= retry_at):
                self.state.pop("resource_wait", None)
                self.save()
                self.event("memory_recovered", job=job_id, required_free_mib=required)
                return
            time.sleep(.5)

    def acquire(self):
        until = min(self.deadline, time.monotonic() + 180)
        while True:
            if (self.path / "STOP").exists() or time.monotonic() >= self.deadline:
                raise h.StopCampaign("stop_or_deadline")
            try:
                return super().acquire()
            except h.CampaignBusy:
                for handle in self.lockfiles:
                    fcntl.flock(handle, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
                    handle.close()
                self.lockfiles.clear()
                if time.monotonic() >= until:
                    raise h.StopCampaign("gpu_lock_timeout")
                time.sleep(min(5, max(0, until - time.monotonic())))

    def execute_job(self, job):
        if job["model"] == "iq3-control":
            return self.checked_execute(job)
        choice = self.selection["models"][job["model"]]["ubatch"]
        if choice is None:
            return {"status": "skipped", "model": job["model"], "task": job["task"],
                    "reason": "no_safe_confirmed_profile"}
        configs = [config for config in job["configs"] if config["ubatch"] == choice]
        if len(configs) != 1:
            raise ValueError("selection must resolve one predeclared configuration")
        resolved = copy.deepcopy(job)
        resolved["configs"] = configs
        return self.checked_execute(resolved)

    def checked_execute(self, job):
        result = super().execute_job(job)
        if result.get("fit_reason", result.get("reason")) in {"cuda_watchdog", "gpu_fault"}:
            raise h.StopCampaign("watchdog_retries_exhausted: " + job["id"])
        if memory_pressure(result.get("fit_reason", result.get("reason"))):
            raise h.StopCampaign("memory_retries_exhausted: " + job["id"])
        if result.get("reason") in {"probe_failed", "probe_incomplete"}:
            raise h.StopCampaign("agentic_instrument_fault: " + result["reason"])
        return result


def summarize(campaign):
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    manifest = h.read_sealed(campaign / "manifest.json")
    for job in manifest["queue"]:
        path = campaign / ("job-" + job["id"] + ".json")
        if path.exists():
            groups[job["model"]].append(h.read_sealed(path))
    summary = {"updated_epoch": time.time(), "state": json.loads((campaign / "state.json").read_text())["status"],
               "models": {}, "scope": "full declared task comparison; configurations are not production-promoted"}
    for model, rows in groups.items():
        measured = [r for r in rows if r["status"] == "passed"]
        probes = [r["probe"] for r in measured]
        summary["models"][model] = {"processed": len(rows), "measured": len(probes),
            "solved": sum(p["task_success"] for p in probes), "timeouts": sum(p["timed_out"] for p in probes),
            "wall_seconds": sum(p["verified_wall_seconds"] for p in probes),
            "prefill_seconds": sum(p["prefill_seconds"] for p in probes),
            "decode_seconds": sum(p["decode_seconds"] for p in probes),
            "output_tokens": sum(p["metrics_delta"].get("decode_tokens", 0) for p in probes),
            "nonpilot_solved": sum(r["probe"]["task_success"] for r in measured if r["task"] not in a.TASK_ORDER)}
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "SUMMARY.json", summary)
    return summary


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("command", choices=["prepare", "resume", "handoff", "status", "stop"])
    parser.add_argument("--campaign", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--assets", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--confirmation", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--reuse-successes-from", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--revalidate-grader", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--retry-watchdog", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--queue-order", choices=["task-first", "model-first"], default="task-first")
    parser.add_argument("--after-campaign", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--after-job")
    parser.add_argument("--deadline-epoch", type=float)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    campaign = args.campaign.resolve()
    if args.revalidate_grader and (args.command != "prepare" or not args.reuse_successes_from):
        parser.error("grader revalidation requires prepare with a stopped source campaign")
    if args.retry_watchdog and args.command != "prepare":
        parser.error("watchdog recovery must be frozen during prepare")
    if args.command == "prepare":
        if args.assets is None or args.confirmation is None:
            parser.error("prepare requires assets and confirmation")
        assets, source = args.assets.resolve(), args.confirmation.resolve()
        tasks = json.loads((assets / "tasks.json").read_text())
        task_ids = [t["task_id"] for t in tasks]
        if len(task_ids) != 78 or len(set(task_ids)) != 78 or not set(a.TASK_ORDER) <= set(task_ids):
            parser.error("full suite requires all 78 unique tasks including the pilot")
        ordered = a.TASK_ORDER + [t for t in task_ids if t not in a.TASK_ORDER]
        h.read_sealed(source / "manifest.json")
        policy = {"confirmation": str(source), "manifest_sha256": digest_file(source / "manifest.json"),
                  "selection": "ub128-vs512-v1", "models": MODELS, "production_promotion": False,
                  "memory_recovery": MEMORY_RECOVERY}
        if args.retry_watchdog:
            policy["watchdog_recovery"] = WATCHDOG_RECOVERY
        if args.queue_order != "task-first":
            policy["queue_order"] = args.queue_order
        h.initialize(campaign, ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models", profiles=suite_queue(ordered, args.queue_order),
                     max_attempts=3, agentic_assets=assets, continuation=policy)
        if args.reuse_successes_from:
            audit = reuse_completed(args.reuse_successes_from, campaign,
                                    allow_queue_reorder=args.queue_order == "model-first",
                                    revalidate_grader=args.revalidate_grader,
                                    allow_watchdog_recovery=args.retry_watchdog)
            print(f"Reused {len(audit['reused'])} valid measurements; requeued {len(audit['requeued'])} invalid jobs")
        print(campaign)
        return
    if args.command == "stop":
        (campaign / "STOP").touch()
        return
    if args.command == "status":
        for name in ("stage-status.json", "selection.json", "SUMMARY.json", "REPORT.md"):
            if (campaign / name).exists():
                print((campaign / name).read_text())
        return
    if args.deadline_epoch is None or not math.isfinite(args.deadline_epoch) or args.deadline_epoch <= time.time():
        parser.error("resume requires a future absolute deadline")
    if args.command == "handoff" and (args.after_campaign is None or args.after_job is None):
        parser.error("handoff requires --after-campaign and --after-job")
    snapshot = campaign / "snapshot"
    if ROOT.resolve() != snapshot.resolve():
        command = [sys.executable, "-m", "scripts.instruments.confirmed_agentic", args.command,
                   "--campaign", str(campaign), "--deadline-epoch", str(args.deadline_epoch)]
        if args.command == "handoff":
            command += ["--after-campaign", str(args.after_campaign.resolve()), "--after-job", args.after_job]
        env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(snapshot), PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1")
        os.chdir(snapshot)
        os.execve(sys.executable, command, env)
    manifest = h.read_sealed(campaign / "manifest.json")
    if args.command == "handoff" and not handoff_at_boundary(
            args.after_campaign, campaign, args.after_job, args.deadline_epoch):
        return
    policy = manifest["continuation"]
    source = Path(policy["confirmation"])
    if digest_file(source / "manifest.json") != policy["manifest_sha256"]:
        raise ValueError("confirmation manifest changed")
    if not a.wait_for_predecessor(campaign, source, args.deadline_epoch):
        return
    selection = resolve_selection(source, campaign)
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "stage-status.json", {"phase": "running_full_suite", "updated": time.time()})
    ConfirmedRunner(campaign, args.deadline_epoch, selection).run()
    result = summarize(campaign)
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "stage-status.json", {"phase": result["state"], "updated": time.time()})
    if result["state"] == "complete":
        from scripts.analysis import session_signatures
        signatures = session_signatures.analyze_campaign(campaign)
        h.atomic_json(campaign / "SESSION-SIGNATURES.json", signatures)
        (campaign / "SESSION-SIGNATURES.md").write_text(session_signatures.render(signatures))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Durable JSONL result emission and shared Codex invocation helpers."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import dataclasses
import datetime as dt
import fcntl
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tomllib
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request
from typing import Any


# Bump when a field changes meaning, not when one is appended: readers tolerate a
# missing key, but not a key whose sense silently moved under them. Rows predating
# this carry null and are session-1 shapes (42, 55 and 59 fields).
SCHEMA_VERSION = 2

RESULT_FIELDS = (
    "schema_version",
    "timestamp",
    "experiment_id",
    "task_id",
    "run_index",
    "lane",
    "quant",
    "gpu",
    "gpu_count",
    "gpu_devices",
    "power_limit_w",
    "gpu_driver",
    "cuda_toolkit",
    "engine",
    "engine_commit",
    "model",
    "model_sha256",
    "served_model_path",
    "context_limit",
    "context_actual",
    "kv_k",
    "kv_v",
    "reasoning_effort",
    "temperature",
    "top_p",
    "top_k",
    "mtp_enabled",
    "mtp_nmax",
    "dflash_enabled",
    "draft_model",
    "prompt_tokens",
    "completion_tokens",
    "cached_prompt_tokens",
    "cached_input_tokens",
    "cache_write_tokens",
    "reasoning_tokens",
    "prefill_tokens",
    "prefill_seconds",
    "decode_tokens",
    "decode_seconds",
    "peak_context_tokens",
    "server_high_water_tokens",
    "context_padding_tokens",
    "draft_tokens",
    "draft_accepted_tokens",
    "drafts",
    "prefill_tps",
    "decode_tps",
    "ttft_ms",
    "wall_seconds",
    "transcript",
    "max_vram_mb",
    "avg_power_w",
    "task_success",
    "tests_passed",
    "timed_out",
    "agent_turns",
    "tool_calls",
    "failed_test_cycles",
    "harness_verified",
    "human_rescue",
    "notes",
)

_CONTEXT_FIELDS = {
    "experiment_id",
    "lane",
    "quant",
    "gpu",
    "gpu_count",
    "gpu_devices",
    "power_limit_w",
    "gpu_driver",
    "cuda_toolkit",
    "engine",
    "engine_commit",
    "model",
    "model_sha256",
    "served_model_path",
    "context_limit",
    "context_actual",
    "kv_k",
    "kv_v",
    "reasoning_effort",
    "temperature",
    "top_p",
    "top_k",
    "mtp_enabled",
    "mtp_nmax",
    "dflash_enabled",
    "draft_model",
}


@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RunContext:
    """Values that remain invariant for every task emitted by one process."""

    out: Path | str
    lane: str
    quant: str
    experiment_id: str | None = None
    gpu: str | None = None
    gpu_count: int | None = None
    gpu_devices: list[dict[str, object]] | None = None
    power_limit_w: float | None = None
    gpu_driver: str | None = None
    cuda_toolkit: str | None = None
    engine: str | None = "llama.cpp"
    engine_commit: str | None = None
    model: str | None = "qwen3.8-27b"
    model_sha256: str | None = None
    served_model_path: str | None = None
    context_limit: int | None = None
    context_actual: int | None = None
    kv_k: str | None = None
    kv_v: str | None = None
    reasoning_effort: str | None = "xhigh"
    temperature: float | None = None
    top_p: float | None = None
    top_k: int | None = None
    mtp_enabled: bool | None = None
    mtp_nmax: int | None = None
    dflash_enabled: bool | None = None
    draft_model: str | None = None


def utc_timestamp() -> str:
    return dt.datetime.now(dt.timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="milliseconds").replace(
        "+00:00", "Z"
    )


def build_row(
    ctx: RunContext, task_id: str, run_index: int, **measures: Any
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build one fixed-shape result row without writing it."""

    if task_id is None or task_id == "":
        raise ValueError("task_id must be non-empty")
    if run_index is None:
        raise ValueError("run_index must not be null")

    unknown = set(measures) - (set(RESULT_FIELDS) - _CONTEXT_FIELDS)
    if unknown:
        raise TypeError(f"unknown result field(s): {', '.join(sorted(unknown))}")
    forbidden = set(measures) & {"schema_version", "timestamp", "task_id", "run_index"}
    if forbidden:
        raise TypeError(f"reserved result field(s): {', '.join(sorted(forbidden))}")

    row = dict.fromkeys(RESULT_FIELDS)
    row["schema_version"] = SCHEMA_VERSION
    row["timestamp"] = utc_timestamp()
    row["task_id"] = task_id
    row["run_index"] = run_index
    for field in dataclasses.fields(ctx):
        if field.name != "out":
            row[field.name] = getattr(ctx, field.name)
    row.update(measures)
    return row


def append_row(out: Path | str, row: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Append and durably sync one JSONL row, whatever its shape.

    Schema-neutral on purpose: the throughput instruments carry their own shapes and
    must not be forced through RESULT_FIELDS, but they still need the lock and the
    fsync -- a plain flush loses the row the reaper is about to make unrepeatable.
    """

    out = Path(out)
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    line = json.dumps(
        row, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False
    ) + "\n"
    with out.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        try:
            handle.write(line)
            handle.flush()
            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
        finally:
            fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    return row


def emit(ctx: RunContext, task_id: str, run_index: int, **measures: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Append and durably sync exactly one fixed-shape JSONL result row."""

    return append_row(ctx.out, build_row(ctx, task_id, run_index, **measures))


def _metrics_url(base_url: str) -> str:
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(base_url.rstrip("/"))
    path = parsed.path
    if path.endswith("/v1"):
        path = path[:-3]
    return urllib.parse.urlunsplit((parsed.scheme, parsed.netloc, path + "/metrics", "", ""))


# Monotonic counters, so a before/after pair around one task yields that task's
# share; the last two are gauges and are read as-is.
_METRIC_NAMES = {
    "llamacpp:prompt_tokens_total": "prompt_tokens_total",
    "llamacpp:prompt_tokens_cached_total": "prompt_tokens_cached_total",
    "llamacpp:prompt_seconds_total": "prompt_seconds_total",
    "llamacpp:tokens_predicted_total": "tokens_predicted_total",
    "llamacpp:tokens_predicted_seconds_total": "tokens_predicted_seconds_total",
    "llamacpp:spec_decode_num_draft_tokens_total": "draft_tokens_total",
    "llamacpp:spec_decode_num_accepted_tokens_total": "draft_accepted_total",
    "llamacpp:spec_decode_num_drafts_total": "drafts_total",
    "llamacpp:n_tokens_max": "n_tokens_max",
    "llamacpp:prompt_tokens_seconds": "prefill_tps",
    "llamacpp:predicted_tokens_seconds": "decode_tps",
    # vLLM's equivalents, so a vLLM leg records the same eight measures rather than
    # nulls. Only one server type ever answers, so the two prefixes cannot collide.
    # Prefill volume must come from request_prefill_kv_computed_tokens_sum, not
    # prompt_tokens_by_source_total: the latter splits across a `source` label the
    # parser drops, so its three series would overwrite each other.
    "vllm:request_prefill_kv_computed_tokens_sum": "prompt_tokens_total",
    "vllm:prefix_cache_hits_total": "prompt_tokens_cached_total",
    "vllm:request_prefill_time_seconds_sum": "prompt_seconds_total",
    "vllm:generation_tokens_total": "tokens_predicted_total",
    "vllm:request_decode_time_seconds_sum": "tokens_predicted_seconds_total",
    "vllm:spec_decode_num_draft_tokens_total": "draft_tokens_total",
    "vllm:spec_decode_num_accepted_tokens_total": "draft_accepted_total",
    "vllm:spec_decode_num_drafts_total": "drafts_total",
}


def metrics_delta(before: dict[str, float], after: dict[str, float]) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Per-task telemetry from a counter pair. Empty if either scrape failed.

    A server restart resets the counters, so a negative delta means the pair does
    not describe one task and the whole reading is dropped rather than clamped.
    """

    if not before or not after:
        return {}
    pairs = {
        "prefill_tokens": "prompt_tokens_total",
        "cached_prompt_tokens": "prompt_tokens_cached_total",
        "prefill_seconds": "prompt_seconds_total",
        "decode_tokens": "tokens_predicted_total",
        "decode_seconds": "tokens_predicted_seconds_total",
        "draft_tokens": "draft_tokens_total",
        "draft_accepted_tokens": "draft_accepted_total",
        "drafts": "drafts_total",
    }
    out: dict[str, float] = {}
    for name, counter in pairs.items():
        if counter not in before or counter not in after:
            continue
        value = after[counter] - before[counter]
        if value < 0:
            return {}
        out[name] = value
    if "n_tokens_max" in after:
        # Process-wide high-water mark. Kept for the record, but per-task depth
        # comes from ContextSampler; this cannot be attributed to one task.
        out["server_high_water_tokens"] = after["n_tokens_max"]
    if out.get("prefill_seconds"):
        out["prefill_tps"] = out["prefill_tokens"] / out["prefill_seconds"]
    if out.get("decode_seconds"):
        out["decode_tps"] = out["decode_tokens"] / out["decode_seconds"]
    return out


def _slots_url(base_url: str) -> str:
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(base_url)
    path = parsed.path.rstrip("/")
    if path.endswith("/v1"):
        path = path[:-3]
    return urllib.parse.urlunsplit((parsed.scheme, parsed.netloc, path + "/slots", "", ""))


class ContextSampler(threading.Thread):
    """Track the deepest context a task actually occupies.

    llama-server's `n_tokens_max` metric is a process-wide high-water mark, so it
    cannot attribute depth to a task. `/slots` reports the live request instead:
    prompt length plus tokens decoded so far is the true occupancy.
    """

    def __init__(self, base_url: str, interval: float = 2.0) -> None:
        super().__init__(daemon=True)
        self.url = _slots_url(base_url)
        self.interval = interval
        self.peak = 0
        self._stop = threading.Event()

    def _sample(self) -> None:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(self.url, timeout=3) as response:
            slots = json.loads(response.read())
        for slot in slots if isinstance(slots, list) else [slots]:
            if not isinstance(slot, dict) or not slot.get("is_processing"):
                continue
            decoded = 0
            nxt = slot.get("next_token")
            if isinstance(nxt, list) and nxt and isinstance(nxt[0], dict):
                decoded = nxt[0].get("n_decoded") or 0
            depth = (slot.get("n_prompt_tokens") or 0) + decoded
            self.peak = max(self.peak, depth)

    def run(self) -> None:
        while not self._stop.wait(self.interval):
            try:
                self._sample()
            except Exception:
                # Sampling is observational: never let it fail a task.
                continue

    def stop(self) -> int:
        self._stop.set()
        return self.peak


def _props_url(base_url: str) -> str:
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(base_url)
    path = parsed.path.rstrip("/")
    if path.endswith("/v1"):
        path = path[:-3]
    return urllib.parse.urlunsplit((parsed.scheme, parsed.netloc, path + "/props", "", ""))


def _models_url(base_url: str) -> str:
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlsplit(base_url)
    path = parsed.path.rstrip("/")
    if not path.endswith("/v1"):
        path += "/v1"
    return urllib.parse.urlunsplit((parsed.scheme, parsed.netloc, path + "/models", "", ""))


def _probe_openai_models(base_url: str, timeout: float) -> dict[str, object]:
    """Fallback for servers without llama.cpp's /props, which vLLM 404s.

    Without it a vLLM leg records no served_model_path at all, so the guard below
    is silently absent on exactly the legs whose checkpoint is a separate download.
    """

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(_models_url(base_url), timeout=timeout) as response:
            body = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
    except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError, ValueError):
        return {}
    data = body.get("data") if isinstance(body, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(data, list) or not data or not isinstance(data[0], dict):
        return {}
    entry = data[0]
    out: dict[str, object] = {}
    if isinstance(entry.get("root"), str):
        out["served_model_path"] = entry["root"]
    if isinstance(entry.get("max_model_len"), int):
        out["context_limit"] = entry["max_model_len"]
    return out


def server_alive(base_url: str, attempts: int = 3, timeout: float = 5.0) -> bool:
    """Is the endpoint still answering? Retried, because one blip must not end a leg.

    A leg that loses its server records every remaining task as a model failure with
    null token counts and still exits 0, and the box carrying the server log is torn
    down by the driver's own EXIT trap.
    """

    for attempt in range(attempts):
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(_models_url(base_url), timeout=timeout) as response:
                body = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
        except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError, ValueError):
            body = None
        if isinstance(body, dict) and isinstance(body.get("data"), list):
            return True
        if attempt + 1 < attempts:
            time.sleep(2.0)
    return False


def probe_server(base_url: str, timeout: float = 10.0) -> dict[str, object]:
    """Read the served config so every row is self-describing.

    Includes `served_model_path`, which is the only way to catch the silent
    catastrophe of this experiment: forgetting to swap the model between quants.
    """

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(_props_url(base_url), timeout=timeout) as response:
            props = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace"))
    except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError, ValueError):
        return _probe_openai_models(base_url, timeout)
    if not isinstance(props, dict):
        return _probe_openai_models(base_url, timeout)
    gen = props.get("default_generation_settings") or {}
    params = gen.get("params") or {}
    out: dict[str, object] = {}
    if isinstance(gen.get("n_ctx"), int):
        out["context_limit"] = gen["n_ctx"]
    for key in ("temperature", "top_p", "top_k"):
        if key in params:
            out[key] = params[key]
    if isinstance(props.get("build_info"), str):
        out["engine_commit"] = props["build_info"]
    if isinstance(props.get("model_path"), str):
        out["served_model_path"] = props["model_path"]
    return out


def scrape_metrics(base_url: str, timeout: float = 5.0) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Return available llama-server counters and gauges, or an empty dict."""

    wanted = _METRIC_NAMES
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(_metrics_url(base_url), timeout=timeout) as response:
            body = response.read().decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
    except (OSError, urllib.error.URLError, UnicodeError):
        return {}

    result: dict[str, float] = {}
    for raw_line in body.splitlines():
        line = raw_line.strip()
        if not line or line.startswith("#"):
            continue
        parts = line.split()
        if len(parts) < 2:
            continue
        metric = parts[0].split("{", 1)[0]
        output_name = wanted.get(metric)
        if output_name is None:
            continue
        try:
            result[output_name] = float(parts[1])
        except ValueError:
            continue
    return result


@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CodexStats:
    agent_turns: int | None
    tool_calls: int | None
    prompt_tokens: int | None
    completion_tokens: int | None
    # Cache reads as the *client* saw them. Unlike the llama-server counters this
    # is lane-independent, so it is the only cache figure comparable between a
    # local quant and an API model.
    cached_input_tokens: int | None = None
    cache_write_tokens: int | None = None
    reasoning_tokens: int | None = None


# C2 (control) is the direct competitor: what would be used instead of owning a GPU.
# C3 (frontier) is the ceiling: separates "quantization broke it" from "27B cannot do it".
LANE_MODELS = {
    "qwen": "qwen3.8-27b",
    "quant": "qwen3.8-27b",
    "control": "gpt-5.6-luna",
    "frontier": "gpt-5.6-sol",
    # The alternative the local card is actually competing with. Two tiers, because
    # which one is the fair comparison is the open question: on measured Polyglot
    # traffic the cheap tier costs about what renting the card does, and the frontier
    # tier costs five to ten times more, so "is local worth it" resolves entirely on
    # which of them this model's capability sits nearer.
    "deepseek-flash": "deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash",
    "deepseek-pro": "deepseek/deepseek-v4-pro",
    # Same cheap tier, two more routes. The vision SKU is 5.5x the input rate of plain
    # Flash and is here to price the multimodal option, not because Polyglot needs images.
    "deepseek-flash-vision": "deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash-vision-exp",
    # GLM's cheap tier, released this month. Its local path is closed -- no GGUF exists --
    # so the API is the only way to place it against the card at all.
    "glm-flash": "z-ai/glm-5.3-flash",
    # The same family served by someone else: qwen-flash is the cheap sibling, and
    # qwen-27b-api is this exact model at someone else's quantization -- fp8 on all but
    # one OpenRouter provider, so it reads as a light-quantization reference rather than
    # a full-precision control, and provider routing decides which.
    "qwen-flash": "qwen/qwen3.8-flash",
    "qwen-27b-api": "qwen/qwen3.8-27b",
    # The control lane again, but billed per token instead of against the Codex weekly
    # limit. Same model as `control`; the pair is what converts an opaque subscription
    # quota into a dollar figure.
    "luna-or": "openai/gpt-5.6-luna",
}

# The only lanes served by a card we rent, and so the only ones that owe a GPU capture.
LOCAL_LANES = {"qwen", "quant"}

# A slash means an OpenRouter model id rather than a first-party one; the provider has
# to be named or Codex routes it to its default and silently measures another model.
_ROUTED_LANES = {lane for lane, model in LANE_MODELS.items() if "/" in model}


def lane_model(lane: str) -> str:
    """Contract: the recorded model and the invoked model come from this one mapping."""

    try:
        return LANE_MODELS[lane]
    except KeyError:
        raise ValueError(
            "lane must be one of: " + ", ".join(sorted(LANE_MODELS))
        ) from None


# Per-process, because the local port is the only thing stopping two legs running at once:
# a profile pins a provider, a provider pins one base_url, and every leg tunnelled its box
# to that same port. A second profile on a second port is what makes legs parallel.
# Selected by environment rather than argument so it reaches `codex_command` and
# `codex_base_url` alike without threading a parameter through every call site -- and the
# endpoint guard in run_polyglot still compares what Codex will call against what is served,
# so a mismatched pair fails closed exactly as before.
CODEX_PROFILE = os.environ.get("QWEN38_CODEX_PROFILE", "qwen38")


def codex_base_url(profile: str = CODEX_PROFILE, home: Path | None = None) -> str | None:
    """The endpoint Codex will actually call, resolved from its own config.

    The local port is not the harness's to choose: the profile names a provider and the
    provider pins a base_url. A run that serves on some other port measures nothing, and
    a probe pointed at the port the harness picked will happily confirm a server Codex
    never talks to -- which is how a 40-task leg came back 0/40.

    Returns None when the config cannot be read, so the caller decides whether that is
    fatal; there is deliberately no guessed default.
    """

    home = home or Path.home() / ".codex"
    try:
        profile_cfg = tomllib.loads((home / f"{profile}.config.toml").read_text())
        provider = profile_cfg.get("model_provider")
        main = tomllib.loads((home / "config.toml").read_text())
        url = (main.get("model_providers", {}).get(provider, {}) or {}).get("base_url")
    except (OSError, ValueError, AttributeError):
        return None
    return url if isinstance(url, str) else None


def codex_command(lane: str, reasoning_effort: str) -> list[str]:
    """Build the single supported headless Codex command path for either lane."""

    command = [
        "codex",
        "exec",
        "--sandbox",
        "workspace-write",
        "--skip-git-repo-check",
        # Package fetches must work or a task with declared dependencies cannot be
        # compiled at all, and the agent codes blind. Set here rather than in the
        # user's config so every lane gets identical policy.
        "-c",
        "sandbox_workspace_write.network_access=true",
        # Compaction must fire below the served window or the server overflows. 110000
        # suits the 131072-token profile; a leg whose engine holds less must say so.
        "-c",
        f"model_auto_compact_token_limit={os.environ.get('QWEN38_AUTO_COMPACT_LIMIT', '110000')}",
    ]
    model = lane_model(lane)
    if lane in LOCAL_LANES:
        # Profile carries model, provider and context window; alias is uniform
        # across quants so the client cannot infer which one is loaded.
        command.extend(["-p", CODEX_PROFILE])
    else:
        command.extend(["-m", model])
        if lane in _ROUTED_LANES:
            command.extend(["-c", "model_provider=openrouter"])
    # Opt-in per leg, because the point of setting it is to compare against a leg that
    # did not. Codex already caps tool output near 12k tokens by default, so only a
    # value below that changes anything.
    cap = os.environ.get("QWEN38_TOOL_OUTPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT")
    if cap:
        command.extend(["-c", f"tool_output_token_limit={int(cap)}"])
    command.extend(["-c", f"model_reasoning_effort={reasoning_effort}", "--json", "-"])
    return command


def parse_codex_stats(output: str) -> CodexStats:
    """Parse Codex JSONL events; unknown values remain null rather than inferred."""

    saw_event = False
    saw_completed_turn = False
    turns = 0
    tool_calls = 0
    prompt_tokens = 0
    completion_tokens = 0
    cached_input_tokens = 0
    cache_write_tokens = 0
    reasoning_tokens = 0
    saw_prompt_tokens = False
    saw_completion_tokens = False
    saw_cached = False
    saw_cache_write = False
    saw_reasoning = False
    tool_item_types = {"command_execution", "mcp_tool_call", "file_change"}

    for line in output.splitlines():
        try:
            event = json.loads(line)
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
            continue
        if not isinstance(event, dict) or not isinstance(event.get("type"), str):
            continue
        saw_event = True
        if event["type"] == "turn.completed":
            turns += 1
            saw_completed_turn = True
            usage = event.get("usage")
            if isinstance(usage, dict):
                value = usage.get("input_tokens")
                if isinstance(value, int):
                    prompt_tokens += value
                    saw_prompt_tokens = True
                value = usage.get("output_tokens")
                if isinstance(value, int):
                    completion_tokens += value
                    saw_completion_tokens = True
                # Codex and the Responses API have both spellings in the wild.
                value = usage.get("cached_input_tokens")
                if not isinstance(value, int):
                    details = usage.get("input_tokens_details")
                    if isinstance(details, dict):
                        value = details.get("cached_tokens")
                if isinstance(value, int):
                    cached_input_tokens += value
                    saw_cached = True
                value = usage.get("reasoning_output_tokens")
                if not isinstance(value, int):
                    details = usage.get("output_tokens_details")
                    if isinstance(details, dict):
                        value = details.get("reasoning_tokens")
                if isinstance(value, int):
                    reasoning_tokens += value
                    saw_reasoning = True
                value = usage.get("cache_write_input_tokens")
                if isinstance(value, int):
                    cache_write_tokens += value
                    saw_cache_write = True
        if event["type"] == "item.completed":
            item = event.get("item")
            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get("type") in tool_item_types:
                tool_calls += 1

    return CodexStats(
        agent_turns=turns if saw_completed_turn else None,
        tool_calls=tool_calls if saw_event else None,
        prompt_tokens=prompt_tokens if saw_prompt_tokens else None,
        completion_tokens=completion_tokens if saw_completion_tokens else None,
        cached_input_tokens=cached_input_tokens if saw_cached else None,
        cache_write_tokens=cache_write_tokens if saw_cache_write else None,
        reasoning_tokens=reasoning_tokens if saw_reasoning else None,
    )


def invoke_codex(
    lane: str,
    reasoning_effort: str,
    prompt: str,
    cwd: Path,
    timeout: float,
    transcript: Path | None = None,
) -> tuple[CodexStats, float]:
    """Run one headless Codex turn and return parsed stats plus wall time.

    `codex exec` writes no rollout file, so the --json event stream on stdout is
    the only record of what was actually sent and returned. It is persisted
    before parsing, and on timeout or non-zero exit too -- a failed task is the
    one whose trace is most worth having.
    """

    started = time.monotonic()
    stdout = ""
    try:
        try:
            completed = subprocess.run(
                codex_command(lane, reasoning_effort),
                cwd=cwd,
                input=prompt,
                text=True,
                capture_output=True,
                timeout=timeout,
                check=True,
            )
            stdout = completed.stdout
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as expired:
            stdout = expired.stdout or ""
            if isinstance(stdout, bytes):
                stdout = stdout.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
            raise
        except subprocess.CalledProcessError as failed:
            stdout = failed.stdout or ""
            raise
    finally:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - started
        if transcript is not None:
            transcript.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with transcript.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                # The prompt is the data-in half and Codex never echoes it, so it
                # is recorded here as an event of our own alongside the stream.
                handle.write(
                    json.dumps(
                        {"type": "harness.prompt", "lane": lane,
                         "reasoning_effort": reasoning_effort, "prompt": prompt},
                        ensure_ascii=False,
                    )
                    + "\n"
                )
                handle.write(stdout)
                if stdout and not stdout.endswith("\n"):
                    handle.write("\n")
                handle.flush()
                os.fsync(handle.fileno())
    return parse_codex_stats(stdout), elapsed


ENV_FIELDS = ("gpu", "gpu_count", "gpu_devices", "gpu_driver", "cuda_toolkit", "power_limit_w")


def load_env(path: str | Path) -> dict[str, object]:
    """Read one scripts/gpuenv.py capture.

    Fails closed rather than recording null: a leg whose rows cannot say which card
    produced them is not worth the GPU time, and the null column is exactly the
    failure this channel exists to end.
    """

    try:
        env = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise SystemExit(f"ENV CAPTURE UNREADABLE at {path}: {exc}") from None
    if not isinstance(env, dict):
        raise SystemExit(f"ENV CAPTURE MALFORMED at {path}: expected a JSON object")
    unknown = set(env) - set(ENV_FIELDS)
    if unknown:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"ENV CAPTURE has unknown field(s) {sorted(unknown)}; regenerate it with "
            "scripts/gpuenv.py rather than hand-editing"
        )
    if not env.get("gpu"):
        raise SystemExit(f"ENV CAPTURE at {path} names no GPU")
    return {k: v for k, v in env.items() if v is not None}


def apply_server_probe(ctx: RunContext, base_url: str) -> RunContext:
    """Fold the served config into the context, refusing a quant/model mismatch.

    Serving the previous quant after a failed swap is the one error that would
    corrupt every row while looking completely normal, so it fails the run.
    """

    probe = probe_server(base_url)
    if not probe:
        # Fail closed: an unreachable server means the quant guard never ran, so
        # the run could silently record the wrong model for every task.
        raise SystemExit(
            f"SERVER PROBE FAILED at {base_url}: cannot confirm which model is "
            "loaded. Start the server. There is deliberately no way to record "
            "blind: --metrics-url '' used to skip this guard, which is how three "
            "legs ran without it."
        )
    served = probe.get("served_model_path")
    if isinstance(served, str) and ctx.quant and ctx.quant not in served:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"QUANT MISMATCH: --quant {ctx.quant!r} but the server is running "
            f"{served!r}. Swap the model or fix the label; do not record this run."
        )
    return dataclasses.replace(ctx, **probe)


def _require_env(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict[str, object]:
    """--dry-run is the no-GPU harness check, so it is the one local path exempt."""

    if getattr(args, "dry_run", False):
        return {}
    if not getattr(args, "env_json", None):
        raise SystemExit(
            "--env-json is required for a local lane: capture it with "
            "`ssh <box> python3 - < scripts/gpuenv.py > env.json`. Without it the rows "
            "cannot say which card produced them, which is how 192 rows of a "
            "GPU-purchase experiment ended up with a null gpu column."
        )
    return load_env(args.env_json)


def make_context(args: argparse.Namespace) -> RunContext:
    frontier = args.lane in {"frontier", "control"}
    hosted = args.lane not in LOCAL_LANES
    return RunContext(
        out=args.out,
        experiment_id=args.experiment_id,
        lane=args.lane,
        quant=args.quant,
        engine=None if frontier else args.engine,
        model=lane_model(args.lane),
        reasoning_effort=args.reasoning_effort,
        **({} if hosted else _require_env(args)),
    )


def add_context_arguments(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
    parser.add_argument(
        "--lane",
        required=True,
        # Derived, not restated: a lane added to LANE_MODELS but missing here parses as an
        # error at launch, which is how the deepseek lanes stayed unrunnable after being
        # wired and unit-tested.
        choices=tuple(sorted(LANE_MODELS)),
        help="qwen/quant uses profile qwen38; control uses gpt-5.6-luna; frontier uses "
        "gpt-5.6-sol; slash-bearing ids route through OpenRouter",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--quant", required=True, help="quantization label recorded verbatim")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--engine",
        default="llama.cpp",
        help="serving engine recorded verbatim; the default is only correct because "
        "every local lane predating this flag served llama-server -- a vLLM or ninfer "
        "run that omits it writes rows labelled llama.cpp",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--run-index", required=True, type=int, help="paired repetition index")
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path, help="append-only result JSONL path")
    parser.add_argument("--experiment-id", help="optional experiment identifier")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--env-json",
        help="scripts/gpuenv.py capture describing the GPU this leg ran on. Required "
        "for local lanes: capture it per leg, after setting any power cap, since the "
        "cap is mutable and is itself an experiment variable",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--reasoning-effort",
        default="xhigh",
        help="Codex model_reasoning_effort; the Qwen template accepts only "
        "xhigh|medium|low, silently aliases high->xhigh, and raises on minimal",
    )


def _main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Append one durable fixed-shape evaluation result row."
    )
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--task-id", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--run-index", required=True, type=int)
    parser.add_argument("--lane", default="qwen")
    parser.add_argument("--quant", default="smoke")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    emit(
        RunContext(out=args.out, lane=args.lane, quant=args.quant),
        args.task_id,
        args.run_index,
        notes="emit.py smoke test",
    )
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(_main())


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Capture the GPU environment as one JSON object the instruments can record.

Ships over `ssh box python3 - < scripts/gpuenv.py`, so it imports nothing from this
repo and nothing outside the standard library.

Capture once per measured leg, not once per box: the power cap is mutable
(`nvidia-smi -pl`) and is itself an experiment variable, so a per-box capture would
stamp every leg of a wattage sweep with the first cap.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys

QUERY = "name,driver_version,power.limit"


def parse(smi_csv: str, nvcc_text: str | None, *, allow_mixed: bool = False) -> dict[str, object]:
    """One row per GPU from `nvidia-smi --query-gpu=... --format=csv,noheader`.

    Refuses heterogeneous cards by default. Opt-in mixed captures preserve each
    card's identity and cap; the homogeneous scalar cap must not become an average.
    """

    cards = []
    for line in smi_csv.splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        parts = [p.strip() for p in line.split(",")]
        if len(parts) < 3:
            raise ValueError(f"unparsable nvidia-smi row: {line!r}")
        name, driver, power = parts[0], parts[1], parts[2]
        watts = None
        if power not in ("", "[N/A]", "N/A"):
            watts = round(float(power.removesuffix("W").strip()), 1)
        cards.append((name, driver, watts))
    if not cards:
        raise ValueError("nvidia-smi returned no GPUs")

    names = {c[0] for c in cards}
    caps = {c[2] for c in cards}
    mixed = len(names) > 1 or len(caps) > 1
    if mixed and not allow_mixed:
        raise ValueError(
            f"heterogeneous GPUs cannot be described by one row: "
            f"names={sorted(names)} power_limit_w={sorted(map(str, caps))}"
        )

    result = {
        "gpu": " + ".join(c[0] for c in cards) if mixed else cards[0][0],
        "gpu_count": len(cards),
        "gpu_driver": cards[0][1],
        "power_limit_w": None if mixed else cards[0][2],
        # nvidia-smi's "CUDA Version" is the driver's supported API level, not the
        # installed toolkit; only nvcc knows what the engine was compiled against.
        "cuda_toolkit": parse_nvcc(nvcc_text),
    }
    if mixed:
        result["gpu_devices"] = [
            {"gpu": name, "gpu_driver": driver, "power_limit_w": watts}
            for name, driver, watts in cards
        ]
    return result


def parse_nvcc(text: str | None) -> str | None:
    if not text:
        return None
    for token in text.replace(",", " ").split():
        if token.startswith("V") and token[1:2].isdigit():
            return token[1:]
    return None


def _run(cmd: list[str]) -> str | None:
    if not shutil.which(cmd[0]):
        return None
    try:
        return subprocess.run(
            cmd, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30, check=True
        ).stdout
    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError):
        return None


def capture(devices: str | None = None, *, allow_mixed: bool = False) -> dict[str, object]:
    # NVML ignores CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES. Require an explicit NVML selection so a
    # single-card arm cannot silently inherit every installed GPU's identity.
    if devices is None and "CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES" in os.environ:
        raise SystemExit("pass --devices with NVIDIA GPU UUIDs when CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES is set")
    command = ["nvidia-smi", f"--query-gpu={QUERY}", "--format=csv,noheader"]
    if devices is not None:
        if not devices.strip():
            raise SystemExit("--devices must select at least one GPU")
        command.append(f"--id={devices}")
    smi = _run(command)
    if smi is None:
        raise SystemExit("nvidia-smi unavailable: cannot identify the GPU")
    return parse(smi, _run(["nvcc", "--version"]), allow_mixed=allow_mixed)


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--devices", help="nvidia-smi GPU UUIDs or indices, comma-separated")
    parser.add_argument("--allow-mixed", action="store_true",
                        help="record per-device names and caps for heterogeneous arms")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    json.dump(capture(args.devices, allow_mixed=args.allow_mixed), sys.stdout, indent=1, sort_keys=True)
    sys.stdout.write("\n")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

class StopCampaign(RuntimeError):
    pass


class RejectArm(RuntimeError):
    pass


class CampaignBusy(RuntimeError):
    pass


def atomic_json(path, value):
    path = Path(path)
    tmp = path.with_name(path.name + ".tmp")
    with tmp.open("w") as stream:
        json.dump(value, stream, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
        stream.write("\n")
        stream.flush()
        os.fsync(stream.fileno())
    os.replace(tmp, path)
    fd = os.open(path.parent, os.O_RDONLY | os.O_DIRECTORY)
    try:
        os.fsync(fd)
    finally:
        os.close(fd)


def sealed_json(path, value):
    atomic_json(path, value)
    atomic_json(str(path) + ".sha256", digest_file(path))


def read_sealed(path):
    expected = json.loads(Path(str(path) + ".sha256").read_text())
    if digest_file(path) != expected:
        raise StopCampaign(f"corrupt immutable record: {path}")
    return json.loads(Path(path).read_text())


def proc_identity(pid):
    try:
        fields = Path(f"/proc/{pid}/stat").read_text().rsplit(")", 1)[1].split()
        return fields[19]
    except (OSError, IndexError):
        return None


def desktop_inventory():
    return subprocess.check_output(
        ["ps", "-u", str(os.getuid()), "-o", "pid=,args="], text=True, timeout=10).splitlines()


def _normal_process_name(value):
    tokens = value.strip().split()
    if tokens and tokens[0].isdigit():
        tokens = tokens[1:]
    return Path(tokens[0] if tokens else "").name.lower()


def require_free_compute(allowed_processes=()):
    allowed = {_normal_process_name(name) for name in allowed_processes} | DISPLAY_COMPUTE_NAMES
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["nvidia-smi", "--query-compute-apps=gpu_uuid,pid,process_name", "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10, check=True)
    for row in csv.reader(result.stdout.splitlines(), skipinitialspace=True):
        if row and row[0] in DEVICES and _normal_process_name(row[2] if len(row) > 2 else "") not in allowed:
            raise StopCampaign("unowned_gpu_workload: " + ",".join(row))


def telemetry():
    fields = "uuid,temperature.gpu,memory.free,power.limit,power.draw,utilization.gpu,pcie.link.gen.current,pcie.link.width.current"
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(["nvidia-smi", "--query-gpu=" + fields,
                                 "--format=csv,noheader,nounits"],
                                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5, check=True)
        rows = {}
        for row in csv.reader(result.stdout.splitlines(), skipinitialspace=True):
            values = [float(value) for value in row[1:]]
            if len(values) != 7 or not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in values):
                raise ValueError("invalid GPU telemetry")
            rows[row[0]] = dict(zip(fields.split(",")[1:], values))
        memory = re.search(r"^MemAvailable:\s+(\d+) kB$", Path("/proc/meminfo").read_text(), re.M)
        if not memory or any(device not in rows for device in DEVICES):
            raise ValueError("missing device or available RAM")
        return {"time": time.time(), "gpus": rows, "ram_available_mib": int(memory[1]) / 1024}
    except (OSError, ValueError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as error:
        raise StopCampaign(f"telemetry_failed: {error}") from error


def safety_check(sample, reserves=True, vram_reserves=(512, 256)):
    if sample["ram_available_mib"] < 2048:
        raise StopCampaign("system_ram_below_2gib")
    if len(vram_reserves) != 2 or any(type(value) is not int or value < 0 for value in vram_reserves):
        raise StopCampaign("invalid_vram_reserves")
    for device, cap, reserve in zip(DEVICES, [150, 140], vram_reserves):
        gpu = sample["gpus"][device]
        if gpu["temperature.gpu"] >= 80:
            raise StopCampaign("thermal_limit: " + device)
        if abs(gpu["power.limit"] - cap) > 0.1:
            raise StopCampaign("power_limit_changed: " + device)
        if reserves and gpu["memory.free"] < reserve:
            raise RejectArm("vram_reserve: " + device)


def endpoint_free(port):
    with socket.socket() as sock:
        # TIME_WAIT is not a competing listener; match the server's reuse policy.
        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", port))
        sock.listen(1)


def classify_exit(log):
    lower = log.lower()
    # Routine startup warnings must never mask a later fatal device error.
    if any(word in lower for word in ("illegal memory access", "device-side assert", "xid",
                                     "launch timed out", "unspecified launch failure", "fallen off the bus")):
        return "gpu_fault"
    if any(word in lower for word in ("out of memory", "cudamalloc", "failed to allocate")):
        return "oom"
    if "llama_params_fit is not implemented for split_mode_tensor" in lower:
        return "vram_fit_unavailable"
    diagnostic = "\n".join(line for line in lower.splitlines() if not any(warning in line for warning in (
        "unsupported responses tool type", "backend sampling not supported", "swa_full is not supported")))
    if any(word in diagnostic for word in ("not supported", "unsupported", "invalid argument", "unknown argument")):
        return "unsupported"
    return "server_failure"


def queue():
    jobs = [{"id": "commission-depth", "kind": "smoke", "model": "iq3-control", "target": 4096},
            {"id": "commission-restart", "kind": "smoke", "model": "iq3-control", "target": 0},
            {"id": "control-single", "kind": "control", "model": "iq3-control", "single": True},
            {"id": "control-pair", "kind": "control", "model": "iq3-control", "single": False}]
    jobs += [{"id": "screen-" + m["id"], "kind": "screen", "model": m["id"]} for m in MODELS[1:]]
    jobs += [{"id": f"mtp-{m['id']}-{maximum}", "kind": "mtp", "model": m["id"], "maximum": maximum}
             for m in MODELS[1:4] for maximum in [2, 4, 6]]
    jobs += [{"id": f"slots-{rank}-{slots}", "kind": "concurrency", "rank": rank, "slots": slots}
             for rank in [0, 1] for slots in [2, 4]]
    jobs += [{"id": f"repeat-{rank}-{task.replace('/', '-')}-{seed}", "kind": "repeat",
              "rank": rank, "task": task, "seed": seed}
             for seed in [1730, 1731, 1732] for rank in [0, 1]
             for task in [TASKS[0], TASKS[2], TASKS[4], TASKS[6]]]
    return jobs


def validate_profiles(jobs, *, allowed_tasks=None):
    if not isinstance(jobs, list) or not jobs:
        raise ValueError("profile queue must be a nonempty array")
    seen = set()
    for job in jobs:
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+", job.get("id", "")) or job["id"] in seen:
            raise ValueError("profile IDs must be unique safe filenames")
        if job.get("kind") != "profile" or job.get("model") not in {m["id"] for m in MODELS}:
            raise ValueError("profile queue must select a pinned model")
        if job.get("base_job") and job["base_job"] not in seen:
            raise ValueError("base_job must precede its consumer")
        seen.add(job["id"])
        if job.get("task", TASKS[0]) not in (TASKS if allowed_tasks is None else allowed_tasks):
            raise ValueError("profile task must belong to the frozen pilot")
        targets = job.get("targets", [98304])
        if not isinstance(targets, list) or not targets or any(type(n) is not int or n < 0 for n in targets):
            raise ValueError("targets must be nonnegative token counts")
        configs = job.get("configs", [{}])
        if not isinstance(configs, list) or not configs:
            raise ValueError("configs must be a nonempty array")
        for config in configs:
            if not isinstance(config, dict) or set(config) - {"context", "slots", "mode", "split", "ubatch", "maximum", "chat_template", "batch", "minimum", "p_min", "draft_k", "draft_v"}:
                raise ValueError("unknown profile setting")
            if "chat_template" in config and (job["model"] not in TEMPLATE_OVERRIDE_MODELS or
                    config["chat_template"] != chat_templates.BARTOWSKI_PREFIX):
                raise ValueError("chat template override must select the pinned leading-instructions adapter")
            for key in ("context", "slots", "ubatch", "maximum", "batch"):
                if key in config and (type(config[key]) is not int or config[key] < 1):
                    raise ValueError("profile sizes must be positive integers")
            minimum = config.get("minimum", 0)
            if type(minimum) is not int or not 0 <= minimum <= config.get("maximum", 3):
                raise ValueError("MTP minimum must be between zero and maximum")
            floor = config.get("p_min", 0)
            if type(floor) not in (int, float) or not math.isfinite(floor) or not 0 <= floor <= 1:
                raise ValueError("MTP probability floor must be between zero and one")
            if any(config.get(key, "f16") not in ("f16", "q8_0") for key in ("draft_k", "draft_v")):
                raise ValueError("draft cache must be f16 or experimental q8_0")
            if config.get("ubatch", 128) > config.get("batch", 2048):
                raise ValueError("microbatch exceeds logical batch")
            if config.get("slots", 1) > 4 or config.get("context", 102400) > 262144:
                raise ValueError("profile exceeds the declared model/slot bounds")
            if config.get("mode", "tensor") not in ("layer", "tensor"):
                raise ValueError("unsupported profile split mode")
            split = [float(n) for n in config.get("split", "69,31").split(",")]
            if len(split) != 2 or any(not math.isfinite(n) or n <= 0 for n in split):
                raise ValueError("profile split must give two positive finite weights")
    return jobs


def initialize(campaign, model_dir, smoke=False, profiles=None, max_attempts=None, agentic_assets=None,
               continuation=None, vram_reserves=(512, 256), measurement_contract=None):
    allowed_tasks = None
    if agentic_assets is not None:
        asset_tasks = json.loads((agentic_assets / "tasks.json").read_text())
        checks = json.loads((agentic_assets / "selftest.json").read_text())
        allowed_tasks = {task["task_id"] for task in asset_tasks}
        verified = {row["task_id"] for row in checks["tasks"]
                    if row["reference_passes"] and row["stub_fails"] and row["expected_passed_tests"] > 0}
        if (not checks["harness_verified"] or len(allowed_tasks) != len(asset_tasks)
                or allowed_tasks != verified):
            raise ValueError("every asset task requires passing reference/stub validation")
    if profiles is not None:
        validate_profiles(profiles, allowed_tasks=allowed_tasks)
    if max_attempts is not None and (type(max_attempts) is not int or max_attempts not in range(1, 6)):
        raise ValueError("max_attempts must be between 1 and 5")
    if len(vram_reserves) != 2 or any(type(value) is not int or value < 0 for value in vram_reserves):
        raise ValueError("vram_reserves must contain two non-negative integers")
    if campaign.exists():
        manifest = read_sealed(campaign / "manifest.json")
        if profiles is not None and profiles != manifest["queue"]:
            raise ValueError("existing campaign has a different frozen queue")
        if smoke and manifest["queue"] != [{"id": "commission", "kind": "smoke", "model": "iq3-control"}]:
            raise ValueError("existing campaign is not a smoke campaign")
        if max_attempts is not None and max_attempts != manifest.get("max_attempts", 2):
            raise ValueError("existing campaign has a different frozen retry policy")
        if continuation is not None and continuation != manifest.get("continuation"):
            raise ValueError("existing campaign has a different continuation policy")
        if measurement_contract is not None and measurement_contract != manifest.get("measurement_contract"):
            raise ValueError("existing campaign has a different measurement contract")
        if SERVER_EXTRA != manifest.get("server_extra", []):
            raise ValueError("existing campaign has different extra server flags")
        if tuple(vram_reserves) != tuple(manifest.get("safety", {}).get("vram_reserves_mib", [512, 256])):
            raise ValueError("existing campaign has different VRAM reserves")
        for model in manifest["models"]:
            expected = model.get("local") or str(model_dir / model["id"] / model["file"])
            if model["path"] != expected:
                raise ValueError("existing campaign has different model paths")
        return
    campaign.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    (campaign / "attempts").mkdir()
    (campaign / "artifacts").mkdir()
    snapshot = campaign / "snapshot"
    snapshot.mkdir()
    shutil.copytree(ROOT / "scripts", snapshot / "scripts", ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns("__pycache__", "*.pyc"))
    shutil.copytree(LLAMA / "gguf-py" / "gguf", snapshot / "gguf-py" / "gguf",
                    ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns("__pycache__", "*.pyc"))
    (snapshot / "corpora").mkdir()
    for name in ["js-ts.txt", "rust.txt"]:
        shutil.copy2(ROOT / "corpora" / name, snapshot / "corpora" / name)
    tasks = json.loads((ROOT / "subsets/polyglot.json").read_text())
    selected = {t["task_id"]: {"task_id": t["task_id"], "language": t["language"],
                               "prompt": bench_mtp.bench_prompt(t, ROOT / "subsets")}
                for t in tasks if t["task_id"] in TASKS}
    if set(selected) != set(TASKS):
        raise ValueError("frozen pilot task is absent")
    atomic_json(snapshot / "tasks.json", selected)
    if agentic_assets is not None:
        shutil.copytree(agentic_assets, snapshot / "agentic-assets")
    models = json.loads(json.dumps(MODELS))
    for model in models:
        if "bytes" not in model:
            model["bytes"] = Path(model["local"]).stat().st_size
        model["path"] = model.get("local") or str(model_dir / model["id"] / model["file"])
    binary = LLAMA / "build/bin/llama-server"
    libraries = sorted((LLAMA / "build/bin").glob("*.so*"))
    pins = {str(p.resolve()): digest_file(p) for p in [binary, *libraries] if p.is_file()}
    frozen = {str(p.relative_to(snapshot)): digest_file(p)
              for p in snapshot.rglob("*") if p.is_file()}
    manifest = {"schema_version": 1, "created": time.time(), "source_root": str(ROOT),
                "engine_commit": COMMIT, "binary": str(binary), "binary_pins": pins,
                "devices": DEVICES, "port": 8081, "models": models,
                "queue": profiles if profiles is not None else (
                    [{"id": "commission", "kind": "smoke", "model": "iq3-control"}] if smoke else queue()),
                "snapshot_pins": frozen, "ratios": RATIOS,
                "safety": {"max_temp_c": 80, "display_free_mib": vram_reserves[0], "other_free_mib": vram_reserves[1],
                           "vram_reserves_mib": list(vram_reserves),
                           "ram_available_mib": 2048, "poll_seconds": 2},
                "max_attempts": max_attempts if max_attempts is not None else 3,
                "scope": "screening only; no graded quality claims or full-suite authorization"}
    if agentic_assets is not None:
        manifest["scope"] = "isolated verified coding tasks declared in queue; no automatic production promotion"
    if continuation is not None:
        manifest["continuation"] = continuation
    if measurement_contract is not None:
        manifest["measurement_contract"] = measurement_contract
        manifest["desktop_inventory_at_prepare"] = desktop_inventory()
    if SERVER_EXTRA:
        manifest["server_extra"] = list(SERVER_EXTRA)
    sealed_json(campaign / "manifest.json", manifest)
    atomic_json(campaign / "state.json", {"completed": {}, "status": "prepared", "owned": []})


class Runner:
    def __init__(self, campaign, budget_hours, deadline_epoch=None):
        self.path = campaign.resolve()
        self.manifest = read_sealed(self.path / "manifest.json")
        self.state = json.loads((self.path / "state.json").read_text())
        self.deadline = time.monotonic() + budget_hours * 3600
        if deadline_epoch is not None:
            self.deadline = min(self.deadline, time.monotonic() + max(0, deadline_epoch - time.time()))
        self.max_attempts = self.manifest.get("max_attempts", 3)
        self.vram_reserves = tuple(self.manifest.get("safety", {}).get("vram_reserves_mib", [512, 256]))
        if type(self.max_attempts) is not int or self.max_attempts not in range(1, 6):
            raise StopCampaign("invalid frozen retry policy")
        self.snapshot = self.path / "snapshot"
        self.url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.manifest['port']}"
        self.children = []
        self.lockfiles = []
        self.stopping = False
        self.current_dir = None
        self.last_sample = self.last_report = 0
        self.samples = []
        self.verified = {}
        self.env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(self.snapshot), PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1",
                        CUDA_DEVICE_ORDER="PCI_BUS_ID", CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=",".join(DEVICES),
                        PATH="/usr/local/cuda-13.2/bin:" + os.environ.get("PATH", ""))
        # An inherited tuning variable must not silently change the control.
        for key in list(self.env):
            if key.startswith(("NCCL_", "GGML_CUDA_")):
                self.env.pop(key)

    def save(self):
        atomic_json(self.path / "state.json", self.state)

    def event(self, kind, **data):
        row = {"time": time.time(), "kind": kind, **data}
        with (self.path / "events.jsonl").open("a") as stream:
            stream.write(json.dumps(row, allow_nan=False) + "\n")
            stream.flush()
            os.fsync(stream.fileno())
        print(json.dumps(row), flush=True)

    def report(self):
        status = {"status": self.state["status"], "active": self.state.get("active"),
                  "completed_jobs": len(self.state["completed"]), "total_jobs": len(self.manifest["queue"]),
                  "remaining_seconds": max(0, self.deadline - time.monotonic()),
                  "updated": time.time(), "pid": os.getpid(), "reason": self.state.get("reason"),
                  "resource_wait": self.state.get("resource_wait"),
                  "telemetry": self.samples[-1] if self.samples else None}
        atomic_json(self.path / "status.json", status)
        lines = ["# Autonomous screening status", "", f"Status: {status['status']}",
                 f"Completed: {status['completed_jobs']}/{status['total_jobs']}",
                 f"Active: {status['active']}", f"Reason: {status['reason']}", "",
                 "These are throughput/allocation probes, not graded coding results.", "",
                 "| Job | Outcome | PP tok/s | TG tok/s | MTP acceptance |",
                 "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|"]
        for job, value in self.state["completed"].items():
            result = read_sealed(self.path / value)
            acceptance = result.get("probe", {}).get("mtp", {}).get("draft_accept_rate")
            rate = "" if acceptance is None else f"{acceptance:.1%}"
            lines.append(f"| {job} | {result['status']} | {result.get('prefill_tps', '')} | {result.get('decode_tps', '')} | {rate} |")
        temp = self.path / "REPORT.md.tmp"
        temp.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
        os.replace(temp, self.path / "REPORT.md")
        self.last_report = time.monotonic()

    def poll(self, reserves=True):
        if self.stopping or (self.path / "STOP").exists():
            raise StopCampaign("requested_stop")
        if time.monotonic() >= self.deadline:
            raise StopCampaign("budget_expired")
        if time.monotonic() - self.last_sample >= 2:
            sample = telemetry()
            self.samples.append(sample)
            dest = self.current_dir or self.path
            with (dest / "telemetry.jsonl").open("a") as stream:
                stream.write(json.dumps(sample) + "\n")
            self.last_sample = time.monotonic()
            safety_check(sample, reserves, self.vram_reserves)
        if time.monotonic() - self.last_report >= 1800:
            self.report()

    def sleep(self, seconds, reserves=True):
        until = time.monotonic() + seconds
        while time.monotonic() < until:
            self.poll(reserves)
            time.sleep(min(0.5, max(0, until - time.monotonic())))

    def spawn(self, command, log, env=None):
        parent = os.getpid()

        def child_setup():
            os.setsid()
            # Parent death must not leave an unguarded inference process running.
            if ctypes.CDLL(None).prctl(1, signal.SIGKILL) != 0 or os.getppid() != parent:
                os._exit(126)

        with Path(log).open("ab") as stream:
            child = subprocess.Popen(command, cwd=self.snapshot, env=env or self.env,
                                     stdout=stream, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                                     stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, preexec_fn=child_setup)
        identity = {"pid": child.pid, "start": proc_identity(child.pid), "command": command}
        self.children.append(child)
        self.state["owned"].append(identity)
        self.save()
        self.event("spawn", **identity)
        return child

    def stop_child(self, child):
        if child.poll() is None:
            os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
            try:
                child.wait(timeout=10)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                os.killpg(child.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
                child.wait(timeout=5)
        self.children = [p for p in self.children if p is not child]
        self.state["owned"] = [p for p in self.state["owned"] if p["pid"] != child.pid]
        self.save()

    def wait_child(self, child, timeout, reserves=True):
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
        try:
            while child.poll() is None:
                self.poll(reserves)
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    raise RejectArm("subprocess_timeout")
                time.sleep(0.5)
            self.poll(reserves)
            return child.returncode
        finally:
            self.stop_child(child)

    def worker(self, arguments, log, timeout=900):
        child = self.spawn([sys.executable, "-m", "scripts.instruments.hillclimb_probe", *arguments], log)
        return self.wait_child(child, timeout)

    def run_probe(self, config, target, task, seed, out):
        return self.worker(["probe", "--url", self.url, "--tasks", str(self.snapshot / "tasks.json"),
                            "--task", task, "--target", str(target), "--max-tokens", "32" if target == 0 else "2048",
                            "--slots", str(config["slots"]), "--seed", str(seed), "--out", str(out)],
                           out / "probe.log", timeout=900)

    def acquire(self):
        runtime = Path(os.environ.get("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR", f"/run/user/{os.getuid()}"))
        for lock in [runtime / "qwen-hillclimb.lock", runtime / "qwen-local-server.lock"]:
            handle = lock.open("a")
            try:
                fcntl.flock(handle, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            except BlockingIOError as error:
                handle.close()
                raise CampaignBusy("another benchmark or local server owns the GPU lock") from error
            self.lockfiles.append(handle)
        for item in self.state.get("owned", []):
            if proc_identity(item["pid"]) == item["start"] and item["start"] is not None:
                raise StopCampaign("owned_process_still_alive: inspect before resume")
        self.state["owned"] = []
        for proc in Path("/proc").iterdir():
            if proc.name.isdigit():
                try:
                    if (proc / "comm").read_text().strip() == "llama-server":
                        raise StopCampaign("existing_llama_server")
                except (FileNotFoundError, PermissionError, ProcessLookupError):
                    pass
        endpoint_free(self.manifest["port"])
        if self.manifest.get("measurement_contract"):
            require_free_compute(self.manifest.get("desktop_inventory_at_prepare", []))
            self.event("desktop_inventory_at_resume", processes=desktop_inventory())
        for relative, expected in self.manifest["snapshot_pins"].items():
            if digest_file(self.snapshot / relative) != expected:
                raise StopCampaign("snapshot_changed: " + relative)
        for path, expected in self.manifest["binary_pins"].items():
            if digest_file(path) != expected:
                raise StopCampaign("engine_changed: " + path)
        commit = subprocess.check_output(["git", "-C", str(LLAMA), "rev-parse", "HEAD"], text=True).strip()
        if commit != COMMIT:
            raise StopCampaign("engine_source_changed")
        for value in self.state["completed"].values():
            read_sealed(self.path / value)
        self.poll()
        self.state["status"] = "running"
        self.state["reason"] = None
        self.state["coordinator"] = {"pid": os.getpid(), "start": proc_identity(os.getpid()),
                                     "pid_namespace": os.readlink("/proc/self/ns/pid")}
        self.save()
        self.report()

    def ensure_model(self, model_id):
        if model_id in self.verified:
            return self.verified[model_id]
        model = next(m for m in self.manifest["models"] if m["id"] == model_id)
        path = Path(model["path"])
        if not path.exists():
            path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            partial = path.with_name(path.name + ".partial")
            required = model["bytes"] - (partial.stat().st_size if partial.exists() else 0)
            if shutil.disk_usage(path.parent).free < required + 10 * 1024**3:
                raise RejectArm("insufficient_disk_space")
            url = f"https://huggingface.co/{model['repo']}/resolve/{model['revision']}/{model['file']}"
            for attempt in range(self.max_attempts):
                self.event("download", model=model_id, attempt=attempt + 1)
                command = ["curl", "--fail", "--location", "--continue-at", "-",
                           "--connect-timeout", "15", "--speed-limit", "1024", "--speed-time", "60",
                           "--output", str(partial), url]
                child = self.spawn(command, self.path / "artifacts" / f"{model_id}.download.log")
                code = self.wait_child(child, min(3600, self.deadline - time.monotonic()))
                if code == 0:
                    break
                if attempt + 1 < self.max_attempts:
                    self.sleep(RETRY_BACKOFF_SECONDS[attempt])
            else:
                raise RejectArm("download_failed")
            inspect_path = partial
        else:
            inspect_path = path
        metadata = self.path / "artifacts" / f"{model_id}.json"
        self.event("verify_model", model=model_id)
        code = self.worker(["inspect", "--path", str(inspect_path), "--sha256", model["sha256"],
                            "--bytes", str(model["bytes"]), "--gguf-path", str(self.snapshot / "gguf-py"),
                            "--out", str(metadata)], metadata.with_suffix(".log"), timeout=600)
        if code != 0:
            raise RejectArm("artifact_verification_failed")
        info = json.loads(metadata.read_text())
        if info["native_context"] < 102400 or info["block_count"] != 65 or info["architecture"] != "qwen35":
            raise RejectArm("unexpected_model_architecture")
        if inspect_path != path:
            os.replace(inspect_path, path)
        self.verified[model_id] = model
        return model

    def template_override(self, model, config, out):
        if "chat_template" not in config:
            return [], None
        if model["id"] not in TEMPLATE_OVERRIDE_MODELS or config["chat_template"] != chat_templates.BARTOWSKI_PREFIX:
            raise RejectArm("unsupported_chat_template_override")
        metadata = json.loads((self.path / "artifacts" / f"{model['id']}.json").read_text())
        effective = chat_templates.bartowski_template(metadata["chat_template"])
        path = out / "chat-template.jinja"
        path.write_text(effective)
        return ["--jinja", "--chat-template-file", str(path)], {
            "adapter": config["chat_template"],
            "source_sha256": chat_templates.BARTOWSKI_TEMPLATE_SHA256,
            "effective_sha256": digest_file(path)}

    def retryable_failure(self, reason):
        return reason in TRANSIENT_FAILURES

    def retryable_gpu_fault(self, log):
        return False

    def retry_limit(self, reason):
        return self.max_attempts

    def retry_count(self, failures, started_attempts):
        return len(failures)

    def retry_backoff(self, failures, count):
        return RETRY_BACKOFF_SECONDS[len(failures) - 1]

    def wait_before_retry(self, job_id, result, seconds):
        self.sleep(seconds)

    def attempt(self, job_id, model, config, target, task=TASKS[0], seed=1729):
        spec = {"job": job_id, "model": model["id"], "config": config,
                "target": target, "task": task, "seed": seed}
        key = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(spec, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
        # Resume preserves successes and deterministic rejections; partial attempts never pass.
        transient_failures = []
        for existing in sorted((self.path / "attempts").glob(key + "-*/result.json")):
            cached = read_sealed(existing)
            if cached["status"] == "passed":
                return cached
            if cached.get("reason") == "gpu_fault":
                raise StopCampaign("gpu_fault")
            if cached["status"] == "failed" and not self.retryable_failure(cached.get("reason")):
                return cached
            if cached["status"] == "failed":
                transient_failures.append(cached)
        started_attempts = len(list((self.path / "attempts").glob(key + "-*")))
        count = self.retry_count(transient_failures, started_attempts)
        if transient_failures and count >= self.retry_limit(transient_failures[-1].get("reason")):
            return transient_failures[-1]
        if transient_failures:
