            delay = self.retry_backoff(transient_failures, count)
            retry_at = transient_failures[-1].get("finished_at", time.time()) + delay
            remaining = max(0, retry_at - time.time())
            self.event("transient_retry", job=job_id, attempt=count + 1,
                       max_attempts=self.retry_limit(transient_failures[-1].get("reason")), backoff_seconds=remaining,
                       reason=transient_failures[-1]["reason"])
            self.wait_before_retry(job_id, transient_failures[-1], remaining)
        number = started_attempts + 1
        out = self.path / "attempts" / f"{key}-{number:02d}"
        out.mkdir()
        self.current_dir = out
        self.samples = []
        self.last_sample = 0
        sealed_json(out / "spec.json", spec)
        result = {**spec, "status": "failed", "attempt": str(out.relative_to(self.path))}
        server = None
        began = time.monotonic()
        try:
            endpoint_free(self.manifest["port"])
            if self.manifest.get("measurement_contract"):
                require_free_compute(self.manifest.get("desktop_inventory_at_prepare", []))
                sealed_json(out / "desktop.json", desktop_inventory())
            self.poll()
            env = dict(self.env, CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=",".join(config["devices"]))
            command = [self.manifest["binary"], "-m", model["path"], "--alias", "local-benchmark",
                       "-c", str(config["context"] * config["slots"]), "-np", str(config["slots"]),
                       "-ctk", "q8_0", "-ctv", "q8_0", "-ngl", "99", "-fa", "on",
                       "--spec-type", "draft-mtp", "--spec-draft-n-max", str(config["maximum"]),
                       "--spec-draft-n-min", str(config.get("minimum", 0)), "--spec-draft-p-min", str(config.get("p_min", 0)),
                       "--spec-draft-type-k", config.get("draft_k", "f16"), "--spec-draft-type-v", config.get("draft_v", "f16"),
                       "-sm", config["mode"], "--tensor-split", config["split"],
                       "-b", str(config.get("batch", 2048)), "-ub", str(config["ubatch"]),
                       "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", str(self.manifest["port"]), "--metrics"]
            if config.get("workload") == "agentic":
                command += ["--temp", "1", "--top-k", "20", "--top-p", "0.95", "--min-p", "0.05"]
            template_args, template_info = self.template_override(model, config, out)
            command += template_args + self.manifest.get("server_extra", [])
            sealed_json(out / "launch.json", {"command": command, "CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES": env["CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"],
                                               "target_kv": "q8_0/q8_0", "draft_kv": config.get("draft_k", "f16") + "/" + config.get("draft_v", "f16"),
                                               "model_sha256": model["sha256"], "chat_template_override": template_info})
            self.event("attempt_start", **spec, directory=str(out))
            server = self.spawn(command, out / "server.log", env)
            until = time.monotonic() + 300
            while True:
                self.poll()
                if server.poll() is not None:
                    raise RejectArm(classify_exit((out / "server.log").read_text(errors="replace")))
                try:
                    if api(self.url, "/health", timeout=1).get("status") == "ok":
                        break
                except (OSError, ValueError):
                    pass
                if time.monotonic() >= until:
                    raise RejectArm("startup_timeout")
                self.sleep(1)
            props = api(self.url, "/props", timeout=3)
            if (props["total_slots"] != config["slots"] or
                    props["default_generation_settings"]["n_ctx"] != config["context"] or
                    Path(props["model_path"]) != Path(model["path"])):
                raise RejectArm("server_configuration_mismatch")
            atomic_json(out / "props.json", props)
            self.sleep(2)
            if self.manifest.get("measurement_contract"):
                atomic_json(out / "memory-loaded.json", telemetry())
            code = self.run_probe(config, target, task, seed, out)
            if code != 0:
                if server.poll() is not None:
                    raise RejectArm(classify_exit((out / "server.log").read_text(errors="replace")))
                if (out / "probe.json").exists():
                    failed_probe = json.loads((out / "probe.json").read_text())
                    if failed_probe.get("failure_class") == "transport_failure":
                        raise RejectArm("transport_failure")
                raise RejectArm("probe_failed")
            probe = json.loads((out / "probe.json").read_text())
            if self.manifest.get("measurement_contract"):
                atomic_json(out / "memory-populated.json", telemetry())
            if not all(probe[k] for k in ["complete", "mtp_live", "depth_verified", "overlap_verified"]):
                raise RejectArm("probe_incomplete")
            result.update(status="passed", probe=probe, decode_tps=probe["metrics_delta"].get("decode_tps"),
                          prefill_tps=probe["metrics_delta"].get("prefill_tps"))
        except RejectArm as error:
            result["reason"] = str(error)
            log = out / "server.log"
            diagnostic = log.read_text(errors="replace") if log.exists() else ""
            if str(error) == "gpu_fault" or classify_exit(diagnostic) == "gpu_fault":
                result["reason"] = "gpu_fault"
                if self.retryable_gpu_fault(diagnostic):
                    result["reason"] = "cuda_watchdog"
                else:
                    raise StopCampaign("gpu_fault") from error
        except StopCampaign as error:
            result.update(status="interrupted", reason=str(error))
            raise
        except Exception as error:
            result.update(status="error", reason=f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}")
            raise StopCampaign("instrument_fault: " + result["reason"]) from error
        finally:
            for child in list(reversed(self.children)):
                self.stop_child(child)
            result["finished_at"] = time.time()
            result["elapsed_seconds"] = time.monotonic() - began
            if self.samples:
                result["minimum_free_mib"] = {gpu: min(s["gpus"][gpu]["memory.free"] for s in self.samples) for gpu in DEVICES}
                result["peak_temperature_c"] = {gpu: max(s["gpus"][gpu]["temperature.gpu"] for s in self.samples) for gpu in DEVICES}
                result["sampled_board_energy_wh"] = sum(
                    (b["time"] - a["time"]) * sum((a["gpus"][gpu]["power.draw"] + b["gpus"][gpu]["power.draw"]) / 2
                                                    for gpu in DEVICES) / 3600
                    for a, b in zip(self.samples, self.samples[1:]))
                result["energy_scope"] = "whole_attempt_including_load_and_probe; trapezoidal_board_power"
            sealed_json(out / "result.json", result)
            self.event("attempt_end", job=job_id, status=result["status"], reason=result.get("reason"))
            self.current_dir = None
        self.sleep(4, reserves=False)
        # Memory recovery is checked before another model is admitted.
        self.last_sample = 0
        try:
            self.poll()
        except RejectArm as error:
            raise StopCampaign("memory_not_recovered") from error
        if self.retryable_failure(result.get("reason")):
            return self.attempt(job_id, model, config, target, task, seed)
        return result

    def best(self):
        choices = []
        for job_id, path in self.state["completed"].items():
            if job_id.startswith(("screen-", "mtp-")):
                result = read_sealed(self.path / path)
                if result["status"] == "passed" and result.get("decode_tps"):
                    choices.append(result)
        choices.sort(key=lambda r: (-r["decode_tps"], -min(r["minimum_free_mib"].values()),
                                    next(i for i, m in enumerate(MODELS) if m["id"] == r["model"])))
        unique = []
        for choice in choices:
            if not any(item["model"] == choice["model"] for item in unique):
                unique.append(choice)
        return unique[:2]

    def execute_job(self, job):
        config = {"devices": DEVICES, "context": 102400, "slots": 1, "mode": "tensor",
                  "split": "69,31", "ubatch": 128, "maximum": 3}
        if job["kind"] == "profile":
            if job.get("base_job"):
                path = self.state["completed"].get(job["base_job"])
                base = read_sealed(self.path / path) if path else {}
                if base.get("status") != "passed":
                    return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "no_safe_base_profile", "model": job["model"]}
                config.update(base["config"])
            model = self.ensure_model(job["model"])
            last = None
            for changes in job.get("configs", [{}]):
                candidate = {**config, **changes}
                targets = job.get("targets", [98304])
                if any(target and target + 2048 > candidate["context"] for target in targets):
                    raise RejectArm("profile_output_exceeds_context")
                last = self.attempt(job["id"] + "-fit", model, candidate, 0)
                if last["status"] != "passed":
                    continue
                rows = []
                for target in targets:
                    last = self.attempt(job["id"], model, candidate, target,
                                        task=job.get("task", TASKS[0]), seed=job.get("seed", 1729))
                    rows.append(last)
                    if last["status"] != "passed":
                        break
                if all(row["status"] == "passed" for row in rows):
                    return {**last, "probes": rows}
            return last
        if job["kind"] in ("concurrency", "repeat"):
            best = self.best()
            if len(best) <= job["rank"]:
                return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "no_safe_candidate"}
            base = best[job["rank"]]
            model_id = base["model"]
            config.update(base["config"])
            config["slots"] = job.get("slots", 1)
        else:
            model_id = job["model"]
        model = self.ensure_model(model_id)
        if job["kind"] == "smoke":
            config.update(context=8192, devices=[GPU0], mode="layer", split="1")
            return self.attempt(job["id"], model, config, job.get("target", 4096))
        if job["kind"] == "control":
            config.update(context=65536, ubatch=512, split="1" if job["single"] else "70,30",
                          devices=[GPU0] if job["single"] else DEVICES,
                          mode="layer" if job["single"] else "tensor")
            rows = [self.attempt(job["id"], model, config, target) for target in [32768, 49152]]
            if not all(row["status"] == "passed" for row in rows):
                return {"status": "failed", "reason": "control_failed", "attempts": rows}
            return {"status": "passed", "attempts": rows,
                    "decode_tps": statistics.median(row["decode_tps"] for row in rows)}
        if job["kind"] == "screen":
            for ratio in self.manifest["ratios"]:
                config["split"] = f"{ratio},{100 - ratio}"
                fit = self.attempt(job["id"] + "-fit", model, dict(config), 0)
                if fit["status"] != "passed":
                    if fit.get("reason") not in ("oom", "unsupported", "vram_reserve: " + GPU0, "vram_reserve: " + GPU1):
                        return fit
                    continue
                depth = self.attempt(job["id"], model, dict(config), 98304)
                if depth["status"] == "passed":
                    return depth
                if depth.get("reason") not in ("oom", "vram_reserve: " + GPU0, "vram_reserve: " + GPU1):
                    return depth
            return {"status": "failed", "reason": "no_safe_ratio", "model": model_id}
        if job["kind"] == "mtp":
            baseline_path = self.state["completed"].get("screen-" + model_id)
            if not baseline_path:
                return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "screen_unavailable"}
            baseline = read_sealed(self.path / baseline_path)
            if baseline["status"] != "passed":
                return {"status": "skipped", "reason": "no_safe_screen"}
            config.update(baseline["config"], maximum=job["maximum"])
        if job["kind"] == "concurrency":
            fit = self.attempt(job["id"] + "-fit", model, config, 0)
            if fit["status"] != "passed":
                return fit
        return self.attempt(job["id"], model, config, 98304,
                            task=job.get("task", TASKS[0]), seed=job.get("seed", 1729))

    def run(self):
        busy = False
        for sig in [signal.SIGTERM, signal.SIGINT, signal.SIGHUP]:
            signal.signal(sig, lambda *_: setattr(self, "stopping", True))
        try:
            self.acquire()
            for job in self.manifest["queue"]:
                if job["id"] in self.state["completed"]:
                    continue
                self.poll()
                dest = self.path / f"job-{job['id']}.json"
                # A crash between sealing the result and saving state must not repeat inference.
                if dest.exists():
                    recovered = read_sealed(dest)
                    if recovered.get("status") not in ("passed", "failed", "skipped"):
                        raise StopCampaign("invalid terminal job record: " + job["id"])
                    self.state["completed"][job["id"]] = dest.name
                    self.save()
                    self.event("job_recovered", job=job["id"], status=recovered["status"])
                    if job["kind"] == "smoke" and recovered["status"] != "passed":
                        raise StopCampaign("commissioning_failed")
                    continue
                self.state["active"] = job
                self.save()
                self.report()
                try:
                    result = self.execute_job(job)
                except RejectArm as error:
                    result = {"status": "failed", "reason": str(error)}
                sealed_json(dest, result)
                self.state["completed"][job["id"]] = str(dest.relative_to(self.path))
                self.save()
                self.event("job_end", job=job["id"], status=result["status"])
                if job["kind"] == "smoke" and result["status"] != "passed":
                    raise StopCampaign("commissioning_failed")
            self.state["status"] = "complete"
            self.state["active"] = None
        except CampaignBusy as error:
            busy = True
            print(str(error), flush=True)
        except StopCampaign as error:
            self.state.update(status="stopped", reason=str(error))
            self.event("campaign_stop", reason=str(error))
        except BaseException as error:
            self.state.update(status="error", reason=f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}")
            self.event("campaign_error", reason=self.state["reason"])
            raise
        finally:
            for child in list(reversed(self.children)):
                self.stop_child(child)
            if not busy:
                self.save()
                self.report()
            for handle in self.lockfiles:
                handle.close()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("command", choices=["run", "resume", "status", "stop", "prepare"])
    parser.add_argument("--campaign", required=True, type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--budget-hours", default=4, type=float)
    parser.add_argument("--model-dir", type=Path, default=ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models")
    parser.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="one small real-GPU commissioning probe only")
    parser.add_argument("--queue-json", type=Path, help="explicit pinned-model follow-up profiles; new campaigns only")
    parser.add_argument("--swift-tuning", action="store_true", help="prepare the pinned Swift IQ4 throughput contract")
    parser.add_argument("--max-attempts", type=int, choices=range(1, 6),
                        help="total transient attempts per configuration, frozen at creation (default: 3)")
    parser.add_argument("--deadline-epoch", type=float,
                        help="absolute wall-clock deadline, preserved across supervised restarts")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if not math.isfinite(args.budget_hours) or args.budget_hours <= 0:
        parser.error("budget must be positive and finite")
    if args.deadline_epoch is not None and (not math.isfinite(args.deadline_epoch) or args.deadline_epoch <= 0):
        parser.error("deadline must be a positive finite epoch")
    if args.max_attempts is not None and args.command not in ("run", "prepare"):
        parser.error("--max-attempts is set only when creating a campaign")
    campaign = args.campaign.resolve()
    if args.command in ("run", "prepare"):
        if args.smoke and args.queue_json:
            parser.error("--smoke and --queue-json are mutually exclusive")
        profiles = json.loads(args.queue_json.read_text()) if args.queue_json else None
        contract = None
        if args.swift_tuning:
            if not profiles or args.smoke:
                parser.error("--swift-tuning requires an explicit --queue-json")
            from scripts.instruments.swift_longtail import MODEL, TUNING_TASKS, tuning_contract
            MODELS[:] = [MODEL]
            TASKS[:] = TUNING_TASKS
            contract = tuning_contract()
        initialize(campaign, args.model_dir.resolve(), smoke=args.smoke, profiles=profiles,
                   max_attempts=args.max_attempts, measurement_contract=contract)
        if args.command == "prepare":
            print(campaign)
            return
    elif args.command == "status":
        state = json.loads((campaign / "state.json").read_text())
        owner = state.get("coordinator", {})
        same_namespace = owner.get("pid_namespace") == os.readlink("/proc/self/ns/pid")
        if state["status"] == "running" and same_namespace and proc_identity(owner.get("pid", -1)) != owner.get("start"):
            print("Coordinator is no longer alive; the report below is its last saved state.")
        print((campaign / "REPORT.md").read_text() if (campaign / "REPORT.md").exists()
              else (campaign / "state.json").read_text())
        return
    elif args.command == "stop":
        (campaign / "STOP").touch()
        return
    if (campaign / "STOP").exists():
        parser.error("STOP marker exists; explicitly archive it before resuming")
    snapshot = campaign / "snapshot"
    if ROOT.resolve() != snapshot.resolve():
        env = dict(os.environ, PYTHONPATH=str(snapshot), PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE="1")
        os.chdir(snapshot)
        command = [sys.executable, "-m", "scripts.instruments.hillclimb", "resume",
                   "--campaign", str(campaign), "--budget-hours", str(args.budget_hours)]
        if args.deadline_epoch is not None:
            command += ["--deadline-epoch", str(args.deadline_epoch)]
        os.execve(sys.executable, command, env)
    Runner(campaign, args.budget_hours, args.deadline_epoch).run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


"""One disposable probe process; the coordinator owns its wall clock and safety guard."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import threading
import time
import urllib.request
import urllib.error
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

from scripts.instruments import bench_concurrency, bench_mtp, emit, longctx


def digest_file(path):
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(8 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def inspect_model(path, expected_sha, expected_bytes, gguf_path):
    if Path(path).stat().st_size != expected_bytes:
        raise ValueError("artifact size differs from pinned size")
    actual = digest_file(path)
    if actual != expected_sha:
        raise ValueError("artifact SHA256 differs from pin")
    sys.path.insert(0, str(gguf_path))
    from gguf import GGUFReader
    reader = GGUFReader(path)
    architecture = reader.fields["general.architecture"].contents()
    blocks = reader.fields[f"{architecture}.block_count"].contents()
    mtp = [{"name": t.name, "type": t.tensor_type.name, "bytes": t.n_bytes}
           for t in reader.tensors if t.name.startswith(f"blk.{blocks - 1}.")]
    if not any("nextn.eh_proj" in t["name"] for t in mtp):
        raise ValueError("embedded MTP projection is missing")
    fields = {}
    for name, field in reader.fields.items():
        if name.startswith("tokenizer."):
            digest = hashlib.sha256()
            for part in field.parts:
                digest.update(part.tobytes())
            fields[name] = digest.hexdigest()
    if "tokenizer.chat_template" not in fields:
        raise ValueError("chat template is missing")
    return {"sha256": actual, "bytes": expected_bytes, "architecture": architecture,
            "block_count": blocks, "native_context": reader.fields[
                f"{architecture}.context_length"].contents(),
            "tokenizer_field_hashes": fields, "mtp_tensors": mtp,
            "chat_template": reader.fields["tokenizer.chat_template"].contents()}


def api(url, endpoint, payload=None, timeout=15):
    request = urllib.request.Request(
        url.rstrip("/") + endpoint,
        None if payload is None else json.dumps(payload).encode(),
        {"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
        return json.load(response)


def prepared_prompt(url, task, target):
    base = task["prompt"]
    if target <= 0:
        return base, None
    padding, _ = longctx.build_padding(task, target, url)
    # Count the template, not just padding; all candidates receive the same budget.
    best = None
    low, high = 0, len(padding)
    for _ in range(20):
        length = (low + high) // 2
        prompt = longctx.wrap_prompt(base, padding[:length])
        formatted = api(url, "/apply-template", {
            "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
            "chat_template_kwargs": {"reasoning_effort": "xhigh"}}, timeout=120)["prompt"]
        count = longctx._tokenize(formatted, url)
        if count <= target:
            best = (prompt, count)
            low = length + 1
        else:
            high = length - 1
        if best and target - best[1] <= 32:
            break
    if best is None or best[1] < target - 512:
        raise ValueError(f"could not populate requested context: {target}, got {best and best[1]}")
    return best


def one_request(url, prompt, seed, max_tokens, index, barrier, out, cache_prompt=False):
    payload = {
        "model": "local-benchmark", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}],
        "temperature": 1.0, "top_k": 20, "top_p": 0.95, "min_p": 0.05,
        "seed": seed, "max_tokens": max_tokens, "cache_prompt": cache_prompt,
        "chat_template_kwargs": {"reasoning_effort": "xhigh"},
        "stream": True, "stream_options": {"include_usage": True},
    }
    barrier.wait()
    started = time.monotonic()
    row = {"request_index": index, "seed": seed, "started_monotonic": started,
           "prompt_sha256": hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode()).hexdigest(), "error": None}
    request = urllib.request.Request(url + "/v1/chat/completions",
                                     json.dumps(payload).encode(),
                                     {"Content-Type": "application/json"})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=900) as response:
            row["http_status"] = response.status
            row["response_headers"] = dict(response.headers)
            with (out / f"request-{index}.sse").open("wb") as raw_stream:
                def captured_lines():
                    for line in response:
                        raw_stream.write(line)
                        raw_stream.flush()
                        yield line
                row.update(bench_concurrency.read_stream(captured_lines(), started))
    except Exception as error:
        row["error"] = f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"
        if isinstance(error, (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError)) or (
                isinstance(error, ValueError) and "missing completion usage" in str(error)):
            row["failure_class"] = "transport_failure"
    row["finished_monotonic"] = time.monotonic()
    row["wall_seconds"] = row["finished_monotonic"] - started
    usage = row.get("usage", {})
    row["actual_context_tokens"] = (usage.get("prompt_tokens", 0) + usage.get("completion_tokens", 0)) if usage else None
    row["early_eos"] = ("stop" in row.get("finish_reasons", []) and
                        usage.get("completion_tokens", max_tokens) < max_tokens)
    (out / f"request-{index}.json").write_text(json.dumps(row, indent=2) + "\n")
    return row


def run_probe(url, task, target, max_tokens, slots, seed, out, cache_prompt=False):
    props = api(url, "/props")
    context = props["default_generation_settings"]["n_ctx"]
    if props["total_slots"] != slots or target + max_tokens > context:
        raise ValueError("probe exceeds effective per-slot context or slot count differs")
    (out / "props.json").write_text(json.dumps(props, indent=2) + "\n")
    prompt, count = prepared_prompt(url, task, target)
    (out / "prompt.txt").write_text(prompt)
    samples, stopped = [], threading.Event()

    def sample():
        while not stopped.is_set():
            try:
                samples.append({"monotonic": time.monotonic(), "slots": api(url, "/slots", timeout=2)})
            except Exception as error:
                samples.append({"error": str(error)})
            stopped.wait(1)

    sampler = threading.Thread(target=sample, daemon=True)
    before = emit.scrape_metrics(url)
    (out / "metrics-before.json").write_text(json.dumps(before) + "\n")
    sampler.start()
    barrier = threading.Barrier(slots)
    try:
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=slots) as pool:
            futures = [pool.submit(one_request, url, prompt, seed + i, max_tokens,
                                   i, barrier, out, cache_prompt) for i in range(slots)]
            rows = [future.result() for future in futures]
    finally:
        stopped.set()
        sampler.join(timeout=4)
    after = emit.scrape_metrics(url)
    (out / "metrics-after.json").write_text(json.dumps(after) + "\n")
    delta = emit.metrics_delta(before, after)
    summary = {
        "task_id": task["task_id"], "templated_prompt_tokens": count,
        "requested_prompt_tokens": target, "max_output_tokens": max_tokens,
        "sampling": {"temperature": 1.0, "top_k": 20, "top_p": 0.95, "min_p": 0.05},
        "cache_prompt": cache_prompt,
        "metrics_scope": "batch", "metrics_delta": delta,
        "mtp": bench_mtp.acceptance(delta),
        "mtp_live": delta.get("draft_tokens", 0) > 0 and delta.get("draft_accepted_tokens", 0) > 0,
        "requests": [{k: v for k, v in row.items() if k != "output_deltas"} for row in rows],
        "slot_samples": samples, **bench_concurrency.summarize(rows, samples),
    }
    # Only actual usage establishes depth; cached/global high-water counters cannot.
    summary["depth_verified"] = all(
        row.get("usage", {}).get("prompt_tokens", 0) >= target - 512
        and row.get("actual_context_tokens") is not None
        and row["actual_context_tokens"] <= context for row in rows)
    summary["overlap_verified"] = target == 0 or slots == 1 or summary["max_observed_busy_slots"] >= slots
    failed = [row for row in rows if row["error"]]
    if failed and all(row.get("failure_class") == "transport_failure" for row in failed):
        summary["failure_class"] = "transport_failure"
    (out / "probe.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2) + "\n")
    return summary


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    inspect = sub.add_parser("inspect")
    inspect.add_argument("--path", required=True)
    inspect.add_argument("--sha256", required=True)
    inspect.add_argument("--bytes", required=True, type=int)
    inspect.add_argument("--gguf-path", required=True)
    inspect.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path)
    probe = sub.add_parser("probe")
    probe.add_argument("--url", required=True)
    probe.add_argument("--tasks", required=True, type=Path)
    probe.add_argument("--task", required=True)
    probe.add_argument("--target", type=int, required=True)
    probe.add_argument("--max-tokens", type=int, required=True)
    probe.add_argument("--slots", type=int, default=1)
    probe.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=1729)
    probe.add_argument("--out", required=True, type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.command == "inspect":
        result = inspect_model(args.path, args.sha256, args.bytes, args.gguf_path)
        args.out.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n")
        return 0
    task = json.loads(args.tasks.read_text())[args.task]
    result = run_probe(args.url, task, args.target, args.max_tokens, args.slots, args.seed, args.out)
    return 0 if (result["complete"] and result["mtp_live"] and result["depth_verified"]
                 and result["overlap_verified"]) else 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


"""Read-isolated task execution; the only network route is a local inference bridge."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import contextlib
from pathlib import Path
import select
import socket
import socketserver
import subprocess
import threading


def relay(left, right):
    readers = [left, right]
    try:
        while readers:
            ready, _, _ = select.select(readers, [], [], 30)
            for source in ready:
                target = right if source is left else left
                data = source.recv(65536)
                if data:
                    target.sendall(data)
                else:
                    readers.remove(source)
                    target.shutdown(socket.SHUT_WR)
    except OSError:
        pass


class ThreadedUnixServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.UnixStreamServer):
    daemon_threads = True


class ThreadedTCPServer(socketserver.ThreadingMixIn, socketserver.TCPServer):
    daemon_threads = True
    allow_reuse_address = True


@contextlib.contextmanager
def inference_bridge(path, port):
    class Handler(socketserver.BaseRequestHandler):
        def handle(self):
            with socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port), timeout=15) as upstream:
                upstream.settimeout(None)
                relay(self.request, upstream)

    server = ThreadedUnixServer(str(path), Handler)
    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, kwargs={"poll_interval": 0.1}, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        server.shutdown()
        server.server_close()
        thread.join(timeout=2)


def sandbox_command(workdir, assets, command, *, bridge=None, client_home=None, readonly=()):
    args = ["/usr/bin/bwrap", "--unshare-all", "--die-with-parent", "--new-session",
            "--cap-drop", "ALL", "--clearenv",
            "--ro-bind", "/usr", "/usr", "--ro-bind", "/lib", "/lib",
            "--ro-bind", "/lib64", "/lib64", "--symlink", "usr/bin", "/bin",
            "--symlink", "usr/sbin", "/sbin", "--proc", "/proc", "--dev", "/dev",
            "--tmpfs", "/tmp", "--tmpfs", "/cargo", "--tmpfs", "/home",
            "--dir", "/home/bench", "--dir", "/etc", "--dir", "/runner",
            "--ro-bind", "/etc/alternatives", "/etc/alternatives",
            "--bind", str(Path(workdir).resolve()), "/work",
            "--ro-bind", assets["node_root"], "/node",
            "--ro-bind", assets["rust_root"], "/rust",
            "--ro-bind", assets["cargo_registry"], "/cargo/registry",
            "--ro-bind", assets["node_modules"], "/node_modules",
            "--ro-bind", assets["codex"], "/runner/codex",
            "--ro-bind", str(Path(__file__).resolve()), "/runner/isolation.py",
            "--setenv", "PATH", "/node/bin:/rust/bin:/usr/bin:/bin",
            "--setenv", "HOME", "/home/bench", "--setenv", "CARGO_HOME", "/cargo",
            "--setenv", "CARGO_NET_OFFLINE", "true", "--setenv", "CARGO_BUILD_JOBS", "2",
            "--setenv", "npm_config_offline", "true",
            "--setenv", "CARGO_TARGET_DIR", "/work/target", "--setenv", "CI", "1",
            "--setenv", "LANG", "C.UTF-8", "--setenv", "TERM", "dumb",
            "--chdir", "/work"]
    for name in ("ld.so.cache", "passwd", "group", "hosts", "nsswitch.conf"):
        path = Path("/etc") / name
        if path.exists():
            args += ["--ro-bind", str(path), str(path)]
    for relative in readonly:
        path = Path(workdir) / relative
        args += ["--ro-bind", str(path.resolve()), "/work/" + relative]
    if client_home is not None:
        args += ["--bind", str(Path(client_home).resolve()), "/codex-state",
                 "--setenv", "CODEX_HOME", "/codex-state"]
    if bridge is not None:
        args += ["--ro-bind", str(Path(bridge).resolve()), "/bridge",
                 "--", "/usr/bin/python3", "/runner/isolation.py", "inside", "--", *command]
    else:
        args += ["--", *command]
    return args


def run(workdir, assets, command, timeout, *, bridge=None, client_home=None, readonly=(),
        input_text=None, stdout_path=None, stderr_path=None):
    args = sandbox_command(workdir, assets, command, bridge=bridge,
                           client_home=client_home, readonly=readonly)
    with contextlib.ExitStack() as stack:
        stdout = stack.enter_context(Path(stdout_path).open("a")) if stdout_path else subprocess.PIPE
        stderr = stack.enter_context(Path(stderr_path).open("a")) if stderr_path else subprocess.PIPE
        process = subprocess.Popen(args, stdin=subprocess.PIPE if input_text is not None else subprocess.DEVNULL,
                                   stdout=stdout, stderr=stderr, text=True)
        try:
            out, err = process.communicate(input_text, timeout=timeout)
        except BaseException:
            process.terminate()
            try:
                process.wait(timeout=5)
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                process.kill()
                process.wait(timeout=5)
            raise
    return subprocess.CompletedProcess(args, process.returncode, out or "", err or "")


def inside(command):
    class Handler(socketserver.BaseRequestHandler):
        def handle(self):
            with socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX) as upstream:
                upstream.connect("/bridge")
                relay(self.request, upstream)

    server = ThreadedTCPServer(("127.0.0.1", 8081), Handler)
    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, kwargs={"poll_interval": 0.1}, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    try:
        return subprocess.call(command)
    finally:
        server.shutdown()
        server.server_close()


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("mode", choices=["inside"])
    parser.add_argument("command", nargs=argparse.REMAINDER)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    command = args.command[1:] if args.command[:1] == ["--"] else args.command
    return inside(command)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Controlled long-context padding for the Polyglot tasks.

Section 12.5 measured how much context a card can *hold*. This measures how much
the model can still *use*: the task is held fixed and only the surrounding
context grows, so a failure at depth is attributable to depth alone.

Padding is placed before the instructions, leaving them last. Recency therefore
favours success, which makes any degradation found a lower bound rather than an
adversarial worst case.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
import urllib.request
from pathlib import Path

from scripts import ROOT

CORPORA = {"rust": ROOT / "corpora" / "rust.txt", "javascript": ROOT / "corpora" / "js-ts.txt"}

# Padding is drawn from the L1 corpus repos, which the leakage test already proves
# disjoint from the task pool. This is the second line of defence: a chunk that
# happens to name the exercise would hand over the answer.
_WORD = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{2,}")

# A leak used to abort the whole sweep, so one unlucky offset cost every task
# behind it. Retrying is bounded: exhausting it still fails loudly.
_OFFSET_ATTEMPTS = 24


def _tokenize(text: str, base_url: str, timeout: float = 120.0) -> int:
    request = urllib.request.Request(
        base_url.rstrip("/").removesuffix("/v1") + "/tokenize",
        json.dumps({"content": text}).encode(),
        {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
    )
    with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:
        return len(json.loads(response.read())["tokens"])


def forbidden_terms(task: dict) -> set[str]:
    """Identifiers whose appearance in padding would leak the exercise."""

    slug = str(task["task_id"]).split("/", 1)[-1]
    terms = {slug, slug.replace("-", "_"), slug.replace("-", "")}
    return {t.lower() for t in terms if len(t) > 3}


def build_padding(task: dict, target_tokens: int, base_url: str) -> tuple[str, int]:
    """Return padding of ~target_tokens for this task, and its exact token count.

    The offset is derived from the task id, so padding varies across tasks but is
    identical for the same task across quants and reruns -- the comparison stays
    paired.
    """

    if target_tokens <= 0:
        return "", 0
    language = str(task.get("language", "")).lower()
    corpus = CORPORA.get("rust" if language in {"rust", "rs"} else "javascript")
    text = corpus.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    banned = forbidden_terms(task)

    # Offsets are tried in a fixed order so a task that needs the third one still
    # gets that same padding on every quant and rerun; the pairing survives.
    leaked: set[str] = set()
    for attempt in range(_OFFSET_ATTEMPTS):
        seed = task["task_id"] if attempt == 0 else f"{task['task_id']}:{attempt}"
        digest = hashlib.sha256(seed.encode()).hexdigest()
        start = int(digest[:12], 16) % max(1, len(text) - 1)

        # ~3.2 bytes/token on this corpus; overshoot then trim by real token count.
        window = text[start:] + text[:start]
        chunk = window[: int(target_tokens * 4.0)]
        found = banned & {m.group(0).lower() for m in _WORD.finditer(chunk)}
        if not found:
            break
        leaked |= found
    else:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"PADDING LEAK for {task['task_id']}: every offset mentions {sorted(leaked)}. "
            "Use a different corpus; do not measure with this padding."
        )

    count = _tokenize(chunk, base_url)
    while count > target_tokens and len(chunk) > 1000:
        chunk = chunk[: int(len(chunk) * target_tokens / count)]
        count = _tokenize(chunk, base_url)
    return chunk, count


def wrap_prompt(prompt: str, padding: str) -> str:
    if not padding:
        return prompt
    return (
        "The following is unrelated reference material from other projects. "
        "It is provided as background and is not part of your task.\n\n"
        "<reference>\n" + padding + "\n</reference>\n\n" + prompt
    )


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Run frozen Polyglot/Exercism tasks with one bounded repair round."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import concurrent.futures
import dataclasses
import json
from pathlib import Path
import os
import re
import shlex
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time
from typing import Any, Callable, Iterable

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import emit
from scripts.instruments.longctx import build_padding, wrap_prompt
from scripts.instruments.emit import (
    CodexStats,
    codex_base_url,
    add_context_arguments,
    emit,
    ContextSampler,
    apply_server_probe,
    invoke_codex,
    metrics_delta,
    scrape_metrics,
    make_context,
    server_alive,
)


DEFAULT_SERVER_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1"


class LegAborted(RuntimeError):
    """The leg cannot produce valid rows any more; stop rather than record failures."""



@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class TaskResult:
    task_id: str
    measures: dict[str, Any]


def load_manifest(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        manifest = json.load(handle)
    if not isinstance(manifest, list):
        raise ValueError("manifest must be a JSON array")
    return manifest


def _remaining(deadline: float) -> float:
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining <= 0:
        raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired("task", 0)
    return remaining


def _test_command(value: Any) -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(value, str):
        command = shlex.split(value)
    elif isinstance(value, list) and all(isinstance(item, str) for item in value):
        command = value
    else:
        raise ValueError("test_cmd must be a command string or an array of arguments")
    if not command:
        raise ValueError("test_cmd must not be empty")
    return command


def task_test_command(task: dict[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    """Return the task command with language-required test activation."""

    command = _test_command(task["test_cmd"])
    if str(task.get("language", "")).lower() != "rust":
        return command
    if Path(command[0]).name != "cargo" or len(command) < 2 or command[1] != "test":
        raise ValueError("Rust test_cmd must invoke cargo test")
    if "--include-ignored" in command:
        return command
    if "--" in command:
        separator = command.index("--")
        return command[: separator + 1] + ["--include-ignored"] + command[separator + 1 :]
    return command + ["--", "--include-ignored"]


def _path_values(value: Any, field: str) -> list[str]:
    if isinstance(value, str) and value:
        return [value]
    if isinstance(value, list) and value and all(isinstance(item, str) and item for item in value):
        return value
    raise ValueError(f"{field} must be a non-empty path string or array of path strings")


def _exercise_path(root: Path, value: str, field: str) -> Path:
    path = Path(value)
    if path.is_absolute():
        raise ValueError(f"{field} paths must be relative to source_dir")
    resolved_root = root.resolve()
    resolved = (root / path).resolve()
    try:
        resolved.relative_to(resolved_root)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{field} path escapes source_dir: {value}") from exc
    return resolved


def task_file_paths(
    task: dict[str, Any], workdir: Path, manifest_field: str, config_field: str
) -> list[Path]:
    """Resolve manifest path overrides or Exercism metadata inside a scratch copy."""

    value = task.get(manifest_field)
    if value is None:
        config_path = workdir / ".meta" / "config.json"
        try:
            with config_path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                config = json.load(handle)
            value = config["files"][config_field]
        except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
            raise ValueError(
                f"cannot derive {manifest_field} from {config_path}: {exc}"
            ) from exc
    return [
        _exercise_path(workdir, item, manifest_field)
        for item in _path_values(value, manifest_field)
    ]


def _javascript_test_paths(task: dict[str, Any], workdir: Path) -> list[Path]:
    # Contract: resolvable after .meta is deleted. prepare_test_harness runs once more
    # before each test cycle, but _strip_reference_material has removed .meta by then,
    # so the config-derived answer is cached on first use rather than re-read.
    cached = task.get("_resolved_test_paths")
    if cached is not None:
        return [workdir / value for value in cached]
    config_path = workdir / ".meta" / "config.json"
    if config_path.is_file():
        try:
            with config_path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                config = json.load(handle)
            values = _path_values(config["files"]["test"], "files.test")
            paths = [_exercise_path(workdir, value, "files.test") for value in values]
            task["_resolved_test_paths"] = [
                str(path.relative_to(workdir)) for path in paths
            ]
            return paths
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
            raise ValueError(
                f"cannot derive JavaScript test files from {config_path}: {exc}"
            ) from exc

    extensions = {".js", ".cjs", ".mjs"}
    paths: list[Path] = []
    for path in workdir.rglob("*"):
        if not path.is_file() or path.suffix.lower() not in extensions:
            continue
        name = path.name.lower()
        parts = {part.lower() for part in path.relative_to(workdir).parts[:-1]}
        if (
            ".test." in name
            or ".spec." in name
            or name.startswith("test")
            or parts.intersection({"test", "tests", "__tests__"})
        ):
            paths.append(path)
    return paths


def _strip_reference_material(workdir: Path) -> None:
    """Remove Exercism's .meta/ from the copy the model is given.

    .meta/ carries the reference solution (proof.ci.js, example.rs). Left in place the
    model can simply read the answer, so task_success measures retrieval, not solving.
    Deleting beats sandboxing here: Codex's workspace-write sandbox restricts writes,
    not reads, and a file that is absent cannot be read by any policy.

    Contract: every consumer of .meta must have cached what it needs before this runs.
    """

    shutil.rmtree(workdir / ".meta", ignore_errors=True)


def _link_shared_node_modules(workdir: Path) -> None:
    """Point a JS exercise at one shared dependency tree.

    Each Exercism exercise declares jest in its own devDependencies; installing per
    exercise is ~20x redundant, so one install is linked in instead. Without it every
    JS task self-tests as BROKEN with "jest: not found" -- technically correct and
    useless, so a missing install is raised loudly rather than scored.
    """

    shared = Path(
        os.environ.get(
            "POLYGLOT_NODE_MODULES",
            str(ROOT / ".cache" / "js-deps" / "node_modules"),
        )
    )
    if not shared.is_dir():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"shared node_modules not found at {shared}; run `npm install` in "
            ".cache/js-deps or set POLYGLOT_NODE_MODULES"
        )
    target = workdir / "node_modules"
    if not target.exists():
        target.symlink_to(shared, target_is_directory=True)


def _declare_reference_deps(task: dict[str, Any], workdir: Path) -> None:
    """Declare crates the shipped reference example uses but Cargo.toml omits.

    Affects the scratch copy only. The stub and the tests use no external crate, so
    the task posed to the model is unchanged -- this repairs the reference so the
    self-test can distinguish a solvable exercise from a broken one. Dropping these
    exercises instead would bias selection toward easy tasks.
    """

    table = ROOT / "subsets" / "rust-deps.json"
    if not table.is_file():
        return
    deps = json.loads(table.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["deps"].get(task.get("task_id"))
    if not deps:
        return
    cargo = workdir / "Cargo.toml"
    text = cargo.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    # Contract: idempotent. This runs again before each test cycle, and a repeated
    # key makes cargo reject the manifest outright -- which scores the task failed
    # however good the solution is.
    declared = set(re.findall(r"(?m)^\s*([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)\s*=", text))
    lines = [
        f'{name} = "{version}"'
        for name, version in sorted(deps.items())
        if name not in declared
    ]
    if not lines:
        return
    if "[dependencies]" in text:
        text = text.replace("[dependencies]", "[dependencies]\n" + "\n".join(lines), 1)
    else:
        text = text.rstrip("\n") + "\n\n[dependencies]\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
    cargo.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")


def prepare_test_harness(task: dict[str, Any], workdir: Path) -> int:
    """Enable tests disabled by Exercism in a disposable exercise copy."""

    if str(task.get("language", "")).lower() in {"rust", "rs"}:
        _declare_reference_deps(task, workdir)
        return 0
    if str(task.get("language", "")).lower() not in {"javascript", "js"}:
        return 0
    _link_shared_node_modules(workdir)
    replacements = 0
    for path in _javascript_test_paths(task, workdir):
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        updated, count = re.subn(r"\bxtest\(", "test(", text)
        if count:
            path.write_text(updated, encoding="utf-8")
            replacements += count
    return replacements


def _run_tests(command: list[str], cwd: Path, timeout: float) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    return subprocess.run(
        command,
        cwd=cwd,
        text=True,
        capture_output=True,
        timeout=timeout,
        check=False,
    )


def _merge_stats(stats: Iterable[CodexStats]) -> dict[str, int | None]:
    values = list(stats)

    def total(field: str) -> int | None:
        items = [getattr(item, field) for item in values]
        return sum(items) if items and all(item is not None for item in items) else None

    return {
        "agent_turns": total("agent_turns"),
        "tool_calls": total("tool_calls"),
        "prompt_tokens": total("prompt_tokens"),
        "completion_tokens": total("completion_tokens"),
        "cached_input_tokens": total("cached_input_tokens"),
        # Billable on some routes even though cache reads are near-free, so dropping it
        # understates cost rather than merely losing detail.
        "cache_write_tokens": total("cache_write_tokens"),
        "reasoning_tokens": total("reasoning_tokens"),
    }


def _prompt(task: dict[str, Any]) -> str:
    return (
        f"Solve the {task['language']} exercise in this working directory. "
        "Read the exercise instructions and existing files, implement the solution, and "
        "run or inspect tests as useful. Do not modify files outside this working directory."
    )


def run_task(
    task: dict[str, Any],
    manifest_dir: Path,
    lane: str,
    reasoning_effort: str,
    timeout: float,
    dry_run: bool,
    harness_verified: bool = False,
    transcript: Path | None = None,
    metrics_url: str | None = None,
    context_padding: int = 0,
) -> TaskResult:
    task_id = "<missing-task-id>"
    wall_seconds = 0.0
    stats: list[CodexStats] = []
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    failed_cycles = 0
    metrics_before = scrape_metrics(metrics_url) if metrics_url else {}
    sampler = None
    if metrics_url:
        sampler = ContextSampler(metrics_url)
        sampler.start()
    try:
        if not isinstance(task, dict):
            raise ValueError("each manifest entry must be a JSON object")
        task_id = str(task.get("task_id", ""))
        for field in ("task_id", "language", "source_dir", "test_cmd"):
            if field not in task:
                raise ValueError(f"task is missing required field {field!r}")
        if not task_id:
            raise ValueError("task_id must be non-empty")
        source = Path(task["source_dir"])
        if not source.is_absolute():
            source = manifest_dir / source
        command = task_test_command(task)

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="polyglot-") as temp_dir:
            workdir = Path(temp_dir) / "exercise"
            shutil.copytree(source, workdir)
            prepare_test_harness(task, workdir)
            _strip_reference_material(workdir)
            prompt = _prompt(task)
            padding_tokens = 0
            if context_padding and not dry_run:
                padding, padding_tokens = build_padding(task, context_padding, metrics_url)
                prompt = wrap_prompt(prompt, padding)
            if not dry_run:
                invocation_started = time.monotonic()
                try:
                    turn_stats, _ = invoke_codex(
                        lane, reasoning_effort, prompt, workdir,
                        _remaining(deadline), transcript,
                    )
                finally:
                    wall_seconds += time.monotonic() - invocation_started
                stats.append(turn_stats)

            prepare_test_harness(task, workdir)
            test = _run_tests(command, workdir, _remaining(deadline))
            if test.returncode != 0:
                failed_cycles = 1
                repair_prompt = (
                    prompt
                    + "\n\nThe test command failed. Diagnose and repair the implementation, then "
                    + "re-run the tests. Test output follows:\n\n"
                    + test.stdout
                    + test.stderr
                )
                if not dry_run:
                    invocation_started = time.monotonic()
                    try:
                        turn_stats, _ = invoke_codex(
                            lane, reasoning_effort, repair_prompt, workdir,
                            _remaining(deadline), transcript,
                        )
                    finally:
                        wall_seconds += time.monotonic() - invocation_started
                    stats.append(turn_stats)
                prepare_test_harness(task, workdir)
                test = _run_tests(command, workdir, _remaining(deadline))

            passed = test.returncode == 0
            measures: dict[str, Any] = {
                "task_success": passed,
                "tests_passed": passed,
                "timed_out": False,
                "failed_test_cycles": failed_cycles,
                "harness_verified": harness_verified,
                "wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 6),
                "context_padding_tokens": padding_tokens,
                "notes": None if passed else "tests failed after one repair round",
            }
            measures.update(_merge_stats(stats))
            if transcript is not None:
                measures["transcript"] = str(transcript)
            if metrics_url:
                measures.update(metrics_delta(metrics_before, scrape_metrics(metrics_url)))
                measures["peak_context_tokens"] = sampler.stop()
            return TaskResult(task_id, measures)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        known_stats = _merge_stats(stats)
        return TaskResult(
            task_id or "<missing-task-id>",
            {
                "task_success": False,
                "tests_passed": False,
                # A wall-clock cut-off is a harness parameter, not a model verdict,
                # so it has to be separable from a genuine test failure downstream.
                "timed_out": True,
                "failed_test_cycles": failed_cycles,
                "harness_verified": harness_verified,
                "wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 6),
                "notes": f"task timed out after {timeout:g}s",
                **known_stats,
                **({"transcript": str(transcript)} if transcript else {}),
                **(metrics_delta(metrics_before, scrape_metrics(metrics_url))
                   if metrics_url else {}),
                **({"peak_context_tokens": sampler.stop()} if sampler else {}),
            },
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        known_stats = _merge_stats(stats)
        note = (
            f"Codex exited with status {exc.returncode}"
            if isinstance(exc, subprocess.CalledProcessError)
            else f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        )
        return TaskResult(
            task_id or "<missing-task-id>",
            {
                "task_success": False,
                "tests_passed": False,
                "timed_out": False,
                "failed_test_cycles": failed_cycles,
                "harness_verified": harness_verified,
                "wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 6),
                "notes": note,
                **known_stats,
                **({"transcript": str(transcript)} if transcript else {}),
                **(metrics_delta(metrics_before, scrape_metrics(metrics_url))
                   if metrics_url else {}),
                **({"peak_context_tokens": sampler.stop()} if sampler else {}),
            },
        )


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Copy and run frozen Exercism/Polyglot tasks, allow exactly one repair after "
            "a failed test cycle, and append one durable result row per task."
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--manifest",
        required=True,
        type=Path,
        help=(
            "JSON array of {task_id, language, source_dir, test_cmd, optional "
            "example_path and solution_path} objects"
        ),
    )
    selftest = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group()
    selftest.add_argument(
        "--selftest-report",
        type=Path,
        help="successful report produced by selftest_polyglot.py",
    )
    selftest.add_argument(
        "--skip-selftest",
        action="store_true",
        help="development only: run with an unverified harness",
    )
    add_context_arguments(parser)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--resume",
        action="store_true",
        help="skip tasks already recorded for this quant and run-index in --out",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--context-padding",
        type=int,
        default=0,
        help="tokens of unrelated real code prepended to each task prompt, to measure "
        "usable context as opposed to the hostable context of section 12.5",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--transcripts",
        type=Path,
        help="directory for raw Codex event streams (default: <out dir>/transcripts)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-transcripts",
        action="store_true",
        help="do not persist raw Codex event streams",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--metrics-url",
        default=DEFAULT_SERVER_URL,
        help="llama-server base URL scraped before and after each task for "
        "per-task prefill/decode/cache counters; empty string disables",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--jobs", type=int, default=1, help="concurrent task workers")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--timeout",
        type=float,
        default=2400,
        help="total seconds allowed per task; must clear the slowest quant's decode "
        "rate or the cut-off itself becomes the difference between quants",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--dry-run",
        action="store_true",
        help="copy tasks and run tests, but skip every Codex invocation",
    )
    return parser


def validate_selftest_report(path: Path, tasks: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
    try:
        with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            report = json.load(handle)
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise SystemExit(f"POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST REFUSED: cannot read {path}: {exc}") from exc
    if not isinstance(report, dict) or not isinstance(report.get("tasks"), list):
        raise SystemExit("POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST REFUSED: report must contain a tasks array")

    manifest_ids: list[str] = []
    for task in tasks:
        if not isinstance(task, dict) or not task.get("task_id"):
            raise SystemExit("POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST REFUSED: manifest task_id is missing")
        manifest_ids.append(str(task["task_id"]))
    if len(set(manifest_ids)) != len(manifest_ids):
        raise SystemExit("POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST REFUSED: manifest task_id values are not unique")

    results: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for item in report["tasks"]:
        if not isinstance(item, dict) or not item.get("task_id"):
            raise SystemExit("POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST REFUSED: invalid task entry in report")
        task_id = str(item["task_id"])
        if task_id in results:
            raise SystemExit(
                f"POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST REFUSED: duplicate report task_id {task_id!r}"
            )
        results[task_id] = item

    failures: list[str] = []
    for task_id in manifest_ids:
        item = results.get(task_id)
        if item is None:
            failures.append(f"{task_id}: missing from report")
            continue
        failed_checks = [
            name
            for name in ("reference_passes", "stub_fails")
            if item.get(name) is not True
        ]
        if failed_checks:
            failures.append(f"{task_id}: failed {', '.join(failed_checks)}")
    if failures:
        details = "\n".join(f"  BROKEN {failure}" for failure in failures)
        raise SystemExit(f"POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST REFUSED:\n{details}")


def completed_task_ids(out: Path, quant: str, run_index: int) -> set[str]:
    """Task ids already recorded for this exact leg.

    Scoped by quant and run_index because one output file holds every leg; matching
    on task_id alone would treat a different quant's result as this leg's work.
    """

    done: set[str] = set()
    if not out.exists():
        return done
    for line in out.read_text().splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        try:
            row = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:  # a torn last line from a killed writer
            continue
        if row.get("quant") == quant and row.get("run_index") == run_index:
            done.add(row.get("task_id"))
    return done


def run_leg(
    tasks: list[dict[str, Any]],
    worker: "Callable[[dict[str, Any]], TaskResult]",
    record: "Callable[[TaskResult], None]",
    metrics_url: str,
    jobs: int,
) -> int:
    """Run the tasks, checking the endpoint between them.

    The check is between tasks, not just at startup: when the server died at task 8 of 40
    the loop ran on and wrote 33 rows with null token counts blaming the model, exited 0,
    and the driver's EXIT trap destroyed the box holding the server log.
    """

    aborted = threading.Event()

    def gated(task: dict[str, Any]) -> TaskResult:
        task_id = (task.get("task_id") or task.get("instance_id")) if isinstance(task, dict) else None
        if aborted.is_set():
            raise LegAborted(f"skipped {task_id}")
        if metrics_url and not server_alive(metrics_url):
            aborted.set()
            raise LegAborted(
                f"the server at {metrics_url} stopped answering before {task_id}. "
                f"Rows already written are valid; the rest of this leg is not. "
                f"Re-run with --resume once the server is back."
            )
        return worker(task)

    with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=jobs) as executor:
        try:
            for result in executor.map(gated, tasks):
                record(result)
        except LegAborted as exc:
            aborted.set()
            print(f"LEG ABORTED: {exc}", file=sys.stderr, flush=True)
            return 5
    return 0


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
    if args.jobs < 1:
        raise SystemExit("--jobs must be at least 1")
    if args.timeout <= 0:
        raise SystemExit("--timeout must be positive")
    tasks = load_manifest(args.manifest)
    if args.skip_selftest:
        print(
            "*** WARNING: POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST SKIPPED; RESULTS ARE UNVERIFIED ***",
            file=sys.stderr,
        )
        harness_verified = False
    else:
        if args.selftest_report is None:
            raise SystemExit(
                "POLYGLOT HARNESS SELF-TEST REQUIRED: pass --selftest-report PATH "
                "or --skip-selftest for development only"
            )
        validate_selftest_report(args.selftest_report, tasks)
        harness_verified = True
    ctx = make_context(args)
    if args.resume:
        done = completed_task_ids(Path(args.out), args.quant, args.run_index)
        tasks = [t for t in tasks if t.get("task_id") not in done]
        print(f"resuming: {len(done)} task(s) already recorded, {len(tasks)} to run", flush=True)
        if not tasks:
            # Exiting 0 here reads as a completed leg. A driver that just rented a box,
            # downloaded the weights and served the model then tears it down reporting
            # success, having measured nothing -- which is what a spent run-index does,
            # including one spent by an earlier *void* attempt.
            raise SystemExit(
                f"NOTHING TO RUN: every task is already recorded for quant "
                f"{args.quant!r} at run-index {args.run_index}. Use a new --run-index "
                f"for a new configuration, or remove the rows if that leg was void."
            )
    # --dry-run must stay offline: it is the no-GPU harness check. External lanes
    # never touch llama-server, so scraping it would attribute local traffic to them.
    local_lane = args.lane in {"qwen", "quant"}
    # The probe and the runner must interrogate the same endpoint. When they diverged --
    # tunnel on 8010, Codex pinned to 8000 -- the fail-closed quant guard confirmed a
    # healthy server on 8010 while every one of 40 tasks died calling 8000.
    if local_lane and not args.dry_run:
        codex_url = codex_base_url()
        if codex_url is None:
            raise SystemExit(
                "cannot read Codex's config to learn which endpoint it will call; "
                "the server probe would be verifying an endpoint nothing uses"
            )
        if args.metrics_url and args.metrics_url.rstrip("/") != codex_url.rstrip("/"):
            raise SystemExit(
                f"ENDPOINT MISMATCH: Codex calls {codex_url} but --metrics-url is "
                f"{args.metrics_url}. Tunnel to the port Codex is configured for, or "
                "change the provider's base_url; do not measure one and serve the other."
            )
        args.metrics_url = args.metrics_url or codex_url
    # Telemetry may be switched off; the identity guard may not. Emptying
    # --metrics-url used to skip apply_server_probe too, so three legs ran with the
    # fail-closed wrong-quant guard silently off.
    metrics_url = args.metrics_url if (local_lane and not args.dry_run) else ""
    if local_lane and not args.dry_run:
        ctx = apply_server_probe(ctx, args.metrics_url or DEFAULT_SERVER_URL)

    transcript_root = args.transcripts
    if transcript_root is None and not args.no_transcripts:
        transcript_root = Path(args.out).resolve().parent / "transcripts"

    def record(result: TaskResult) -> None:
        emit(ctx, result.task_id, args.run_index, **result.measures)

    def worker(task: dict[str, Any]) -> TaskResult:
        transcript = None
        if transcript_root is not None:
            task_id = str(task.get("task_id", "unknown")) if isinstance(task, dict) else "unknown"
            safe = task_id.replace("/", "__")
            transcript = (transcript_root
                          / f"{args.quant}__run{args.run_index}__{safe}.jsonl")
        return run_task(
            task,
            args.manifest.resolve().parent,
            args.lane,
            args.reasoning_effort,
            args.timeout,
            args.dry_run,
            harness_verified,
            transcript,
            metrics_url,
            args.context_padding,
        )

    return run_leg(tasks, worker, record, metrics_url, args.jobs)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Create SWE-bench prediction patches without running evaluation containers."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import concurrent.futures
import dataclasses
import fcntl
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from typing import Any

from scripts.instruments.emit import add_context_arguments, emit, invoke_codex, make_context
from scripts.instruments.run_polyglot import DEFAULT_SERVER_URL, completed_task_ids, run_leg


@dataclasses.dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class InstanceResult:
    task_id: str
    patch: str
    measures: dict[str, Any]
    # The run never reached the model. Kept off the row -- it is not a measurement --
    # and used to withhold the prediction, so the harness reports the instance
    # incomplete instead of binning a patch that was never attempted.
    infra_failure: bool = False


def load_instances(path: Path) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    instances: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        for line_number, line in enumerate(handle, 1):
            if not line.strip():
                continue
            value = json.loads(line)
            if not isinstance(value, dict):
                raise ValueError(f"line {line_number} must contain a JSON object")
            instances.append(value)
    return instances


def _remaining(deadline: float) -> float:
    remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining <= 0:
        raise subprocess.TimeoutExpired("task", 0)
    return remaining


def _git(args: list[str], cwd: Path | None, timeout: float) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    return subprocess.run(
        ["git", *args],
        cwd=cwd,
        text=True,
        capture_output=True,
        timeout=timeout,
        check=True,
    )


def _cache_name(repo: str) -> str:
    stem = Path(repo.rstrip("/")).name.removesuffix(".git") or "repo"
    safe_stem = "".join(character if character.isalnum() else "-" for character in stem)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(repo.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]
    return f"{safe_stem}-{digest}.git"


def _ensure_cache(repo: str, cache_dir: Path, deadline: float) -> Path:
    cache_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    cached = cache_dir / _cache_name(repo)
    lock_path = cache_dir / (_cache_name(repo) + ".lock")
    with lock_path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as lock:
        fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        try:
            if not cached.exists():
                with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="clone-", dir=cache_dir) as temp_dir:
                    staged = Path(temp_dir) / "repo.git"
                    _git(["clone", "--mirror", repo, str(staged)], None, _remaining(deadline))
                    os.replace(staged, cached)
        finally:
            fcntl.flock(lock.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)
    return cached


def _resolve_repo(repo: str, instances_dir: Path) -> str:
    candidate = Path(repo)
    if not candidate.is_absolute() and (instances_dir / candidate).exists():
        return str((instances_dir / candidate).resolve())
    if "://" not in repo and repo.count("/") == 1:
        return f"https://github.com/{repo.removesuffix('.git')}.git"
    return repo


def run_instance(
    instance: dict[str, Any],
    instances_dir: Path,
    cache_dir: Path,
    lane: str,
    reasoning_effort: str,
    timeout: float,
    dry_run: bool,
) -> InstanceResult:
    task_id = "<missing-instance-id>"
    wall_seconds = 0.0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    try:
        if not isinstance(instance, dict):
            raise ValueError("each instances line must be a JSON object")
        task_id = str(instance.get("instance_id", ""))
        for field in ("instance_id", "repo", "base_commit", "problem_statement"):
            if field not in instance:
                raise ValueError(f"instance is missing required field {field!r}")
        if not task_id:
            raise ValueError("instance_id must be non-empty")
        repo = _resolve_repo(str(instance["repo"]), instances_dir)
        base_commit = str(instance["base_commit"])
        cached = _ensure_cache(repo, cache_dir, deadline)

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="swebench-") as temp_dir:
            workdir = Path(temp_dir) / "repo"
            _git(["clone", "--shared", str(cached), str(workdir)], None, _remaining(deadline))
            _git(["checkout", "--detach", base_commit], workdir, _remaining(deadline))
            _git(["clean", "-xfd"], workdir, _remaining(deadline))

            stats = None
            if not dry_run:
                invocation_started = time.monotonic()
                try:
                    stats, _ = invoke_codex(
                        lane,
                        reasoning_effort,
                        str(instance["problem_statement"]),
                        workdir,
                        _remaining(deadline),
                    )
                finally:
                    wall_seconds += time.monotonic() - invocation_started

            _git(["add", "-N", "--all"], workdir, _remaining(deadline))
            diff = _git(
                ["diff", "--binary", base_commit, "--"], workdir, _remaining(deadline)
            ).stdout
            success = bool(diff)
            # A patch is not a solve. Only grade_swebench.py, reading the evaluation
            # containers' verdict, may set these -- anything else reads "the model
            # edited a file" as "the tests pass", which on this dataset is wrong about
            # half the time even for a strong model.
            return InstanceResult(
                task_id,
                diff,
                {
                    "task_success": None,
                    "tests_passed": None,
                    "failed_test_cycles": 0,
                    "agent_turns": stats.agent_turns if stats else None,
                    "tool_calls": stats.tool_calls if stats else None,
                    "prompt_tokens": stats.prompt_tokens if stats else None,
                    "completion_tokens": stats.completion_tokens if stats else None,
                    "wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 6),
                    "notes": (
                        None
                        if success
                        else "dry-run produced an empty diff"
                        if dry_run
                        else "Codex produced an empty diff"
                    ),
                },
            )
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        return InstanceResult(
            task_id or "<missing-instance-id>",
            "",
            {
                "task_success": False,
                "tests_passed": None,
                "failed_test_cycles": 0,
                "agent_turns": None,
                "tool_calls": None,
                "wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 6),
                "notes": f"task timed out after {timeout:g}s",
            },
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        note = (
            f"subprocess exited with status {exc.returncode}"
            if isinstance(exc, subprocess.CalledProcessError)
            else f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
        )
        # Not False: a clone that failed or a disk that filled says nothing about the
        # quant, and counting it as an unsolved instance charges the arm that happened
        # to be running at the time. The timeout above stays False because running out
        # of the configured budget *is* an outcome of the configuration.
        return InstanceResult(
            task_id or "<missing-instance-id>",
            "",
            {
                "task_success": None,
                "tests_passed": None,
                "failed_test_cycles": 0,
                "agent_turns": None,
                "tool_calls": None,
                "wall_seconds": round(wall_seconds, 6),
                "notes": note,
            },
            infra_failure=True,
        )


def infra_failed_ids(out: Path, quant: str, run_index: int) -> set[str]:
    """Instances this leg recorded without ever reaching the model.

    --resume must retry these. They were written to keep the failure visible, not because
    anything was measured, and skipping them turns one bad clone into a permanent hole in
    the arm -- which a paired design then drops from *both* arms.
    """
    failed: set[str] = set()
    if not out.exists():
        return failed
    for line in out.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        if not line.strip():
            continue
        try:
            row = json.loads(line)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:  # a torn last line from a killed writer
            continue
        if (row.get("quant") == quant and row.get("run_index") == run_index
                and row.get("task_success") is None
                and not str(row.get("notes") or "").startswith("Codex produced")):
            failed.add(row.get("task_id"))
    return failed


def _append_prediction(path: Path, row: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    line = json.dumps(
        row, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False
    ) + "\n"
    with path.open("a", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        try:
            handle.write(line)
            handle.flush()
            os.fsync(handle.fileno())
        finally:
            fcntl.flock(handle.fileno(), fcntl.LOCK_UN)


def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description=(
            "Generate SWE-bench-format prediction patches only. This script does not run "
            "SWE-bench evaluation containers; evaluate the predictions separately."
        ),
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter,
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--instances",
        required=True,
        type=Path,
        help="JSONL input with instance_id, repo, base_commit, and problem_statement",
    )
    add_context_arguments(parser)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--predictions", required=True, type=Path, help="append-only SWE-bench prediction JSONL"
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--repo-cache",
        type=Path,
        default=Path("./.repo-cache"),
        help="reusable bare repository cache shared across configurations",
    )
    parser.add_argument("--jobs", type=int, default=1, help="concurrent instance workers")
    parser.add_argument("--timeout", type=float, default=900, help="total seconds allowed per instance")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--dry-run",
        action="store_true",
        help="prepare repos and produce empty-diff predictions, but skip Codex",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--resume",
        action="store_true",
        help="skip instances already recorded for this quant and run-index in --out",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--metrics-url",
        default=DEFAULT_SERVER_URL,
        help="endpoint checked between instances; empty disables the check",
    )
    return parser


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = build_parser().parse_args(argv)
