    if args.jobs < 1:
        raise SystemExit("--jobs must be at least 1")
    if args.timeout <= 0:
        raise SystemExit("--timeout must be positive")
    instances = load_instances(args.instances)
    ctx = make_context(args)
    if args.resume:
        done = completed_task_ids(Path(args.out), args.quant, args.run_index)
        done -= infra_failed_ids(Path(args.out), args.quant, args.run_index)
        instances = [i for i in instances if str(i.get("instance_id")) not in done]
        print(f"resuming: {len(done)} instance(s) already recorded, "
              f"{len(instances)} to run", flush=True)
        if not instances:
            # Exiting 0 here reads as a completed leg, having measured nothing.
            raise SystemExit(
                f"NOTHING TO RUN: every instance is already recorded for quant "
                f"{args.quant!r} at run-index {args.run_index}. Use a new --run-index "
                f"for a new configuration, or remove the rows if that leg was void."
            )

    def worker(instance: dict[str, Any]) -> InstanceResult:
        return run_instance(
            instance,
            args.instances.resolve().parent,
            args.repo_cache.resolve(),
            args.lane,
            args.reasoning_effort,
            args.timeout,
            args.dry_run,
        )

    def record(result: InstanceResult) -> None:
        # An instance that never reached the model has no patch to submit. Submitting the
        # empty one anyway puts it in the harness's empty_patch_ids, which grades as a
        # model failure -- charging a failed clone to the quant under test.
        if not result.infra_failure:
            _append_prediction(
                args.predictions,
                {
                    "instance_id": result.task_id,
                    # The harness names its report and its logs after this, so every leg
                    # needs its own: two quants writing `qwen3.8-27b` would grade the
                    # second against the first's report directory.
                    "model_name_or_path": f"{ctx.model}-{ctx.quant}-r{args.run_index}",
                    "model_patch": result.patch,
                },
            )
        emit(ctx, result.task_id, args.run_index, **result.measures)

    # --dry-run must stay offline: it is the no-GPU harness check. External lanes never
    # touch llama-server, so probing it would attribute local traffic to them.
    local_lane = args.lane in {"qwen", "quant"}
    metrics_url = args.metrics_url if (local_lane and not args.dry_run) else ""
    return run_leg(instances, worker, record, metrics_url, args.jobs)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


"""Bounded unattended continuation of the existing Swift tuning screens."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from datetime import datetime, time, timedelta
import json
from pathlib import Path
import statistics
import subprocess
import time as walltime

from scripts.instruments import swift_longtail


SERVE = Path("/home/xanewok/.codex/skills/local-qwen-server/bin/serve.sh")
WRAPPER = Path("/home/xanewok/repos/qwen38-eval/scripts/instruments/run_swift_tuning_supervised.sh")
STAGES = ("batch", "mtp", "probability", "minimum", "draft-cache", "swarm-allocation")
PROFILE_KEYS = frozenset({
    "context", "slots", "mode", "split", "ubatch", "maximum", "chat_template",
    "batch", "minimum", "p_min", "draft_k", "draft_v",
})


def deadline(now: datetime, target: time = time(8, 0)) -> datetime:
    value = now.replace(hour=target.hour, minute=target.minute, second=0, microsecond=0)
    return value if value > now else value + timedelta(days=1)


def read_json(path: Path, default=None):
    try:
        return json.loads(path.read_text())
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return default


def run_command(args: list[str], *, check=False) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
    return subprocess.run(args, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30, check=check)


def stop_interactive(log) -> None:
    """Stop only our interactive unit; an already absent unit is a safe no-op."""
    stopped = run_command([str(SERVE), "stop"])
    if stopped.returncode == 0:
        return
    active = run_command(["systemctl", "--user", "is-active", "qwen-local-server.service"])
    if active.stdout.strip() in {"active", "activating", "deactivating"}:
        raise RuntimeError(f"interactive stop failed: {stopped.stderr.strip()}")
    log({"reason": "interactive_already_stopped", "detail": stopped.stderr.strip()})


def campaign_status(path: Path) -> dict:
    state = read_json(path / "state.json", {}) or {}
    return {"status": state.get("status"),
            "completed": len(state.get("completed", {})),
            "total": len((read_json(path / "manifest.json", {}) or {}).get("queue", [])),
            "reason": state.get("reason")}


def passing_results(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    rows = []
    for result_path in path.glob("attempts/*/result.json"):
        result = read_json(result_path)
        if result and result.get("status") == "passed" and result.get("target", 0) > 0:
            rows.append(result)
    return rows


def profile_config(config: dict) -> dict:
    return {key: config[key] for key in PROFILE_KEYS if key in config}


def result_score(row: dict):
    if row.get("decode_tps") is not None:
        return row["decode_tps"]
    return ((row.get("probe") or {}).get("metrics_delta") or {}).get("decode_tps")


def same_profile(left: dict, right: dict) -> bool:
    return profile_config(left) == profile_config(right)


def config_key(config: dict, stage: str):
    if stage == "batch":
        return config.get("batch", 2048)
    if stage == "mtp":
        return config.get("maximum", 3)
    if stage == "probability":
        return config.get("p_min", 0)
    if stage == "minimum":
        return config.get("minimum", 0)
    if stage == "draft-cache":
        return (config.get("draft_k", "f16"), config.get("draft_v", "f16"))
    return None


def select_config(path: Path, base: dict, stage: str) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    rows = passing_results(path)
    prefixes = {
        "batch": "batch-batch-",
        "mtp": "mtp-maximum-",
        "probability": "probability-p_min-",
        "minimum": "minimum-minimum-",
        "draft-cache": "draft-cache-draft-q8-experimental-",
    }
    prefix = prefixes.get(stage)
    candidate_rows = rows
    if prefix:
        candidate_rows = [row for row in rows if str(row.get("job", "")).startswith(prefix)]
    grouped: dict[object, list[dict]] = {}
    for row in candidate_rows:
        grouped.setdefault(config_key(row.get("config", {}), stage), []).append(row)
    candidates = []
    for key, values in grouped.items():
        coverage = {(row.get("task"), row.get("target")) for row in values}
        if len(coverage) < 4:
            continue
        scores = [result_score(row) for row in values if result_score(row) is not None]
        if len(scores) < 4:
            continue
        representative = profile_config(values[0]["config"])
        candidates.append((statistics.mean(scores), key, representative, len(values)))
    candidates.sort(key=lambda item: (item[0], str(item[1])), reverse=True)
    base_rows = [row for row in rows if same_profile(row.get("config", {}), base)]
    matched_prefix = f"{stage}-matched-control-"
    matched_rows = [row for row in base_rows if str(row.get("job", "")).startswith(matched_prefix)]
    if matched_rows:
        base_rows = matched_rows
    base_coverage = {(row.get("task"), row.get("target")) for row in base_rows}
    base_scores = [result_score(row) for row in base_rows if result_score(row) is not None]
    base_mean = statistics.mean(base_scores) if len(base_coverage) >= 4 and len(base_scores) >= 4 else None
    eligible = [candidate for candidate in candidates
                if base_mean is not None and candidate[0] >= base_mean * 1.05]
    if eligible:
        score, key, selected, count = eligible[0]
        return selected, {"stage": stage, "selected": key, "mean_decode_tps": score,
                          "coverage_rows": count, "candidates": len(candidates),
                          "baseline_mean_decode_tps": base_mean,
                          "minimum_gain_fraction": 0.05,
                          "decision": "highest mean decode among fully covered arms beating base by at least 5%"}
    decision = "retain base; no fully covered passing candidate"
    if candidates and base_mean is None:
        decision = "retain base; base coverage is incomplete"
    elif candidates:
        decision = "retain base; no candidate beats base by at least 5%"
    return profile_config(base), {"stage": stage, "selected": config_key(base, stage),
                                  "mean_decode_tps": None, "coverage_rows": 0,
                                  "candidates": len(candidates),
                                  "baseline_mean_decode_tps": base_mean,
                                  "minimum_gain_fraction": 0.05, "decision": decision}


def prepare_and_launch(root: Path, selected: dict, stage: str, now_tag: str,
                       log, budget_hours: float) -> tuple[Path, str]:
    queue = root / f"swift-autonomous-{stage}-{now_tag}-queue.json"
    swift_longtail.tuning_main([stage, "--selected-config",
                                str(root / "autonomous-selected.json"), "--out", str(queue)])
    campaign = root / f"campaign-swift-autonomous-{stage}-{now_tag}"
    run_command(["python3", "-m", "scripts.instruments.hillclimb", "prepare",
                 "--swift-tuning", "--queue-json", str(queue), "--campaign", str(campaign)], check=True)
    stop_interactive(log)
    unit = f"qwen-tuning-autonomous-{stage}-{now_tag}"
    launched = run_command(["systemd-run", "--user", "--no-block", f"--unit={unit}",
                            "--collect", "--service-type=oneshot",
                            "--working-directory=/home/xanewok/repos/qwen38-eval",
                            str(WRAPPER), str(campaign), str(budget_hours)])
    if launched.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(f"systemd-run failed: {launched.stdout.strip()} {launched.stderr.strip()}")
    log({"reason": "launched", "stage": stage, "unit": unit, "campaign": str(campaign),
         "queue": str(queue)})
    return campaign, unit


def log_event(out: Path, row: dict):
    row = {"time": walltime.time(), **row}
    out.with_name(out.stem + "-latest.json").write_text(json.dumps(row, indent=2) + "\n")
    with out.open("a") as stream:
        stream.write(json.dumps(row, sort_keys=True) + "\n")


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--root", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--current-campaign", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--base-config", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--interval", type=int, default=60)
    parser.add_argument("--nothing-threshold", type=int, default=3)
    parser.add_argument("--budget-hours", type=float, default=8)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    if args.interval < 1 or args.nothing_threshold < 1:
        parser.error("interval and nothing-threshold must be positive")
    args.out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    state_path = args.root / "autonomous-dispatch-state.json"
    state = read_json(state_path, {}) or {"stage_index": 0, "campaign": str(args.current_campaign),
                                          "base_config": str(args.base_config), "nothing": 0}
    current_file = args.root / "autonomous-current-campaign.txt"
    current_file.write_text(str(state["campaign"]) + "\n")
    stop_at = deadline(datetime.now().astimezone())

    def log(row):
        log_event(args.out, row)

    while datetime.now().astimezone() < stop_at:
        campaign = Path(state["campaign"])
        status = campaign_status(campaign)
        log({"reason": "check", "stage": STAGES[state["stage_index"]],
             "campaign": str(campaign), "status": status})
        if status["status"] in {"prepared", "running"}:
            state["nothing"] = 0
        elif status["status"] == "complete":
            stage = STAGES[state["stage_index"]]
            if stage == "swarm-allocation":
                log({"reason": "no_auto_promotion", "stage": stage,
                     "detail": "swarm allocation requires separate concurrency qualification"})
                state["nothing"] += 1
            else:
                base = profile_config(read_json(Path(state["base_config"]), {}) or {})
                selected, evidence = select_config(campaign, base, stage)
                selected = profile_config(selected)
                selected_path = args.root / "autonomous-selected.json"
                selected_path.write_text(json.dumps(selected, indent=2) + "\n")
                log({"reason": "selected", "stage": stage, "evidence": evidence,
                     "config": selected})
                next_index = state["stage_index"] + 1
                if next_index >= len(STAGES):
                    state["nothing"] += 1
                else:
                    next_stage = STAGES[next_index]
                    try:
                        next_campaign, _ = prepare_and_launch(
                            args.root, selected, next_stage,
                            datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S"), log, args.budget_hours)
                    except Exception as error:
                        log({"reason": "dispatch_error", "stage": next_stage,
                             "error": f"{type(error).__name__}: {error}"})
                        state["nothing"] += 1
                    else:
                        state.update(stage_index=next_index, campaign=str(next_campaign),
                                     base_config=str(selected_path), nothing=0)
                        current_file.write_text(str(next_campaign) + "\n")
        else:
            state["nothing"] += 1
        state_path.write_text(json.dumps(state, indent=2) + "\n")
        if state["nothing"] >= args.nothing_threshold:
            log({"reason": "nothing_to_do_threshold", "consecutive": state["nothing"]})
            return 0
        remaining = max(0, min(args.interval,
                               (stop_at - datetime.now().astimezone()).total_seconds()))
        walltime.sleep(remaining)
    log({"reason": "deadline_reached"})
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


"""Sequential, resumable full 78-task campaigns for the three Swift variants."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from collections import defaultdict
import json
import math
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import agentic_campaign as a
from scripts.instruments import confirmed_agentic as c
from scripts.instruments import hillclimb as h
from scripts.instruments.chat_templates import BARTOWSKI_PREFIX


# Separate child campaigns preserve each model's measured context and VRAM policy.
MODELS = [
    {"id": "swift-iq4-xs", "repo": "ukisai/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF", "revision": "b12fc8f44af7bdf31a3c3309891acb345d3106a2",
     "file": "Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-IQ4_XS.gguf", "bytes": 15688288352,
     "sha256": "27f5531a012a4bb3c3e379318dc62216f057131d34347498fabfc23186a4ffda",
     "local": str(ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models/swift-iq4-xs/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-IQ4_XS.gguf")},
    {"id": "swift-q4-m", "repo": "ukisai/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF", "revision": "b12fc8f44af7bdf31a3c3309891acb345d3106a2",
     "file": "Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-Q4_K_M.gguf", "bytes": 18024380576,
     "sha256": "ad5811e291431bd0de1cec0c4004a5eac98daee9850882edac69a823209e88ab",
     "local": str(ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models/swift-q4-m/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-Q4_K_M.gguf")},
    {"id": "swift-iq3-xxs", "repo": "ukisai/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF", "revision": "b12fc8f44af7bdf31a3c3309891acb345d3106a2",
     "file": "Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-IQ3_XXS.gguf", "bytes": 12532504672,
     "sha256": "1d16444b9164595fb59cdfc1b11c24b1e167c68238d33a0e4d261495a5178f60",
     "local": str(ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models/swift-iq3-xxs/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-IQ3_XXS.gguf")},
]
PROFILE = {
    "swift-iq4-xs": {"context": 102400, "split": "69,31", "ubatch": 128, "reserves": [512, 256], "recovery_extra_headroom_mib": 128},
    "swift-q4-m": {"context": 92160, "split": "68.75,31.25", "ubatch": 128, "reserves": [128, 256], "recovery_extra_headroom_mib": 0},
    "swift-iq3-xxs": {"context": 102400, "split": "69,31", "ubatch": 128, "reserves": [512, 256], "recovery_extra_headroom_mib": 128},
}


def configure(model_id, tasks):
    model = next(model for model in MODELS if model["id"] == model_id)
    h.MODELS[:] = [json.loads(json.dumps(model))]
    h.TASKS[:] = list(tasks)


def task_ids(assets):
    rows = json.loads((assets / "tasks.json").read_text())
    ids = [row["task_id"] for row in rows]
    if len(ids) != 78 or len(set(ids)) != 78:
        raise ValueError("trusted assets must contain 78 unique tasks")
    return a.TASK_ORDER + [task for task in ids if task not in a.TASK_ORDER]


def queue(model_id, tasks):
    p = PROFILE[model_id]
    config = {key: p[key] for key in ("context", "split", "ubatch")}
    config.update(slots=1, mode="tensor", maximum=3, chat_template=BARTOWSKI_PREFIX)
    return [{"id": "solve-" + model_id + "-" + task.replace("/", "-"), "kind": "profile",
             "model": model_id, "task": task, "workload": "agentic", "targets": [0],
             "configs": [config]} for task in tasks]


def child_policy(model_id):
    p = PROFILE[model_id]
    return {"models": [model_id], "profile": {key: p[key] for key in ("context", "split", "ubatch")},
            "queue_order": "model-first", "production_promotion": False,
            "watchdog_recovery": c.WATCHDOG_RECOVERY,
            "recovery_extra_headroom_mib": p["recovery_extra_headroom_mib"]}


def summarize_child(campaign):
    manifest = h.read_sealed(campaign / "manifest.json")
    rows = []
    for job in manifest["queue"]:
        path = campaign / ("job-" + job["id"] + ".json")
        if path.exists():
            rows.append(h.read_sealed(path))
    measured = [row for row in rows if row.get("status") == "passed" and row.get("probe")]
    probes = [row["probe"] for row in measured]
    metrics = [probe.get("metrics_delta", {}) for probe in probes]
    output = sum(metric.get("decode_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics)
    decode = sum(probe.get("decode_seconds", 0) for probe in probes)
    model = manifest["models"][0]["id"]
    result = {"updated_epoch": time.time(), "state": json.loads((campaign / "state.json").read_text())["status"],
              "model": model, "processed": len(rows), "measured": len(measured),
              "solved": sum(bool(probe.get("task_success")) for probe in probes),
              "timeouts": sum(bool(probe.get("timed_out")) for probe in probes),
              "wall_seconds": sum(probe.get("verified_wall_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
              "prefill_seconds": sum(probe.get("prefill_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
              "decode_seconds": decode, "output_tokens": output,
              "decode_tps": output / decode if decode else None,
              "peak_context_tokens": max((probe.get("peak_context_tokens", 0) for probe in probes), default=0),
              "draft_acceptance": (sum(metric.get("draft_accepted_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics) /
                                   sum(metric.get("draft_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics)
                                   if sum(metric.get("draft_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics) else None),
              "config": PROFILE[model]}
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "SUMMARY.json", result)
    return result


def write_parent_summary(campaign, children, state):
    result = {"updated_epoch": time.time(), "state": state, "scope": "three sequential Swift full 78-task campaigns; no production promotion",
              "children": {model_id: summarize_child(child) for model_id, child in children.items()}}
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "SUMMARY.json", result)
    lines = ["# Swift full-suite comparison", "", json.dumps(result, indent=2), "",
             "| Model | State | Processed | Solved | Wall h | Decode tok/s | Output tokens |", "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|"]
    for model_id, row in result["children"].items():
        lines.append("| %s | %s | %s | %s | %.2f | %s | %s |" %
                     (model_id, row["state"], row["processed"], row["solved"], row["wall_seconds"] / 3600,
                      "" if row["decode_tps"] is None else f"{row['decode_tps']:.2f}", row["output_tokens"]))
    (campaign / "REPORT.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    return result


def prepare(campaign, assets, tasks):
    campaign.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    children = {}
    for model in MODELS:
        model_id = model["id"]
        child = campaign / model_id
        configure(model_id, tasks)
        p = PROFILE[model_id]
        h.initialize(child, ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models", profiles=queue(model_id, tasks), max_attempts=3,
                     agentic_assets=assets, continuation=child_policy(model_id),
                     vram_reserves=tuple(p["reserves"]))
        children[model_id] = child
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "manifest.json", {"schema_version": 1, "models": [m["id"] for m in MODELS],
                                                 "children": {k: str(v) for k, v in children.items()},
                                                 "tasks": tasks, "assets": str(assets), "created": time.time()})
    return children


def run_child(model_id, child, deadline):
    state = json.loads((child / "state.json").read_text())
    if state["status"] == "complete" and not state.get("owned"):
        return summarize_child(child)
    if state["status"] in {"stopped", "error"} and not state.get("owned"):
        reason = state.get("reason", "")
        if reason.startswith("memory_") or reason.startswith("memory_pressure"):
            return summarize_child(child)
        raise RuntimeError(f"child {model_id} stopped: {reason}")
    manifest = h.read_sealed(child / "manifest.json")
    configure(model_id, [job["task"] for job in manifest["queue"]])
    selection = {"models": {model_id: {"ubatch": PROFILE[model_id]["ubatch"]}}}
    c.ConfirmedRunner(child, deadline, selection).run()
    result = summarize_child(child)
    if result["state"] == "stopped":
        reason = json.loads((child / "state.json").read_text()).get("reason", "")
        if not (reason.startswith("memory_") or reason.startswith("memory_pressure")):
            raise RuntimeError(f"child {model_id} stopped: {reason}")
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("command", choices=["run", "resume", "status", "stop"])
    parser.add_argument("--campaign", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--assets", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--deadline-epoch", type=float)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    campaign = args.campaign.resolve()
    if args.command == "status":
        for name in ("SUMMARY.json", "REPORT.md"):
            if (campaign / name).exists():
                print((campaign / name).read_text())
        return
    if args.command == "stop":
        (campaign / "STOP").touch()
        for model in MODELS:
            child = campaign / model["id"]
            if child.exists():
                (child / "STOP").touch()
        return
    if args.deadline_epoch is None or not math.isfinite(args.deadline_epoch) or args.deadline_epoch <= time.time():
        parser.error("run/resume requires a future absolute deadline")
    if args.command == "run":
        if args.assets is None:
            parser.error("run requires --assets")
        assets = args.assets.resolve()
        tasks = task_ids(assets)
        if campaign.exists():
            manifest = json.loads((campaign / "manifest.json").read_text())
            if manifest["tasks"] != tasks:
                raise ValueError("existing parent campaign has different task order")
            children = {model["id"]: Path(manifest["children"][model["id"]]) for model in MODELS}
        else:
            children = prepare(campaign, assets, tasks)
    else:
        manifest = json.loads((campaign / "manifest.json").read_text())
        children = {model["id"]: Path(manifest["children"][model["id"]]) for model in MODELS}
    if (campaign / "STOP").exists():
        raise RuntimeError("parent STOP marker exists")
    for model in MODELS:
        result = run_child(model["id"], children[model["id"]], args.deadline_epoch)
        write_parent_summary(campaign, children, "running")
        if result["state"] not in {"complete", "stopped"}:
            raise RuntimeError(f"child {model['id']} did not reach a terminal state")
    write_parent_summary(campaign, children, "complete")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


"""Run a Swift IQ3_XXS two-GPU long-tail comparison campaign."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import agentic_campaign as agentic
from scripts.instruments.chat_templates import BARTOWSKI_PREFIX
from scripts.instruments import hillclimb as h


LONG_TAIL_TASKS = [
    "rust/doubly-linked-list",
    "rust/book-store",
    "rust/react",
    "rust/alphametics",
    "rust/forth",
    "rust/xorcism",
    "rust/nucleotide-codons",
    "javascript/complex-numbers",
]
FIT_TASK = "javascript/affine-cipher"
MODEL_PATH = ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models/swift-iq3-xxs/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-IQ3_XXS.gguf"
MODEL = {
    "id": "swift-iq3-xxs",
    "repo": "ukisai/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF",
    "revision": "b12fc8f44af7bdf31a3c3309891acb345d3106a2",
    "file": MODEL_PATH.name,
    "bytes": 12532504672,
    "sha256": "1d16444b9164595fb59cdfc1b11c24b1e167c68238d33a0e4d261495a5178f60",
    "local": str(MODEL_PATH),
}


def queue():
    return [{
        "id": "solve-swift-iq3-xxs-" + task.replace("/", "-"),
        "kind": "profile", "model": MODEL["id"], "task": task,
        "workload": "agentic", "targets": [0],
        "configs": [{"context": 102400, "slots": 1, "mode": "tensor",
                     "split": "69,31", "ubatch": 128, "maximum": 3,
                     "chat_template": BARTOWSKI_PREFIX}],
    } for task in LONG_TAIL_TASKS]


def configure_globals():
    h.MODELS[:] = [MODEL]
    h.TASKS[:] = list(dict.fromkeys([FIT_TASK, *LONG_TAIL_TASKS]))


def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(8 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


class SwiftRunner(agentic.AgenticRunner):
    def execute_job(self, job):
        config = {"devices": h.DEVICES, **job["configs"][0]}
        model = self.ensure_model(job["model"])
        fit = self.attempt("agentic-fit-" + job["model"], model, config, 0)
        if fit["status"] != "passed":
            return {"status": "skipped", "model": job["model"], "task": job["task"],
                    "reason": "no_safe_model_fit", "fit_reason": fit.get("reason")}
        config["workload"] = "agentic"
        return {**self.attempt(job["id"], model, config, 0, task=job["task"]), "workload": "agentic"}


def summary(campaign: Path):
    rows = []
    for job in queue():
        path = campaign / ("job-" + job["id"] + ".json")
        if path.exists(): rows.append(h.read_sealed(path))
    passed = [row for row in rows if row.get("status") == "passed" and row.get("probe")]
    probes = [row["probe"] for row in passed]
    metrics = [probe.get("metrics_delta", {}) for probe in probes]
    drafts = sum(metric.get("draft_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics)
    result = {
        "updated_epoch": time.time(), "state": json.loads((campaign / "state.json").read_text())["status"],
        "model": MODEL["id"], "tasks": len(rows),
        "solved": sum(bool(probe.get("task_success")) for probe in probes),
        "timeouts": sum(bool(probe.get("timed_out")) for probe in probes),
        "wall_seconds": sum(probe.get("verified_wall_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
        "output_tokens": sum(metric.get("decode_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics),
        "prefill_seconds": sum(probe.get("prefill_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
        "decode_seconds": sum(probe.get("decode_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
        "peak_context_tokens": max((probe.get("peak_context_tokens", 0) for probe in probes), default=0),
        "draft_acceptance": (sum(metric.get("draft_accepted_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics) / drafts if drafts else None),
    }
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "SUMMARY.json", result)
    lines = ["# Swift IQ3_XXS long-tail campaign", "", json.dumps(result, indent=2), "",
             "| Task | Result | Wall s | Output tokens | Peak context |", "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|"]
    for row in rows:
        probe = row.get("probe", {})
        lines.append("| %s | %s | %.2f | %s | %s |" % (
            row.get("task", ""), "solved" if probe.get("task_success") else row.get("status", ""),
            probe.get("verified_wall_seconds", 0), probe.get("metrics_delta", {}).get("decode_tokens", ""),
            probe.get("peak_context_tokens", "")))
    (campaign / "REPORT.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--campaign", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--assets", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--deadline-epoch", type=float, default=time.time() + 12 * 3600)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    configure_globals()
    if not MODEL_PATH.is_file() or MODEL_PATH.stat().st_size != MODEL["bytes"]:
        raise SystemExit("Swift IQ3_XXS artifact is missing or incomplete")
    if sha256(MODEL_PATH) != MODEL["sha256"]:
        raise SystemExit("Swift IQ3_XXS artifact checksum mismatch")
    campaign = args.campaign.resolve()
    h.initialize(campaign, ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models", profiles=queue(),
                 max_attempts=3, agentic_assets=args.assets.resolve())
    SwiftRunner(campaign, 12, args.deadline_epoch).run()
    print(json.dumps(summary(campaign), indent=2), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


"""Run a separate, resumable Swift-Qwen IQ4_XS long-tail coding campaign."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import agentic_campaign as agentic
from scripts.instruments.chat_templates import BARTOWSKI_PREFIX
from scripts.instruments import hillclimb as h


LONG_TAIL_TASKS = [
    "rust/doubly-linked-list",
    "rust/book-store",
    "rust/react",
    "rust/alphametics",
    "rust/forth",
    "rust/xorcism",
    "rust/nucleotide-codons",
    "javascript/complex-numbers",
]
FIT_TASK = "javascript/affine-cipher"

MODEL_PATH = ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models/swift-iq4-xs/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-IQ4_XS.gguf"
MODEL = {
    "id": "swift-iq4-xs",
    "repo": "ukisai/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF",
    "revision": "b12fc8f44af7bdf31a3c3309891acb345d3106a2",
    "file": MODEL_PATH.name,
    "bytes": 15688288352,
    # HF's X-Linked-Etag for the pinned blob is used as the artifact digest.
    "sha256": "27f5531a012a4bb3c3e379318dc62216f057131d34347498fabfc23186a4ffda",
    "local": str(MODEL_PATH),
}

TUNING_TASKS = ["javascript/affine-cipher", "rust/forth"]
TUNING_SEEDS = [1729, 1901, 2027]
TUNING_BASELINE = {
    "context": 102400, "slots": 1, "mode": "tensor", "split": "69,31",
    "batch": 2048, "ubatch": 128, "maximum": 3, "minimum": 0, "p_min": 0,
    "draft_k": "f16", "draft_v": "f16", "chat_template": BARTOWSKI_PREFIX,
}
TUNING_STAGES = ("baseline", "split", "microbatch", "batch", "interaction", "layer",
                 "mtp", "probability", "minimum", "draft-cache", "swarm-allocation")


def tuning_contract():
    return {
        "name": "swift-iq4-single-agent-and-swarm-v1", "model_sha256": MODEL["sha256"],
        "template_adapter": BARTOWSKI_PREFIX, "target_kv": "q8_0/q8_0",
        "sampling": {"temperature": 1, "top_k": 20, "top_p": 0.95, "min_p": 0.05},
        "seeds": TUNING_SEEDS, "throughput_tasks": TUNING_TASKS,
        "input_targets": [8192, 49152, 98304], "throughput_output_budget": 2048,
        "admission_output_budget": 32, "reasoning_effort": "xhigh",
        "cache_prompt": False, "context_unit": "tokens_per_slot; server_total=context*slots",
        "metrics_scope": "server counters belong to the batch, never individual requests",
        "production_promotion": False, "minimum_gain_fraction": 0.05,
        "single_agent_min_context": 95000, "deep_decode_target_tps": 35,
        "required_before_run": "idle interactive service explicitly stopped; no unowned compute workloads",
        "required_after_run": "release benchmark GPU memory, restore selected skill serving profile",
        "agentic_output_budget": "full client budget; throughput cap does not apply",
    }


def tuning_queue(stage, selected=None, *, repeat=False, splits=None, microbatches=None):
    """Generate one stage; selection of later-stage inputs requires measured evidence."""
    if stage not in TUNING_STAGES:
        raise ValueError("unknown tuning stage")
    if stage not in ("baseline", "split") and selected is None:
        raise ValueError("this stage requires an explicitly selected configuration")
    config = {**TUNING_BASELINE, **(selected or {})}
    if config["context"] != 102400 or config["slots"] != 1:
        raise ValueError("single-agent tuning retains context102400 and one slot")
    if config["chat_template"] != BARTOWSKI_PREFIX:
        raise ValueError("tuning must retain the selected template")
    candidates = []
    if stage == "baseline":
        candidates = [("single-agent-baseline", dict(TUNING_BASELINE))]
    elif stage == "split":
        candidates = [(f"split-{left}", {**TUNING_BASELINE, "split": f"{left},{100-left}"})
                      for left in (65, 67, 71, 73, 75)]
    elif stage == "interaction":
        if not splits or not microbatches or len(set(splits)) != 2 or len(set(microbatches)) != 2:
            raise ValueError("interaction requires two distinct selected splits and microbatches")
        candidates = [(f"cross-{i}-{batch}", {**config, "split": split, "ubatch": batch})
                      for i, split in enumerate(splits) for batch in microbatches]
    elif stage == "layer":
        candidates = [("layer-control", {**config, "mode": "layer"})]
    elif stage == "draft-cache":
        candidates = [("draft-q8-experimental", {**config, "draft_k": "q8_0", "draft_v": "q8_0"})]
    elif stage == "swarm-allocation":
        candidates = [(f"swarm-{slots}-context{context}", {**config, "slots": slots, "context": context})
                      for slots in (2, 3, 4) for context in (32768, 65536, 102400)]
    else:
        key, values = {
            "microbatch": ("ubatch", (64, 256, 512)), "batch": ("batch", (1024, 4096)),
            "mtp": ("maximum", (2, 4, 6)), "probability": ("p_min", (0.5, 0.75)),
            "minimum": ("minimum", (2,)),
        }[stage]
        candidates = [(f"{key}-{str(value).replace('.', '_')}", {**config, key: value})
                      for value in values]
    seeds = TUNING_SEEDS if repeat or stage == "baseline" else TUNING_SEEDS[:1]
    targets = [8192, 49152, 98304] if stage == "baseline" else [49152, 98304]

    def jobs(label, settings, depths, run_seeds):
        return [{"id": f"{stage}-{label}-{task.replace('/', '-')}-{target}-{seed}",
                 "kind": "profile", "model": MODEL["id"], "task": task, "seed": seed,
                 "targets": [target], "configs": [dict(settings)]}
                for target in depths for task in TUNING_TASKS for seed in run_seeds]

    result = []
    if stage != "baseline":
        result += jobs("baseline-before", TUNING_BASELINE, [49152, 98304], seeds)
        if config != TUNING_BASELINE and stage != "swarm-allocation":
            result += jobs("matched-control", config, targets, seeds)
    for label, settings in candidates:
        if stage == "swarm-allocation":
            result.append({"id": label, "kind": "profile", "model": MODEL["id"],
                           "task": TUNING_TASKS[0], "seed": TUNING_SEEDS[0],
                           "targets": [0], "configs": [settings]})
        else:
            result += jobs(label, settings, targets, seeds)
    if stage != "baseline":
        result += jobs("baseline-after", TUNING_BASELINE, [49152, 98304], seeds)
    return result


def tuning_main(argv):
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Prepare Swift tuning stages without launching inference")
    parser.add_argument("stage", choices=TUNING_STAGES)
    parser.add_argument("--out", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--selected-config", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument("--repeat", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--splits", nargs=2)
    parser.add_argument("--microbatches", nargs=2, type=int)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    selected = json.loads(args.selected_config.read_text()) if args.selected_config else None
    jobs = tuning_queue(args.stage, selected, repeat=args.repeat,
                        splits=args.splits, microbatches=args.microbatches)
    h.MODELS[:] = [MODEL]
    h.TASKS[:] = TUNING_TASKS
    h.validate_profiles(jobs)
    # Queue files are planning artifacts, never mutable running manifests.
    with args.out.open("x") as stream:
        json.dump(jobs, stream, indent=2, allow_nan=False)
        stream.write("\n")
    print(args.out)


def queue():
    return [{
        "id": "solve-swift-iq4-xs-" + task.replace("/", "-"),
        "kind": "profile",
        "model": MODEL["id"],
        "task": task,
        "workload": "agentic",
        "targets": [0],
        "configs": [{"context": 102400, "slots": 1, "mode": "tensor",
                     "split": "69,31", "ubatch": 128, "maximum": 3,
                     "chat_template": BARTOWSKI_PREFIX}],
    } for task in LONG_TAIL_TASKS]


def configure_globals():
    # h.initialize validates profile model IDs against this process-local registry.
    h.MODELS[:] = [MODEL]
    # The generic fit probe uses this small task before the long-tail queue starts.
    h.TASKS[:] = list(dict.fromkeys([FIT_TASK, *LONG_TAIL_TASKS]))


def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(8 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def summary(campaign: Path):
    rows = []
    for job in queue():
        path = campaign / ("job-" + job["id"] + ".json")
        if path.exists():
            rows.append(h.read_sealed(path))
    passed = [row for row in rows if row.get("status") == "passed" and row.get("probe")]
    probes = [row["probe"] for row in passed]
    metrics = [probe.get("metrics_delta", {}) for probe in probes]
    result = {
        "updated_epoch": time.time(),
        "state": json.loads((campaign / "state.json").read_text())["status"],
        "model": MODEL["id"],
        "tasks": len(rows),
        "solved": sum(bool(probe.get("task_success")) for probe in probes),
        "timeouts": sum(bool(probe.get("timed_out")) for probe in probes),
        "wall_seconds": sum(probe.get("verified_wall_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
        "output_tokens": sum(metric.get("decode_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics),
        "prefill_seconds": sum(probe.get("prefill_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
        "decode_seconds": sum(probe.get("decode_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
        "peak_context_tokens": max((probe.get("peak_context_tokens", 0) for probe in probes), default=0),
        "draft_acceptance": (
            sum(metric.get("draft_accepted_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics)
            / sum(metric.get("draft_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics)
            if sum(metric.get("draft_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics) else None
        ),
    }
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "SUMMARY.json", result)
    lines = ["# Swift-Qwen long-tail campaign", "", json.dumps(result, indent=2), "", "| Task | Result | Wall s | Output tokens | Peak context |", "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|"]
    for row in rows:
        probe = row.get("probe", {})
        lines.append("| %s | %s | %.2f | %s | %s |" % (
            row.get("task", ""), "solved" if probe.get("task_success") else row.get("status", ""),
            probe.get("verified_wall_seconds", 0), probe.get("metrics_delta", {}).get("decode_tokens", ""),
            probe.get("peak_context_tokens", "")))
    (campaign / "REPORT.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--campaign", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--assets", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--deadline-epoch", type=float, default=time.time() + 12 * 3600)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    configure_globals()
    if not MODEL_PATH.is_file() or MODEL_PATH.stat().st_size != MODEL["bytes"]:
        raise SystemExit("Swift artifact is missing or incomplete")
    if sha256(MODEL_PATH) != MODEL["sha256"]:
        raise SystemExit("Swift artifact checksum mismatch")
    campaign = args.campaign.resolve()
    h.initialize(campaign, ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models", profiles=queue(),
                 max_attempts=3, agentic_assets=args.assets.resolve())
    runner = agentic.AgenticRunner(campaign, 12, args.deadline_epoch)
    runner.run()
    result = summary(campaign)
    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys
    if len(sys.argv) > 1 and sys.argv[1] == "tuning-plan":
        tuning_main(sys.argv[2:])
    else:
        main()


"""Run a separate, resumable Swift Q4_K_M long-tail coding campaign."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import time

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import agentic_campaign as agentic
from scripts.instruments.chat_templates import BARTOWSKI_PREFIX
from scripts.instruments import hillclimb as h


LONG_TAIL_TASKS = [
    "rust/doubly-linked-list",
    "rust/book-store",
    "rust/react",
    "rust/alphametics",
    "rust/forth",
    "rust/xorcism",
    "rust/nucleotide-codons",
    "javascript/complex-numbers",
]
FIT_TASK = "javascript/affine-cipher"
MODEL_PATH = ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models/swift-q4-m/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-Q4_K_M.gguf"
MODEL = {
    "id": "swift-q4-m",
    "repo": "ukisai/Swift-Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF",
    "revision": "b12fc8f44af7bdf31a3c3309891acb345d3106a2",
    "file": MODEL_PATH.name,
    "bytes": 18024380576,
    "sha256": "ad5811e291431bd0de1cec0c4004a5eac98daee9850882edac69a823209e88ab",
    "local": str(MODEL_PATH),
}


def queue(context=102400, split="69,31", ubatch=64):
    return [{
        "id": "solve-swift-q4-m-" + task.replace("/", "-"),
        "kind": "profile",
        "model": MODEL["id"],
        "task": task,
        "workload": "agentic",
        "targets": [0],
        "configs": [{"context": context, "slots": 1, "mode": "tensor",
                     "split": split, "ubatch": ubatch, "maximum": 3,
                     "chat_template": BARTOWSKI_PREFIX}],
    } for task in LONG_TAIL_TASKS]


def normalized_context(value: int) -> int:
    # llama-server rounds slot contexts down to a 2048-token graph multiple.
    context = value - value % 2048
    if context < 2048:
        raise ValueError("context rounds below one graph block")
    return context


def configure_globals():
    h.MODELS[:] = [MODEL]
    h.TASKS[:] = list(dict.fromkeys([FIT_TASK, *LONG_TAIL_TASKS]))


def sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as stream:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: stream.read(8 * 1024 * 1024), b""):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def summary(campaign: Path, context=102400, split="69,31", ubatch=64):
    rows = []
    for job in queue(context, split, ubatch):
        path = campaign / ("job-" + job["id"] + ".json")
        if path.exists():
            rows.append(h.read_sealed(path))
    passed = [row for row in rows if row.get("status") == "passed" and row.get("probe")]
    probes = [row["probe"] for row in passed]
    metrics = [probe.get("metrics_delta", {}) for probe in probes]
    draft_tokens = sum(metric.get("draft_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics)
    result = {
        "updated_epoch": time.time(),
        "state": json.loads((campaign / "state.json").read_text())["status"],
        "model": MODEL["id"],
        "tasks": len(rows),
        "solved": sum(bool(probe.get("task_success")) for probe in probes),
        "timeouts": sum(bool(probe.get("timed_out")) for probe in probes),
        "wall_seconds": sum(probe.get("verified_wall_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
        "output_tokens": sum(metric.get("decode_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics),
        "prefill_seconds": sum(probe.get("prefill_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
        "decode_seconds": sum(probe.get("decode_seconds", 0) for probe in probes),
        "peak_context_tokens": max((probe.get("peak_context_tokens", 0) for probe in probes), default=0),
        "draft_acceptance": (sum(metric.get("draft_accepted_tokens", 0) for metric in metrics) / draft_tokens
                             if draft_tokens else None),
    }
    h.atomic_json(campaign / "SUMMARY.json", result)
    lines = ["# Swift-Qwen Q4_K_M long-tail campaign", "", json.dumps(result, indent=2), "",
             "| Task | Result | Wall s | Output tokens | Peak context |", "|---|---|---:|---:|---:|"]
    for row in rows:
        probe = row.get("probe", {})
        lines.append("| %s | %s | %.2f | %s | %s |" % (
            row.get("task", ""), "solved" if probe.get("task_success") else row.get("status", ""),
            probe.get("verified_wall_seconds", 0), probe.get("metrics_delta", {}).get("decode_tokens", ""),
            probe.get("peak_context_tokens", "")))
    (campaign / "REPORT.md").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
    return result


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--campaign", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--assets", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--context", type=int, default=102400)
    parser.add_argument("--split", default="69,31")
    parser.add_argument("--ubatch", type=int, default=64)
    parser.add_argument("--reserve", type=int, default=256)
    parser.add_argument("--deadline-epoch", type=float, default=time.time() + 12 * 3600)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    if args.context < 1 or args.context > 262144:
        raise SystemExit("context must be between 1 and 262144")
    if args.ubatch < 1 or args.ubatch > 4096:
        raise SystemExit("ubatch must be between 1 and 4096")
    if args.reserve < 0:
        raise SystemExit("reserve must be non-negative")
    context = normalized_context(args.context)
    try:
        split_values = [float(value) for value in args.split.split(",")]
    except ValueError:
        raise SystemExit("split must be two comma-separated numbers")
    if len(split_values) != 2 or any(value <= 0 for value in split_values):
        raise SystemExit("split must be two positive comma-separated numbers")
    configure_globals()
    if not MODEL_PATH.is_file() or MODEL_PATH.stat().st_size != MODEL["bytes"]:
        raise SystemExit("Swift Q4_K_M artifact is missing or incomplete")
    if sha256(MODEL_PATH) != MODEL["sha256"]:
        raise SystemExit("Swift Q4_K_M artifact checksum mismatch")
    campaign = args.campaign.resolve()
    # Reserve is campaign-scoped; ordinary campaigns retain the 512/256 MiB defaults.
    h.initialize(campaign, ROOT / ".cache/hillclimb-models", profiles=queue(context, args.split, args.ubatch),
                 max_attempts=3, agentic_assets=args.assets.resolve(), vram_reserves=(args.reserve, args.reserve))
    agentic.AgenticRunner(campaign, 12, args.deadline_epoch).run()
    print(json.dumps(summary(campaign, context, args.split, args.ubatch), indent=2), flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


from collections import Counter
import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from scripts.instruments import agentic_campaign as a
from scripts.instruments import hillclimb as h


class AgenticQueueTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_eight_tasks_cover_control_and_five_quants_task_first(self):
        jobs = h.validate_profiles(a.task_queue())
        self.assertEqual(len(jobs), 48)
        self.assertEqual(Counter(job["model"] for job in jobs), dict.fromkeys(a.MODEL_ORDER, 8))
        self.assertEqual([job["model"] for job in jobs[:6]], a.MODEL_ORDER)
        self.assertEqual({job["task"] for job in jobs[:6]}, {a.TASK_ORDER[0]})
        for job in jobs:
            self.assertEqual(job["workload"], "agentic")
            self.assertEqual(job["configs"][0]["slots"], 1)
            self.assertEqual(job["configs"][0]["maximum"], 3)
            self.assertEqual(job["configs"][0]["context"], 65536 if job["model"] == "iq3-control" else 102400)

    def test_predecessor_safety_stop_does_not_admit_task_stage(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            campaign, predecessor = Path(temp) / "next", Path(temp) / "previous"
            campaign.mkdir()
            predecessor.mkdir()
            h.atomic_json(predecessor / "state.json", {"status": "stopped", "reason": "thermal_limit", "completed": {}})
            self.assertFalse(a.wait_for_predecessor(campaign, predecessor, time.time() + 60))
            self.assertEqual(json.loads((campaign / "stage-status.json").read_text())["phase"], "blocked")
            h.atomic_json(predecessor / "state.json", {"status": "complete", "completed": {}})
            self.assertTrue(a.wait_for_predecessor(campaign, predecessor, time.time() + 60))

    def test_control_is_single_gpu_and_failed_fit_skips_coding(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            h.sealed_json(root / "manifest.json", {"port": 8081, "queue": a.task_queue(), "max_attempts": 3})
            h.atomic_json(root / "state.json", {"status": "prepared", "completed": {}, "owned": []})
            runner = a.AgenticRunner(root, 1)
            with patch.object(runner, "ensure_model", return_value={"id": "iq3-control"}), \
                    patch.object(runner, "attempt", return_value={"status": "failed", "reason": "oom"}) as attempt:
                result = runner.execute_job(a.task_queue()[0])
            self.assertEqual(result["status"], "skipped")
            self.assertEqual(attempt.call_count, 1)
            config = attempt.call_args.args[2]
            self.assertEqual(config["devices"], [h.GPU0])
            self.assertEqual(config["mode"], "layer")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import http.server
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import threading
import unittest

from scripts.instruments import agentic_probe as probe
from scripts.instruments import isolated_exec


@unittest.skipUnless(os.environ.get("QWEN38_ISOLATION_TESTS") == "1", "requires host namespaces and prepared tool assets")
class IsolatedTrustedGraderTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_only_trusted_tests_count_and_starter_doctests_cannot_disappear(self):
        assets = json.loads(Path(".cache/agentic-assets-20260913/tools.json").read_text())
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="qwen-grader-regression-") as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            source = root / "sources/fixture"
            (source / "src").mkdir(parents=True)
            (source / "tests").mkdir()
            cargo = '[package]\nname="fixture"\nversion="0.1.0"\nedition="2021"\n'
            (source / "Cargo.toml").write_text(cargo)
            (source / "src/lib.rs").write_text(
                '/// ```compile_fail\n/// let _: fixture::Input = fixture::compute();\n/// ```\n'
                'pub struct Input;\npub struct Compute;\n'
                'pub fn compute() -> Compute { Compute }\npub fn answer() -> u8 { 42 }\n')
            trusted = '#[test]\n#[ignore]\nfn answer() { assert_eq!(fixture::answer(), 42); }\n'
            (source / "tests/main.rs").write_text(trusted)
            task = {"source_dir": "sources/fixture", "language": "rust", "test_paths": ["tests/main.rs"]}
            for name, code, expected in (
                ("valid", 'pub struct Input; pub struct Compute; pub fn compute() -> Compute { Compute }', True),
                ("bad_types", 'pub struct Input; pub type Compute = Input; pub fn compute() -> Compute { Input }', False),
            ):
                with self.subTest(name=name):
                    work = root / name
                    (work / "src").mkdir(parents=True)
                    (work / "tests").mkdir()
                    (work / "Cargo.toml").write_text(cargo)
                    (work / "tests/main.rs").write_text(trusted)
                    (work / "src/lib.rs").write_text(code + '\n'
                        '/// ```\n/// panic!("candidate doctest must not affect grading");\n/// ```\n'
                        'pub fn answer() -> u8 { 42 }\n'
                        '#[cfg(test)] mod own { #[test] fn fails() { panic!("not trusted"); } }\n')
                    grade = probe.test_suite(task, work, assets, 60, asset_dir=root)
                    self.assertEqual(grade["returncode"] == 0, expected, grade["output"])
                    self.assertEqual(grade["passed"], 2 if expected else 1, grade["output"])
                    self.assertEqual(grade["failed"], 0 if expected else 1, grade["output"])
                    self.assertEqual(grade["ignored"], 0)
                    self.assertTrue(grade["starter_doctests"])


@unittest.skipUnless(os.environ.get("QWEN38_ISOLATION_TESTS") == "1", "requires host namespaces and prepared tool assets")
class IsolatedClientTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_codex_tool_roundtrip_through_only_the_inference_bridge(self):
        assets = json.loads(Path(".cache/agentic-assets-20260913/tools.json").read_text())
        requests = []
        class Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def log_message(self, *args):
                pass

            def do_GET(self):
                self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(b'{"data":[]}')

            def do_POST(self):
                request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"])))
                requests.append(request)
                if len(requests) == 1:
                    choices = []
                    for tool in request.get("tools", []):
                        if tool.get("type") == "namespace":
                            choices.extend((child, tool["name"]) for child in tool["tools"])
                        else:
                            choices.append((tool, None))
                    tool, namespace = next((tool, namespace) for tool, namespace in choices
                                           if tool.get("name") in ("exec_command", "shell"))
                    command = "test ! -e /home/xanewok/repos/qwen38-eval && test ! -e /work/.meta && echo ISOLATION_OK"
                    arguments = {"cmd": command} if tool["name"] == "exec_command" else {"command": ["sh", "-c", command]}
                    item = {"type": "function_call", "call_id": "isolation-test", "name": tool["name"],
                            "arguments": json.dumps(arguments)}
                    if namespace:
                        item["namespace"] = namespace
                else:
                    item = {"type": "message", "role": "assistant", "id": "done",
                            "content": [{"type": "output_text", "text": "Isolation check complete."}]}
                response_id = f"mock-{len(requests)}"
                events = [{"type": "response.created", "response": {"id": response_id}},
                          {"type": "response.output_item.done", "item": item},
                          {"type": "response.completed", "response": {"id": response_id,
                           "usage": {"input_tokens": 10, "output_tokens": 10, "total_tokens": 20}}}]
                self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header("Content-Type", "text/event-stream")
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write("".join("data: " + json.dumps(event) + "\n\n" for event in events).encode())

        server = http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
        thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        try:
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="qwen-isolation-test-") as temp:
                root = Path(temp)
                work, client_home = root / "work", root / "client"
                work.mkdir()
                client_home.mkdir()
                (client_home / "config.toml").write_text(probe.client_config(102400))
                with isolated_exec.inference_bridge(root / "bridge.sock", server.server_port):
                    result = isolated_exec.run(work, assets,
                        ["/runner/codex", "exec", "--skip-git-repo-check", "--json", "-"],
                        60, bridge=root / "bridge.sock", client_home=client_home,
                        input_text="Check the isolated workspace using a shell command.")
                self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr + result.stdout)
                self.assertGreaterEqual(len(requests), 2, result.stderr + result.stdout)
                self.assertEqual(requests[0].get("reasoning", {}).get("effort"), "xhigh")
                self.assertNotEqual(requests[0].get("max_output_tokens"), 2048)
                outputs = [item for request in requests[1:] for item in request.get("input", [])
                           if item.get("type") == "function_call_output"]
                self.assertTrue(any("ISOLATION_OK" in str(item.get("output")) for item in outputs), outputs)
                denied = isolated_exec.run(work, assets, ["python3", "-c",
                    "import socket; s=socket.socket(); s.settimeout(.2); "
                    "assert s.connect_ex(('1.1.1.1',443)) != 0; print('external-network-denied')"], 10)
                self.assertEqual(denied.returncode, 0, denied.stderr)
        finally:
            server.shutdown()
            server.server_close()
            thread.join(timeout=2)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import tomllib
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch
import subprocess

from scripts.instruments import agentic_probe as p
from scripts.instruments import isolated_exec as sandbox


class TrustedGraderTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_rust_targets_include_all_trusted_files_but_no_candidate_tests(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            source, work = root / "sources/exercise", root / "work"
            (source / "tests").mkdir(parents=True)
            (source / "src").mkdir()
            work.mkdir()
            (source / "tests/main.rs").write_text("#[test] fn trusted() {}")
            (source / "tests/extra.rs").write_text("#[test] fn extra() {}")
            (source / "src/lib.rs").write_text("pub fn f() {}")
            task = {"source_dir": "sources/exercise", "language": "rust", "test_paths": ["tests/main.rs"]}
            result = subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 0, "test result: ok. 2 passed; 0 failed; 0 ignored;", "")
            with patch.object(p.isolated_exec, "run", return_value=result) as run:
                grade = p.test_suite(task, work, {}, 30, asset_dir=root)
            self.assertEqual(run.call_args.args[2], ["/rust/bin/cargo", "test", "--offline",
                             "--test", "extra", "--test", "main", "--", "--include-ignored"])
            self.assertEqual(run.call_args.kwargs["readonly"], ["tests/extra.rs", "tests/main.rs"])
            self.assertEqual(grade["passed"], 2)
            self.assertEqual(grade["test_scope"], p.TEST_SCOPE)

    def test_starter_docs_are_not_read_from_the_candidate(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            (root / "src").mkdir()
            (root / "tests").mkdir()
            (root / "tests/main.rs").write_text("")
            (root / "src/lib.rs").write_text("/// ```compile_fail\n/// trusted();\n/// ```\npub struct X;")
            tests, docs = p.rust_test_plan({"source_dir": ".", "test_paths": ["tests/main.rs"]}, root)
            self.assertEqual(tests, ["tests/main.rs"])
            self.assertEqual(docs, "```compile_fail\ntrusted();\n```")

    def test_enable_all_supplied_javascript_test_forms(self):
        source = "  xtest('a', f);\n  xit('b', f);\n  test.skip('c', f);\ndescribe.skip.each(rows)(f);\nconst s = 'test.skip(';\n"
        enabled = p.enable_js_tests(source)
        self.assertEqual(enabled, "  test('a', f);\n  it('b', f);\n  test('c', f);\ndescribe.each(rows)(f);\nconst s = 'test.skip(';\n")
        self.assertEqual(p.enable_js_tests(enabled), enabled)

    def test_fresh_server_peak_does_not_double_count_output(self):
        self.assertEqual(p.fresh_context_peak({'n_tokens_max': 0}, {'n_tokens_max': 60459.0}, 65536), 60459)
        for before, after in [({'n_tokens_max': 1}, {'n_tokens_max': 2}),
                              ({'n_tokens_max': 0}, {'n_tokens_max': 80805}),
                              ({'n_tokens_max': 0}, {'n_tokens_max': float('nan')}),
                              ({'n_tokens_max': 0}, {})]:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                p.fresh_context_peak(before, after, 65536)

    def test_cargo_roundtrip_and_only_dependency_changes_enter_grader(self):
        trusted = {"package": {"name": "exercise", "edition": "2021"},
                   "dependencies": {"rand": "0.8"}}
        self.assertEqual(tomllib.loads(p.dump_toml(trusted)), trusted)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            work = Path(temp) / "work"
            grade = Path(temp) / "grade"
            work.mkdir()
            grade.mkdir()
            (work / "Cargo.toml").write_text(p.dump_toml({**trusted, "package": {
                **trusted["package"], "autotests": False}, "lib": {"test": False},
                "dependencies": {"rand": {"version": "0.8", "features": ["std"]}}}))
            (grade / "Cargo.toml").write_text(p.dump_toml(trusted))
            ignored = p.transfer_solution({"solution_path": ["Cargo.toml"]}, work, grade)
            result = tomllib.loads((grade / "Cargo.toml").read_text())
            self.assertEqual(result["package"], trusted["package"])
            self.assertNotIn("lib", result)
            self.assertEqual(result["dependencies"]["rand"]["version"], "0.8")
            self.assertEqual(set(ignored), {"package", "lib"})

    def test_modified_tests_and_package_scripts_are_not_copied(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            work, grade = Path(temp) / "work", Path(temp) / "grade"
            work.mkdir()
            grade.mkdir()
            (work / "solution.js").write_text("implementation")
            (work / "tests.js").write_text("weakened tests")
            (work / "package.json").write_text('{"scripts":{"test":"true"}}')
            (grade / "tests.js").write_text("trusted tests")
            (grade / "package.json").write_text("trusted package")
            p.transfer_solution({"solution_path": ["solution.js"]}, work, grade)
            self.assertEqual((grade / "solution.js").read_text(), "implementation")
            self.assertEqual((grade / "tests.js").read_text(), "trusted tests")
            self.assertEqual((grade / "package.json").read_text(), "trusted package")

    def test_symlinks_and_local_dependency_escapes_are_rejected(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            work, grade = Path(temp) / "work", Path(temp) / "grade"
            work.mkdir()
            grade.mkdir()
            (work / "solution.js").symlink_to("/etc/passwd")
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "escapes"):
                p.transfer_solution({"solution_path": ["solution.js"]}, work, grade)
            (work / "Cargo.toml").write_text('[dependencies]\nanswer = {path="/oracle"}\n')
            (grade / "Cargo.toml").write_text('[package]\nname="exercise"\n')
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "cached registry"):
                p.transfer_solution({"solution_path": ["Cargo.toml"]}, work, grade)

    def test_client_context_matches_server_without_probe_output_cap(self):
        for context, compact in ((65536, 58000), (102400, 86016)):
            config = tomllib.loads(p.client_config(context))
            self.assertEqual(config["model_context_window"], context)
            self.assertEqual(config["model_auto_compact_token_limit"], compact)
            self.assertLess(compact, context)
            self.assertEqual(config["web_search"], "disabled")
            self.assertNotIn("max_tokens", config)
            self.assertFalse(config["model_providers"]["benchmark"]["requires_openai_auth"])

    def test_outer_sandbox_has_no_host_home_or_network_mount(self):
        assets = {key: "/fake/" + key for key in
                  ("node_root", "rust_root", "cargo_registry", "node_modules", "codex")}
        args = sandbox.sandbox_command("/workdir", assets, ["true"])
        self.assertIn("--unshare-all", args)
        self.assertIn("--clearenv", args)
        self.assertNotIn("--share-net", args)
        self.assertNotIn("/home/xanewok", args)
        self.assertNotIn("/", args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import copy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
import subprocess
from unittest.mock import patch

from scripts.instruments import agentic_campaign as a, confirmed_agentic as c, hillclimb as h
from tests.test_hillclimb import safe_sample


class RecoveryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.path = Path(self.temp.name)
        for name in ('snapshot', 'attempts', 'artifacts'):
            (self.path / name).mkdir()
        h.sealed_json(self.path / 'manifest.json', {'port': 8081, 'queue': [], 'max_attempts': 3})
        h.atomic_json(self.path / 'state.json', {'completed': {}, 'status': 'prepared', 'owned': []})
        self.runner = c.ConfirmedRunner(self.path, time.time() + 3600, {'models': {}})
        self.runner.last_report = time.monotonic()

    def failure(self, finished=1000):
        attempt = self.path / 'attempts/pressure-01'
        attempt.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        before = safe_sample()
        before['gpus'][h.GPU0]['memory.free'] = 15000
        (attempt / 'telemetry.jsonl').write_text(json.dumps(before) + '\n')
        return {'reason': 'vram_reserve: ' + h.GPU0, 'config': {'devices': [h.GPU0]},
                'attempt': 'attempts/pressure-01', 'finished_at': finished,
                'minimum_free_mib': {h.GPU0: 400}}

    def test_actual_timeout_signature_stops_without_retry_despite_warning(self):
        def reject(_):
            (self.runner.current_dir / 'server.log').write_text(
                "unsupported Responses tool type 'namespace' skipped\n"
                "CUDA error: the launch timed out and was terminated\n")
            raise h.RejectArm('probe_failed')
        with patch.object(h, 'endpoint_free', side_effect=reject) as launch, \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'poll'), patch.object(self.runner, 'event'):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'gpu_fault'):
                self.runner.attempt('fault', {'id': 'test'}, {}, 0)
        self.assertEqual(launch.call_count, 1)
        result = h.read_sealed(next((self.path / 'attempts').glob('*/result.json')))
        self.assertEqual(result['reason'], 'gpu_fault')

    def test_pressure_retries_are_bounded_and_persist_across_resume(self):
        with patch.object(h, 'endpoint_free', side_effect=h.RejectArm('vram_reserve: ' + h.GPU0)) as launch, \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'poll'), patch.object(self.runner, 'sleep'), \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'event'), patch.object(self.runner, 'wait_before_retry') as wait:
            result = self.runner.attempt('pressure', {'id': 'test'}, {}, 0)
            self.assertEqual(launch.call_count, 3)
            self.assertEqual(wait.call_count, 2)
            resumed = c.ConfirmedRunner(self.path, time.time() + 3600, {'models': {}})
            self.assertEqual(resumed.attempt('pressure', {'id': 'test'}, {}, 0), result)
            self.assertEqual(launch.call_count, 3)

    def test_exhausted_pressure_keeps_job_pending_and_does_not_skip_next_tasks(self):
        jobs = c.suite_queue(['javascript/affine-cipher'])
        self.runner.manifest['queue'] = jobs
        row = {'status': 'skipped', 'reason': 'no_safe_model_fit', 'fit_reason': 'oom'}
        with patch.object(self.runner, 'acquire'), patch.object(self.runner, 'poll'), \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'report'), patch.object(self.runner, 'event'), \
                patch.object(a.AgenticRunner, 'execute_job', return_value=row) as execute:
            self.runner.run()
        self.assertEqual(execute.call_count, 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.runner.state['status'], 'stopped')
        self.assertEqual(self.runner.state['completed'], {})
        self.assertFalse(list(self.path.glob('job-*.json')))

    def test_wait_requires_stable_headroom_and_preserves_deadline(self):
        result = self.failure()
        clock = [1005.0]
        def poll(reserves=True):
            self.assertFalse(reserves)
            sample = safe_sample()
            sample['gpus'][h.GPU0]['memory.free'] = 15300 if clock[0] >= 1008 else 15100
            self.runner.samples.append(sample)
        def sleep(seconds):
            clock[0] += seconds
        with patch.object(c.time, 'time', side_effect=lambda: clock[0]), \
                patch.object(c.time, 'monotonic', side_effect=lambda: clock[0]), \
                patch.object(c.time, 'sleep', side_effect=sleep), patch.object(self.runner, 'poll', side_effect=poll), \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'event') as event, patch.object(self.runner, 'report'):
            self.runner.wait_before_retry('one', result, 5)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(clock[0], 1018)
        started = next(x for x in event.call_args_list if x.args[0] == 'memory_wait')
        self.assertEqual(started.kwargs['required_free_mib'], {h.GPU0: 15240})
        self.assertEqual(started.kwargs['until_epoch'], 1900)
        self.assertNotIn('resource_wait', self.runner.state)

    def test_expired_recovery_is_not_extended_by_restart(self):
        result = self.failure(time.time() - 901)
        with patch.object(self.runner, 'poll'), patch.object(self.runner, 'event'), patch.object(self.runner, 'report'):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'memory_pressure_unresolved'):
                self.runner.wait_before_retry('one', result, 0)

    def test_wait_still_honors_stop_deadline_and_thermal_limits(self):
        result = self.failure(time.time())
        for condition in ('stop', 'deadline', 'thermal'):
            with self.subTest(condition=condition):
                self.runner.deadline = 0 if condition == 'deadline' else time.monotonic() + 100
                self.runner.stopping = condition == 'stop'
                self.runner.last_sample = 0
                sample = safe_sample()
                if condition == 'thermal':
                    sample['gpus'][h.GPU0]['temperature.gpu'] = 80
                with patch.object(h, 'telemetry', return_value=sample), patch.object(self.runner, 'event'), \
                        patch.object(self.runner, 'report'):
                    with self.assertRaises(h.StopCampaign):
                        self.runner.wait_before_retry('one', result, 0)

    def test_memory_wait_refuses_live_children(self):
        self.runner.children = [object()]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'stopped_children'):
            self.runner.wait_before_retry('one', {'reason': 'oom'}, 0)


class WatchdogRecoveryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    failure = RecoveryTests.failure

    def setUp(self):
        RecoveryTests.setUp(self)
        self.runner.manifest['continuation'] = {'watchdog_recovery': c.WATCHDOG_RECOVERY}
        h.sealed_json(self.path / 'manifest.json', self.runner.manifest)
        self.runner.watchdog_policy = c.WATCHDOG_RECOVERY

    def test_only_the_observed_timeout_signature_is_retryable(self):
        timeout = 'CUDA error: the launch timed out and was terminated\n'
        self.assertTrue(self.runner.retryable_gpu_fault(timeout))
        self.assertTrue(self.runner.retryable_gpu_fault(timeout + 'NVRM: Xid (PCI:0000:08:00): 8, pid=42'))
        for text in ('illegal memory access', 'device-side assert', 'unspecified launch failure',
                     'fallen off the bus', 'NVRM: Xid (PCI:0000:08:00): 79, pid=42'):
            self.assertFalse(self.runner.retryable_gpu_fault(timeout + text))
        self.assertFalse(self.runner.retryable_gpu_fault('NVRM: Xid: 8'))
        self.assertFalse(h.Runner.retryable_gpu_fault(self.runner, timeout))

    def test_kernel_check_requires_xid8_and_rejects_other_or_unreadable_faults(self):
        good = 'NVRM: Xid (PCI:0000:08:00): 8, pid=42\n'
        for output, code, accepted in ((good, 0, True), (good + 'NVRM: Xid: 31', 0, False),
                                       ('', 1, False), (good, 2, False)):
            with self.subTest(output=output, code=code), patch.object(c.subprocess, 'run',
                    return_value=subprocess.CompletedProcess([], code, output, '')):
                if accepted:
                    self.assertEqual(c.watchdog_kernel_check(1000), good)
                else:
                    with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'kernel_health'):
                        c.watchdog_kernel_check(1000)
        with patch.object(c.subprocess, 'run', side_effect=subprocess.TimeoutExpired('journalctl', 10)):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'kernel_health'):
                c.watchdog_kernel_check(1000)

    def test_three_retries_are_persistent_and_exhaustion_keeps_job_pending(self):
        def reject(_):
            (self.runner.current_dir / 'server.log').write_text(
                'unsupported Responses tool type namespace skipped\n'
                'CUDA error: the launch timed out and was terminated\n')
            raise h.RejectArm('probe_failed')
        with patch.object(h, 'endpoint_free', side_effect=reject) as launch, \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'poll'), patch.object(self.runner, 'sleep'), \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'event'), patch.object(self.runner, 'wait_before_retry') as wait:
            result = self.runner.attempt('watchdog', {'id': 'test'}, {}, 0)
        self.assertEqual(result['reason'], 'cuda_watchdog')
        self.assertEqual(launch.call_count, 4)
        self.assertEqual(wait.call_count, 3)
        self.assertEqual([self.runner.retry_backoff([result] * n, n) for n in (1, 2, 3)], [20, 40, 60])
        resumed = c.ConfirmedRunner(self.path, time.time() + 3600, {'models': {}})
        with patch.object(h, 'endpoint_free') as launch:
            self.assertEqual(resumed.attempt('watchdog', {'id': 'test'}, {}, 0), result)
            launch.assert_not_called()
        with patch.object(a.AgenticRunner, 'execute_job', return_value=result):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'watchdog_retries_exhausted'):
                resumed.checked_execute({'id': 'watchdog'})
        self.assertEqual(resumed.state['completed'], {})

    def test_partial_retry_does_not_reset_the_retry_budget(self):
        failure = {'reason': 'cuda_watchdog'}
        self.assertEqual(self.runner.retry_count([failure], 4), 4)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(self.runner.retry_count([failure], 4), self.runner.retry_limit('cuda_watchdog'))
        self.assertEqual(self.runner.retry_count([{'reason': 'oom'}], 4), 1)

    def test_success_after_watchdog_uses_a_new_attempt_and_is_not_repeated(self):
        self.runner.manifest['binary'] = '/fake/server'
        model = {'id': 'test', 'path': '/fake/model', 'sha256': 'test'}
        config = {'devices': [h.GPU0], 'context': 65536, 'slots': 1, 'maximum': 3,
                  'mode': 'layer', 'split': '1', 'ubatch': 512, 'workload': 'agentic'}
        visited = []
        def endpoint(_):
            visited.append(self.runner.current_dir)
            if len(visited) == 1:
                (self.runner.current_dir / 'server.log').write_text('CUDA error: the launch timed out and was terminated')
                raise h.RejectArm('gpu_fault')
        def api(url, path, **kwargs):
            return {'status': 'ok'} if path == '/health' else {
                'total_slots': 1, 'default_generation_settings': {'n_ctx': 65536}, 'model_path': '/fake/model'}
        def run_probe(config, target, task, seed, out):
            h.atomic_json(out / 'probe.json', {'complete': True, 'mtp_live': True, 'depth_verified': True,
                'overlap_verified': True, 'task_success': True, 'metrics_delta': {'decode_tps': 42}})
            return 0
        from unittest.mock import Mock
        server = Mock()
        server.poll.return_value = None
        with patch.object(h, 'endpoint_free', side_effect=endpoint) as launch, \
                patch.object(h, 'api', side_effect=api), patch.object(self.runner, 'spawn', return_value=server), \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'poll'), patch.object(self.runner, 'sleep'), patch.object(self.runner, 'event'), \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'wait_before_retry'), patch.object(self.runner, 'run_probe', side_effect=run_probe):
            result = self.runner.attempt('watchdog', model, config, 0)
            self.assertEqual(result['status'], 'passed')
            self.assertEqual(launch.call_count, 2)
            self.assertNotEqual(visited[0], visited[1])
            self.assertEqual(h.read_sealed(visited[0] / 'result.json')['reason'], 'cuda_watchdog')
            self.assertEqual(self.runner.attempt('watchdog', model, config, 0), result)
            self.assertEqual(launch.call_count, 2)

    def test_a_different_gpu_fault_stays_fatal_even_on_resume(self):
        def reject(_):
            (self.runner.current_dir / 'server.log').write_text('CUDA illegal memory access')
            raise h.RejectArm('probe_failed')
        with patch.object(h, 'endpoint_free', side_effect=reject), \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'poll'), patch.object(self.runner, 'event'):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'gpu_fault'):
                self.runner.attempt('fatal', {'id': 'test'}, {}, 0)
        with patch.object(h, 'endpoint_free') as launch:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'gpu_fault'):
                self.runner.attempt('fatal', {'id': 'test'}, {}, 0)
