            launch.assert_not_called()

    def watchdog_failure(self, finished):
        result = self.failure(finished)
        result.update(reason='cuda_watchdog', elapsed_seconds=400)
        return result

    def test_recovery_waits_for_stable_released_memory_and_backoff(self):
        result = self.watchdog_failure(1000)
        clock = [1005.0]
        def poll(reserves=True):
            self.assertFalse(reserves)
            sample = safe_sample()
            sample['gpus'][h.GPU0]['memory.free'] = 15300 if clock[0] >= 1018 else 15100
            self.runner.samples.append(sample)
        with patch.object(c.time, 'time', side_effect=lambda: clock[0]), \
                patch.object(c.time, 'monotonic', side_effect=lambda: clock[0]), \
                patch.object(c.time, 'sleep', side_effect=lambda seconds: clock.__setitem__(0, clock[0] + seconds)), \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'poll', side_effect=poll), patch.object(self.runner, 'event') as event, \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'report'), patch.object(c, 'watchdog_kernel_check', return_value='Xid8') as kernel, \
                patch.object(h, 'endpoint_free') as endpoint:
            self.runner.wait_before_retry('watchdog', result, 15)
        self.assertEqual(clock[0], 1028)
        self.assertEqual(kernel.call_count, 2)
        endpoint.assert_called_once()
        waiting = next(call for call in event.call_args_list if call.args[0] == 'watchdog_wait')
        self.assertEqual(waiting.kwargs['until_epoch'], 1120)
        self.assertNotIn('resource_wait', self.runner.state)

    def test_expired_health_window_and_live_children_block_restart(self):
        with patch.object(c, 'watchdog_kernel_check', return_value='Xid8'), \
                patch.object(self.runner, 'poll'), patch.object(self.runner, 'event'), patch.object(self.runner, 'report'):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'watchdog_health_unresolved'):
                self.runner.wait_before_retry('watchdog', self.watchdog_failure(time.time() - 121), 0)
        self.runner.state['owned'] = [{'pid': 1}]
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, 'stopped_children'):
            self.runner.wait_before_retry('watchdog', self.watchdog_failure(time.time()), 0)

    def test_wait_honors_stop_deadline_temperature_power_and_ram_guards(self):
        for condition in ('stop', 'deadline', 'thermal', 'power', 'ram'):
            with self.subTest(condition=condition):
                self.runner.deadline = 0 if condition == 'deadline' else time.monotonic() + 100
                self.runner.stopping = condition == 'stop'
                self.runner.last_sample = 0
                sample = safe_sample()
                if condition == 'thermal':
                    sample['gpus'][h.GPU0]['temperature.gpu'] = 80
                if condition == 'power':
                    sample['gpus'][h.GPU0]['power.limit'] = 180
                if condition == 'ram':
                    sample['ram_available_mib'] = 2047
                with patch.object(h, 'telemetry', return_value=sample), \
                        patch.object(c, 'watchdog_kernel_check', return_value='Xid8'), \
                        patch.object(self.runner, 'event'), patch.object(self.runner, 'report'):
                    with self.assertRaises(h.StopCampaign):
                        self.runner.wait_before_retry('watchdog', self.watchdog_failure(time.time()), 0)


class MigrationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def fixture(self, root):
        source, target = root / 'source', root / 'target'
        for path in (source, target):
            (path / 'attempts').mkdir(parents=True)
        jobs = [{'id': i, 'model': 'iq3-control', 'task': 'javascript/' + i} for i in ('done', 'failed', 'pending')]
        manifest = {k: k for k in ('engine_commit', 'binary', 'binary_pins', 'devices', 'port', 'models', 'safety')}
        manifest.update(queue=jobs, max_attempts=3, continuation={'confirmation': 'same'},
                        snapshot_pins={'scripts/instruments/hillclimb.py': 'old', 'grader': 'unchanged'})
        h.sealed_json(source / 'manifest.json', manifest)
        manifest = copy.deepcopy(manifest)
        manifest['snapshot_pins']['scripts/instruments/hillclimb.py'] = 'new'
        manifest['continuation']['memory_recovery'] = c.MEMORY_RECOVERY
        h.sealed_json(target / 'manifest.json', manifest)
        (source / 'attempts/abc-01').mkdir()
        (source / 'attempts/abc-01/server.log').write_text('preserved log')
        (source / 'attempts/abc-01/link').symlink_to('/absent-node-modules')
        h.sealed_json(source / 'job-done.json', {'status': 'passed', 'model': 'iq3-control',
                      'task': 'javascript/done', 'attempt': 'attempts/abc-01'})
        h.sealed_json(source / 'job-failed.json', {'status': 'failed', 'reason': 'vram_reserve'})
        h.atomic_json(source / 'state.json', {'status': 'stopped', 'owned': [],
                      'completed': {'done': 'job-done.json', 'failed': 'job-failed.json'}})
        h.atomic_json(target / 'state.json', {'status': 'prepared', 'owned': [], 'completed': {}})
        return source, target

    def test_only_valid_results_are_reused_with_verified_logs_and_symlinks(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
            audit = c.reuse_completed(source, target)
            self.assertEqual([r['job'] for r in audit['reused']], ['done'])
            self.assertEqual([r['job'] for r in audit['requeued']], ['failed'])
            self.assertEqual(c.reuse_completed(source, target), audit)
            self.assertEqual(c.tree_fingerprint(source / 'attempts/abc-01'),
                             c.tree_fingerprint(target / 'attempts/abc-01'))
            self.assertTrue((target / 'attempts/abc-01/link').is_symlink())
            self.assertFalse((target / 'job-failed.json').exists())
            self.assertTrue((source / 'job-failed.json').exists())
            self.assertEqual(json.loads((target / 'state.json').read_text())['completed'], {'done': 'job-done.json'})

    def test_changed_safety_or_grader_prevents_import(self):
        for key in ('safety', 'grader'):
            with self.subTest(key=key), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
                source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
                manifest = h.read_sealed(target / 'manifest.json')
                if key == 'grader':
                    manifest['snapshot_pins']['grader'] = 'changed'
                else:
                    manifest['safety'] = 'changed'
                h.sealed_json(target / 'manifest.json', manifest)
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    c.reuse_completed(source, target)

    def test_watchdog_policy_change_requires_explicit_bounded_opt_in(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
            manifest = h.read_sealed(target / 'manifest.json')
            manifest['continuation']['watchdog_recovery'] = copy.deepcopy(c.WATCHDOG_RECOVERY)
            h.sealed_json(target / 'manifest.json', manifest)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'selection policy'):
                c.reuse_completed(source, target)
            audit = c.reuse_completed(source, target, allow_watchdog_recovery=True)
            self.assertTrue(audit['watchdog_recovery_changed'])
            manifest['continuation']['watchdog_recovery']['max_retries'] = 100
            h.sealed_json(target / 'manifest.json', manifest)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'bounded watchdog'):
                c.reuse_completed(source, target, allow_watchdog_recovery=True)

    def test_grader_migration_requires_opt_in_and_revalidation_for_each_import(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
            manifest = h.read_sealed(target / 'manifest.json')
            manifest['snapshot_pins']['scripts/instruments/agentic_probe.py'] = 'fixed'
            manifest['snapshot_pins']['agentic-assets/selftest.json'] = 'new-checks'
            h.sealed_json(target / 'manifest.json', manifest)
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                c.reuse_completed(source, target)
            with patch.object(c, 'revalidate_measurement', return_value={'eligible': False}) as regrade:
                audit = c.reuse_completed(source, target, revalidate_grader=True)
            self.assertEqual(regrade.call_count, 1)
            self.assertEqual(audit['reused'], [])
            self.assertEqual(audit['requeued'][0]['reason'], 'grader_revalidation_required_rerun')
            self.assertFalse((target / 'job-done.json').exists())
            (target / 'revalidation').mkdir()
            h.sealed_json(target / 'revalidation/done.json', {'eligible': True})
            with patch.object(c, 'revalidate_measurement', return_value={'eligible': True}):
                audit = c.reuse_completed(source, target, revalidate_grader=True)
            self.assertTrue(audit['grader_revalidated'])
            self.assertEqual([r['job'] for r in audit['reused']], ['done'])

    def test_grader_migration_still_rejects_changes_to_task_sources(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
            manifest = h.read_sealed(target / 'manifest.json')
            manifest['snapshot_pins']['agentic-assets/sources/rust/decimal/tests/decimal.rs'] = 'changed'
            h.sealed_json(target / 'manifest.json', manifest)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'grader assets'):
                c.reuse_completed(source, target, revalidate_grader=True)

    def test_revalidation_requeues_false_repairs_or_changed_counts_without_regrading(self):
        from scripts.instruments import agentic_probe as probe
        for change in ({'grading_rounds': 2}, {'expected_passed_tests': 45}, {'timed_out': True}):
            with self.subTest(change=change), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
                source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
                assets = target / 'snapshot/agentic-assets'
                assets.mkdir(parents=True)
                h.atomic_json(assets / 'selftest.json', {'test_scope': probe.TEST_SCOPE, 'harness_verified': True,
                              'tasks': [{'task_id': 'javascript/done', 'expected_passed_tests': 44}]})
                row = {'probe': {'complete': True, 'task_success': True, 'grading_rounds': 1,
                                'expected_passed_tests': 44, **change}}
                with patch.object(probe, 'test_suite') as grade:
                    report = c.revalidate_measurement(source, target,
                        {'id': 'done', 'task': 'javascript/done'}, row, 'tree')
                    self.assertFalse(report['eligible'])
                    grade.assert_not_called()
                    self.assertEqual(c.revalidate_measurement(source, target,
                        {'id': 'done', 'task': 'javascript/done'}, row, 'tree'), report)
                    with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'inputs changed'):
                        c.revalidate_measurement(source, target,
                            {'id': 'done', 'task': 'javascript/done'}, row, 'different-tree')

    def test_queue_reorder_requires_opt_in_and_preserves_every_job_definition(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
            manifest = h.read_sealed(target / 'manifest.json')
            manifest['queue'].reverse()
            manifest['continuation']['queue_order'] = 'model-first'
            h.sealed_json(target / 'manifest.json', manifest)
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                c.reuse_completed(source, target)
            audit = c.reuse_completed(source, target, allow_queue_reorder=True)
            self.assertTrue(audit['queue_order_changed'])
            self.assertEqual(len(audit['reused']), 1)
        for change in ('duplicate', 'missing', 'config'):
            with self.subTest(change=change), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
                source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
                manifest = h.read_sealed(target / 'manifest.json')
                if change == 'duplicate':
                    manifest['queue'].append(manifest['queue'][0])
                elif change == 'missing':
                    manifest['queue'].pop()
                else:
                    manifest['queue'][0]['configs'] = [{'context': 32768}]
                h.sealed_json(target / 'manifest.json', manifest)
                with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                    c.reuse_completed(source, target, allow_queue_reorder=True)

    def test_live_source_or_changed_import_is_rejected(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
            c.reuse_completed(source, target)
            (target / 'attempts/abc-01/server.log').write_text('changed')
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                c.reuse_completed(source, target)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            source, target = self.fixture(Path(temp))
            state = json.loads((source / 'state.json').read_text())
            state['status'] = 'running'
            h.atomic_json(source / 'state.json', state)
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                c.reuse_completed(source, target)


class BoundaryHandoffTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.source, self.target = Path(self.temp.name) / 'source', Path(self.temp.name) / 'target'
        self.source.mkdir(); self.target.mkdir()
        h.sealed_json(self.source / 'manifest.json', {'queue': [{'id': 'current'}]})
        self.source_state = {'status': 'running', 'completed': {}, 'owned': [{'pid': 123}],
                             'coordinator': {'pid': 123, 'start': 'identity'}}
        h.atomic_json(self.source / 'state.json', self.source_state)
        self.deadline = time.time() + 1000

    def finish(self):
        h.sealed_json(self.source / 'job-current.json', {'status': 'passed', 'probe': {'task_success': False}})
        self.source_state['completed']['current'] = 'job-current.json'
        h.atomic_json(self.source / 'state.json', self.source_state)

    def import_results(self, source, target, *, allow_queue_reorder):
        self.assertTrue(allow_queue_reorder)
        self.assertEqual((source, target), (self.source, self.target))
        h.sealed_json(target / 'RECOVERY.json', {'source': str(source), 'reused': ['current']})

    def test_waits_for_record_then_stops_and_imports_only_after_coordinator_exits(self):
        ticks = [0]
        def step(_):
            ticks[0] += 1
            if ticks[0] == 1:
                self.assertFalse((self.source / 'STOP').exists())
                self.finish()
            elif ticks[0] == 2:
                self.assertTrue((self.source / 'STOP').exists())
                self.source_state.update(status='stopped', reason='requested_stop', owned=[])
                h.atomic_json(self.source / 'state.json', self.source_state)
        def identity(_):
            return None if ticks[0] >= 3 else 'identity'
        with patch.object(c.time, 'sleep', side_effect=step), patch.object(h, 'proc_identity', side_effect=identity), \
                patch.object(c, 'reuse_completed', side_effect=self.import_results) as reuse:
            self.assertTrue(c.handoff_at_boundary(self.source, self.target, 'current', self.deadline))
            self.assertEqual(reuse.call_count, 1)
            self.assertTrue(c.handoff_at_boundary(self.source, self.target, 'current', self.deadline))
            self.assertEqual(reuse.call_count, 1)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(ticks[0], 3)

    def test_source_safety_fault_blocks_even_when_boundary_was_completed(self):
        for reason in ('gpu_fault', 'memory_pressure_unresolved', 'budget_expired', 'requested_stop'):
            with self.subTest(reason=reason):
                self.finish()
                self.source_state.update(status='stopped', reason=reason, owned=[])
                h.atomic_json(self.source / 'state.json', self.source_state)
                with patch.object(c, 'reuse_completed') as reuse:
                    self.assertFalse(c.handoff_at_boundary(self.source, self.target, 'current', self.deadline))
                    reuse.assert_not_called()
                self.assertFalse((self.source / 'STOP').exists())

    def test_external_stop_and_destination_stop_do_not_stop_or_restart_source(self):
        (self.source / 'STOP').touch()
        self.assertFalse(c.handoff_at_boundary(self.source, self.target, 'current', self.deadline))
        self.assertFalse((self.target / 'HANDOFF-STOP.json').exists())
        (self.target / 'STOP').touch()
        self.assertFalse(c.handoff_at_boundary(self.source, self.target, 'current', self.deadline))

    def test_deadline_and_incomplete_record_do_not_interrupt_active_task(self):
        h.atomic_json(self.source / 'job-current.json', {'status': 'passed'})
        clock = [self.deadline - .1]
        with patch.object(c.time, 'time', side_effect=lambda: clock[0]), \
                patch.object(c.time, 'sleep', side_effect=lambda _: clock.__setitem__(0, clock[0] + 1)):
            self.assertFalse(c.handoff_at_boundary(self.source, self.target, 'current', self.deadline))
        self.assertFalse((self.source / 'STOP').exists())

    def test_shutdown_timeout_is_fixed_across_restarts(self):
        self.finish()
        h.sealed_json(self.target / 'HANDOFF-STOP.json', {'requested_at': time.time() - 181})
        with patch.object(h, 'proc_identity', return_value='identity'), patch.object(c, 'reuse_completed') as reuse:
            self.assertFalse(c.handoff_at_boundary(self.source, self.target, 'current', self.deadline))
            reuse.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(json.loads((self.target / 'stage-status.json').read_text())['reason'], 'source_shutdown_timeout')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()


from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
    "analyze_paired", ROOT / "scripts" / "analysis" / "analyze_paired.py"
)
analyze = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(analyze)


class DiscordantTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_counts_each_direction_separately(self) -> None:
        a = {"t1": True, "t2": False, "t3": True, "t4": True}
        b = {"t1": True, "t2": True, "t3": False, "t4": True}
        self.assertEqual(analyze.discordant(a, b), (4, 1, 1))

    def test_ignores_tasks_missing_from_either_side(self) -> None:
        # A crashed task must shrink n, not silently count as agreement.
        a = {"t1": True, "t2": False}
        b = {"t1": False}
        self.assertEqual(analyze.discordant(a, b), (1, 1, 0))


class McNemarTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_no_discordant_pairs_is_not_evidence(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(analyze.exact_mcnemar_p(0, 0), 1.0)

    def test_symmetric_split_is_not_significant(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(analyze.exact_mcnemar_p(5, 5), 1.0)

    def test_lopsided_split_is_significant(self) -> None:
        # 0-vs-8 is the two-sided sign test: 2 * 0.5**8.
        self.assertAlmostEqual(analyze.exact_mcnemar_p(0, 8), 2 * 0.5**8)
        self.assertLess(analyze.exact_mcnemar_p(0, 8), 0.05)

    def test_is_direction_agnostic(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(analyze.exact_mcnemar_p(2, 9), analyze.exact_mcnemar_p(9, 2))

    def test_small_n_cannot_reach_significance(self) -> None:
        # Guards the reading error this analysis exists to prevent: at n=4 even a
        # clean sweep is p>0.05, so a 4-task gap is never on its own a result.
        self.assertGreater(analyze.exact_mcnemar_p(0, 4), 0.05)


class TimeoutDerivationTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_recovers_the_flag_from_the_note_the_harness_wrote(self) -> None:
        row = analyze.derive_timed_out(
            {"timed_out": None, "notes": "task timed out after 900s"}
        )
        self.assertTrue(row["timed_out"])

    def test_a_test_failure_is_not_a_timeout(self) -> None:
        row = analyze.derive_timed_out(
            {"timed_out": None, "notes": "tests failed after one repair round"}
        )
        self.assertFalse(row["timed_out"])

    def test_an_explicit_flag_is_never_overridden(self) -> None:
        row = analyze.derive_timed_out({"timed_out": False, "notes": "task timed out after 900s"})
        self.assertFalse(row["timed_out"])

    def test_absent_notes_are_not_a_timeout(self) -> None:
        self.assertFalse(analyze.derive_timed_out({"timed_out": None, "notes": None})["timed_out"])


class TimeoutExclusionTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def _rows(self):
        return {
            ("A", 1, "t1"): {"task_success": True, "timed_out": False},
            ("A", 1, "t2"): {"task_success": False, "timed_out": True},
        }

    def test_timed_out_tasks_are_missing_not_failures(self) -> None:
        # Scoring a cut-off as a failure lets a slower quant look less capable.
        self.assertEqual(analyze.outcomes(self._rows(), "A", 1), {"t1": True})

    def test_they_can_be_included_deliberately(self) -> None:
        both = analyze.outcomes(self._rows(), "A", 1, include_timeouts=True)
        self.assertEqual(both, {"t1": True, "t2": False})


class DirectionVsMagnitudeTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """Total disagreement is direction-blind and must not gate a conclusion."""

    def test_symmetric_churn_is_no_evidence_however_large(self) -> None:
        lo, hi = analyze.paired_difference_ci(40, 10, 10)
        self.assertEqual(analyze.exact_mcnemar_p(10, 10), 1.0)
        self.assertLess(lo, 0.0)
        self.assertGreater(hi, 0.0)

    def test_a_smaller_one_sided_loss_is_evidence(self) -> None:
        # Six one-directional losses: half the disagreement of the case above,
        # yet the only one of the two that supports a conclusion.
        lo, hi = analyze.paired_difference_ci(40, 0, 6)
        self.assertLess(analyze.exact_mcnemar_p(0, 6), 0.05)
        self.assertGreater(lo, 0.0)

    def test_interval_is_symmetric_under_swapping_the_arms(self) -> None:
        a = analyze.paired_difference_ci(40, 3, 9)
        b = analyze.paired_difference_ci(40, 9, 3)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(a[0], -b[1])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(a[1], -b[0])

    def test_no_shared_tasks_yields_no_interval(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(analyze.paired_difference_ci(0, 0, 0), (0.0, 0.0))


class LoadTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_rows_key_on_quant_run_and_task(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            p = Path(d) / "r.jsonl"
            p.write_text(
                "\n".join(
                    json.dumps(r)
                    for r in (
                        {"quant": "A", "run_index": 1, "task_id": "t", "task_success": True},
                        {"quant": "A", "run_index": 2, "task_id": "t", "task_success": False},
                        {"quant": "B", "run_index": 1, "task_id": "t", "task_success": True},
                    )
                )
            )
            rows = analyze.load(p)
            self.assertEqual(len(rows), 3, "same task across runs/quants must not collide")
            self.assertEqual(analyze.outcomes(rows, "A", 1), {"t": True})
            self.assertEqual(analyze.outcomes(rows, "A", 2), {"t": False})


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import json
import unittest

from scripts.instruments.bench_concurrency import read_stream, summarize


def event(value):
    return ("data: " + json.dumps(value) + "\n").encode()


class StreamTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_reasoning_is_first_output_and_timings_survive_empty_usage_event(self):
        rows = [event({"choices": [{"delta": {"role": "assistant"}}]}),
                event({"choices": [{"delta": {"reasoning_content": "think"}}]}),
                event({"choices": [{"delta": {}, "finish_reason": "length"}],
                       "timings": {"predicted_n": 5}}),
                event({"choices": [], "usage": {"completion_tokens": 5}}), b"data: [DONE]\n"]
        result = read_stream(rows, 10, now=lambda: 12)
        self.assertEqual(result["ttft_seconds"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(result["timings"]["predicted_n"], 5)
        self.assertEqual(result["usage"]["completion_tokens"], 5)

    def test_missing_usage_and_stream_errors_fail_closed(self):
        for rows in ([b"data: [DONE]\n"], [event({"error": "OOM"})]):
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                read_stream(rows, 0)


class SummaryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_overlapping_rates_use_wall_span_not_summed_request_durations(self):
        rows = [{"started_monotonic": 10, "finished_monotonic": 20,
                 "ttft_seconds": 2, "usage": {"completion_tokens": 100}, "error": None},
                {"started_monotonic": 11, "finished_monotonic": 21,
                 "ttft_seconds": 3, "usage": {"completion_tokens": 100}, "error": None}]
        result = summarize(rows, [{"slots": [{"is_processing": True}, {"is_processing": True}]}])
        self.assertEqual(result["makespan_seconds"], 11)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(result["output_tokens_per_wall_second"], 200 / 11)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(result["output_tokens_per_first_token_to_last_finish_second"], 200 / 9)
        self.assertEqual(result["max_observed_busy_slots"], 2)

    def test_failed_batch_has_no_successful_throughput_number(self):
        result = summarize([{"started_monotonic": 1, "finished_monotonic": 2, "error": "failed"}], [])
        self.assertFalse(result["complete"])
        self.assertIsNone(result["output_tokens_per_wall_second"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from scripts.instruments import bench_mtp  # noqa: E402


def _tasks() -> list[dict]:
    return sorted(
        json.loads((ROOT / "subsets" / "polyglot.json").read_text()),
        key=lambda t: t["task_id"],
    )


class AcceptanceTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_no_drafts_yields_no_rate(self) -> None:
        # An MTP-off row must stay empty rather than report a rate of zero, which
        # would read as "the head drafted and was always wrong".
        self.assertEqual(bench_mtp.acceptance({"drafts": 0, "draft_tokens": 0}), {})

    def test_rates_are_per_token_and_per_draft(self) -> None:
        got = bench_mtp.acceptance(
            {"drafts": 100, "draft_tokens": 300, "draft_accepted_tokens": 180}
        )
        self.assertAlmostEqual(got["draft_accept_rate"], 0.6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(got["accepted_per_draft"], 1.8)


class BenchPromptTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_prompt_carries_stub_and_tests(self) -> None:
        for task in _tasks()[:2] + [t for t in _tasks() if t["language"] == "rust"][:2]:
            prompt = bench_mtp.bench_prompt(task, ROOT / "subsets")
            self.assertIn(task["solution_path"][0], prompt, task["task_id"])
            self.assertRegex(prompt, r"(?i)(test|spec)", task["task_id"])

    def test_reference_solution_never_reaches_the_prompt(self) -> None:
        # .meta holds the worked answer. Leaking it would measure copying, not
        # generation, and draft acceptance on copied text is meaninglessly high.
        for task in _tasks():
            base = (ROOT / "subsets" / task["source_dir"]).resolve()
            reference = base / task["example_path"][0]
            if not reference.is_file():
                continue
            body = reference.read_text(errors="replace").strip()
            self.assertNotIn(body, bench_mtp.bench_prompt(task, ROOT / "subsets"), task["task_id"])

    def test_the_meta_exclusion_is_not_vacuous(self) -> None:
        # If no task shipped a .meta reference, the guard above would pass on nothing.
        found = [
            t
            for t in _tasks()
            if ((ROOT / "subsets" / t["source_dir"]).resolve() / t["example_path"][0]).is_file()
        ]
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(found), 20, "no .meta references present to exclude")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


"""`box.sh rent` must not pick a host whose bandwidth price dominates the leg cost.

Offers are sorted by `-o dph`, which sees only the hourly rate. Instance 49058864 billed
$0.059 of GPU time and $0.711 of download -- 90 percent of the leg -- on a host whose
hourly rate looked competitive, while the same pool held bandwidth at a fifteenth the
price. This drives the real script against a stub `vastai` and asserts on the offer id
that reaches `create instance`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BOX = ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh"

# Ordered as `-o dph` would return them: the cheap-by-the-hour offer is also the one that
# charges fifteen times as much per GB, which is the trap the cap exists to catch.
STUB = r"""#!/usr/bin/env bash
case "$1 $2" in
  "search offers")
    cat <<'JSON'
[{"id": 111, "machine_id": 11, "dph_total": 0.16, "inet_down_cost": 0.0390,
  "gpu_max_power": 400, "reliability2": 0.999, "inet_down": 1000,
  "cpu_cores_effective": 32, "cuda_max_good": 13.5, "verification": "verified"},
 {"id": 222, "machine_id": 22, "dph_total": 0.17, "inet_down_cost": 0.0026,
  "gpu_max_power": 400, "reliability2": 0.999, "inet_down": 1000,
  "cpu_cores_effective": 32, "cuda_max_good": 13.5, "verification": "verified"}]
JSON
    ;;
  "create instance") echo "$3" >> "$OFFER_LOG"; echo '{"new_contract": 4242424}' ;;
  "show instance")   echo '{"actual_status": "running", "status_msg": "",
                          "ssh_host": "stub.invalid", "ssh_port": 2222}' ;;
  "destroy instance") echo "destroyed $3" ;;
  *) : ;;
esac
"""


class OfferCostTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        d = Path(self.tmp.name)
        bindir = d / "bin"
        bindir.mkdir()
        for name, body in (("vastai", STUB),
                           ("ssh", "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nexit 1\n"),
                           ("sleep", "#!/usr/bin/env bash\nexec /bin/sleep 0.05\n")):
            f = bindir / name
            f.write_text(body)
            f.chmod(0o755)
        self.offer_log = d / "offers.txt"
        self.env = {
            **os.environ,
            "PATH": f"{bindir}:{os.environ['PATH']}",
            "OFFER_LOG": str(self.offer_log),
            "STATE": str(d / "instances.log"),
            "QUARANTINE": str(d / "quarantine.log"),
            "RENT_CAP": "1",
            "RENT_WAIT": "1",
            "RENT_TRIES": "1",
        }
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)

    def _rent(self, **extra):
        subprocess.run([str(BOX), "rent", "test-ttl4", "RTX 3090", "1", "80"],
                       capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120,
                       env={**self.env, **extra})
        return self.offer_log.read_text().split() if self.offer_log.exists() else []

    def test_the_default_cap_skips_the_expensive_bandwidth_offer(self):
        self.assertEqual(["222"], self._rent(),
                         "rent took the $0.0390/GB offer over the $0.0026/GB one")

    def test_a_zero_cap_disables_the_filter(self):
        self.assertEqual(["111"], self._rent(DLCOSTMAX="0"),
                         "DLCOSTMAX=0 must not filter on bandwidth price")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


"""`box.sh rent` must not leave a billing box behind when it is killed.

Between `vastai create instance` and the id being printed, `rent` polls for up to
RENT_CAP*20s. In that window the caller has no id, so its teardown trap has nothing to
destroy -- a kill there used to leave a box billing until the reaper's TTL caught it.
This drives the real script against a stub `vastai`, so the assertion is about the
shell's control flow rather than a mock's expectations.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import signal
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BOX = ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh"
INSTANCE_ID = "4242424"

STUB = r"""#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Stands in for the vast.ai CLI. Records every destroy so the test can assert on it.
case "$1 $2" in
  "search offers")
    cat <<'JSON'
[{"id": 999, "machine_id": 111, "dph_total": 0.10, "gpu_max_power": 400,
  "reliability2": 0.999, "inet_down": 1000, "cpu_cores_effective": 32,
  "cuda_max_good": 13.5, "verification": "verified"}]
JSON
    ;;
  "create instance") echo '{"new_contract": __ID__}' ;;
  "show instance")   echo '{"actual_status": "running", "status_msg": "",
                          "ssh_host": "stub.invalid", "ssh_port": 2222}' ;;
  "destroy instance") echo "$3" >> "$DESTROY_LOG"; echo "destroyed $3" ;;
  "logs "*|"logs") : ;;
  *) : ;;
esac
""".replace("__ID__", INSTANCE_ID)


class RentOrphanTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        d = Path(self.tmp.name)
        bindir = d / "bin"
        bindir.mkdir()
        stub = bindir / "vastai"
        stub.write_text(STUB)
        stub.chmod(0o755)
        # `endpoint` shells out to ssh; a stub that always fails keeps rent in its poll
        # loop, which is exactly the window under test.
        ssh = bindir / "ssh"
        ssh.write_text("#!/usr/bin/env bash\nexit 1\n")
        ssh.chmod(0o755)
        # rent's poll loop sleeps 20s a tick. Shorten it so the window under test is
        # seconds wide rather than minutes; the control flow is identical.
        slp = bindir / "sleep"
        slp.write_text("#!/usr/bin/env bash\nexec /bin/sleep 0.2\n")
        slp.chmod(0o755)
        self.destroy_log = d / "destroys.txt"
        self.env = {
            **os.environ,
            "PATH": f"{bindir}:{os.environ['PATH']}",
            "DESTROY_LOG": str(self.destroy_log),
            "STATE": str(d / "instances.log"),
            "QUARANTINE": str(d / "quarantine.log"),
            # Generous, so the poll loop is still running when the test kills it.
            "RENT_CAP": "600",
            "RENT_WAIT": "600",
            "RENT_TRIES": "1",
        }
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)

    def _rent(self):
        return subprocess.Popen(
            [str(BOX), "rent", "test-ttl4", "RTX 3090", "1", "80"],
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, text=True,
            env=self.env, start_new_session=True,
        )

    def _wait_until_created(self, proc, timeout=30):
        """The box exists once rent has recorded it; that is the window's start."""
        state = Path(self.env["STATE"])
        deadline = time.time() + timeout
        while time.time() < deadline:
            if state.exists() and INSTANCE_ID in state.read_text():
                return True
            if proc.poll() is not None:
                self.fail(f"rent exited early: {proc.communicate()}")
            time.sleep(0.2)
        self.fail("rent never created an instance")

    def _destroyed(self):
        return self.destroy_log.read_text().split() if self.destroy_log.exists() else []

    def test_sigterm_during_the_poll_destroys_the_box(self):
        proc = self._rent()
        self._wait_until_created(proc)
        proc.terminate()
        proc.communicate(timeout=60)
        self.assertIn(INSTANCE_ID, self._destroyed(),
                      "rent was killed after creating a box and did not destroy it")

    def test_sigint_during_the_poll_destroys_the_box(self):
        proc = self._rent()
        self._wait_until_created(proc)
        os.killpg(os.getpgid(proc.pid), signal.SIGINT)
        proc.communicate(timeout=60)
        self.assertIn(INSTANCE_ID, self._destroyed())

    def test_a_box_handed_to_the_caller_is_not_destroyed(self):
        """The trap must disown on success, or rent would destroy what it just returned."""
        ssh = Path(self.env["PATH"].split(":")[0]) / "ssh"
        ssh.write_text("#!/usr/bin/env bash\nexit 0\n")   # endpoint now succeeds
        proc = self._rent()
        out, err = proc.communicate(timeout=120)
        self.assertEqual(proc.returncode, 0, err)
        self.assertEqual(out.strip(), INSTANCE_ID)
        self.assertEqual(self._destroyed(), [],
                         "rent destroyed the box it had just handed to the caller")


from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from scripts.corpora import build_kld_corpora as builder


def candidate(repo: str, path: str, content: bytes, *, test: bool = False) -> builder.Candidate:
    return builder.Candidate(
        repo=repo,
        sha=("a" if repo == "one/repo" else "b") * 40,
        path=path,
        content=content,
        sha256=hashlib.sha256(content).hexdigest(),
        is_test=test,
    )


class KldCorpusBuilderTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_normalisation_and_rejection_rules(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(builder._normalise_content(b"a\r\nb\rc\n"), b"a\nb\nc\n")
        self.assertIsNone(builder._normalise_content(b"prefix\0suffix"))
        self.assertIsNone(builder._normalise_content(b"\xff"))
        self.assertTrue(builder._is_excluded("src/generated/table.rs", "rust"))
        self.assertTrue(builder._is_excluded("src/types.d.ts", "js-ts"))
        self.assertTrue(builder._is_excluded("src/code.min.js", "js-ts"))
        self.assertFalse(builder._is_excluded("tests/client.test.ts", "js-ts"))

    def test_exact_dedup_keeps_lexicographically_first_path(self) -> None:
        content = b" ".join([b"token"] * 30)
        rows = builder.deduplicate(
            [
                candidate("two/repo", "z.rs", content),
                candidate("one/repo", "a.rs", content),
            ]
        )
        self.assertEqual([(row.repo, row.path) for row in rows], [("one/repo", "a.rs")])

    def test_minhash_drops_near_duplicate(self) -> None:
        original = " ".join(f"token{index}" for index in range(100)).encode()
        near_copy = original.replace(b"token50", b"changed50")
        rows = builder.deduplicate(
            [
                candidate("two/repo", "z.rs", near_copy),
                candidate("one/repo", "a.rs", original),
            ]
        )
        self.assertEqual([row.path for row in rows], ["a.rs"])

    def test_linguist_attributes_are_honoured(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            root = Path(temp_dir)
            subprocess.run(
                ["git", "init", "--quiet"], cwd=root, check=True, timeout=10
            )
            (root / ".gitattributes").write_text(
                "generated.rs linguist-generated\n"
                "vendored.rs linguist-vendored=true\n"
                "hidden.rs linguist-detectable=false\n",
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            subprocess.run(
                ["git", "add", ".gitattributes"], cwd=root, check=True, timeout=10
            )
            source = builder.Source("fixture/repo", "a" * 40, root)
            self.assertEqual(
                builder._attribute_exclusions(
                    source,
                    ["generated.rs", "vendored.rs", "hidden.rs", "plain.rs"],
                    10,
                ),
                {"generated.rs", "vendored.rs", "hidden.rs"},
            )

    def test_round_robin_serialization_offsets_and_test_cap(self) -> None:
        groups = [
            [candidate("one/repo", "a.rs", b"aaa"), candidate("one/repo", "c.rs", b"ccc")],
            [
                candidate("two/repo", "b.rs", b"bbb"),
                candidate("two/repo", "d.rs", b"d" * 20, test=True),
            ],
        ]
        artifact = builder.serialize_candidates(builder.round_robin(groups), 100)
        self.assertEqual(artifact.data, b"aaa\n\nbbb\n\nccc\n")
        rows = [json.loads(line) for line in artifact.manifest.splitlines()]
        self.assertEqual([row["path"] for row in rows], ["a.rs", "b.rs", "c.rs"])
        for row in rows:
            span = artifact.data[row["byte_start"] : row["byte_end"]]
            self.assertEqual(hashlib.sha256(span).hexdigest(), row["sha256"])

    def test_record_uses_only_supplied_timestamp(self) -> None:
        sources = json.loads((ROOT / "corpora" / "sources.json").read_text())
        artifact = builder.CorpusArtifact(b"x\n", b"{}\n", 1)
        record = builder.build_record(
            sources,
            {"general": artifact, "rust": artifact, "js-ts": artifact},
            "literal-time",
        )
        self.assertEqual(record["timestamp"], "literal-time")
        self.assertEqual(record["corpora"]["rust"]["byte_count"], 2)

    def test_verify_detects_manifest_mismatch(self) -> None:
        artifact = builder.CorpusArtifact(b"x\n", b"{}\n", 1)
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            root = Path(temp_dir)
            (root / "BUILD.json").write_text(
                json.dumps(
                    {
                        "corpora": {
                            name: {
                                "sha256": hashlib.sha256(artifact.data).hexdigest(),
                                "byte_count": 2,
                                "file_count": 1,
                            }
                            for name in ("general", "rust", "js-ts")
                        }
                    }
                )
            )
            for name in ("general", "rust", "js-ts"):
                (root / f"{name}.manifest.jsonl").write_bytes(artifact.manifest)
            self.assertEqual(
                builder._verify(root, {name: artifact for name in ("general", "rust", "js-ts")}),
                [],
            )
            (root / "rust.manifest.jsonl").write_text("corrupt\n")
            self.assertIn("rust: rebuilt manifest differs", builder._verify(root, {"rust": artifact}))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import copy
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import resource
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import agentic_campaign as agentic
from scripts.instruments import chat_templates as templates
from scripts.instruments import hillclimb as h


class TemplateOverrideTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_only_new_bartowski_jobs_select_the_adapter(self):
        jobs = h.validate_profiles(agentic.task_queue())
        for job in jobs:
            config = job['configs'][0]
            if job['model'] == 'bartowski-iq4-xs':
                self.assertEqual(config['chat_template'], templates.BARTOWSKI_PREFIX)
            else:
                self.assertNotIn('chat_template', config)
        bart = next(job for job in jobs if job['model'] == 'bartowski-iq4-xs')
        for key, value in [('model', 'iq3-control'), ('configs', [{'chat_template': '/tmp/other.jinja'}])]:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'pinned Bartowski'):
                h.validate_profiles([{**bart, key: value}])

    def test_changed_source_is_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'pinned source'):
            templates.bartowski_template('changed')

    def test_launch_uses_per_attempt_override_with_provenance(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
            root = Path(temporary)
            (root / 'artifacts').mkdir()
            out = root / 'attempt'
            out.mkdir()
            (root / 'artifacts/bartowski-iq4-xs.json').write_text(json.dumps({'chat_template': 'original'}))
            runner = h.Runner.__new__(h.Runner)
            runner.path = root
            with patch.object(templates, 'bartowski_template', return_value='patched') as adapt:
                args, info = runner.template_override({'id': 'bartowski-iq4-xs'},
                    {'chat_template': templates.BARTOWSKI_PREFIX}, out)
            adapt.assert_called_once_with('original')
            self.assertEqual(args, ['--jinja', '--chat-template-file', str(out / 'chat-template.jinja')])
            self.assertEqual(info['effective_sha256'], hashlib.sha256(b'patched').hexdigest())
            self.assertEqual(info['source_sha256'], templates.BARTOWSKI_TEMPLATE_SHA256)
            self.assertEqual(runner.template_override({'id': 'iq3-control'}, {}, out), ([], None))
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.RejectArm, 'unsupported_chat_template'):
                runner.template_override({'id': 'iq3-control'}, {'chat_template': templates.BARTOWSKI_PREFIX}, out)


@unittest.skipUnless(os.environ.get('QWEN38_TEMPLATE_TESTS') == '1', 'requires pinned GGUF and llama.cpp renderer')
class RealTemplateTests(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        sys.path.insert(0, str(h.LLAMA / 'gguf-py'))
        from gguf import GGUFReader
        model = h.ROOT / '.cache/hillclimb-models/bartowski-iq4-xs/Qwen3.8-27B-IQ4_XS.gguf'
        cls.source = GGUFReader(model).fields['tokenizer.chat_template'].contents()
        cls.fixed = templates.bartowski_template(cls.source)

    def render(self, template, messages, *, common=False, failure=False, **settings):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temporary:
            root = Path(temporary)
            path, request, output = root / 'template.jinja', root / 'request.json', root / 'output.txt'
            path.write_text(template)
            payload = {'messages': messages, 'tools': [], 'add_generation_prompt': True,
                       'reasoning_effort': 'xhigh', **settings}
            request.write_text(json.dumps(payload))
            args = [str(h.LLAMA / 'build/bin/test-chat-template'), '--json', str(request),
                    '--output', str(output), str(path)]
            if not common:
                args.append('--no-common')
            result = subprocess.run(args, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30,
                                    preexec_fn=lambda: resource.setrlimit(resource.RLIMIT_CORE, (0, 0)))
            if failure:
                self.assertNotEqual(result.returncode, 0)
                return result.stderr + result.stdout
            self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 0, result.stderr + result.stdout)
            return output.read_text()

    def test_only_the_instruction_normalization_is_added(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.fixed.replace(templates.LEADING_INSTRUCTIONS, '', 1), self.source)

    def test_existing_single_instruction_and_user_only_prompts_are_identical(self):
        for prefix in [[], [{'role': 'system', 'content': ' instructions '}]]:
            messages = prefix + [{'role': 'user', 'content': 'question'}]
            for common in [False, True]:
                self.assertEqual(self.render(self.source, messages, common=common),
                                 self.render(self.fixed, messages, common=common))

    def test_multiple_system_and_developer_messages_merge_in_order(self):
        messages = [{'role': 'system', 'content': ' FIRST '},
                    {'role': 'developer', 'content': [{'type': 'text', 'text': 'SECOND'}]},
                    {'role': 'system', 'content': 'THIRD'},
                    {'role': 'user', 'content': 'question'}]
        merged = [{'role': 'system', 'content': 'FIRST\nSECOND\nTHIRD'}, messages[-1]]
        for common in [False, True]:
            self.assertEqual(self.render(self.fixed, messages, common=common),
                             self.render(self.source, merged, common=common))
        error = self.render(self.source, messages, failure=True)
        self.assertTrue('Unexpected message role' in error or 'System message must be at the beginning' in error)

    def test_tools_reasoning_and_thinking_mode_are_preserved(self):
        tool = {'type': 'function', 'function': {'name': 'run', 'parameters': {'type': 'object',
                'properties': {'cmd': {'type': 'string'}}}}}
        messages = [{'role': 'system', 'content': 'FIRST'}, {'role': 'developer', 'content': 'SECOND'},
                    {'role': 'user', 'content': 'question'},
                    {'role': 'assistant', 'content': '', 'reasoning_content': 'THOUGHT',
                     'tool_calls': [{'type': 'function', 'id': 'c1', 'function': {
                         'name': 'run', 'arguments': {'cmd': 'echo hello'}}}]},
                    {'role': 'tool', 'tool_call_id': 'c1', 'content': 'hello'}]
        merged = [{'role': 'system', 'content': 'FIRST\nSECOND'}, *messages[2:]]
        for settings in [{}, {'enable_thinking': False}, {'preserve_thinking': False}, {'reasoning_effort': 'medium'}]:
            self.assertEqual(self.render(self.fixed, messages, tools=[tool], **settings),
                             self.render(self.source, merged, tools=[tool], **settings))

    def test_late_instructions_are_not_hoisted_or_silently_discarded(self):
        for role in ['system', 'developer']:
            messages = [{'role': 'system', 'content': 'FIRST'}, {'role': 'user', 'content': 'question'},
                        {'role': role, 'content': 'LATE'}]
            self.render(self.fixed, messages, failure=True)

    def test_developer_only_and_empty_leading_blocks(self):
        messages = [{'role': 'developer', 'content': ''}, {'role': 'system', 'content': None},
                    {'role': 'developer', 'content': 'ONLY'}, {'role': 'user', 'content': 'question'}]
        self.assertEqual(self.render(self.fixed, messages), self.render(self.source,
            [{'role': 'system', 'content': 'ONLY'}, messages[-1]]))
        invalid = copy.deepcopy(messages)
        invalid[0]['content'] = [{'type': 'image', 'image': 'bad'}]
        self.assertIn('cannot contain images', self.render(self.fixed, invalid, failure=True))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()


class SwiftDropTracesTests(unittest.TestCase):
    SOURCE = (ROOT / 'results/session2/pair-5060ti-2060s/campaign-swift-fullsuite-20260915'
              / 'swift-iq4-xs/attempts/66ebeeaec6a7043b-01/chat-template.jinja')

    def test_changed_source_is_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'pinned source'):
            templates.swift_drop_traces_template('changed')

    def test_pinned_source_still_on_disk(self):
        if not self.SOURCE.exists():
            self.skipTest('pinned Swift template not present')
        digest = hashlib.sha256(self.SOURCE.read_text().encode()).hexdigest()
        self.assertEqual(digest, templates.SWIFT_TEMPLATE_SHA256)

    def test_only_the_guard_line_changes(self):
        if not self.SOURCE.exists():
            self.skipTest('pinned Swift template not present')
        source = self.SOURCE.read_text()
        patched = templates.swift_drop_traces_template(source)
        self.assertNotIn(templates.PRESERVE_THINKING_GUARD, patched)
        self.assertEqual(patched.count(templates.DROP_THINKING_GUARD), 1)
        differing = [(a, b) for a, b in zip(source.splitlines(), patched.splitlines()) if a != b]
        self.assertEqual(len(differing), 1)
        self.assertEqual(len(source.splitlines()), len(patched.splitlines()))

    def test_in_flight_chain_still_keeps_its_traces(self):
        # The `loop.index0 > ns.last_query_index` arm is what protects the current
        # tool-call chain; losing it would strip reasoning the model is still acting on.
        self.assertIn('loop.index0 > ns.last_query_index', templates.DROP_THINKING_GUARD)


import copy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from scripts import ROOT
from scripts.instruments import agentic_campaign as a, confirmed_agentic as c, hillclimb as h


def observations(candidate_tg=32):
    rows = []
    for model in c.MODELS[1:]:
        for task in c.CONFIRM_TASKS:
            for seed in c.SEEDS:
                for ubatch in (128, 512):
                    rows.append({'model': model, 'task': task, 'seed': seed, 'status': 'passed',
                                 'config': {'context': 102400, 'slots': 1, 'mode': 'tensor',
                                            'split': '69,31', 'maximum': 3, 'ubatch': ubatch},
                                 'prefill_tps': 400 if ubatch == 128 else 450,
                                 'decode_tps': 30 if ubatch == 128 else candidate_tg,
                                 'probe': dict.fromkeys(['complete', 'mtp_live', 'depth_verified', 'overlap_verified'], True)})
    return rows


class ConfirmationTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_improvement_selects_candidate_but_does_not_promote_production(self):
        selected = c.choose_microbatches(observations(36))
        for value in selected.values():
            self.assertEqual(value['ubatch'], 512)
            self.assertTrue(value['near_100k_speed_gate'])
            self.assertFalse(value['production_qualified'])
            self.assertEqual(value['valid_repeats'], {'128': 12, '512': 12})

    def test_decode_regression_keeps_baseline_despite_better_prefill(self):
        self.assertTrue(all(v['ubatch'] == 128 for v in c.choose_microbatches(observations(25)).values()))

    def test_memory_failure_or_idle_mtp_is_not_a_valid_confirmation(self):
        for change in ({'status': 'failed'}, {'probe': {'complete': True, 'mtp_live': False}}):
            rows = observations()
            rows[1].update(change)
            selected = c.choose_microbatches(rows)[c.MODELS[1]]
            self.assertEqual(selected['ubatch'], 128)
            self.assertEqual(selected['valid_repeats']['512'], 11)

    def test_no_safe_observations_excludes_model(self):
        rows = observations()
        for row in rows:
            row['status'] = 'failed'
        self.assertTrue(all(v['ubatch'] is None for v in c.choose_microbatches(rows).values()))

    def test_missing_duplicate_and_changed_context_fail_closed(self):
        rows = observations()
        invalid = [rows[:-1], rows + [rows[0]]]
        altered = copy.deepcopy(rows)
        altered[0]['config']['context'] = 65536
        invalid.append(altered)
        for sample in invalid:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                c.choose_microbatches(sample)

    def test_full_suite_covers_all_tasks_and_only_declared_variants(self):
        tasks = [t['task_id'] for t in json.loads((ROOT / 'subsets/polyglot.json').read_text())]
        jobs = h.validate_profiles(c.suite_queue(tasks), allowed_tasks=set(tasks))
        self.assertEqual(len(jobs), 234)
        self.assertEqual(len({j['id'] for j in jobs}), 234)
        for model in c.MODELS:
            arm = [j for j in jobs if j['model'] == model]
            self.assertEqual({j['task'] for j in arm}, set(tasks))
            for job in arm:
                self.assertEqual({v['slots'] for v in job['configs']}, {1})
                self.assertEqual({v['ubatch'] for v in job['configs']}, {512} if model == 'iq3-control' else {128, 512})

    def test_runtime_resolves_only_predeclared_configuration(self):
        runner = c.ConfirmedRunner.__new__(c.ConfirmedRunner)
        runner.selection = {'models': {c.MODELS[1]: {'ubatch': 512}}}
        job = c.suite_queue(['javascript/affine-cipher'])[1]
        original = copy.deepcopy(job)
        with patch.object(a.AgenticRunner, 'execute_job', return_value={'status': 'passed'}) as execute:
            runner.execute_job(job)
        self.assertEqual(execute.call_args.args[0]['configs'][0]['ubatch'], 512)
        self.assertEqual(job, original)
        runner.selection['models'][c.MODELS[1]]['ubatch'] = 256
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            runner.execute_job(job)

    def test_model_first_is_a_stable_permutation_with_unchanged_jobs(self):
        tasks = ['javascript/affine-cipher', 'rust/forth', 'javascript/bowling']
        old, new = c.suite_queue(tasks), c.suite_queue(tasks, 'model-first')
        self.assertEqual({j['id']: j for j in old}, {j['id']: j for j in new})
        self.assertEqual([j['model'] for j in new], [m for m in c.MODELS for _ in tasks])
        for model in c.MODELS:
            self.assertEqual([j['task'] for j in new if j['model'] == model], tasks)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            c.suite_queue(tasks, 'unknown')

    def test_predecessor_error_can_recover_without_losing_continuation(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            campaign, previous = Path(temp) / 'next', Path(temp) / 'previous'
            campaign.mkdir(); previous.mkdir()
            h.atomic_json(previous / 'state.json', {'status': 'error', 'completed': {}})
            def recover(_):
                h.atomic_json(previous / 'state.json', {'status': 'complete', 'completed': {}})
            with patch.object(a.time, 'sleep', side_effect=recover):
                self.assertTrue(a.wait_for_predecessor(campaign, previous, time.time() + 60))

    def test_persistent_predecessor_error_blocks_instead_of_waiting_all_day(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            campaign, previous = Path(temp) / 'next', Path(temp) / 'previous'
            campaign.mkdir(); previous.mkdir()
            h.atomic_json(previous / 'state.json', {'status': 'error', 'completed': {}})
            with patch.object(a.time, 'monotonic', side_effect=[0, 0, 181]), patch.object(a.time, 'sleep'):
                self.assertFalse(a.wait_for_predecessor(campaign, previous, time.time() + 1000))
            self.assertEqual(json.loads((campaign / 'stage-status.json').read_text())['phase'], 'blocked')

    def test_instrument_failure_stops_but_failed_task_is_still_measured(self):
        runner = c.ConfirmedRunner.__new__(c.ConfirmedRunner)
        job = c.suite_queue(['javascript/affine-cipher'])[0]
        for reason in ('probe_failed', 'probe_incomplete'):
            with patch.object(a.AgenticRunner, 'execute_job', return_value={'status': 'failed', 'reason': reason}):
                with self.assertRaises(h.StopCampaign):
                    runner.execute_job(job)
        row = {'status': 'passed', 'probe': {'task_success': False}}
        with patch.object(a.AgenticRunner, 'execute_job', return_value=row):
            self.assertEqual(runner.execute_job(job), row)

    def test_handoff_lock_race_retries_without_touching_the_other_server(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            runner = c.ConfirmedRunner.__new__(c.ConfirmedRunner)
            runner.path = Path(temp)
            runner.deadline = time.monotonic() + 300
            runner.lockfiles = []
            with patch.object(h.Runner, 'acquire', side_effect=[h.CampaignBusy('busy'), None]) as acquire, \
                    patch.object(c.time, 'sleep'):
                runner.acquire()
            self.assertEqual(acquire.call_count, 2)

    def test_fit_rejection_maps_to_declared_case_not_placeholder_prompt(self):
        row = observations()[1]
        job = {'id': 'one', 'task': row['task'], 'model': row['model'], 'seed': row['seed']}
        rejected = {**row, 'job': 'one-fit', 'target': 0, 'task': 'placeholder', 'seed': 1729,
                    'status': 'failed', 'reason': 'vram_reserve'}
        mapped = c.confirmation_observation(job, rejected)
        self.assertEqual((mapped['task'], mapped['seed']), (job['task'], job['seed']))
        self.assertEqual(rejected['task'], 'placeholder')
        rows = observations()
        rows[1] = mapped
        self.assertEqual(c.choose_microbatches(rows)[job['model']]['valid_repeats']['512'], 11)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            c.confirmation_observation(job, {**rejected, 'status': 'passed'})

    def test_selection_is_persisted_and_rejects_changed_evidence(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            source, campaign = Path(temp) / 'source', Path(temp) / 'campaign'
            source.mkdir(); campaign.mkdir()
            jobs = []
            for n, row in enumerate(observations()):
                job = {'id': str(n), 'task': row['task'], 'model': row['model'], 'seed': row['seed']}
                jobs.append(job)
                h.sealed_json(source / f'job-{n}.json', {**row, 'job': str(n), 'target': 98304})
            h.sealed_json(source / 'manifest.json', {'queue': jobs})
            selected = c.resolve_selection(source, campaign)
            self.assertEqual(c.resolve_selection(source, campaign), selected)
            changed = h.read_sealed(source / 'job-0.json')
            changed['prefill_tps'] += 1
            h.sealed_json(source / 'job-0.json', changed)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'changed after freezing'):
                c.resolve_selection(source, campaign)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()


from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import multiprocessing
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest import mock


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from scripts.instruments import emit


def _emit_many(path: str, prefix: str, count: int, start: object) -> None:
    context = emit.RunContext(out=path, lane="qwen", quant="Q4")
    start.wait()  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    for index in range(count):
        emit.emit(context, f"{prefix}-{index}", 0, task_success=True)


class EmitTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_row_shape_is_fixed_and_missing_values_are_null(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            output = Path(temp_dir) / "rows.jsonl"
            context = emit.RunContext(out=output, lane="qwen", quant="Q4")
            first = emit.emit(context, "alpha", 3, task_success=True)
            second = emit.emit(context, "beta", 3, notes="zażółć")

            rows = [json.loads(line) for line in output.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()]
            self.assertEqual(set(first), set(emit.RESULT_FIELDS))
            self.assertEqual(set(rows[0]), set(rows[1]))
            self.assertIsNone(rows[0]["decode_tps"])
            self.assertEqual(rows[1]["notes"], "zażółć")
            self.assertEqual(list(rows[0]), sorted(rows[0]))
            self.assertEqual(second["task_id"], "beta")

    def test_append_flush_fsync_and_lock(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            output = Path(temp_dir) / "rows.jsonl"
            context = emit.RunContext(out=output, lane="qwen", quant="Q4")
            real_fsync = os.fsync
            real_flock = emit.fcntl.flock
            with mock.patch("scripts.instruments.emit.os.fsync", wraps=real_fsync) as fsync_mock, mock.patch(
                "scripts.instruments.emit.fcntl.flock", wraps=real_flock
            ) as flock_mock:
                emit.emit(context, "one", 0)
                emit.emit(context, "two", 0)

            self.assertEqual(fsync_mock.call_count, 2)
            self.assertEqual(flock_mock.call_count, 4)
            self.assertEqual(len(output.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()), 2)

    def test_flock_contention_between_processes(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            output = str(Path(temp_dir) / "rows.jsonl")
            start = multiprocessing.Event()
            processes = [
                multiprocessing.Process(target=_emit_many, args=(output, prefix, 40, start))
                for prefix in ("left", "right")
            ]
            for process in processes:
                process.start()
            start.set()
            for process in processes:
                process.join(15)
                self.assertEqual(process.exitcode, 0)

            rows = [json.loads(line) for line in Path(output).read_text().splitlines()]
            self.assertEqual(len(rows), 80)
            self.assertEqual(len({row["task_id"] for row in rows}), 80)

    def test_metrics_parser_tolerates_labels_and_absence(self) -> None:
        body = b"""# TYPE llamacpp:prompt_tokens_seconds gauge
llamacpp:prompt_tokens_seconds{model=\"qwen\"} 123.5
llamacpp:predicted_tokens_seconds 45
"""
        response = mock.MagicMock()
        response.__enter__.return_value.read.return_value = body
        with mock.patch("scripts.instruments.emit.urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=response):
            self.assertEqual(
                emit.scrape_metrics("http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1"),
                {"prefill_tps": 123.5, "decode_tps": 45.0},
            )
        with mock.patch("scripts.instruments.emit.urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=OSError("offline")):
            self.assertEqual(emit.scrape_metrics("http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1"), {})

    def test_identity_fields_are_required(self) -> None:
        context = emit.RunContext(out="unused", lane="qwen", quant="Q4")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            emit.build_row(context, "", 0)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            emit.build_row(context, "task", None)  # type: ignore[arg-type]

    def test_lane_selects_one_codex_invocation_path(self) -> None:
        qwen = emit.codex_command("qwen", "medium")
        control = emit.codex_command("control", "high")
        frontier = emit.codex_command("frontier", "high")

        for command in (qwen, control, frontier):
            self.assertEqual(command[-1], "-", "prompt must arrive on stdin")

        # Local arms select by profile, never by -m, so the served alias stays uniform.
        self.assertIn("qwen38", qwen)
        self.assertNotIn("-m", qwen)

        # control is C2, the direct competitor; frontier is C3, the ceiling.
        self.assertIn("gpt-5.6-luna", control)
        self.assertIn("gpt-5.6-sol", frontier)
        self.assertNotEqual(control, frontier, "external lanes must not collapse to one model")

        for command in (control, frontier):
            self.assertNotIn("qwen38", command)

    def test_unknown_lane_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            emit.codex_command("local", "high")

    def test_recorded_model_matches_the_invoked_model(self) -> None:
        # A row that names a model the lane did not actually invoke corrupts the
        # analysis silently, so the mapping must have exactly one source.
        # Parse real arguments rather than hand-building a Namespace: a hand-built
        # one silently lacks each newly added context flag, so this test broke on the
        # addition of --engine instead of catching anything.
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        env = Path(tmp.name) / "env.json"
        env.write_text(json.dumps({"gpu": "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090", "gpu_count": 1}))
        for lane in ("qwen", "quant", "control", "frontier"):
            parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
            emit.add_context_arguments(parser)
            args = parser.parse_args(
                ["--lane", lane, "--quant", "Q4", "--run-index", "0", "--out", "unused",
                 "--env-json", str(env)]
            )
            context = emit.make_context(args)
            command = emit.codex_command(lane, "xhigh")
            if lane in {"qwen", "quant"}:
                self.assertEqual(context.model, "qwen3.8-27b")
            else:
                self.assertIn(context.model, command)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest import mock

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from scripts.analysis import engine_table


def row(label: str, gpu: str, decode: float, **extra: object) -> str:
    return json.dumps({"label": label, "gpu": gpu, "decode_tok_s": decode,
                       "completion_tokens": 100, "total_s": 2.0, **extra})


class GroupingTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def rows_from(self, *lines: str) -> dict:
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        speed = Path(tmp.name)
        (speed / "engines.jsonl").write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
        with mock.patch.object(engine_table, "SPEED", speed):
            return engine_table.rows()

    def test_one_label_on_two_cards_stays_two_rows(self) -> None:
        # The regression this guards: grouping on the label alone merged a quant
        # benched on two different boxes into one row and let the card read last win,
        # which averaged 70.4 and 40.6 into a 55.5 that described neither.
        data = self.rows_from(
            row("q4|mtp", "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5070 Ti", 70.4, gpu_count=2),
            row("q4|mtp", "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5060 Ti", 40.6, gpu_count=2),
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(data), 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            sorted(v["decode_tok_s"][0] for v in data.values()), [40.6, 70.4]
        )

    def test_one_card_at_two_power_caps_stays_two_rows(self) -> None:
        data = self.rows_from(
            row("q4|mtp", "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090", 60.1, power_limit_w=390.0),
            row("q4|mtp", "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090", 52.0, power_limit_w=250.0),
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(data), 2)

    def test_same_config_and_card_accumulates(self) -> None:
        data = self.rows_from(
            row("q4|mtp", "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090", 60.0, power_limit_w=390.0),
            row("q4|mtp", "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090", 62.0, power_limit_w=390.0),
        )
        self.assertEqual(len(data), 1)
        self.assertEqual(len(next(iter(data.values()))["decode_tok_s"]), 2)


class CardNameTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_canonical_nvidia_name(self) -> None:
        # The old parser took the first word and rendered this as "NVIDIA".
        self.assertEqual(engine_table.card_name("NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090", 1), "3090")

    def test_keeps_the_suffix(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(
            engine_table.card_name("NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5070 Ti", 1), "5070 Ti"
        )

    def test_drops_a_wattage_token_from_a_hand_typed_value(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(engine_table.card_name("RTX 3090 390W", 1), "3090")

    def test_counts_multi_gpu(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(
            engine_table.card_name("NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5070 Ti", 2), "2x5070 Ti"
        )

    def test_passes_a_bad_value_through_unchanged(self) -> None:
        # 36 historical rows hold a rental label here; normalising would hide them.
        self.assertEqual(
            engine_table.card_name("qwen38-2x5070ti-ttl4", None), "qwen38-2x5070ti-ttl4"
        )

    def test_missing_gpu_is_marked(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(engine_table.card_name(None, None), "?")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


"""freegpu.sh is the gate every measurement sits behind, and it reads nvidia-smi, which
prints one line per card. These pin the multi-GPU arithmetic and the fail-closed contract."""
import os
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
FREEGPU = ROOT / "serving" / "freegpu.sh"


class FreeGpuTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def run_with(self, smi_body: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
        """Run freegpu.sh against a stub nvidia-smi. sleep is stubbed too, or the
        held-memory path burns its full 60 s retry budget in the suite."""
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
            bindir = Path(td) / "bin"
            bindir.mkdir()
            (bindir / "nvidia-smi").write_text("#!/usr/bin/env bash\n" + smi_body)
            (bindir / "sleep").write_text("#!/usr/bin/env bash\nexit 0\n")
            # freegpu kills discovered engine PIDs; unit tests must never discover host processes.
            (bindir / "pgrep").write_text("#!/usr/bin/env bash\nexit 1\n")
            for f in bindir.iterdir():
                f.chmod(0o755)
            env = dict(os.environ, PATH=f"{bindir}:{os.environ['PATH']}", WORK=td)
            return subprocess.run([str(FREEGPU)], capture_output=True, text=True,
                                  env=env, timeout=120)

    def test_two_idle_cards_sum_below_the_threshold(self):
        p = self.run_with('printf "5\\n7\\n"\n')
        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stderr)
        self.assertIn("12 MiB", p.stdout)

    def test_one_busy_card_holds_the_gate_shut(self):
        """The single-GPU form errored on a two-line value and failed closed by accident;
        this must fail closed because the memory is actually held."""
        p = self.run_with('printf "5\\n8000\\n"\n')
        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 1)
        self.assertIn("GPU STILL HELD", p.stderr)

    def test_a_broken_nvidia_smi_is_not_read_as_free(self):
        p = self.run_with("exit 1\n")
        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 1)
        self.assertNotIn("gpu free", p.stdout)

    def test_single_card_still_passes(self):
        p = self.run_with('printf "3\\n"\n')
        self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0, p.stderr)
        self.assertIn("3 MiB", p.stdout)


from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from scripts.instruments import emit
from scripts.instruments import gpuenv

ONE_CARD = "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090, 580.173.02, 390.00 W\n"
TWO_CARDS = ONE_CARD + "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090, 580.173.02, 390.00 W\n"
NVCC = "Cuda compilation tools, release 13.0, V13.0.88\n"


class GpuEnvParseTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_parses_a_single_card(self) -> None:
        env = gpuenv.parse(ONE_CARD, NVCC)
        self.assertEqual(env["gpu"], "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090")
        self.assertEqual(env["gpu_count"], 1)
        self.assertEqual(env["gpu_driver"], "580.173.02")
        self.assertEqual(env["power_limit_w"], 390.0)
        self.assertEqual(env["cuda_toolkit"], "13.0.88")

    def test_counts_identical_cards(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(gpuenv.parse(TWO_CARDS, NVCC)["gpu_count"], 2)

    def test_refuses_mixed_cards(self) -> None:
        mixed = ONE_CARD + "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5070 Ti, 580.173.02, 300.00 W\n"
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            gpuenv.parse(mixed, NVCC)

    def test_refuses_one_card_model_at_two_power_caps(self) -> None:
        # The cap is the experiment variable; averaging or picking the first would
        # attribute a number to a wattage the card never ran at.
        skewed = ONE_CARD + "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090, 580.173.02, 350.00 W\n"
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            gpuenv.parse(skewed, NVCC)

    def test_mixed_capture_preserves_each_card_and_cap(self) -> None:
        mixed = ONE_CARD + "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 5070 Ti, 580.173.02, 300.00 W\n"
        env = gpuenv.parse(mixed, NVCC, allow_mixed=True)
        self.assertEqual(env["gpu_count"], 2)
        self.assertIsNone(env["power_limit_w"])
        self.assertEqual([d["power_limit_w"] for d in env["gpu_devices"]], [390.0, 300.0])
        self.assertIn("RTX 5070 Ti", env["gpu"])

    def test_cuda_visibility_requires_explicit_nvml_selection(self) -> None:
        with patch.dict(os.environ, {"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES": "0"}):
            with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
                gpuenv.capture()

    def test_device_selection_reaches_nvml(self) -> None:
        with patch.object(gpuenv, "_run", side_effect=[ONE_CARD, NVCC]) as run:
            self.assertEqual(gpuenv.capture("GPU-test")["gpu_count"], 1)
        self.assertIn("--id=GPU-test", run.call_args_list[0].args[0])

    def test_missing_nvcc_leaves_the_toolkit_unknown(self) -> None:
        # nvidia-smi's "CUDA Version" is the driver API level, not the toolkit, so
        # there is no second source to fall back on.
        self.assertIsNone(gpuenv.parse(ONE_CARD, None)["cuda_toolkit"])

    def test_absent_power_reading_is_null_not_zero(self) -> None:
        env = gpuenv.parse("NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090, 580.173.02, [N/A]\n", NVCC)
        self.assertIsNone(env["power_limit_w"])

    def test_no_gpus_is_an_error(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            gpuenv.parse("\n", NVCC)


class EnvChannelTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.dir = Path(tmp.name)

    def context(self, lane: str, *extra: str) -> emit.RunContext:
        parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
        emit.add_context_arguments(parser)
        args = parser.parse_args(
            ["--lane", lane, "--quant", "Q4", "--run-index", "0",
             "--out", str(self.dir / "out.jsonl"), *extra]
        )
        return emit.make_context(args)

    def write_env(self, **fields: object) -> Path:
        path = self.dir / "env.json"
        path.write_text(json.dumps({"gpu": "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090", **fields}))
        return path

    def test_capture_reaches_the_row(self) -> None:
        env = self.write_env(gpu_count=2, power_limit_w=390.0, gpu_driver="580.173.02")
        row = emit.build_row(self.context("quant", "--env-json", str(env)), "t", 0)
        self.assertEqual(row["gpu"], "NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090")
        self.assertEqual(row["gpu_count"], 2)
        self.assertEqual(row["power_limit_w"], 390.0)
        self.assertEqual(row["schema_version"], emit.SCHEMA_VERSION)

    def test_mixed_devices_reach_the_row(self) -> None:
        captured = gpuenv.parse(ONE_CARD + "RTX 2060 SUPER, 580.173.02, 175 W\n",
                                NVCC, allow_mixed=True)
        path = self.dir / "mixed.json"
        path.write_text(json.dumps(captured))
        row = emit.build_row(self.context("quant", "--env-json", str(path)), "t", 0)
        self.assertEqual(row["gpu_devices"], captured["gpu_devices"])
        self.assertIsNone(row["power_limit_w"])

    def test_local_lane_without_env_refuses(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            self.context("quant")

    def test_unreadable_env_refuses(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            self.context("quant", "--env-json", str(self.dir / "absent.json"))

    def test_env_naming_no_gpu_refuses(self) -> None:
        path = self.dir / "env.json"
        path.write_text(json.dumps({"gpu": None, "gpu_count": 1}))
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            self.context("quant", "--env-json", str(path))

    def test_hand_edited_field_refuses(self) -> None:
        # A field gpuenv.py does not produce means the file was written by hand,
        # which is the labeling channel this replaces.
        path = self.dir / "env.json"
        path.write_text(json.dumps({"gpu": "RTX 3090", "watts": 390}))
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            self.context("quant", "--env-json", str(path))

    def test_frontier_lane_needs_no_gpu(self) -> None:
        self.assertIsNone(self.context("frontier").gpu)

    def test_no_hosted_lane_demands_a_gpu_capture(self) -> None:
