        # An API lane has no card to capture, and the guard used to name only two lanes,
        # so every routed lane exited at launch asking for env.json.
        for lane in emit.LANE_MODELS:
            if lane in emit.LOCAL_LANES:
                continue
            self.assertIsNone(self.context(lane).gpu, lane)



class CodexEndpointTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """The probe must interrogate the endpoint Codex actually calls.

    A leg was served on 8010 while Codex was pinned to 8000. The quant guard confirmed a
    healthy server on 8010 and all 40 tasks then died calling 8000, so the run came back
    0/40 with rc=0 and every row blaming the model.
    """

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.home = Path(tmp.name)

    def write(self, provider: str = "vast", url: str = "http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1") -> None:
        (self.home / "qwen38.config.toml").write_text(f'model_provider = "{provider}"\n')
        (self.home / "config.toml").write_text(
            f'[model_providers.{provider}]\nbase_url = "{url}"\n'
        )

    def test_resolves_through_profile_and_provider(self) -> None:
        self.write()
        self.assertEqual(
            emit.codex_base_url(home=self.home), "http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1"
        )

    def test_missing_config_is_none_not_a_guess(self) -> None:
        self.assertIsNone(emit.codex_base_url(home=self.home))

    def test_unknown_provider_is_none(self) -> None:
        (self.home / "qwen38.config.toml").write_text('model_provider = "absent"\n')
        (self.home / "config.toml").write_text('[model_providers.vast]\nbase_url = "x"\n')
        self.assertIsNone(emit.codex_base_url(home=self.home))

    def test_the_real_config_still_resolves(self) -> None:
        # Guards against a Codex config change silently disabling the check.
        url = emit.codex_base_url()
        self.assertTrue(url is None or url.startswith("http"), url)

class ParallelLegProfileTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """Two legs at once need two local ports, and a profile is what picks one.

    A profile pins a provider and a provider pins one base_url, so every leg used to queue
    behind 8000. The guard that catches a served/called mismatch reads the same profile, so
    it must follow the selection rather than stay on the default.

    Driven through a subprocess rather than importlib.reload: reload mutates the module in
    place, so restoring the environment afterwards also rewrites the object under test and
    the assertions read the default no matter what was selected.
    """

    def probe(self, profile: str | None, expr: str) -> str:
        env = {**os.environ}
        env.pop("QWEN38_CODEX_PROFILE", None)
        if profile is not None:
            env["QWEN38_CODEX_PROFILE"] = profile
        out = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-c",
             "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')\nfrom scripts.instruments import emit; print(" + expr + ")"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60, env=env,
            cwd=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]),
        )
        self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
        return out.stdout.strip()

    def test_the_default_is_unchanged(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.probe(None, "emit.CODEX_PROFILE"), "qwen38")

    def test_the_selected_profile_reaches_the_codex_command(self) -> None:
        self.assertIn("qwen38b", self.probe("qwen38b", "emit.codex_command('quant','xhigh')"))

    def test_each_profile_resolves_to_its_own_port(self) -> None:
        # Two profiles on one port would silently share a tunnel, and each leg would
        # measure whichever server won the race.
        urls = {p: self.probe(p, "emit.codex_base_url()")
                for p in ("qwen38", "qwen38b", "qwen38c")}
        present = {p: u for p, u in urls.items() if u and u != "None"}
        self.assertEqual(len(present), 3, urls)
        self.assertEqual(len(set(present.values())), 3, present)


class ToolOutputCapTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """The cap is a leg variable, so it must be absent unless a leg asks for it."""

    def probe(self, value: str | None) -> str:
        env = {**os.environ}
        env.pop("QWEN38_TOOL_OUTPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT", None)
        if value is not None:
            env["QWEN38_TOOL_OUTPUT_TOKEN_LIMIT"] = value
        out = subprocess.run(
            [sys.executable, "-c",
             "import sys; sys.path.insert(0, '.')\nfrom scripts.instruments import emit; "
             "print(' '.join(emit.codex_command('glm-flash','xhigh')))"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60, env=env,
            cwd=str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]),
        )
        self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
        return out.stdout

    def test_absent_by_default(self) -> None:
        self.assertNotIn("tool_output_token_limit", self.probe(None))

    def test_present_when_the_leg_sets_it(self) -> None:
        self.assertIn("tool_output_token_limit=3000", self.probe("3000"))


class ApiLaneTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """The lanes the local card is measured against.

    Naming the provider is not optional: a slash-bearing id with no provider goes to
    Codex's default, which answers happily and produces rows labelled deepseek that
    were served by something else.
    """

    def test_a_routed_lane_names_its_provider(self) -> None:
        command = emit.codex_command("deepseek-flash", "xhigh")
        self.assertIn("deepseek/deepseek-v4-flash", command)
        self.assertIn("model_provider=openrouter", command)

    def test_a_first_party_lane_does_not(self) -> None:
        self.assertNotIn("model_provider=openrouter", emit.codex_command("frontier", "xhigh"))

    def test_the_local_lane_still_uses_its_profile(self) -> None:
        command = emit.codex_command("quant", "xhigh")
        self.assertIn("-p", command)
        self.assertNotIn("model_provider=openrouter", command)

    def test_both_tiers_are_reachable_and_distinct(self) -> None:
        self.assertNotEqual(emit.lane_model("deepseek-flash"), emit.lane_model("deepseek-pro"))

    def test_every_routed_lane_names_its_provider(self) -> None:
        # A lane added without the provider flag reaches Codex's default silently, so the
        # check is over the whole table rather than the two lanes that existed when it
        # was written.
        for lane, model in emit.LANE_MODELS.items():
            if "/" in model:
                command = emit.codex_command(lane, "xhigh")
                self.assertIn(model, command, lane)
                self.assertIn("model_provider=openrouter", command, lane)

    def test_the_new_cheap_tiers_are_distinct_routes(self) -> None:
        ids = {lane: emit.lane_model(lane)
               for lane in ("deepseek-flash", "deepseek-flash-vision", "glm-flash")}
        self.assertEqual(len(set(ids.values())), 3, ids)

    def test_every_lane_in_the_table_is_launchable(self) -> None:
        # The gap this closes: emit knew the deepseek lanes, argparse did not, so they were
        # unit-tested and still rejected at the command line.
        parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
        emit.add_context_arguments(parser)
        for lane in emit.LANE_MODELS:
            args = parser.parse_args(["--lane", lane, "--quant", "x", "--run-index", "0",
                                      "--out", "/tmp/x.jsonl"])
            self.assertEqual(args.lane, lane)

    def test_an_unknown_lane_still_refuses(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            emit.lane_model("deepseek-v9")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


"""Grading maps the harness's verdict onto rows, and refuses where it has none.

The failure this guards is the one the repo keeps paying for: an infra failure and a
wrong patch are the same empty result to anything that counts successes, and only one
of them belongs to the quant under test.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import contextlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from scripts.analysis import grade_swebench


def report(**kwargs) -> dict:
    base = {k: [] for k in ("resolved_ids", "unresolved_ids", "empty_patch_ids",
                            "error_ids", "incomplete_ids", "infra_failure_ids",
                            "ambiguous_failure_ids")}
    return base | kwargs


def row(task_id: str, **extra) -> dict:
    return {"task_id": task_id, "quant": "Q4", "run_index": 1,
            "task_success": None, "tests_passed": None, "notes": None} | extra


class VerdictTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_resolved_is_a_solve(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(grade_swebench.verdicts(report(resolved_ids=["a"]))["a"], (True, None))

    def test_unresolved_is_a_failure(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(grade_swebench.verdicts(report(unresolved_ids=["a"]))["a"], (False, None))

    def test_an_empty_patch_is_the_models_failure(self) -> None:
        # The model was asked and produced nothing. That is a result, not a gap.
        self.assertEqual(grade_swebench.verdicts(report(empty_patch_ids=["a"]))["a"], (False, None))

    def test_an_infra_failure_is_not_charged_to_the_model(self) -> None:
        # The harness leaves infra failures in unresolved_ids as well; reading that
        # list first would score a container that never built as a wrong patch.
        table = grade_swebench.verdicts(
            report(unresolved_ids=["a"], infra_failure_ids=["a"])
        )
        self.assertEqual(table["a"], (None, "infra_failure"))

    def test_an_ambiguous_failure_stays_an_unresolved_instance(self) -> None:
        # The harness separates ambiguous from confirmed-environment faults so the
        # ambiguous ones are never read as confirmed, and keeps them in unresolved_ids
        # on purpose. Excluding them here would discard failures the patch may have
        # caused -- the opposite bias to charging infra failures to the model.
        table = grade_swebench.verdicts(report(unresolved_ids=["a"], ambiguous_failure_ids=["a"]))
        self.assertEqual(table["a"], (False, None))

    def test_an_error_has_no_verdict(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(grade_swebench.verdicts(report(error_ids=["a"]))["a"], (None, "error"))

    def test_resolved_beats_a_stray_classification(self) -> None:
        # Tests that pass are the definition of solved, whatever else was logged.
        table = grade_swebench.verdicts(report(resolved_ids=["a"], ambiguous_failure_ids=["a"]))
        self.assertEqual(table["a"], (True, None))


class GradeTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_verdicts_reach_both_fields(self) -> None:
        graded, tally = grade_swebench.grade([row("a")], report(resolved_ids=["a"]))
        self.assertTrue(graded[0]["task_success"])
        self.assertTrue(graded[0]["tests_passed"])
        self.assertEqual(tally["resolved"], 1)

    def test_a_row_the_report_never_mentions_stays_ungraded(self) -> None:
        graded, tally = grade_swebench.grade([row("a")], report())
        self.assertIsNone(graded[0]["task_success"])
        self.assertIn("absent from the report", graded[0]["notes"])
        self.assertEqual(tally["ungraded"], 1)

    def test_an_existing_note_is_kept(self) -> None:
        graded, _ = grade_swebench.grade([row("a", notes="Codex produced an empty diff")],
                                         report(error_ids=["a"]))
        self.assertIn("Codex produced an empty diff", graded[0]["notes"])
        self.assertIn("ungraded: error", graded[0]["notes"])

    def test_the_input_rows_are_not_mutated(self) -> None:
        # --rows is the append-only record of what the run measured.
        original = row("a")
        grade_swebench.grade([original], report(resolved_ids=["a"]))
        self.assertIsNone(original["task_success"])


class CliTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.dir = Path(tmp.name)
        self.rows = self.dir / "leg.jsonl"
        self.rows.write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(r) for r in [
            row("a"), row("b"), row("c"),
            row("a", quant="IQ3"),  # a different leg in the same file
        ]) + "\n")
        self.report = self.dir / "r.json"
        self.report.write_text(json.dumps(report(
            resolved_ids=["a"], unresolved_ids=["b", "c"], infra_failure_ids=["c"])))

    def run_cli(self, *argv: str) -> str:
        out = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
            grade_swebench.main(list(argv))
        return out.getvalue()

    def base(self) -> list[str]:
        return ["--rows", str(self.rows), "--quant", "Q4", "--run-index", "1",
                "--report", str(self.report), "--out", str(self.dir / "g.jsonl")]

    def test_grades_only_the_named_leg(self) -> None:
        self.run_cli(*self.base())
        graded = [json.loads(l) for l in (self.dir / "g.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
        self.assertEqual({r["quant"] for r in graded}, {"Q4"})
        self.assertEqual(len(graded), 3)

    def test_the_pass_rate_excludes_ungraded_instances(self) -> None:
        # 1 resolved, 1 unresolved, 1 infra failure -> 1/2, not 1/3.
        text = self.run_cli(*self.base())
        self.assertIn("pass rate 0.500 over 2 graded", text)
        self.assertIn("1 ungraded", text)

    def test_an_unknown_leg_is_refused(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            self.run_cli("--rows", str(self.rows), "--quant", "Q6", "--run-index", "1",
                         "--report", str(self.report))

    def test_report_and_run_are_mutually_exclusive(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            self.run_cli(*self.base(), "--run")

    def test_a_file_that_is_not_a_report_is_refused(self) -> None:
        bad = self.dir / "bad.json"
        bad.write_text(json.dumps({"hello": "world"}))
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            self.run_cli("--rows", str(self.rows), "--quant", "Q4", "--run-index", "1",
                         "--report", str(bad))


class InfraFailureTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """An instance that never reached the model must not be graded, resumed past, or
    submitted as an empty patch -- three separate ways one bad clone becomes a data point
    attributed to the quant under test."""

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
        from scripts.instruments import run_swebench
        self.run_swebench = run_swebench
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.dir = Path(tmp.name)

    def test_an_infra_failure_is_marked_off_the_row(self) -> None:
        result = self.run_swebench.InstanceResult("x", "", {}, infra_failure=True)
        self.assertNotIn("infra_failure", result.measures)

    def test_resume_retries_an_infra_failure_but_not_an_empty_diff(self) -> None:
        out = self.dir / "rows.jsonl"
        out.write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(r) for r in [
            {"task_id": "clone-died", "quant": "Q", "run_index": 1,
             "task_success": None, "notes": "FileNotFoundError: disk full"},
            {"task_id": "no-diff", "quant": "Q", "run_index": 1,
             "task_success": None, "notes": "Codex produced an empty diff"},
            {"task_id": "other-leg", "quant": "IQ3", "run_index": 1,
             "task_success": None, "notes": "OSError: boom"},
        ]) + "\n")
        self.assertEqual(self.run_swebench.infra_failed_ids(out, "Q", 1), {"clone-died"})


class HarnessCommandTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.dir = Path(tmp.name)

    def test_the_run_is_scoped_to_the_submitted_instances(self) -> None:
        # The pinned export holds 300 instances; without --instance_ids the harness
        # reports the 248 nobody submitted as incomplete, hiding a real omission.
        predictions = self.dir / "p.jsonl"
        predictions.write_text("\n".join(json.dumps(
            {"instance_id": i, "model_name_or_path": "m", "model_patch": "x"})
            for i in ("b", "a")) + "\n")
        seen = {}

        def fake_run(command, check):
            seen["command"] = command
            (self.dir / "reports").mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            (self.dir / "reports" / "m.rid.json").write_text(json.dumps(report()))
            return None

        original = grade_swebench.subprocess.run
        grade_swebench.subprocess.run = fake_run
        self.addCleanup(lambda: setattr(grade_swebench.subprocess, "run", original))
        args = grade_swebench.build_parser().parse_args([
            "--rows", str(predictions), "--quant", "q", "--run-index", "0",
            "--predictions", str(predictions), "--run",
            "--report-dir", str(self.dir / "reports"), "--run-id", "rid"])
        grade_swebench.run_harness(args)
        command = seen["command"]
        at = command.index("--instance_ids")
        self.assertEqual(command[at + 1:at + 3], ["a", "b"])

    def test_an_empty_prediction_file_is_refused(self) -> None:
        predictions = self.dir / "p.jsonl"
        predictions.write_text("")
        args = grade_swebench.build_parser().parse_args([
            "--rows", str(predictions), "--quant", "q", "--run-index", "0",
            "--predictions", str(predictions), "--run", "--run-id", "rid"])
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            grade_swebench.run_harness(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import copy
import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch, MagicMock

from scripts.instruments import hillclimb as h
from scripts.instruments import hillclimb_probe as p


def safe_sample():
    return {"time": time.time(), "ram_available_mib": 8192,
            "gpus": {gpu: {"temperature.gpu": 50, "memory.free": 4000,
                            "power.limit": cap, "power.draw": 30}
                     for gpu, cap in zip(h.DEVICES, [150, 140])}}


class SafetyTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_safe(self):
        h.safety_check(safe_sample())

    def test_thermal_and_caps_stop_entire_campaign(self):
        for gpu in h.DEVICES:
            for field, value in [("temperature.gpu", 80), ("power.limit", 180)]:
                sample = safe_sample()
                sample["gpus"][gpu][field] = value
                with self.assertRaises(h.StopCampaign):
                    h.safety_check(sample)

    def test_vram_rejects_arm_without_relaxing_reserve(self):
        for gpu, minimum in zip(h.DEVICES, [512, 256]):
            sample = safe_sample()
            sample["gpus"][gpu]["memory.free"] = minimum
            h.safety_check(sample)
            sample["gpus"][gpu]["memory.free"] -= 1
            with self.assertRaises(h.RejectArm):
                h.safety_check(sample)
            h.safety_check(sample, reserves=False)

    def test_ram_and_missing_telemetry_fail_closed(self):
        sample = safe_sample()
        sample["ram_available_mib"] = 2047
        with self.assertRaises(h.StopCampaign):
            h.safety_check(sample)
        with patch.object(h.subprocess, "run", side_effect=OSError("NVML unavailable")):
            with self.assertRaises(h.StopCampaign):
                h.telemetry()

    def test_occupied_port_including_non_http_service(self):
        with socket.socket() as sock:
            sock.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
            with self.assertRaises(OSError):
                h.endpoint_free(sock.getsockname()[1])

    def test_closed_server_time_wait_is_not_an_occupied_listener(self):
        with socket.socket() as server:
            server.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
            server.bind(("127.0.0.1", 0))
            port = server.getsockname()[1]
            server.listen(1)
            with socket.create_connection(("127.0.0.1", port)) as client:
                accepted, _ = server.accept()
                accepted.close()
                self.assertEqual(client.recv(1), b"")
        h.endpoint_free(port)

    def test_exit_classes(self):
        self.assertEqual(h.classify_exit("CUDA out of memory"), "oom")
        self.assertEqual(h.classify_exit("operation not supported"), "unsupported")
        self.assertEqual(h.classify_exit("CUDA illegal memory access"), "gpu_fault")
        self.assertEqual(h.classify_exit("unexpected EOF"), "server_failure")

    def test_gpu_fault_overrides_earlier_unsupported_and_allocation_messages(self):
        prefix = "unsupported Responses tool type 'namespace' skipped\nfailed to allocate scratch\n"
        for failure in ("CUDA error: the launch timed out and was terminated",
                        "CUDA illegal memory access", "device-side assert triggered",
                        "NVRM: Xid (PCI:0000:08:00): 8", "unspecified launch failure"):
            self.assertEqual(h.classify_exit(prefix + failure), "gpu_fault")

    def test_benign_capability_warnings_do_not_classify_a_server_crash(self):
        self.assertEqual(h.classify_exit("unsupported Responses tool type 'namespace' skipped\n"
                                         "backend sampling not supported with SPLIT_MODE_TENSOR\n"
                                         "unexpected EOF"), "server_failure")


class PersistenceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_preparing_existing_campaign_is_idempotent_but_rejects_changes(self):
        jobs = [{"id": "one", "kind": "profile", "model": "unsloth-q4-m"}]
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            campaign = Path(temp)
            manifest = {"queue": jobs, "models": [], "max_attempts": 3}
            h.sealed_json(campaign / "manifest.json", manifest)
            h.atomic_json(campaign / "state.json", {"status": "running", "completed": {"one": "job-one.json"}})
            state = (campaign / "state.json").read_bytes()
            with patch.object(h.shutil, "copytree") as copytree:
                h.initialize(campaign, campaign, profiles=jobs, max_attempts=3)
                h.initialize(campaign, campaign)
            copytree.assert_not_called()
            self.assertEqual((campaign / "state.json").read_bytes(), state)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "different frozen queue"):
                h.initialize(campaign, campaign, profiles=[dict(jobs[0], id="two")])
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "different frozen retry policy"):
                h.initialize(campaign, campaign, max_attempts=5)
            with patch.object(h, "SERVER_EXTRA", ["-bs"]), \
                    self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "different extra server flags"):
                h.initialize(campaign, campaign)

    def test_profile_queue_validates_fractional_splits_and_dependencies(self):
        jobs = [{"id": "fit", "kind": "profile", "model": "unsloth-q4-xl",
                 "configs": [{"split": "69.5,30.5", "ubatch": 64}]},
                {"id": "compare", "kind": "profile", "model": "unsloth-q4-xl", "base_job": "fit"}]
        self.assertEqual(h.validate_profiles(jobs), jobs)
        for bad in [dict(jobs[0], id="../escape"), dict(jobs[0], base_job="missing"),
                    dict(jobs[0], configs=[{"split": "nan,1"}]),
                    dict(jobs[0], configs=[{"ubatch": 0}]),
                    dict(jobs[0], configs=[{"model": "/unreviewed/model"}])]:
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                h.validate_profiles([bad])

    def test_sealed_records_fail_on_corruption(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            path = Path(temp) / "result.json"
            h.sealed_json(path, {"status": "passed"})
            self.assertEqual(h.read_sealed(path), {"status": "passed"})
            path.write_text('{"status":"failed"}')
            with self.assertRaises(h.StopCampaign):
                h.read_sealed(path)

    def test_atomic_writes_leave_no_partial_target(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            path = Path(temp) / "state.json"
            h.atomic_json(path, {"completed": []})
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                h.atomic_json(path, {"bad": float("nan")})
            self.assertEqual(json.loads(path.read_text()), {"completed": []})

    def test_queue_is_finite_and_prioritizes_breadth(self):
        jobs = h.queue()
        self.assertEqual(len(jobs), len({j["id"] for j in jobs}))
        self.assertEqual([j["kind"] for j in jobs[:9]], ["smoke"] * 2 + ["control"] * 2 + ["screen"] * 5)
        self.assertEqual([j["model"] for j in jobs[4:9]], [m["id"] for m in h.MODELS[1:]])
        self.assertTrue(all(j.get("slots", 1) in (1, 2, 4) for j in jobs))

    def test_remaining_quants_queue_covers_each_model_without_fallback_sweeps(self):
        path = h.ROOT / "orchestration/local/remaining-quants-overnight.json"
        jobs = h.validate_profiles(json.loads(path.read_text()))
        models = {"unsloth-q4-m", "unsloth-iq4-xs", "jpetrina-iq4-pure"}
        tasks = {"javascript/affine-cipher", "javascript/bowling", "rust/word-count", "rust/forth"}
        self.assertEqual(len(jobs), 66)
        self.assertEqual({job["model"] for job in jobs[:3]}, models)
        for model in models:
            selected = [job for job in jobs if job["model"] == model]
            self.assertEqual(len(selected), 22)
            repeats = [job for job in selected if job["id"].startswith("repeat-")]
            self.assertEqual({(job["task"], job["seed"]) for job in repeats},
                             {(task, seed) for task in tasks for seed in (1730, 1731, 1732)})
            self.assertEqual({job["configs"][0]["slots"] for job in selected
                              if job["id"].startswith("slots-")}, {2, 4})
            self.assertEqual({job["configs"][0]["ubatch"] for job in selected
                              if job["id"].startswith("ubatch-")}, {64, 256, 512})
            self.assertEqual({job["configs"][0]["split"] for job in selected
                              if job["id"].startswith("split-")},
                             {"60,40", "65,35", "70,30", "75,25", "80,20"})
        for job in jobs:
            self.assertEqual(len(job["configs"]), 1)
            self.assertEqual(job["targets"], [98304])
            config = job["configs"][0]
            self.assertEqual(config["context"], 102400)
            self.assertEqual(config["maximum"], 3)
            self.assertEqual(config["mode"], "tensor")
            if not job["id"].startswith("slots-"):
                self.assertEqual(config["slots"], 1)
            if not job["id"].startswith("ubatch-"):
                self.assertEqual(config["ubatch"], 128)
            if not job["id"].startswith("split-"):
                self.assertEqual(config["split"], "69,31")

    def test_process_identity_detects_live_process(self):
        self.assertIsNotNone(h.proc_identity(os.getpid()))
        self.assertIsNone(h.proc_identity(99999999))


class RunnerTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        path = Path(self.temp.name)
        (path / "snapshot").mkdir()
        (path / "attempts").mkdir()
        (path / "artifacts").mkdir()
        manifest = {"port": 8081, "queue": [], "models": h.MODELS, "ratios": h.RATIOS}
        h.sealed_json(path / "manifest.json", manifest)
        h.atomic_json(path / "state.json", {"completed": {}, "status": "prepared", "owned": []})
        self.runner = h.Runner(path, 1)
        self.runner.last_report = time.monotonic()

    def test_deadline_and_stop_markers(self):
        self.runner.deadline = 0
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, "budget_expired"):
            self.runner.poll()
        self.runner.deadline = time.monotonic() + 100
        (self.runner.path / "STOP").touch()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, "requested_stop"):
            self.runner.poll()

    def test_profile_advances_only_after_safe_fit_and_keeps_depth_results(self):
        job = {"id": "profile", "kind": "profile", "model": "unsloth-q4-xl",
               "configs": [{"ubatch": 128}, {"ubatch": 64}], "targets": [32768, 98304]}
        with patch.object(self.runner, "ensure_model", return_value={"id": job["model"]}), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "attempt", side_effect=[
                    {"status": "failed", "reason": "oom"}, {"status": "passed"},
                    {"status": "passed", "target": 32768},
                    {"status": "passed", "target": 98304}]) as attempt:
            result = self.runner.execute_job(job)
        self.assertEqual([call.args[3] for call in attempt.call_args_list], [0, 0, 32768, 98304])
        self.assertEqual(len(result["probes"]), 2)

    def test_profile_does_not_inherit_failed_fit_or_overflow_context(self):
        job = {"id": "profile", "kind": "profile", "model": "unsloth-q4-xl", "base_job": "failed"}
        with patch.object(self.runner, "ensure_model") as ensure:
            self.assertEqual(self.runner.execute_job(job)["status"], "skipped")
            ensure.assert_not_called()
        job.pop("base_job")
        job["configs"] = [{"context": 102400}]
        job["targets"] = [102400]
        with patch.object(self.runner, "ensure_model", return_value={"id": job["model"]}):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.RejectArm, "exceeds_context"):
                self.runner.execute_job(job)
    def test_hung_child_is_killed_and_owned_record_cleared(self):
        child = self.runner.spawn([sys.executable, "-c", "import time; time.sleep(60)"],
                                  self.runner.path / "child.log")
        with patch.object(self.runner, "poll"):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.RejectArm, "subprocess_timeout"):
                self.runner.wait_child(child, 0.05)
        self.assertIsNotNone(child.poll())
        self.assertEqual(self.runner.state["owned"], [])

    def test_safety_stop_kills_child(self):
        child = self.runner.spawn([sys.executable, "-c", "import time; time.sleep(60)"],
                                  self.runner.path / "child.log")
        with patch.object(self.runner, "poll", side_effect=h.StopCampaign("thermal_limit")):
            with self.assertRaises(h.StopCampaign):
                self.runner.wait_child(child, 10)
        self.assertIsNotNone(child.poll())

    def test_completed_jobs_are_not_executed_on_resume(self):
        dest = self.runner.path / "job-done.json"
        h.sealed_json(dest, {"status": "passed"})
        self.runner.state["completed"] = {"done": dest.name}
        self.runner.manifest["queue"] = [{"id": "done", "kind": "control"}]
        with patch.object(self.runner, "acquire"), patch.object(self.runner, "execute_job") as execute:
            self.runner.run()
        execute.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(self.runner.state["status"], "complete")

    def test_sealed_job_is_recovered_after_crash_before_state_save(self):
        self.runner.manifest["queue"] = [{"id": "done", "kind": "profile"}]
        h.sealed_json(self.runner.path / "job-done.json", {"status": "passed", "decode_tps": 36})
        with patch.object(self.runner, "acquire"), patch.object(self.runner, "poll"), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "execute_job") as execute:
            self.runner.run()
        execute.assert_not_called()
        self.assertEqual(self.runner.state["completed"], {"done": "job-done.json"})

    def test_duplicate_coordinator_does_not_write_live_state_or_report(self):
        with patch.object(self.runner, "acquire", side_effect=h.CampaignBusy("busy")), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "save") as save, patch.object(self.runner, "report") as report:
            self.runner.run()
        save.assert_not_called()
        report.assert_not_called()

    def test_actual_gpu_lock_contention_preserves_state(self):
        state_path = self.runner.path / "state.json"
        before = state_path.read_bytes()
        with (self.runner.path / "qwen-hillclimb.lock").open("a") as owner:
            h.fcntl.flock(owner, h.fcntl.LOCK_EX | h.fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            with patch.dict(os.environ, {"XDG_RUNTIME_DIR": str(self.runner.path)}):
                self.runner.run()
        self.assertEqual(state_path.read_bytes(), before)
        self.assertFalse((self.runner.path / "REPORT.md").exists())

    def test_absolute_deadline_survives_new_runner_budget(self):
        with patch.object(h.time, "time", return_value=1000), \
                patch.object(h.time, "monotonic", return_value=100):
            runner = h.Runner(self.runner.path, 8, deadline_epoch=1060)
            expired = h.Runner(self.runner.path, 8, deadline_epoch=900)
        self.assertEqual(runner.deadline, 160)
        self.assertEqual(expired.deadline, 100)

    def test_transient_retries_are_bounded_backed_off_and_persist_across_resume(self):
        for reason in sorted(h.TRANSIENT_FAILURES):
            with self.subTest(reason=reason), \
                    patch.object(h, "endpoint_free", side_effect=h.RejectArm(reason)) as endpoint, \
                    patch.object(self.runner, "poll"), patch.object(self.runner, "sleep") as sleep, \
                    patch.object(self.runner, "event") as event:
                result = self.runner.attempt(reason, {"id": "test"}, {}, 0)
                self.assertEqual(result["reason"], reason)
                self.assertEqual(endpoint.call_count, 3)
                retries = [call for call in event.call_args_list if call.args[0] == "transient_retry"]
                self.assertEqual([call.kwargs["attempt"] for call in retries], [2, 3])
                for call, expected in zip(retries, (10, 30)):
                    self.assertAlmostEqual(call.kwargs["backoff_seconds"], expected, delta=1)
                resumed = h.Runner(self.runner.path, 1)
                self.assertEqual(resumed.attempt(reason, {"id": "test"}, {}, 0), result)
                self.assertEqual(endpoint.call_count, 3)
                self.assertTrue(sleep.called)

    def test_five_attempt_policy_and_deterministic_failures(self):
        self.runner.max_attempts = 5
        with patch.object(h, "endpoint_free", side_effect=h.RejectArm("server_failure")) as endpoint, \
                patch.object(self.runner, "poll"), patch.object(self.runner, "sleep"), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "event") as event:
            self.runner.attempt("five", {"id": "test"}, {}, 0)
            self.assertEqual(endpoint.call_count, 5)
            retries = [call for call in event.call_args_list if call.args[0] == "transient_retry"]
            self.assertEqual([call.kwargs["attempt"] for call in retries], [2, 3, 4, 5])
        for reason in ("oom", "unsupported", "vram_reserve: " + h.GPU0, "probe_incomplete"):
            with self.subTest(reason=reason), \
                    patch.object(h, "endpoint_free", side_effect=h.RejectArm(reason)) as endpoint, \
                    patch.object(self.runner, "poll"), patch.object(self.runner, "sleep"), \
                    patch.object(self.runner, "event"):
                self.runner.attempt(reason, {"id": "test"}, {}, 0)
                self.assertEqual(endpoint.call_count, 1)

    def test_retry_backoff_honors_safety_stop(self):
        def stop_backoff(seconds, reserves=True):
            if reserves:
                raise h.StopCampaign("thermal_limit")

        with patch.object(h, "endpoint_free", side_effect=h.RejectArm("server_failure")) as endpoint, \
                patch.object(self.runner, "poll"), patch.object(self.runner, "sleep", side_effect=stop_backoff), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "event"):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, "thermal_limit"):
                self.runner.attempt("safety", {"id": "test"}, {}, 0)
        self.assertEqual(endpoint.call_count, 1)

    def test_failed_fit_never_advances_to_depth(self):
        job = {"id": "screen-x", "kind": "screen", "model": "unsloth-q4-xl"}
        with patch.object(self.runner, "ensure_model", return_value={"id": job["model"]}), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "attempt", return_value={"status": "failed", "reason": "oom"}) as attempt:
            result = self.runner.execute_job(job)
        self.assertEqual(result["reason"], "no_safe_ratio")
        self.assertEqual(attempt.call_count, len(h.RATIOS))
        self.assertTrue(all(call.args[3] == 0 for call in attempt.call_args_list))

    def test_deterministic_oom_is_not_retried_after_resume(self):
        config = {"test": 1}
        spec = {"job": "test", "model": "test", "config": config, "target": 0,
                "task": h.TASKS[0], "seed": 1729}
        key = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(spec, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
        dest = self.runner.path / "attempts" / (key + "-01")
        dest.mkdir()
        h.sealed_json(dest / "result.json", {"status": "failed", "reason": "oom"})
        with patch.object(self.runner, "spawn") as spawn:
            result = self.runner.attempt("test", {"id": "test"}, config, 0)
        self.assertEqual(result["reason"], "oom")
        spawn.assert_not_called()

    def test_download_retries_resume_same_partial_and_verify_before_promotion(self):
        model = copy.deepcopy(h.MODELS[2])
        model["path"] = str(self.runner.path / "models" / "model.gguf")
        model["bytes"] = 3
        self.runner.manifest["models"] = [model]
        partial = Path(model["path"] + ".partial")

        def fake_worker(args, *unused, **kwargs):
            partial.write_bytes(b"abc")
            Path(args[args.index("--out") + 1]).write_text(json.dumps({"native_context": 262144, "block_count": 65, "architecture": "qwen35"}))
            return 0

        with patch.object(self.runner, "spawn") as spawn, \
                patch.object(self.runner, "wait_child", side_effect=[18, 0]), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "sleep"), patch.object(self.runner, "worker", side_effect=fake_worker):
            result = self.runner.ensure_model(model["id"])
        self.assertEqual(spawn.call_count, 2)
        self.assertIn("--continue-at", spawn.call_args.args[0])
        self.assertTrue(Path(result["path"]).exists())
        self.assertFalse(partial.exists())

    def test_bad_artifact_is_not_promoted(self):
        model = copy.deepcopy(h.MODELS[2])
        model["path"] = str(self.runner.path / "models" / "model.gguf")
        self.runner.manifest["models"] = [model]
        with patch.object(self.runner, "spawn"), patch.object(self.runner, "wait_child", return_value=0), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "worker", return_value=1):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.RejectArm, "artifact_verification_failed"):
                self.runner.ensure_model(model["id"])
        self.assertFalse(Path(model["path"]).exists())


    def test_manifest_server_extra_is_appended_to_the_launched_command(self):
        self.runner.manifest.update(binary="/fake/server", server_extra=["-bs", "--spec-mtp-fuse-catchup"])
        model = {"id": "test", "path": "/fake/model", "sha256": "test"}
        config = {"devices": [h.GPU0], "context": 4096, "slots": 1, "maximum": 3,
                  "mode": "layer", "split": "1", "ubatch": 512}
        def api(url, path, **kwargs):
            return {"status": "ok"} if path == "/health" else {
                "total_slots": 1, "default_generation_settings": {"n_ctx": 4096}, "model_path": "/fake/model"}
        server = MagicMock()
        server.poll.return_value = None
        with patch.object(h, "endpoint_free"), patch.object(h, "api", side_effect=api), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "spawn", return_value=server) as spawn, \
                patch.object(self.runner, "poll"), patch.object(self.runner, "sleep"), \
                patch.object(self.runner, "event"), patch.object(self.runner, "run_probe", return_value=1):
            self.runner.attempt("extra", model, config, 0)
        command = spawn.call_args[0][0]
        self.assertEqual(command[-2:], ["-bs", "--spec-mtp-fuse-catchup"])
        launch = h.read_sealed(next((self.runner.path / "attempts").glob("*/launch.json")))
        self.assertEqual(launch["command"], command)


class ProbeTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_incomplete_stream_is_persisted_and_classified_for_bounded_retry(self):
        response = MagicMock()
        response.__enter__.return_value = response
        response.status = 200
        response.headers = {}
        raw = b'data: {"choices":[{"delta":{"reasoning_content":"partial"}}]}\n'
        response.__iter__.return_value = iter([raw])
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp, patch.object(p.urllib.request, "urlopen", return_value=response):
            result = p.one_request("http://local", "task", 1, 2048, 0, threading.Barrier(1), Path(temp))
            self.assertEqual(result["failure_class"], "transport_failure")
            self.assertEqual((Path(temp) / "request-0.sse").read_bytes(), raw)

    def test_artifact_size_and_hash_checked_before_loading_parser(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            path = Path(temp) / "test.gguf"
            path.write_bytes(b"abc")
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "size"):
                p.inspect_model(path, "bad", 2, temp)
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "SHA256"):
                p.inspect_model(path, "bad", 3, temp)

    def test_prompt_count_includes_template(self):
        task = {"task_id": "javascript/affine-cipher", "prompt": "stub"}
        with patch.object(p.longctx, "build_padding", return_value=("x" * 2000, 2000)), \
                patch.object(p, "api", side_effect=lambda u, e, payload, **kw: {"prompt": "t" * 50 + payload["messages"][0]["content"]}), \
                patch.object(p.longctx, "_tokenize", side_effect=lambda text, url: len(text)):
            prompt, count = p.prepared_prompt("http://local", task, 1000)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(count, 968)
        self.assertLessEqual(count, 1000)
        self.assertEqual(count, len(prompt) + 50)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from scripts.corpora import make_subsets


def _jsonl(path: Path) -> list[dict]:
    return [
        json.loads(line)
        for line in path.read_text().splitlines()
        if line.strip()
    ]


def _l1_repos() -> set[str]:
    sources = json.loads((ROOT / "corpora" / "sources.json").read_text())
    return {
        item["repo"].casefold()
        for language in ("rust", "js-ts")
        for item in sources[language]
    }


def _l2_repos() -> set[str]:
    # Polyglot contributes only its own container repo, which cannot collide with
    # anything; asserting on it would pass vacuously. Diff-XYZ rows carry the real
    # upstream repository each diff came from, so that is the L2 exposure.
    rows = _jsonl(ROOT / "subsets" / "diffxyz.jsonl")
    return {row["repo"].casefold() for row in rows if row.get("repo")}


def _l3_repos() -> set[str]:
    return {row["repo"].casefold() for row in _jsonl(ROOT / "subsets" / "swebench.jsonl")}


class LeakagePartitionTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_layers_draw_from_disjoint_repositories(self) -> None:
        l1, l2, l3 = _l1_repos(), _l2_repos(), _l3_repos()

        # An empty or singleton set is disjoint from everything, so a selection bug
        # that emptied a layer would look like an enforced partition.
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(l1), 10, "L1 repo set implausibly small")
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(l2), 20, "L2 upstream repo set implausibly small")
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(l3), 3, "L3 repo set implausibly small")

        for name, overlap in (("L1/L2", l1 & l2), ("L1/L3", l1 & l3), ("L2/L3", l2 & l3)):
            self.assertFalse(overlap, f"{name} share repositories: {sorted(overlap)}")

    def test_overlap_detection_actually_fires(self) -> None:
        # The assertion above must be capable of failing, or it enforces nothing.
        l1 = _l1_repos()
        colliding = {next(iter(sorted(l1)))}
        self.assertTrue(l1 & colliding, "overlap detection failed on a planted collision")

    def test_agentic_subset_is_fifty_two_distinct_instances(self) -> None:
        # 50 is the floor a paired McNemar design needs to separate a 10 pp pass-rate
        # gap; unpaired the same gap would take ~376 per arm.
        rows = _jsonl(ROOT / "subsets" / "swebench.jsonl")
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 52)
        self.assertEqual(len({row["instance_id"] for row in rows}), 52)

    def test_the_agentic_subset_still_contains_the_original_ten(self) -> None:
        # Widening must extend the instance list, never resample it: legs already run
        # against the first ten stay comparable only if those ten are still in it.
        rows = _jsonl(ROOT / "subsets" / "swebench.jsonl")
        self.assertTrue(
            set(make_subsets.ORIGINAL_AGENTIC_IDS) <= {row["instance_id"] for row in rows}
        )

    def test_no_repo_supplies_more_than_three_instances(self) -> None:
        rows = _jsonl(ROOT / "subsets" / "swebench.jsonl")
        worst = Counter(row["repo"] for row in rows).most_common(1)[0]
        self.assertLessEqual(worst[1], 3, worst)

    def test_every_instance_stays_inside_the_discriminating_window(self) -> None:
        # Outside 0.25-0.80 an instance scores the same for every quant and separates
        # nothing, which is the whole reason the subset is filtered rather than sampled.
        for row in _jsonl(ROOT / "subsets" / "swebench.jsonl"):
            self.assertGreaterEqual(row["solve_rate"], 0.25, row["instance_id"])
            self.assertLessEqual(row["solve_rate"], 0.80, row["instance_id"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


"""The liveness gate between tasks.

Run 8 lost its server at task 8 of 40. The loop wrote 33 rows with null token counts,
returned 0, and the driver tore down the box, so the crash reason was unrecoverable.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import http.server
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import threading
import unittest
from unittest import mock

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from scripts.instruments import emit, run_polyglot


class Result:
    def __init__(self, task_id: str) -> None:
        self.task_id = task_id
        self.measures: dict[str, object] = {}


TASKS = [{"task_id": f"t{i}"} for i in range(5)]


class RunLegTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def leg(self, alive, metrics_url: str = "http://x/v1", jobs: int = 1):
        recorded: list[str] = []
        ran: list[str] = []

        def worker(task):
            ran.append(task["task_id"])
            return Result(task["task_id"])

        with mock.patch.object(run_polyglot, "server_alive", alive):
            rc = run_polyglot.run_leg(
                TASKS, worker, lambda r: recorded.append(r.task_id), metrics_url, jobs
            )
        return rc, ran, recorded

    def test_healthy_server_runs_every_task(self) -> None:
        rc, ran, recorded = self.leg(lambda url: True)
        self.assertEqual(rc, 0)
        self.assertEqual(recorded, [t["task_id"] for t in TASKS])

    def test_death_mid_leg_stops_instead_of_recording_failures(self) -> None:
        calls = {"n": 0}

        def alive(url: str) -> bool:
            calls["n"] += 1
            return calls["n"] <= 2

        rc, ran, recorded = self.leg(alive)
        self.assertEqual(rc, 5)
        # The two tasks that ran against a live server are kept; nothing after is invented.
        self.assertEqual(recorded, ["t0", "t1"])
        self.assertEqual(ran, ["t0", "t1"])

    def test_no_further_tasks_run_after_the_abort(self) -> None:
        # With jobs > 1 the remaining tasks are already queued; the event must stop them
        # calling the model rather than letting them each burn a reconnect timeout.
        calls = {"n": 0}
        lock = threading.Lock()

        def alive(url: str) -> bool:
            with lock:
                calls["n"] += 1
                return calls["n"] <= 1

        rc, ran, _ = self.leg(alive, jobs=4)
        self.assertEqual(rc, 5)
        self.assertLessEqual(len(ran), 1)

    def test_external_lane_has_no_endpoint_to_check(self) -> None:
        def alive(url: str) -> bool:
            raise AssertionError("must not probe when there is no local server")

        rc, _, recorded = self.leg(alive, metrics_url="")
        self.assertEqual(rc, 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(recorded), len(TASKS))


class ServerAliveTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """Against a real socket, so the URL shape and the failure path are both exercised."""

    class Handler(http.server.BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
        body = b'{"data": [{"id": "m"}]}'

        def do_GET(self) -> None:  # noqa: N802
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(self.body)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(self.body)

        def log_message(self, *args: object) -> None:
            pass

    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.server = http.server.HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), self.Handler)
        threading.Thread(target=self.server.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        self.url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{self.server.server_address[1]}/v1"

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.server.shutdown()
        self.server.server_close()

    def test_answering_server_is_alive(self) -> None:
        self.assertTrue(emit.server_alive(self.url))

    def test_dead_port_is_not_alive(self) -> None:
        dead = http.server.HTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), self.Handler)
        url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{dead.server_address[1]}/v1"
        dead.server_close()
        self.assertFalse(emit.server_alive(url, attempts=1, timeout=1.0))

    def test_a_single_blip_does_not_end_a_leg(self) -> None:
        # One dropped probe must not discard the remaining 30-odd tasks of a rented hour.
        calls = {"n": 0}
        real = emit.urllib.request.urlopen

        def flaky(url, timeout=None):
            calls["n"] += 1
            if calls["n"] == 1:
                raise OSError("connection reset")
            return real(url, timeout=timeout)

        with mock.patch.object(emit.urllib.request, "urlopen", flaky):
            self.assertTrue(emit.server_alive(self.url, attempts=3, timeout=2.0))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


"""The shell layer's wait primitives, driven through bash.

Only the two functions with real logic are tested. `say` is a wrapper and a test of it
would assert that echo echoes; the value here is that `proc_state` distinguishes three
outcomes, because collapsing "ssh failed" into "process is gone" is what recorded a
context rung as refused and put the wrong ceiling in a results table.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
LIB = ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "lib.sh"


def run(script: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    return subprocess.run(
        ["bash", "-c", f'set -uo pipefail\n. "{LIB}"\n{script}'],
        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60,
    )


class ProcStateTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """A fake runner stands in for ssh, so the three outcomes are reachable offline."""

    def state(self, runner_body: str) -> int:
        return run(
            f"runner () {{ {runner_body}; }}\nproc_state runner someproc\necho \"rc=$?\""
        ).stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1].removeprefix("rc=")

    def test_running_is_zero(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.state('echo UP'), "0")

    def test_gone_is_one(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.state('echo DOWN'), "1")

    def test_ssh_failure_is_unknown_not_gone(self) -> None:
        # ssh exits 255 for any client-side failure. Reading that as "the process died"
        # is the bug: a transient drop then ends a wait loop early.
        self.assertEqual(self.state('return 255'), "2")

    def test_unrecognised_output_is_unknown(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.state('echo something-else'), "2")

    def test_empty_output_is_unknown(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.state('true'), "2")


class AwaitServerTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def await_with(self, curl_body: str, runner_body: str, expect: str = "") -> str:
        script = f"""
        curl () {{ {curl_body}; }}
        runner () {{ {runner_body}; }}
        await_server 65535 2 0 runner someproc {expect}
        echo "rc=$?"
        """
        return run(script).stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1].removeprefix("rc=")

    def test_healthy_without_identity_check(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.await_with('echo ok', 'echo UP'), "0")

    def test_confirmed_dead_returns_one(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.await_with('true', 'echo DOWN'), "1")

    def test_ssh_flake_keeps_waiting_then_times_out(self) -> None:
        # Must exhaust its tries rather than return 1 on the first failed probe.
        self.assertEqual(self.await_with('true', 'return 255'), "1")

    def test_stale_server_serving_another_model_is_rejected(self) -> None:
        # A server that outlived its kill answers /health from its previous config.
        out = run(f"""
        curl () {{ echo ok; }}
        served_model () {{ echo "/models/OLD-QUANT.gguf"; }}
        runner () {{ echo UP; }}
        await_server 65535 1 0 runner someproc NEW-QUANT
        echo "rc=$?"
        """).stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1]
        self.assertEqual(out, "rc=3")

    def test_matching_model_is_accepted(self) -> None:
        out = run(f"""
        curl () {{ echo ok; }}
        served_model () {{ echo "/models/NEW-QUANT.gguf"; }}
        runner () {{ echo UP; }}
        await_server 65535 1 0 runner someproc NEW-QUANT
        echo "rc=$?"
        """).stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1]
        self.assertEqual(out, "rc=0")


class TunnelPatternTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """box.sh's tunnel gate must match a real ssh command line.

    The first version matched "ssh -N -L <port>:", which cannot occur: SSH_OPTS are
    interpolated between `ssh` and `-N`. As a stale-tunnel killer that failed silently;
    promoted to a success gate it failed every launch.
    """

    ARGV = ("ssh -i /home/u/.ssh/id_ed25519_vast -o IdentitiesOnly=yes "
            "-o StrictHostKeyChecking=no -N -L 127.0.0.1:8010:127.0.0.1:8000 "
            "-o ExitOnForwardFailure=yes -p 55192 root@ssh9.vast.ai")

    def test_the_old_pattern_could_never_match(self) -> None:
        self.assertNotIn("ssh -N -L 8010:", self.ARGV)

    def test_the_forward_spec_matches(self) -> None:
        self.assertIn("-L 127.0.0.1:8010:127.0.0.1:8000", self.ARGV)

    def test_box_sh_uses_the_forward_spec(self) -> None:
        box = (ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh").read_text()
        self.assertIn('-L 127.0.0.1:$lport:127.0.0.1:$rport', box)
        self.assertNotIn('pgrep -f "ssh -N -L', box)

    def test_box_sh_gates_on_port_ownership(self) -> None:
        # A bare connect test cannot tell our forward from a squatter, and checking our
        # ssh is alive races its own startup. Both were tried against a real squatted
        # port and both passed when they should have failed.
        box = (ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh").read_text()
        self.assertIn("ss -ltnpH", box)
        self.assertIn("/proc/$pid/cmdline", box)
        self.assertNotIn("/dev/tcp/127.0.0.1/$lport", box)

    def test_forward_binds_one_explicit_address(self) -> None:
        # "-L $lport:" binds 127.0.0.1 and ::1 both, so a squatter holding only IPv4 lets
        # ssh bind IPv6 and look healthy while clients still reach the squatter.
        box = (ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh").read_text()
        self.assertIn('spec="-L 127.0.0.1:$lport:127.0.0.1:$rport"', box)


class RentPriceCapTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """The retry loop walks *up* the price-sorted offer list.

    Two machines that took a rental and then refused the ssh key pushed one arm from the
    $0.115 market floor to $0.412/hr, and nothing in the loop noticed it had left the band
    the arm was budgeted for.
    """

    BOX = (ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh").read_text()

    def test_the_cap_is_applied_before_the_first_offer_is_taken(self) -> None:
        self.assertIn("dphmax = float(os.environ['DPHMAX'])", self.BOX)
        self.assertIn("if (o.get('dph_total') or 1e9) <= dphmax", self.BOX)

    def test_the_cap_has_a_default_so_it_binds_unset(self) -> None:
        # An env-only cap protects nothing: every existing caller predates it.
        self.assertIn("DPHMAX=${DPHMAX:-0.22}", self.BOX)

    def test_no_offer_under_the_cap_is_a_refusal_not_an_escalation(self) -> None:
        self.assertIn("no offer for $gpu x$n under", self.BOX)


class RentPatienceTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """RENT_CAP must outlast a cold host's image pull.

    At 60 ticks (20 min) a 3090 was destroyed mid-`Pulling fs layer`, after paying for the
    rental and most of a ~5 GB cudnn-devel download, and the retry drew another cold machine.
    """

    BOX = (ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh").read_text()

    def test_the_wall_clock_budget_covers_a_cold_pull(self) -> None:
        self.assertIn("${RENT_CAP:-90}", self.BOX)

    def test_reachability_probes_stay_separately_bounded(self) -> None:
        # One budget for both would let a slow pull spend the probe allowance and
        # condemn the machine as having refused the key.
        self.assertIn("${RENT_WAIT:-15}", self.BOX)
        self.assertIn('if [ "$state" = running ]; then', self.BOX)


class SkipListTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """Machines that refuse the ssh key must stay excluded across runs, but not forever.

    28852 and 27960 each took a rental and refused the key twice in one day, because
    `skip` lived only for the run that discovered them. A permanent denylist is the wrong
    answer -- the fault is usually transient -- so quarantine entries age out.
    """

    BOX = ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh"

    def skip_list(self, quarantine: str, exclude: str = "", ttl: int = 14400) -> str:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            f = Path(d) / "q.log"
            f.write_text(quarantine)
            out = subprocess.run(
                ["bash", str(self.BOX), "skiplist"],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60,
                env={**os.environ, "QUARANTINE": str(f),
                     "QUARANTINE_TTL": str(ttl), "EXCLUDE_MACHINES": exclude},
            )
            # Not `2>/dev/null`: box.sh dies at `cmd=$1` under `set -u` when sourced, and
            # a silenced failure returns "" -- which two of these tests assert.
            self.assertEqual(out.returncode, 0, out.stderr)
            return out.stdout.strip()

    def fresh(self, *machines: int) -> str:
        now = int(time.time())
        return "".join(f"{now - 60} {m}\n" for m in machines)

    def stale(self, *machines: int) -> str:
        old = int(time.time()) - 20000        # older than the 4h default
        return "".join(f"{old} {m}\n" for m in machines)

    def test_a_recent_failure_is_skipped(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.skip_list(self.fresh(28852)), "28852")

    def test_an_expired_failure_comes_back(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.skip_list(self.stale(28852)), "")

    def test_the_permanent_set_and_the_quarantine_merge(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(
            self.skip_list(self.fresh(28852), exclude="28070,28077"),
            "28070,28077,28852",
        )

    def test_a_machine_in_both_appears_once(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.skip_list(self.fresh(28070), exclude="28070"), "28070")

    def test_no_quarantine_file_is_empty_not_an_error(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.skip_list(""), "")

    def test_a_repeat_offender_is_listed_once(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(self.skip_list(self.fresh(28852) * 3), "28852")


class PowerCapTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """A throttled card must not take a throughput leg.

    Run 10 drew a 3090 capped at 200 W to pair against a 390 W baseline. Decode is what the
    leg measures, so the rows measure the cap; they are void.
    """

    BOX = (ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh").read_text()
    DRIVER = (ROOT / "orchestration" / "session2" / "polyglot-cost.sh").read_text()

    def test_the_offer_filter_reads_the_cap(self) -> None:
        self.assertIn("powermin = float(os.environ['POWERMIN'])", self.BOX)
        self.assertIn("and (o.get('gpu_max_power') or 0) >= powermin", self.BOX)

    def test_it_defaults_off_so_fit_only_arms_are_unaffected(self) -> None:
        # A context-ceiling ladder does not care about wattage, and a floor it did not ask
        # for would shrink its pool for nothing.
        self.assertIn("POWERMIN=${POWERMIN:-0}", self.BOX)

    def test_the_speed_leg_sets_a_floor(self) -> None:
        self.assertIn("export POWERMIN=${POWERMIN:-340}", self.DRIVER)


class StrictModesRepairTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """Some hosts deliver the key with ownership OpenSSH StrictModes rejects.

    Measured: `vastai logs` on such a box shows `Authentication refused: bad ownership or
    modes for file /root/.ssh/authorized_keys` beside `Failed publickey` for our exact
    fingerprint, while a healthy box logs `Accepted key ... found at
    /root/.ssh/authorized_keys:1`. An onstart that only chmods still fails; chown fixes it.
    """

    BOX = (ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh").read_text()

    def test_the_instance_repairs_ownership_not_just_mode(self) -> None:
        self.assertIn("chown -R root:root /root/.ssh", self.BOX)
        self.assertIn("chmod 600 /root/.ssh/authorized_keys", self.BOX)

    def test_the_repair_is_bounded(self) -> None:
        # The file appears after onstart starts, so a single pass can miss it; an unbounded
        # loop would outlive the rent window and keep a core busy for the whole leg.
        self.assertIn("for i in $(seq 1 60); do chown", self.BOX)

    def test_a_failed_draw_records_why(self) -> None:
        # "never accepted the key" cost five rentals because the reason lives only in the
        # container's sshd log, which the destroy threw away.
        self.assertIn('vastai logs "$id"', self.BOX)
        self.assertIn("Failed publickey|Accepted key", self.BOX)


class SyntaxTest(unittest.TestCase):
    # Recursive, and floored: a flat glob silently stops covering shell the moment a file
    # moves into a subdirectory, and the test keeps passing. Raise the floor when adding
    # shell; lower it only alongside a deletion.
    SHELL_DIRS = ("scripts", "serving", "orchestration")
    SHELL_FLOOR = 58

    def test_every_shell_file_parses(self) -> None:
        files = sorted(p for d in self.SHELL_DIRS for p in (ROOT / d).rglob("*.sh"))
        self.assertGreaterEqual(len(files), self.SHELL_FLOOR)
        for path in files:
            with self.subTest(path=str(path.relative_to(ROOT))):
                self.assertEqual(
                    subprocess.run(["bash", "-n", str(path)],
                                   capture_output=True, text=True).returncode,
                    0, f"{path} failed bash -n",
                )



class NoArgvTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """Every driver rents a box within seconds of starting, so argv must be refused before
    the spend. `./serve --help` rented one before this guard existed."""

    DRIVERS = [ROOT / "orchestration" / "serve-box.sh", *sorted(
        (ROOT / "orchestration" / "session2").glob("*.sh"))]

    def test_help_prints_the_header_and_exits_clean(self):
        for path in self.DRIVERS:
            with self.subTest(driver=path.name):
                p = subprocess.run([str(path), "--help"], capture_output=True,
                                   text=True, timeout=60)
                self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 0)
                self.assertGreater(len(p.stdout.strip().splitlines()), 3)
                self.assertNotIn("#", p.stdout.splitlines()[0])

    def test_any_positional_argument_is_refused(self):
        for path in self.DRIVERS:
            with self.subTest(driver=path.name):
                p = subprocess.run([str(path), "bogus"], capture_output=True,
                                   text=True, timeout=60)
                self.assertEqual(p.returncode, 2)
                self.assertIn("takes no arguments", p.stderr)

    def test_every_renting_driver_installs_the_guard(self):
        """A new driver that forgets no_argv is the failure this class exists to prevent."""
        for path in self.DRIVERS:
            with self.subTest(driver=path.name):
                self.assertIn('no_argv "$@"', path.read_text())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import sys
import unittest
import unittest.mock
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
from scripts.instruments import longctx  # noqa: E402


class ForbiddenTermsTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_covers_the_slug_and_its_identifier_spellings(self) -> None:
        terms = longctx.forbidden_terms({"task_id": "rust/robot-name"})
        self.assertIn("robot-name", terms)
        self.assertIn("robot_name", terms)
        self.assertIn("robotname", terms)


class LeakGuardTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_padding_naming_the_exercise_aborts(self) -> None:
        """Padding that mentions the exercise would hand over the answer.

        The guard must fail the run rather than quietly measure with it.
        """

        original = longctx.CORPORA.copy()
        try:
            import tempfile

            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
                poisoned = Path(directory) / "rust.txt"
                poisoned.write_text("fn robot_name() {}\n" * 4000)
                longctx.CORPORA["rust"] = poisoned
                with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
                    longctx.build_padding(
                        {"task_id": "rust/robot-name", "language": "rust"},
                        1000,
                        "http://127.0.0.1:9/v1",
                    )
        finally:
            longctx.CORPORA.clear()
            longctx.CORPORA.update(original)


class WrapTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_instructions_come_last_so_recency_favours_success(self) -> None:
        wrapped = longctx.wrap_prompt("SOLVE THIS", "PAD" * 100)
        self.assertTrue(wrapped.rstrip().endswith("SOLVE THIS"))
        self.assertLess(wrapped.index("PAD"), wrapped.index("SOLVE THIS"))

    def test_zero_padding_leaves_the_prompt_untouched(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(longctx.wrap_prompt("SOLVE THIS", ""), "SOLVE THIS")


class DeterminismTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_offset_is_a_function_of_the_task_id_alone(self) -> None:
        # Padding must be identical for the same task across quants and reruns,
        # or the comparison stops being paired.
        import hashlib

        a = int(hashlib.sha256(b"rust/forth").hexdigest()[:12], 16)
        b = int(hashlib.sha256(b"rust/forth").hexdigest()[:12], 16)
        c = int(hashlib.sha256(b"rust/bowling").hexdigest()[:12], 16)
        self.assertEqual(a, b)
        self.assertNotEqual(a, c)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


class OffsetRetryTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_a_leaking_offset_is_replaced_not_fatal(self) -> None:
        """One unlucky offset used to abort the sweep, taking every task behind it."""

        task = {"task_id": "javascript/binary", "language": "javascript"}
        text = longctx.CORPORA["javascript"].read_text()
        banned = longctx.forbidden_terms(task)

        def leaks(attempt: int) -> bool:
            seed = task["task_id"] if attempt == 0 else f"{task['task_id']}:{attempt}"
            start = int(hashlib.sha256(seed.encode()).hexdigest()[:12], 16) % (len(text) - 1)
            window = (text[start:] + text[:start])[: int(64000 * 4.0)]
            return bool(banned & {m.group(0).lower() for m in longctx._WORD.finditer(window)})

        self.assertTrue(leaks(0), "fixture no longer leaks; the retry path is untested")
        self.assertFalse(all(leaks(a) for a in range(longctx._OFFSET_ATTEMPTS)))

    def test_exhausting_every_offset_still_fails_loudly(self) -> None:
        # Retrying must not become a way to silently accept leaked padding.
        task = {"task_id": "rust/x", "language": "rust", "solution_path": ["src/lib.rs"]}
        with unittest.mock.patch.object(longctx, "forbidden_terms", return_value={"the"}):
            with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
                longctx.build_padding(task, 4000, "http://127.0.0.1:1")


from __future__ import annotations

from collections import Counter
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from scripts.corpora import make_subsets


class FrozenSubsetsTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_codegen_is_ranked_per_language_and_runner_compatible(self) -> None:
        tasks = json.loads((ROOT / "subsets" / "polyglot.json").read_text())
        self.assertEqual(len(tasks), 78)
        self.assertEqual(Counter(task["language"] for task in tasks), {"rust": 30, "javascript": 48})
        for language in ("rust", "javascript"):
            selected = [task for task in tasks if task["language"] == language]
            self.assertEqual([task["rank"] for task in selected], sorted(task["rank"] for task in selected))
            for task in selected:
                self.assertEqual(
                    task["rank"], make_subsets._rank("codegen", task["task_id"])
                )
                self.assertTrue(task["task_id"].startswith(language + "/"))
                self.assertIn("solution_path", task)
                self.assertIn("example_path", task)
        self.assertTrue(
            all(task["test_cmd"] == ["cargo", "test", "--", "--include-ignored"] for task in tasks[:20])
        )

    def test_diffxyz_has_two_targets_and_caps_each_repo(self) -> None:
        rows = [json.loads(line) for line in (ROOT / "subsets" / "diffxyz.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 50)
        self.assertEqual(Counter(row["language"] for row in rows), {"rust": 25, "javascript": 25})
        for language in ("rust", "javascript"):
            language_rows = [row for row in rows if row["language"] == language]
            self.assertLessEqual(
                max(Counter(row["repo"].casefold() for row in language_rows).values()), 3
            )
        for row in rows:
            self.assertIn("udiff", row)
            self.assertIn("search-replace", row)
            self.assertEqual(row["rank"], make_subsets._rank("diffxyz", row["task_id"]))
            self.assertTrue(200 <= len(row["old_code"]) <= 12_000)

    def test_agentic_ids_and_images_are_exact(self) -> None:
        rows = [json.loads(line) for line in (ROOT / "subsets" / "swebench.jsonl").read_text().splitlines()]
        self.assertEqual([row["instance_id"] for row in rows], make_subsets.EXPECTED_AGENTIC_IDS)
        for row in rows:
            expected = (
                "swebench/sweb.eval.x86_64."
                + row["instance_id"].replace("__", "_1776_")
                + ":latest"
            )
            self.assertEqual(row["image"], expected)
            self.assertTrue(0.25 <= row["solve_rate"] <= 0.80)

    def test_build_hashes_cover_frozen_outputs(self) -> None:
        build = json.loads((ROOT / "subsets" / "BUILD.json").read_text())
        for name, recorded in build["outputs"].items():
            data = (ROOT / "subsets" / name).read_bytes()
            self.assertEqual(recorded["byte_count"], len(data))
            self.assertEqual(recorded["sha256"], hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest import mock

from scripts.analysis import night_tuning_loop as loop


class NightLoopTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_deadline_rolls_to_next_morning(self):
        now = datetime(2026, 9, 15, 23, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        self.assertEqual(loop.morning_deadline(now).isoformat(),
                         "2026-09-16T08:00:00+00:00")

    def test_snapshot_marks_running_campaign_actionable(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            path = Path(tmp) / "campaign-swift-tuning-x" / "state.json"
            path.parent.mkdir()
            path.write_text(json.dumps({"status": "running", "completed": {}}))
            with mock.patch.object(loop, "units", return_value=[]), \
                    mock.patch.object(loop, "gpu_processes", return_value=[]):
                self.assertEqual(loop.snapshot(Path(tmp))["reason"], "campaign_running")

    def test_snapshot_is_idle_without_campaign(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            with mock.patch.object(loop, "units", return_value=[]), \
                    mock.patch.object(loop, "gpu_processes", return_value=[]):
                self.assertEqual(loop.snapshot(Path(tmp))["reason"], "nothing_to_do")

import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest

from scripts.analysis import overnight_summary as report
from scripts.instruments.hillclimb import atomic_json, sealed_json


class CorrectedComparisonTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.temp.cleanup)
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.original = self.root / 'campaign-agentic-20260913'
        self.corrected = self.root / 'corrected-bartowski'
        self.original.mkdir()
        self.corrected.mkdir()
        jobs = []
        for task, seconds in [('javascript/one', 100), ('rust/two', 200)]:
            self.save(self.original, 'iq3-control', task, seconds)
            name = self.save(self.original, 'bartowski-iq4-xs', task, None)
            jobs.append({'id': name, 'model': 'bartowski-iq4-xs', 'task': task})
        sealed_json(self.corrected / 'manifest.json', {'queue': jobs})

    def save(self, campaign, model, task, seconds):
        name = 'solve-' + model + '-' + task.replace('/', '-')
        row = {'model': model, 'task': task, 'status': 'failed' if seconds is None else 'passed'}
        if seconds is None:
            row['reason'] = 'probe_failed'
        else:
            row['probe'] = {'complete': True, 'task_success': True, 'timed_out': False,
                            'verified_wall_seconds': seconds, 'prefill_seconds': seconds / 10,
                            'decode_seconds': seconds * .8, 'metrics_delta': {'decode_tokens': 500}}
        sealed_json(campaign / ('job-' + name + '.json'), row)
        return name

    def test_default_keeps_original_failed_arm(self):
        rows = report.coding_rows(self.original)
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 4)
        self.assertEqual(sum(r['status'] == 'failed' for _, r in rows), 2)

    def test_partial_corrected_arm_replaces_old_failures_without_filling_pending(self):
        self.save(self.corrected, 'bartowski-iq4-xs', 'javascript/one', 50)
        summary = report.summarize(self.root, self.corrected)
        row = next(r for r in summary['real_task_totals'] if r['model'] == 'bartowski-iq4-xs')
        self.assertEqual((row['measured_tasks'], row['processed']), (1, 1))
        pair = summary['paired_both_solved_only'][0]
        self.assertEqual(pair['both_solved_pairs'], 1)
        self.assertEqual(pair['candidate_over_baseline_total'], .5)
        text = report.render(summary)
        self.assertIn('pending', text)
        self.assertNotIn('probe_failed', text)

    def test_completed_comparison_is_written_separately(self):
        old = self.root / 'OVERNIGHT-REPORT.md'
        old.write_text('original report')
        self.save(self.corrected, 'bartowski-iq4-xs', 'javascript/one', 50)
        self.save(self.corrected, 'bartowski-iq4-xs', 'rust/two', 100)
        report.write_report(self.root, self.corrected, 'PILOT-COMPARISON')
        self.assertEqual(old.read_text(), 'original report')
        result = json.loads((self.root / 'PILOT-COMPARISON-SUMMARY.json').read_text())
        self.assertEqual(result['paired_both_solved_only'][0]['both_solved_pairs'], 2)
        self.assertEqual(result['paired_both_solved_only'][0]['candidate_over_baseline_total'], .5)

    def test_wrong_model_or_task_set_is_rejected(self):
        self.save(self.corrected, 'unsloth-q4-m', 'javascript/one', 40)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'declared Bartowski job'):
            report.coding_rows(self.original, self.corrected)
        sealed_json(self.corrected / 'manifest.json', {'queue': []})
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'exactly the original'):
            report.coding_rows(self.original, self.corrected)

    def test_completion_watcher_finishes_on_success_or_safety_stop(self):
        for state, expected in [('running', False), ('complete', True), ('stopped', True), ('error', False)]:
            atomic_json(self.corrected / 'state.json', {'status': state})
            self.assertEqual(report.campaign_terminal(self.corrected), expected)


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()


"""The paired comparator, which exists because the unpaired reading gave a wrong answer.

The regression it guards is concrete: a mean over per-task totals reported Q3 at 1.39x Q4
on data whose paired ratio is 1.28x with a sign test of 23/39, i.e. no difference at all.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import contextlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))

from scripts.analysis import paired_runs

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
SESSION1 = ROOT / "results/session1/codegen/polyglot.jsonl"
SESSION2 = ROOT / "results/session2/q3-token-cost/polyglot.jsonl"


def row(quant: str, run: int, task: str, tokens: int, ok: bool = True) -> str:
    return json.dumps({"quant": quant, "run_index": run, "task_id": task,
                       "completion_tokens": tokens, "task_success": ok})


def ratios(cand: dict[str, int], base: dict[str, int]) -> list[float]:
    """Per-task cand/base ratios over the tasks both legs hold, as main() forms them."""
    return [cand[t] / base[t] for t in sorted(set(cand) & set(base)) if base[t]]


class LoadTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.dir = Path(tmp.name)

    def write(self, name: str, *lines: str) -> Path:
        path = self.dir / name
        path.write_text("\n".join(lines) + "\n")
        return path

    def test_reads_legs_out_of_several_files(self) -> None:
        # The two legs worth comparing were written by different sessions.
        a = self.write("a.jsonl", row("Q4", 1, "t1", 100))
