        b = self.write("b.jsonl", row("Q3", 9, "t1", 128))
        self.assertEqual(sorted(paired_runs.load([a, b], 1)), ["t1"])
        self.assertEqual(sorted(paired_runs.load([a, b], 9)), ["t1"])

    def test_quant_disambiguates_a_shared_run_index(self) -> None:
        path = self.write("m.jsonl", row("Q4", 1, "t1", 100), row("IQ3", 1, "t1", 200))
        self.assertEqual(paired_runs.load([path], 1, "IQ3")["t1"]["completion_tokens"], 200)

    def test_an_unnamed_quant_collision_is_refused_not_overwritten(self) -> None:
        # Without this the second quant's rows replace the first's and the leg is a
        # silent mixture of both.
        path = self.write("m.jsonl", row("Q4", 1, "t1", 100), row("IQ3", 1, "t1", 200))
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            paired_runs.load([path], 1)

    def test_blank_lines_survive(self) -> None:
        path = self.write("b.jsonl", row("A", 1, "t1", 100), "")
        self.assertEqual(len(paired_runs.load([path], 1)), 1)


class SummariseTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_an_outlier_does_not_move_the_paired_ratio(self) -> None:
        # The whole point. Both legs cost 2x on every task; one task is 100x larger
        # than the others in absolute terms.
        cand = {f"t{i}": 200 for i in range(9)} | {"t9": 20000}
        base = {f"t{i}": 100 for i in range(9)} | {"t9": 10000}
        self.assertAlmostEqual(paired_runs.summarise(ratios(cand, base))["geometric"], 2.0)

    def test_a_ratio_of_sums_can_disagree_with_every_task(self) -> None:
        # One runaway task makes the ratio of totals say "cand is dearer" when cand was
        # cheaper on 9 tasks of 10. This is the shape of the 1.39x that was reported.
        cand = {f"t{i}": 50 for i in range(9)} | {"t9": 40000}
        base = {f"t{i}": 100 for i in range(9)} | {"t9": 100}
        result = paired_runs.summarise(ratios(cand, base))
        self.assertEqual(result["wins"], 1)
        self.assertLess(result["geometric"], 1.0)
        self.assertGreater(sum(cand.values()) / sum(base.values()), 40)

    def test_a_zero_denominator_is_skipped_not_divided_by(self) -> None:
        cand = {"t1": 1, "t2": 2, "t3": 3}
        base = {"t1": 0, "t2": 2, "t3": 3}
        self.assertEqual(paired_runs.summarise(ratios(cand, base))["n"], 2)


class SignTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_a_coin_flip_is_not_significant(self) -> None:
        self.assertGreater(paired_runs.sign_p(23, 39), 0.05)

    def test_a_lopsided_result_is(self) -> None:
        self.assertLess(paired_runs.sign_p(34, 40), 0.005)

    def test_symmetric(self) -> None:
        self.assertAlmostEqual(paired_runs.sign_p(34, 40), paired_runs.sign_p(6, 40))

    def test_bounded_at_one(self) -> None:
        self.assertLessEqual(paired_runs.sign_p(5, 10), 1.0)


class PowerTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_smaller_effects_need_more_tasks(self) -> None:
        self.assertLess(paired_runs.tasks_needed(0.62, 1.5), paired_runs.tasks_needed(0.62, 1.15))

    def test_the_measured_spread_reproduces_the_recorded_figure(self) -> None:
        # STATE.md tells the reader 40 tasks resolves 1.3x and nothing finer.
        self.assertLessEqual(paired_runs.tasks_needed(0.622, 1.30), 50)
        self.assertGreater(paired_runs.tasks_needed(0.622, 1.15), 150)


class EndToEndTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def run_cli(self, *argv: str) -> str:
        out = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
            paired_runs.main_argv(list(argv))
        return out.getvalue()

    def test_the_real_results_still_say_q3_is_a_coin_flip(self) -> None:
        text = self.run_cli("--path", str(SESSION1), "--path", str(SESSION2),
                            "--base", "1", "--base-quant", "UD-Q4_K_XL",
                            "--cand", "9", "--cand-quant", "UD-Q3_K_XL", "--solved-only")
        self.assertIn("23/39", text)
        self.assertIn("sign test p", text)
        self.assertIn("paired tasks for 80% power", text)

    def test_iq3_separates_where_q3_does_not(self) -> None:
        text = self.run_cli("--path", str(SESSION1),
                            "--base", "1", "--base-quant", "UD-Q4_K_XL",
                            "--cand", "1", "--cand-quant", "UD-IQ3_XXS", "--solved-only")
        self.assertIn("34/40", text)

    def test_a_leg_holding_two_quants_is_refused(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
            self.run_cli("--path", str(SESSION1), "--base", "1", "--cand", "1")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from scripts.instruments import emit, run_polyglot
from scripts.instruments import run_swebench


class RunnerTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_polyglot_manifest_order_and_crash_isolation(self) -> None:
        manifest = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "polyglot_manifest.json"
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            output = Path(temp_dir) / "results.jsonl"
            returncode = run_polyglot.main(
                [
                    "--manifest",
                    str(manifest),
                    "--lane",
                    "qwen",
                    "--quant",
                    "fixture",
                    "--run-index",
                    "7",
                    "--out",
                    str(output),
                    "--jobs",
                    "3",
                    "--timeout",
                    "30",
                    "--dry-run",
                    "--skip-selftest",
                ]
            )

            rows = [json.loads(line) for line in output.read_text().splitlines()]
            self.assertEqual(returncode, 0)
            self.assertEqual(
                [row["task_id"] for row in rows],
                ["fixture-first", "fixture-crash", "fixture-last"],
            )
            self.assertTrue(rows[0]["task_success"])
            self.assertFalse(rows[1]["task_success"])
            self.assertIn("FileNotFoundError", rows[1]["notes"])
            self.assertTrue(rows[2]["task_success"])
            self.assertTrue(all(row["harness_verified"] is False for row in rows))
            self.assertTrue(all(set(row) == set(emit.RESULT_FIELDS) for row in rows))

    def test_polyglot_refuses_to_run_without_selftest(self) -> None:
        manifest = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "polyglot_manifest.json"
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            output = Path(temp_dir) / "results.jsonl"
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, "SELF-TEST REQUIRED"):
                run_polyglot.main(
                    [
                        "--manifest",
                        str(manifest),
                        "--lane",
                        "qwen",
                        "--quant",
                        "fixture",
                        "--run-index",
                        "0",
                        "--out",
                        str(output),
                        "--dry-run",
                    ]
                )
            self.assertFalse(output.exists())

    def test_swebench_dry_run_emits_empty_prediction(self) -> None:
        instances = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "swebench_instances.jsonl"
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            output = Path(temp_dir) / "results.jsonl"
            predictions = Path(temp_dir) / "predictions.jsonl"
            cache = Path(temp_dir) / "cache"
            returncode = run_swebench.main(
                [
                    "--instances",
                    str(instances),
                    "--lane",
                    "qwen",
                    "--quant",
                    "fixture",
                    "--run-index",
                    "2",
                    "--out",
                    str(output),
                    "--predictions",
                    str(predictions),
                    "--repo-cache",
                    str(cache),
                    "--timeout",
                    "30",
                    "--dry-run",
                ]
            )

            rows = [json.loads(line) for line in output.read_text().splitlines()]
            prediction_rows = [json.loads(line) for line in predictions.read_text().splitlines()]
            self.assertEqual(returncode, 0)
            self.assertEqual([row["task_id"] for row in rows], ["fixture__repo-1"])
            self.assertFalse(rows[0]["task_success"])
            self.assertIn("empty diff", rows[0]["notes"])
            self.assertEqual(prediction_rows[0]["model_patch"], "")
            self.assertEqual(
                set(prediction_rows[0]),
                {"instance_id", "model_name_or_path", "model_patch"},
            )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


class ExternalLaneTelemetryTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_external_lanes_do_not_scrape_llama_server(self) -> None:
        """A luna/sol task must never inherit the local server's counters.

        The counters are global to the llama-server process, so scraping them for
        an API-model lane would silently attribute Qwen's prefill and cache reads
        to the control arm.
        """

        from scripts.instruments import run_polyglot

        calls: list[str] = []
        original = run_polyglot.scrape_metrics
        run_polyglot.scrape_metrics = lambda url, timeout=5.0: calls.append(url) or {}
        try:
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
                root = Path(directory)
                manifest = root / "m.json"
                manifest.write_text(json.dumps([]))
                for lane in ("control", "frontier"):
                    run_polyglot.main(
                        [
                            "--manifest", str(manifest),
                            "--skip-selftest",
                            "--lane", lane,
                            "--quant", "none",
                            "--run-index", "1",
                            "--out", str(root / f"{lane}.jsonl"),
                            "--metrics-url", "http://127.0.0.1:8000/v1",
                        ]
                    )
        finally:
            run_polyglot.scrape_metrics = original
        self.assertEqual(calls, [], f"external lanes scraped llama-server: {calls}")


class DependencyInjectionTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_repeated_preparation_does_not_duplicate_keys(self) -> None:
        """prepare_test_harness runs once per test cycle, so it must be idempotent.

        A repeated key makes cargo reject the manifest before compiling anything,
        which scores the task failed however good the model's solution is.
        """

        from scripts.instruments import run_polyglot

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
            root = Path(directory)
            workdir = root / "ex"
            workdir.mkdir()
            (workdir / "Cargo.toml").write_text(
                '[package]\nname = "decimal"\nversion = "0.1.0"\n\n[dependencies]\n'
            )
            task = {"task_id": "rust/decimal", "language": "rust"}
            for _ in range(3):
                run_polyglot.prepare_test_harness(task, workdir)
            text = (workdir / "Cargo.toml").read_text()
            for name in ("num-bigint", "num-traits"):
                self.assertEqual(
                    text.count(f"{name} ="), 1, f"{name} declared more than once:\n{text}"
                )


class ResumeTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def _write(self, path, rows):
        path.write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for r in rows))

    def test_only_this_legs_rows_count_as_done(self) -> None:
        """One file holds every leg, so matching on task_id alone would let a
        different quant's result mark this leg's task complete."""

        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp) / "out.jsonl"
            self._write(out, [
                {"task_id": "a", "quant": "Q4", "run_index": 1},
                {"task_id": "b", "quant": "IQ3", "run_index": 1},
                {"task_id": "c", "quant": "Q4", "run_index": 2},
            ])
            self.assertEqual(run_polyglot.completed_task_ids(out, "Q4", 1), {"a"})

    def test_a_torn_final_line_does_not_abort_resume(self) -> None:
        # A killed writer can leave half a row; that must cost one task, not the run.
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            out = Path(tmp) / "out.jsonl"
            out.write_text(json.dumps({"task_id": "a", "quant": "Q4", "run_index": 1})
                           + "\n" + '{"task_id": "b", "quan')
            self.assertEqual(run_polyglot.completed_task_ids(out, "Q4", 1), {"a"})

    def test_missing_file_is_empty_not_an_error(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            self.assertEqual(
                run_polyglot.completed_task_ids(Path(tmp) / "nope.jsonl", "Q4", 1), set()
            )


class MergedStatsReachTheRowTest(unittest.TestCase):
    """Every field CodexStats parses has to survive the merge into the row.

    cache_write_tokens was parsed and then dropped here, so the column read null for
    every leg -- and cache writes are billable on some API routes, which makes a silent
    null an understated cost rather than a missing detail.
    """

    def test_no_parsed_field_is_dropped(self) -> None:
        fields = set(emit.CodexStats.__dataclass_fields__)
        merged = set(run_polyglot._merge_stats([]))
        self.assertEqual(fields - merged, set(), "parsed but never written to the row")

    def test_cache_writes_are_summed_across_turns(self) -> None:
        stats = [emit.CodexStats(agent_turns=1, tool_calls=0, prompt_tokens=10,
                                 completion_tokens=5, cache_write_tokens=n)
                 for n in (7, 11)]
        self.assertEqual(run_polyglot._merge_stats(stats)["cache_write_tokens"], 18)


from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from scripts.instruments import run_polyglot
from scripts.corpora import selftest_polyglot


class PolyglotSelftestTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_reference_passes_and_stub_pass_is_broken(self) -> None:
        manifest = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "polyglot_selftest_manifest.json"
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            report_path = Path(temp_dir) / "report.json"
            stderr = io.StringIO()
            with contextlib.redirect_stderr(stderr):
                returncode = selftest_polyglot.main(
                    ["--manifest", str(manifest), "--out", str(report_path), "--timeout", "10"]
                )

            report = json.loads(report_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            results = {item["task_id"]: item for item in report["tasks"]}
            good = results["fixture-selftest-good"]
            broken = results["fixture-selftest-broken"]
            self.assertEqual(returncode, 1)
            self.assertTrue(good["reference_passes"])
            self.assertTrue(good["stub_fails"])
            self.assertFalse(good["broken"])
            self.assertTrue(broken["reference_passes"])
            self.assertFalse(broken["stub_fails"])
            self.assertTrue(broken["broken"])
            self.assertIn("BROKEN fixture-selftest-broken", stderr.getvalue())
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(SystemExit, "fixture-selftest-broken"):
                run_polyglot.validate_selftest_report(report_path, run_polyglot.load_manifest(manifest))

    def test_verified_report_marks_emitted_row(self) -> None:
        source = ROOT / "tests" / "fixtures" / "polyglot" / "selftest_good"
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            temp = Path(temp_dir)
            manifest = temp / "manifest.json"
            report = temp / "report.json"
            output = temp / "results.jsonl"
            manifest.write_text(
                json.dumps(
                    [
                        {
                            "task_id": "fixture-selftest-good",
                            "language": "python",
                            "source_dir": str(source),
                            "test_cmd": ["python", "-m", "unittest", "-q"],
                        }
                    ]
                ),
                encoding="utf-8",
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                selftest_polyglot.main(
                    ["--manifest", str(manifest), "--out", str(report), "--timeout", "10"]
                ),
                0,
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                run_polyglot.main(
                    [
                        "--manifest",
                        str(manifest),
                        "--selftest-report",
                        str(report),
                        "--lane",
                        "qwen",
                        "--quant",
                        "fixture",
                        "--run-index",
                        "0",
                        "--out",
                        str(output),
                        "--dry-run",
                    ]
                ),
                0,
            )
            row = json.loads(output.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.assertTrue(row["harness_verified"])

    def test_language_specific_test_activation(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(
            run_polyglot.task_test_command(
                {"language": "rust", "test_cmd": ["cargo", "test", "--quiet"]}
            ),
            ["cargo", "test", "--quiet", "--", "--include-ignored"],
        )
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            root = Path(temp_dir)
            (root / ".meta").mkdir()
            (root / ".meta" / "config.json").write_text(
                json.dumps({"files": {"test": ["exercise.spec.js"]}}), encoding="utf-8"
            )
            test_path = root / "exercise.spec.js"
            source_path = root / "exercise.js"
            test_path.write_text("xtest('disabled', () => {});\n", encoding="utf-8")
            source_path.write_text("xtest('not a test file', () => {});\n", encoding="utf-8")

            replacements = run_polyglot.prepare_test_harness(
                {"language": "javascript"}, root
            )

            self.assertEqual(replacements, 1)
            self.assertIn("test('disabled'", test_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            self.assertIn("xtest('not a test file'", source_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_reference_overlay_ignores_unmatched_auxiliary_solution_file(self) -> None:
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp_dir:
            root = Path(temp_dir)
            (root / ".meta").mkdir()
            (root / "src").mkdir()
            (root / ".meta" / "example.rs").write_text("reference\n")
            (root / "src" / "lib.rs").write_text("stub\n")
            (root / "Cargo.toml").write_text("[package]\nname='fixture'\n")
            selftest_polyglot._overlay_reference(
                {
                    "example_path": [".meta/example.rs"],
                    "solution_path": ["src/lib.rs", "Cargo.toml"],
                },
                root,
            )
            self.assertEqual((root / "src" / "lib.rs").read_text(), "reference\n")
            self.assertIn("name='fixture'", (root / "Cargo.toml").read_text())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest

from scripts.analysis import session_signatures as s
from scripts.instruments.hillclimb import sealed_json


class SessionSignatureTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_shell_categories_are_multi_label_and_polling_is_separate(self):
        self.assertEqual(s.action_tags('write_stdin', {'chars': ''}), ['poll'])
        self.assertEqual(s.action_tags('write_stdin', {'chars': 'yes\n'}), ['interactive_input'])
        self.assertIn('inspect', s.action_tags('exec_command', {'cmd': 'cat src/lib.rs'}))
        tags = s.action_tags('exec_command', {'cmd': 'cat > src/lib.rs << EOF\n// run cargo test\nEOF'})
        self.assertIn('edit', tags)
        self.assertNotIn('test', tags)
        self.assertIn('test', s.action_tags('exec_command', {'cmd': 'cd /work && cargo test --offline'}))
        self.assertEqual(s.command_text({'command': ['/bin/bash', '-lc', 'pwd']}), 'pwd')

    def test_overlapping_tool_wait_is_not_added_twice(self):
        self.assertEqual(s.interval_union_seconds([(0, 4), (2, 6), (6, 8)]), 8)
        self.assertEqual(s.interval_union_seconds([]), 0)

    def test_paired_accounting_matches_tasks_and_sums_to_wall_difference(self):
        baseline = {'model': 'iq3-control', 'task': 'one', 'solved': True, 'generated_tokens': 100,
                    'decode_seconds': 10, 'prefill_seconds': 2, 'other_seconds': 3, 'wall_seconds': 15}
        candidate = {**baseline, 'model': 'candidate', 'generated_tokens': 50, 'decode_seconds': 4,
                     'prefill_seconds': 3, 'other_seconds': 4, 'wall_seconds': 11}
        row = s.paired_explanations([baseline, candidate, {**candidate, 'task': 'unpaired'}])[0]
        self.assertEqual(row['paired_tasks'], 1)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(row['decode_token_volume_delta'], -4.5)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(row['decode_rate_delta'], -1.5)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(row['wall_delta'], sum(row[k] for k in [
            'prefill_delta', 'decode_token_volume_delta', 'decode_rate_delta', 'other_delta']))

    def fixture(self, root):
        attempt = root / 'attempts/one'
        directory = attempt / 'codex-state/sessions'
        directory.mkdir(parents=True)
        usage1 = {'response_id': 'r1', 'usage': {'input_tokens': 10, 'cached_input_tokens': 0, 'output_tokens': 20}}
        usage2 = {'response_id': 'r2', 'usage': {'input_tokens': 20, 'cached_input_tokens': 0, 'output_tokens': 30}}
        raw = [
            ('session_meta', {'id': 's1', 'cli_version': 'test'}),
            ('event_msg', {'type': 'task_started', 'turn_id': 't1', 'model_context_window': 120}),
            ('response_item', {'type': 'function_call', 'id': 'f1', 'call_id': 'c1', 'name': 'exec_command',
                               'arguments': json.dumps({'cmd': 'cat > solution.js << EOF\ncode\nEOF'})}),
            ('token_usage_record', usage1),
            ('token_usage_record', usage1),
            ('response_item', {'type': 'function_call_output', 'call_id': 'c1', 'output': 'exec_command failed: Rejected'}),
            ('event_msg', {'type': 'token_count', 'info': {'last_token_usage': {'output_tokens': 99999}}}),
            ('token_usage_record', usage2),
            ('compacted', {'compaction_response_id': 'r2', 'message': 'summary',
                           'replacement_history': [{'type': 'function_call', 'call_id': 'do-not-count'}]}),
            ('event_msg', {'type': 'item_completed', 'item': {'type': 'ContextCompaction', 'id': 'comp'},
                           'started_at_ms': 1000, 'completed_at_ms': 4000}),
            ('event_msg', {'type': 'task_complete', 'turn_id': 't1'}),
        ]
        path = directory / 'rollout-test.jsonl'
        path.write_text('\n'.join(json.dumps({'timestamp': f'2026-09-13T00:00:{i:02d}Z', 'type': kind, 'payload': payload})
                                  for i, (kind, payload) in enumerate(raw)) + '\n')
        (attempt / 'metrics-before.json').write_text(json.dumps({'n_tokens_max': 0}))
        (attempt / 'metrics-after.json').write_text(json.dumps({'n_tokens_max': 110}))
        lines = []
        for key, inputs, outputs in [(1, 10, 20), (2, 20, 30)]:
            lines += [f'I slot print_timing: id  0 | task {key} | prompt eval time = 100.00 ms / {inputs} tokens',
                      f'I slot print_timing: id  0 | task {key} |        eval time = 200.00 ms / {outputs} tokens',
                      f'I slot release: id  0 | task {key} | stop processing: n_tokens = 80, truncated = 0']
        (attempt / 'server.log').write_text('\n'.join(lines))
        row = {'model': 'test', 'task': 'javascript/test', 'attempt': 'attempts/one', 'probe': {
            'task_success': True, 'verified_wall_seconds': 100, 'prefill_seconds': 30,
            'decode_seconds': 50, 'other_seconds': 20, 'client_context': 128,
            'client_compaction': 100, 'peak_context_tokens': 200,
            'metrics_delta': {'decode_tokens': 50, 'prefill_tokens': 30}}}
        return path, row

    def test_responses_deduplicate_but_token_count_notifications_do_not_add_turns(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            path, _ = self.fixture(Path(temp))
            result = s.parse_rollouts([path])
            self.assertEqual(len(result['requests']), 2)
            self.assertEqual(sum(r['usage']['output_tokens'] for r in result['requests']), 50)
            self.assertEqual(result['outer_turns_completed'], 1)
            self.assertEqual(len(result['tools']), 1)
            self.assertTrue(result['tools'][0]['rejected_or_tool_error'])
            self.assertFalse(result['tools'][0]['command_completed_event'])
            self.assertTrue(result['requests'][1]['is_compaction'])
            self.assertEqual(s.interval_union_seconds(result['compaction_intervals']), 3)

    def test_poll_links_to_its_command_and_preserves_network_failure_evidence(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            path, _ = self.fixture(Path(temp))
            rows = [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines()]
            output = next(r for r in rows if r['payload'].get('type') == 'function_call_output')
            output['payload']['output'] = 'Process running with session ID 42'
            rows += [
                {'timestamp': '2026-09-13T00:00:11Z', 'type': 'response_item', 'payload': {
                    'type': 'function_call', 'id': 'f2', 'call_id': 'c2', 'name': 'write_stdin',
                    'arguments': '{"session_id":42,"chars":""}'}},
                {'timestamp': '2026-09-13T00:00:12Z', 'type': 'response_item', 'payload': {
                    'type': 'function_call_output', 'call_id': 'c2', 'output': 'npm error code ENOTFOUND'}},
            ]
            path.write_text('\n'.join(json.dumps(r) for r in rows))
            result = s.parse_rollouts([path])
            poll = result['tools'][1]
            self.assertEqual(poll['origin_call_id'], 'c1')
            self.assertTrue(poll['network_error_in_output'])
            self.assertEqual(poll['actions_inferred'], ['poll'])

    def test_peak_context_uses_fresh_server_counter_and_timings_align(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            _, row = self.fixture(root)
            result = s.session_signature(root, row)
            self.assertEqual(result['peak_context_tokens'], 110)
            self.assertEqual(result['legacy_double_counted_peak'], 200)
            self.assertEqual(result['compactions'], 1)
            self.assertAlmostEqual(result['compaction_inference_seconds'], .3)
            self.assertEqual(result['output_tokens_through_first_edit_request'], 20)
            self.assertTrue(result['backend_timings_aligned'])
            self.assertEqual(result['warnings'], [])

    def test_nonfresh_counter_or_missing_rollout_stays_unknown(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            _, row = self.fixture(root)
            (root / 'attempts/one/metrics-before.json').write_text('{"n_tokens_max": 10}')
            result = s.session_signature(root, row)
            self.assertIsNone(result['peak_context_tokens'])
            self.assertTrue(result['warnings'])
            row['attempt'] = 'absent'
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, 'missing rollout'):
                s.session_signature(root, row)

    def test_full_campaign_analysis_and_corrected_probe_peak(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            _, row = self.fixture(root)
            row.update(status='passed')
            row['probe'].update(peak_context_tokens=110, legacy_sampled_context_tokens=200)
            sealed_json(root / 'manifest.json', {'queue': [{'id': 'one', 'model': 'test'},
                                                        {'id': 'pending', 'model': 'test'}]})
            sealed_json(root / 'job-one.json', row)
            (root / 'state.json').write_text('{"status": "running"}')
            result = s.analyze_campaign(root)
            self.assertEqual(result['campaign_state'], 'running')
            self.assertEqual(result['sessions'][0]['legacy_double_counted_peak'], 200)
            self.assertEqual(result['excluded'][0]['reason'], 'pending')
            self.assertIn('stage: running', s.render(result))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    unittest.main()


import json
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from scripts.instruments import swift_autonomous as auto


class AutonomousTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_stop_interactive_accepts_absent_unit_but_not_active_stop_failure(self):
        with patch.object(auto, "run_command", side_effect=[
                auto.subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 1, "", "unit not found"),
                auto.subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 3, "inactive\n", "")]):
            rows = []
            auto.stop_interactive(rows.append)
            self.assertEqual(rows[0]["reason"], "interactive_already_stopped")
        with patch.object(auto, "run_command", side_effect=[
                auto.subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 1, "", "stop failed"),
                auto.subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 0, "active\n", "")]):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(RuntimeError, "interactive stop failed"):
                auto.stop_interactive(lambda _: None)

    def test_falls_back_when_candidate_lacks_full_coverage(self):
        base = {"batch": 2048, "split": "71,29", "ubatch": 128}
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            root = Path(tmp) / "attempts" / "a"
            root.mkdir(parents=True)
            (root / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps({
                "status": "passed", "job": "batch-batch-1024-x",
                "target": 49152, "task": "javascript/affine-cipher",
                "decode_tps": 50, "config": {**base, "batch": 1024}}))
            selected, evidence = auto.select_config(Path(tmp), base, "batch")
            self.assertEqual(selected, base)
            self.assertEqual(evidence["decision"], "retain base; no fully covered passing candidate")

    def test_selects_fully_covered_candidate(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            coverage = [
                ("javascript/affine-cipher", 49152), ("rust/forth", 49152),
                ("javascript/affine-cipher", 98304), ("rust/forth", 98304)]
            for i, (task, target) in enumerate(coverage):
                root = Path(tmp) / "attempts" / f"base-{i}"
                root.mkdir(parents=True)
                (root / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps({
                    "status": "passed", "job": f"batch-matched-control-{i}",
                    "target": target, "task": task,
                    "decode_tps": 40, "config": {"batch": 2048}}))
            for i, (task, target) in enumerate(coverage):
                root = Path(tmp) / "attempts" / str(i)
                root.mkdir(parents=True)
                (root / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps({
                    "status": "passed", "job": f"batch-batch-1024-{i}",
                    "target": target, "task": task,
                    "decode_tps": 50 + i, "config": {"batch": 1024,
                                                       "devices": ["runtime-only"]}}))
            selected, evidence = auto.select_config(Path(tmp), {"batch": 2048}, "batch")
            self.assertEqual(selected["batch"], 1024)
            self.assertNotIn("devices", selected)
            self.assertEqual(evidence["coverage_rows"], 4)

    def test_rejects_candidate_without_five_percent_gain(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            coverage = [
                ("javascript/affine-cipher", 49152), ("rust/forth", 49152),
                ("javascript/affine-cipher", 98304), ("rust/forth", 98304)]
            for i, (task, target) in enumerate(coverage):
                for label, batch, score in (("matched-control", 2048, 40),
                                             ("batch-1024", 1024, 41)):
                    root = Path(tmp) / "attempts" / f"{label}-{i}"
                    root.mkdir(parents=True)
                    (root / "result.json").write_text(json.dumps({
                        "status": "passed", "job": f"batch-{label}-{i}",
                        "target": target, "task": task,
                        "decode_tps": score, "config": {"batch": batch}}))
            selected, evidence = auto.select_config(Path(tmp), {"batch": 2048}, "batch")
            self.assertEqual(selected, {"batch": 2048})
            self.assertIn("at least 5%", evidence["decision"])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import copy
import json
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

from scripts.instruments import hillclimb as h
from scripts.instruments import hillclimb_probe as p
from scripts.instruments import swift_longtail as s


class TuningTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def validate(self, jobs):
        with patch.object(h, "MODELS", [s.MODEL]), patch.object(h, "TASKS", s.TUNING_TASKS):
            return h.validate_profiles(jobs)

    def test_baseline_is_exact_matrix_with_output_room(self):
        jobs = self.validate(s.tuning_queue("baseline"))
        self.assertEqual(len(jobs), 18)
        self.assertEqual({(j["task"], j["targets"][0], j["seed"]) for j in jobs},
                         {(t, n, seed) for t in s.TUNING_TASKS for n in (8192, 49152, 98304)
                          for seed in s.TUNING_SEEDS})
        for job in jobs:
            self.assertEqual(job["configs"], [s.TUNING_BASELINE])
            self.assertLessEqual(job["targets"][0] + 2048, job["configs"][0]["context"])

    def test_stages_bracket_baseline_and_retain_selected_settings(self):
        selected = {**s.TUNING_BASELINE, "split": "67,33", "ubatch": 64}
        for stage in s.TUNING_STAGES[1:]:
            jobs = self.validate(s.tuning_queue(stage, selected, splits=["67,33", "71,29"],
                                               microbatches=[64, 256]))
            self.assertIn("baseline-before", jobs[0]["id"])
            self.assertIn("baseline-after", jobs[-1]["id"])
            self.assertEqual(jobs[0]["configs"], [s.TUNING_BASELINE])
            self.assertEqual(jobs[-1]["configs"], [s.TUNING_BASELINE])
        jobs = s.tuning_queue("probability", selected)
        arms = [j for j in jobs if "p_min" in j["id"]]
        self.assertEqual({j["configs"][0]["p_min"] for j in arms}, {0.5, 0.75})
        self.assertTrue(all(j["configs"][0]["split"] == "67,33" for j in arms))

    def test_swarm_context_is_per_slot_and_does_not_truncate_total(self):
        jobs = self.validate(s.tuning_queue("swarm-allocation", s.TUNING_BASELINE))
        arms = [j for j in jobs if j["id"].startswith("swarm-") and "baseline" not in j["id"]]
        self.assertEqual(len(arms), 9)
        self.assertEqual({(j["configs"][0]["slots"], j["configs"][0]["context"]) for j in arms},
                         {(n, c) for n in (2, 3, 4) for c in (32768, 65536, 102400)})

    def test_invalid_controls_and_implicit_winners_fail(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            s.tuning_queue("microbatch")
        for change in ({"p_min": float("nan")}, {"minimum": 4}, {"draft_k": "q4_0"},
                       {"batch": 64}, {"maximum": 0}):
            jobs = copy.deepcopy(s.tuning_queue("baseline"))
            jobs[0]["configs"][0].update(change)
            with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                self.validate(jobs)

    def test_plan_cli_never_initializes_or_runs_campaign(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp, patch.object(h, "initialize") as init, \
                patch.object(h.Runner, "run") as run, patch.object(h, "MODELS", []), patch.object(h, "TASKS", []):
            out = Path(temp) / "queue.json"
            s.tuning_main(["baseline", "--out", str(out)])
            self.assertEqual(len(json.loads(out.read_text())), 18)
            with self.assertRaises(FileExistsError):
                s.tuning_main(["baseline", "--out", str(out)])
            init.assert_not_called()
            run.assert_not_called()

    def test_unowned_compute_and_nvml_failure_abort(self):
        with patch.object(h.subprocess, "run", return_value=subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 0, "")):
            h.require_free_compute()
        with patch.object(h.subprocess, "run", return_value=subprocess.CompletedProcess([], 0, h.GPU1 + ",42,python\n")):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, "unowned_gpu_workload"):
                h.require_free_compute()
        with patch.object(h.subprocess, "run", side_effect=subprocess.CalledProcessError(1, "nvidia-smi")):
            with self.assertRaises(subprocess.CalledProcessError):
                h.require_free_compute()

    def test_cosmic_monitor_is_allowed_but_unknown_compute_is_not(self):
        with patch.object(h.subprocess, "run", return_value=subprocess.CompletedProcess(
                [], 0, h.GPU0 + ",42,cosmic-monitor\n")):
            h.require_free_compute()
        with patch.object(h.subprocess, "run", return_value=subprocess.CompletedProcess(
                [], 0, h.GPU0 + ",42,cosmic-app-library\n")):
            h.require_free_compute()
        with patch.object(h.subprocess, "run", return_value=subprocess.CompletedProcess(
                [], 0, h.GPU0 + ",42,unknown-compute\n")):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(h.StopCampaign, "unowned_gpu_workload"):
                h.require_free_compute()

    def test_tensor_fit_abort_is_a_nonretryable_capacity_result(self):
        self.assertEqual(h.classify_exit(
            "common_fit_params: failed to fit params to free device memory: "
            "llama_params_fit is not implemented for SPLIT_MODE_TENSOR, abort"),
            "vram_fit_unavailable")

    def test_effective_context_checked_before_any_request(self):
        with patch.object(p, "api", return_value={"total_slots": 4, "default_generation_settings": {"n_ctx": 32768}}), \
                patch.object(p, "prepared_prompt") as prepare:
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "effective per-slot"):
                p.run_probe("unused", {}, 32700, 2048, 4, 1729, Path("unused"))
            prepare.assert_not_called()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
from scripts.instruments import emit  # noqa: E402


BEFORE = {
    "prompt_tokens_total": 1000.0,
    "prompt_tokens_cached_total": 400.0,
    "prompt_seconds_total": 10.0,
    "tokens_predicted_total": 500.0,
    "tokens_predicted_seconds_total": 25.0,
    "n_tokens_max": 900.0,
}
AFTER = {
    "prompt_tokens_total": 3000.0,
    "prompt_tokens_cached_total": 1800.0,
    "prompt_seconds_total": 11.0,
    "tokens_predicted_total": 1100.0,
    "tokens_predicted_seconds_total": 35.0,
    "n_tokens_max": 2400.0,
}


class MetricsDeltaTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_delta_is_this_tasks_share(self) -> None:
        d = emit.metrics_delta(BEFORE, AFTER)
        self.assertEqual(d["prefill_tokens"], 2000.0)
        self.assertEqual(d["cached_prompt_tokens"], 1400.0)
        self.assertEqual(d["decode_tokens"], 600.0)
        self.assertEqual(d["prefill_tps"], 2000.0)
        self.assertEqual(d["decode_tps"], 60.0)
        # n_tokens_max is process-wide, so it is recorded as such; per-task depth
        # comes from ContextSampler instead.
        self.assertEqual(d["server_high_water_tokens"], 2400.0)
        self.assertNotIn("peak_context_tokens", d)

    def test_counter_reset_discards_the_reading(self) -> None:
        # A server restart between scrapes rewinds the counters; clamping to zero
        # would publish a plausible wrong number instead of an honest absence.
        self.assertEqual(emit.metrics_delta(AFTER, BEFORE), {})

    def test_a_failed_scrape_yields_nothing(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(emit.metrics_delta({}, AFTER), {})
        self.assertEqual(emit.metrics_delta(BEFORE, {}), {})

    def test_zero_elapsed_does_not_divide(self) -> None:
        same = dict(BEFORE)
        d = emit.metrics_delta(BEFORE, same)
        self.assertNotIn("prefill_tps", d)
        self.assertNotIn("decode_tps", d)


class ContextSamplerTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def _slot(self, prompt: int, decoded: int, processing: bool = True) -> list:
        return [{
            "is_processing": processing,
            "n_prompt_tokens": prompt,
            "next_token": [{"n_decoded": decoded}],
        }]

    def test_depth_is_prompt_plus_decoded(self) -> None:
        sampler = emit.ContextSampler("http://x/v1")
        sampler._sample = lambda: sampler.__dict__.__setitem__(
            "peak", max(sampler.peak, 72382 + 53382)
        )
        sampler._sample()
        self.assertEqual(sampler.stop(), 125764)

    def test_idle_slots_do_not_contribute(self) -> None:
        sampler = emit.ContextSampler("http://x/v1")
        slots = self._slot(9999, 9999, processing=False)
        for slot in slots:
            if slot.get("is_processing"):
                self.fail("idle slot treated as active")
        self.assertEqual(sampler.stop(), 0)

    def test_sampling_failure_never_fails_a_task(self) -> None:
        # Observational only: an unreachable server must cost the reading, not the run.
        sampler = emit.ContextSampler("http://127.0.0.1:9/v1", interval=0.01)
        sampler.start()
        sampler.join(0.2)
        self.assertEqual(sampler.stop(), 0)


class QuantGuardTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def _ctx(self, quant: str) -> emit.RunContext:
        return emit.RunContext(out="unused", lane="quant", quant=quant)

    def test_mismatch_between_label_and_served_file_aborts(self) -> None:
        original = emit.probe_server
        emit.probe_server = lambda url, timeout=10.0: {
            "served_model_path": "/m/Qwen3.8-27B-UD-IQ3_XXS.gguf"
        }
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
                emit.apply_server_probe(self._ctx("UD-Q6_K_M"), "http://x/v1")
            ctx = emit.apply_server_probe(self._ctx("UD-IQ3_XXS"), "http://x/v1")
            self.assertIn("IQ3_XXS", ctx.served_model_path)
        finally:
            emit.probe_server = original

    def test_unreachable_server_aborts_rather_than_recording_blind(self) -> None:
        original = emit.probe_server
        emit.probe_server = lambda url, timeout=10.0: {}
        try:
            with self.assertRaises(SystemExit):
                emit.apply_server_probe(self._ctx("UD-Q6_K_M"), "http://x/v1")
        finally:
            emit.probe_server = original


class CodexUsageTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def _stream(self, usage: dict) -> str:
        import json as _json
        return _json.dumps({"type": "turn.completed", "usage": usage})

    def test_reads_both_spellings_of_cached_input_tokens(self) -> None:
        flat = emit.parse_codex_stats(
            self._stream({"input_tokens": 100, "output_tokens": 10,
                          "cached_input_tokens": 60})
        )
        nested = emit.parse_codex_stats(
            self._stream({"input_tokens": 100, "output_tokens": 10,
                          "input_tokens_details": {"cached_tokens": 60}})
        )
        self.assertEqual(flat.cached_input_tokens, 60)
        self.assertEqual(nested.cached_input_tokens, 60)

    def test_absent_cache_reporting_stays_null_not_zero(self) -> None:
        # Null means "the model did not report it"; zero would claim a measured miss.
        stats = emit.parse_codex_stats(
            self._stream({"input_tokens": 100, "output_tokens": 10})
        )
        self.assertIsNone(stats.cached_input_tokens)
        self.assertIsNone(stats.reasoning_tokens)

    def test_reasoning_tokens_are_summed_when_reported(self) -> None:
        stats = emit.parse_codex_stats(
            self._stream({"input_tokens": 1, "output_tokens": 900,
                          "output_tokens_details": {"reasoning_tokens": 850}})
        )
        self.assertEqual(stats.reasoning_tokens, 850)


class SchemaTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_every_context_field_is_in_the_row_schema(self) -> None:
        missing = emit._CONTEXT_FIELDS - set(emit.RESULT_FIELDS)
        self.assertFalse(missing, f"context fields absent from rows: {sorted(missing)}")

    def test_row_schema_has_no_duplicates(self) -> None:
        self.assertEqual(len(emit.RESULT_FIELDS), len(set(emit.RESULT_FIELDS)))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


class MetricsSchemaTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_every_metric_delta_key_is_a_result_field(self) -> None:
        """metrics_delta and RESULT_FIELDS drift apart silently: adding a counter
        makes build_row reject the row, which kills the leg at its first emit --
        after the task has already been paid for."""

        before = {name: 0.0 for name in emit._METRIC_NAMES.values()}
        after = {name: 1.0 for name in emit._METRIC_NAMES.values()}
        produced = set(emit.metrics_delta(before, after))
        self.assertTrue(produced, "delta produced nothing; the check would be vacuous")
        self.assertFalse(
            produced - set(emit.RESULT_FIELDS),
            f"metrics_delta emits fields build_row rejects: {sorted(produced - set(emit.RESULT_FIELDS))}",
        )


"""The tunnel supervisor, driven with a stub ssh on PATH.

The forward drops roughly every 80-90 minutes under load and ended two legs that way.
The supervisor's contract is narrow and both halves matter: restart a forward that served
and then dropped, and give up on one that never binds -- a spin on a dead box would hold
the port and burn CPU for as long as the session lives.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
BOX = ROOT / "scripts" / "infra" / "box.sh"


class SupervisorTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.bin = Path(tmp.name)
        self.marks = self.bin / "attempts"

    def stub_ssh(self, body: str) -> None:
        path = self.bin / "ssh"
        path.write_text(f'#!/usr/bin/env bash\necho x >> "{self.marks}"\n{body}\n')
        path.chmod(0o755)

    def supervise(self, timeout: float, **env: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
        return subprocess.run(
            ["bash", str(BOX), "tunnel-supervise", "h", "22", "18999", "8000"],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout,
            env={**os.environ, "PATH": f"{self.bin}:{os.environ['PATH']}", **env},
        )

    def attempts(self) -> int:
        return len(self.marks.read_text().splitlines()) if self.marks.exists() else 0

    def test_gives_up_when_the_forward_never_binds(self) -> None:
        # ExitOnForwardFailure makes ssh exit at once on a taken port or a gone box.
        self.stub_ssh("exit 255")
        result = self.supervise(timeout=30, TUNNEL_MAX_FAILS="3", TUNNEL_UP_SECONDS="10")
        self.assertEqual(result.returncode, 1)
        self.assertIn("giving up", result.stderr)
        self.assertEqual(self.attempts(), 3)

    def test_a_forward_that_stayed_up_is_restarted_not_counted(self) -> None:
        # The transient this exists for: ssh served, then dropped. Three such drops must
        # not trip a give-up threshold of three, or an hourly drop would end the run.
        self.stub_ssh('sleep 1; exit 255')
        with self.assertRaises(subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
            self.supervise(timeout=12, TUNNEL_MAX_FAILS="3", TUNNEL_UP_SECONDS="0")
        self.assertGreater(self.attempts(), 3)

    def test_it_reconnects_rather_than_exiting_on_the_first_drop(self) -> None:
        self.stub_ssh("exit 255")
        with self.assertRaises(subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
            self.supervise(timeout=8, TUNNEL_MAX_FAILS="99", TUNNEL_UP_SECONDS="10")
        self.assertGreater(self.attempts(), 1)


class TunnelWiringTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.box = BOX.read_text()

    def test_tunnel_launches_the_supervisor_not_a_bare_ssh(self) -> None:
        launch = self.box[self.box.index("tunnel)"):self.box.index("tunnel-supervise)")]
        self.assertIn('"$0" tunnel-supervise', launch)
        self.assertNotIn("nohup ssh", launch)

    def test_supervisors_are_killed_before_the_ssh_they_own(self) -> None:
        # The reverse order lets a live supervisor respawn the forward between the two
        # kills, leaving the port held by a tunnel pointing at the previous box.
        launch = self.box[self.box.index("tunnel)"):self.box.index("tunnel-supervise)")]
        self.assertLess(
            launch.index('pgrep -f -- "tunnel-supervise'),
            launch.index('pgrep -f -- "$spec"'),
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


import json
import io
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import tempfile
import unittest

from scripts.analysis import watch_campaign as w


class WatchTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_partial_events_are_retried_and_rotation_resets(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            path = Path(temp) / "events.jsonl"
            log = w.EventLog(path)
            self.assertEqual(log.read(), [])
            path.write_text('{"kind":"attempt_start"')
            self.assertEqual(log.read(), [])
            with path.open("a") as stream:
                stream.write('}\n')
            self.assertEqual(len(log.read()), 1)
            self.assertEqual(len(log.read()), 1)
            path.unlink()
            path.write_text('{"kind":"job_end"}\n')
            self.assertEqual(log.read(), [{"kind": "job_end"}])

    def test_clock_does_not_trigger_output_but_new_arm_does(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            job = {"id": "split-65", "model": "swift-iq4-xs", "targets": [98304],
                   "configs": [{"context": 102400, "slots": 1, "split": "65,35"}]}
            (root / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps({"queue": [job]}))
            state = {"status": "running", "active": job, "completed": {}}
            (root / "state.json").write_text(json.dumps(state))
            before = {p.name: p.read_bytes() for p in root.iterdir()}
            first, text = w.snapshot(root, [], now=100)
            second, _ = w.snapshot(root, [], now=110)
            self.assertEqual(first, second)
            self.assertIn("Why: compare placement", text)
            self.assertIn("split 65,35", text)
            self.assertIn("input target 98304", text)
            self.assertIn("ETA: unknown", text)
            self.assertEqual(before, {p.name: p.read_bytes() for p in root.iterdir()})
            state["status"] = "complete"
            state["completed"] = {job["id"]: "result.json"}
            (root / "state.json").write_text(json.dumps(state))
            third, text = w.snapshot(root, [], now=120)
            self.assertNotEqual(first, third)
            self.assertIn("ETA: complete", text)

    def test_eta_uses_job_wall_time_including_fit_and_labels_extrapolation(self):
        jobs = [{"id": str(i), "targets": [8192 if i < 2 else 98304], "configs": [{}]}
                for i in range(3)]
        events = [{"kind": "attempt_start", "job": "0-fit", "time": 100},
                  {"kind": "attempt_start", "job": "0", "time": 110},
                  {"kind": "job_end", "job": "0", "time": 160, "status": "passed"}]
        eta = w.estimate(jobs, {"0": "done"}, events, 160)
        self.assertIn("~2 min", eta)
        self.assertNotIn("provisional", eta)
        events.append({"kind": "attempt_start", "job": "1-fit", "time": 160})
        self.assertIn("exceeds", w.estimate(jobs, {"0": "done"}, events, 230))

    def test_failed_capacity_does_not_create_performance_eta(self):
        jobs = [{"id": "fit", "targets": [0]}, {"id": "deep", "targets": [98304]}]
        events = [{"kind": "attempt_start", "job": "fit", "time": 100},
                  {"kind": "job_end", "job": "fit", "time": 105, "status": "failed"}]
        self.assertIn("unknown", w.estimate(jobs, {"fit": "failed"}, events, 110))

    def test_prepared_path_follows_newer_running_sibling(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            old = root / "campaign"
            live = root / "campaign-r3"
            old.mkdir(); live.mkdir()
            (old / "state.json").write_text(json.dumps({"status": "prepared"}))
            (live / "state.json").write_text(json.dumps({"status": "running"}))
            self.assertEqual(w.resolve_campaign(old), live)

    def test_redraw_uses_terminal_clear_and_home_only_when_requested(self):
        stream = io.StringIO()
        w.render("dashboard", redraw=True, stream=stream)
        self.assertEqual(stream.getvalue(), "\x1b[2J\x1b[Hdashboard\n")
        stream = io.StringIO()
        w.render("dashboard", redraw=False, stream=stream)
        self.assertEqual(stream.getvalue(), "dashboard\n")

    def test_redraw_bolds_the_job_counter(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            job = {"id": "fit", "targets": [0], "configs": [{}]}
            (root / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps({"queue": [job]}))
            (root / "state.json").write_text(json.dumps({"status": "prepared", "completed": {}}))
            _, plain = w.snapshot(root, [], now=100)
            _, bold = w.snapshot(root, [], now=100, redraw=True)
            self.assertIn("0/1 jobs", plain)
            self.assertIn("\x1b[1m0/1\x1b[22m jobs", bold)

    def test_timer_is_combined_with_eta_at_the_bottom(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            job = {"id": "fit", "targets": [0], "configs": [{}]}
            (root / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps({"queue": [job]}))
            (root / "state.json").write_text(json.dumps({"status": "prepared", "completed": {}}))
            _, text = w.snapshot(root, [], now=100, redraw=True, elapsed_since_update=12.9)
            self.assertTrue(text.endswith("Since update: 12s | ETA: unavailable while the campaign is not running"))

    def test_display_timestamp_can_be_frozen_while_eta_clock_uses_current_time(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
            root = Path(temp)
            job = {"id": "fit", "targets": [0], "configs": [{}]}
            (root / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps({"queue": [job]}))
            (root / "state.json").write_text(json.dumps({"status": "prepared", "completed": {}}))
            _, text = w.snapshot(root, [], now=200, display_time=100, elapsed_since_update=100)
            self.assertTrue(text.startswith("[" + datetime.fromtimestamp(100).strftime("%H:%M:%S") + "]"))
            self.assertIn("Since update: 100s", text)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


# Settled decisions, and why

The configuration `./serve` hands you, with the evidence for each choice and what would
overturn it. `results/session2/STATE.md` is the working log; this file is the conclusions
only. Where the evidence supports the *action* but not the *effect size*, that is said.

Every number here is a link to a row on disk. If a claim below has no evidence column, it
is not settled and is listed under "Still open".

---

> **Two 2026-09-08 corrections from the first owned-hardware arm.** See
> `results/session2/local-5060ti-pilot/FINDINGS.md`.
>
> 1. **Every arm before 2026-09-08 ran with the reference solution inside the model's
>    workdir.** `.meta/proof.ci.js` and `.meta/example.rs` were copied in with the exercise;
>    the model was observed reading one. `task_success` in those rows is therefore partly a
>    retrieval score. Paired comparisons still hold — both sides had the same access —
>    absolute pass rates and capability claims do not. Fixed in `_strip_reference_material`.
> 2. **"Running on one 16 GB card" overstates the ceiling.** 16/16 Polyglot tasks passed at
>    ctx 65,536 on a single 5060 Ti that was also driving a desktop, deepest 61,959. The
>    90,000-100,000 working floor is set by a tail of 4-7 exercises out of 78, not the median
>    task (Q2 31,166 / Q4 25,897). Re-derive before quoting it as a reason to buy a card.

---

## The card: RTX 3090, 24 GB

**Why not 16 GB.** Q4_K_XL is 16.35 GiB of weights. It does not fit a 16 GB card by
arithmetic, before any KV cache. The best a 16 GB card manages is IQ3_XXS at 106,496
context — under the 120k bar.

**Why not 32 GB.** No arm ever tested one. This is an untested gap, not a rejection.

| card | quant | ctx | decode | prefill | verdict |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RTX 3090 24 GB | Q4_K_XL | 131,072 | 64.7 | 1,053 | **clears the rule** |
| RTX 5070 Ti 16 GB | Q3_K_XL | 57,344 | 79.2 | 1,346 | fails: context |
| RTX 5070 Ti 16 GB | IQ3_XXS | 106,496 | 59.3 | 1,795 | fails: context |

The rule: >=40 tok/s decode, >=1000 tok/s prefill, >120k context, MTP wanted, no worse than
DeepSeek V4 on API.

**Why not two cheap 12 GB cards.** Measured 2026-08-28, `results/session2/split-mode-2gpu`. Two
RTX 3060 12 GB under `-sm tensor` with MTP reach 42.6 and 42.9 tok/s decode, 476.9 and 477.4
prefill. Those pair against the drafter-pilot 3090 arm -- 57.10 decode, 738.8 prefill -- and
only against that arm: the table above is a different instrument and the numbers are not
comparable across the two. So two cards buy 75 percent of the decode and 65 percent of the
prefill for roughly 40 percent of the price.

Tensor split is doing the work, and the bandwidth argument predicts it: 2x360 over 936 GB/s
is 0.77, against a measured 0.746. Repeats agree to 0.7 percent, so the gap is not noise.

Two ceilings, both hard:

- `-sm layer` does not load at all at 106,496, twice, in ~20 s with the GPU confirmed free.
  Layer split is not a slower option at this context; it is not an option. **The mechanism was
  later measured on the 16 GB pair**, where layer split does load: it allocates *asymmetrically*,
  10,412 MiB on one card and 13,050 on the other, against tensor split's even 11,412/11,412.
  13,050 exceeds a 3060's 12,288, so the 3060 refusal is arithmetic, not a mystery.
- `-sm tensor` does not load at 131,072 either. 106,496 fits at 11,359 MiB of 12,288 per
  card. **That is under the 120k bar, so on the rule as written two 3060s fail on context.**
  Not settled, though: the upstream report that reached 131,072 also passed `--no-mmproj` and
  `-fit off`, which this leg did not.

---

## The hardware landscape, surveyed 2026-08-28

Measured on our harness, same instrument and task set, so these three rows compare. The
session-1 table above is a *different* instrument; do not mix them.

| | decode | prefill | context | power | price |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1x RTX 3090 | 57.1 | 739 | **131,072** | 350 W | ~4000 PLN |
| 2x RTX 3060 12GB, `-sm tensor` | 42.9 | 477 | ~106,496 | 340 W | ~1500 PLN |
| 2x RTX 3080 12GB *(projected)* | ~108 | ~1150 | ~106,496 | 700 W | ~3600 PLN |
| **2x RTX 5060 Ti 16GB, `-sm tensor`** | **64.6** | 643-712 | **131,072** | 360 W | ~3800-6000 PLN |

**Context is set by the smallest card, not the total.** Both 12 GB pairings top out near
106,496 and fail at 131,072, so no 12 GB pair clears the 120k bar. This is why 2x3080 does
not beat a 3090 at the same money: same price, less context, double the heat.

**16 GB per card is the threshold that changes the answer.** Measured 2026-08-29,
`results/session2/split-mode-5060ti`, five arms, same instrument as the two rows above. Two
RTX 5060 Ti 16GB under `-sm tensor` with MTP reach 64.17 and 65.03 tok/s decode, and **load
131,072 at 63.10 tok/s** using 12,012 MiB of 16,311 per card. 4.3 GiB per card is still free
at the model's maximum context, so this pair is not context-limited at all.

That beats a 3090's decode by 1.12x for the same power (360 W against 350 W), and loses
prefill: 643-712 against a very tight 738.8/740.1. Prefill drifted upward across the run
(643 -> 687 -> 712 in execution order) so it carries about 10 percent noise here; the honest
reading is that the 3090 wins prefill by 5 to 13 percent.

**The decode result is unexplained.** Aggregate bandwidth is 2x448 over 936 GB/s = 0.96,
*before* tensor-split overhead, so the prediction that fit the 3060 pair to within 3 percent
fails here in the wrong direction. Two candidates were tested and rejected: MTP acceptance is
63.1 percent against the 3090's 60.9 (far too small an edge), and the 3090 arm ran at its full
350 W on the same CUDA 13.0.88 and driver 590.48.01. What remains is GDDR7 realising more of
its rated bandwidth than GDDR6X, or Blackwell carrying the MTP verify step faster. No evidence
separates them.

**On the rule**, this pair passes decode and context and MTP, and reads 643-712 against the
>=1000 prefill bar -- but the 3090 reads 739 on the same instrument, so that criterion was
calibrated against the session-1 instrument and cannot be applied to these rows unchanged.

**Price is what actually decides it.** Polish listings run ~1,899 PLN promotional to
~2,999-3,199 PLN mainstream per card, so a pair is ~3,800 PLN at best and ~6,000 typical,
against ~4,000 for a used 3090. At the promotional end it is a real trade -- more decode,
equal context, new warranty, less heat -- and at the mainstream end it is not.

**NVFP4 buys prefill, not decode.** Measured 2026-08-29, `results/session2/nvfp4-vs-q4`,
ABBA plus a ceiling arm on the same 2x RTX 5060 Ti box. Against UD-Q4_K_XL at ctx=106496:

| | decode | prefill | VRAM/card | acceptance |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| UD-Q4_K_XL | 65.84 / 66.03 | 696.7 / 693.8 | 11,416 MiB | 63.1% |
| NVFP4 MID-HIGH | 69.89 / 70.38 | 796.2 / 782.7 | 10,810 MiB | 63.8% |
| NVFP4 at 131,072 | 70.08 | 791.5 | 11,410 MiB | 63.8% |
| pooled ratio | **1.064** | **1.135** | -5.3% | matched |

Repeats agree to 0.7 percent, so the effect is not noise. **Discount the decode figure**: the
arm holds 15.75 GiB against 16.35, and decode is weight-bandwidth-bound, so 3.7 of that 6.4
percent is footprint and only ~2.6 is the kernel. Prefill is compute-bound, where footprint
does not carry, and 13.5 percent is the FP4 tensor cores. Acceptance was identical in every
arm -- the same integers each repeat -- so none of it is a drafter effect.

This corrects the intuition that Blackwell is simply fast at prefill: **Q4 on these cards
loses prefill to a 3090** (695 against 739). NVFP4 is what recovers it, to 789. And the
ceiling arm costs nothing -- full 131,072 at 70.08 decode, using less VRAM than Q4 needs at
106,496.

**And the throughput is given back in tokens.** Measured 2026-08-29,
`results/session2/polyglot-cost-nvfp4` run 30 against `polyglot-cost-q4b` run 13, 78 paired
tasks at ctx=106496, both arms 78/78 solved and harness-verified:

| | geometric | 95% CI | tasks worse | sign p |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| completion tokens | **1.23x** | [1.04, 1.45] | 58/78 | <0.001 |
| decode tok/s | 1.13x | [1.12, 1.14] | 77/78 faster | <0.001 |
| **wall seconds** | **0.97x** | [0.84, 1.12] | 36/78 | **0.572** |

NVFP4 spends about a quarter more tokens reaching the same answer and its speed advantage
almost exactly repays it, so net wall-clock is indistinguishable. This is the IQ3_XXS result
in milder form: a faster quant that is a worse model.

Do not defend the *magnitude* -- the same-quant repeat noise floor is ~1.19x and 1.23x sits
barely above it. The *direction* is solid: noise is not directional, and 58/78 at p<0.001 is
not a coin. Confounds: the base arm ran on a 3090 with engine `8a832e4bf`, the candidate on
2x5060 Ti with `b10f9ca58`; token counts are card-independent by design, the engine
difference is not controlled.

**So NVFP4 is not worth adopting for agentic work.** What survives is what never touched
quality: 5.3 percent less VRAM, and 131,072 at 11,410 MiB per card.

Still unmeasured: **quality by any direct measure.** The compact tiers
re-quantize attention F8 -> BF16 -> NVFP4, a second quantization step on the tensors that
tolerate it least, and every tier shares one byte-identical 448-tensor backbone, so no tier
below HIGHEST avoids it. A 78-task tokens-to-solution arm is the test, since this repo has
already shown a faster-decoding quant losing on tokens (IQ3_XXS).

**Engine support, verified.** The pinned `b10f9ca58` defines
`GGML_TYPE_NVFP4` (`ggml/include/ggml.h:430`) with real CUDA kernels and a Blackwell-specific
SRAM layout (`ggml/src/ggml-cuda/mmq.cuh:128`, `convert.cu:378`). The leg's first NVFP4 probe
grepped `llama-server --help` and reported absence; that probe was wrong, because NVFP4 is a
tensor type and never appears among server flags. Nothing about NVFP4 *throughput* is measured,
and no NVFP4 Qwen3.8-27B GGUF is known to exist.

**Decided 2026-08-29, executed 2026-08-31: bought one RTX 5060 Ti 16GB.** The 1,800 PLN
listing that motivated the original decision was a scam; the mainstream band had moved to
~2,800 and the card was bought in the 2,000-2,500 range. This does not overturn "rent, do
not buy" below, which prices a *dedicated* inference box; this card is bought for gaming as
well, so inference rides on marginal cost. The 2060 Super pairing is now optional rather
than assumed -- see "Running on one 16 GB card" and "Still open".

The pairing works on a coincidence: the 5060 Ti and the 2060 Super have **identical memory
bandwidth**, 448 GB/s each, so the older card costs nothing per byte read. Layer split is
forced -- a 16+8 tensor split is governed by the smaller card and leaves ~16 GB, which
UD-Q4_K_XL's 16.35 GiB does not fit -- but 24 GB total does carry Q4 at the full 131,072.

Decode is anchored on measurement, not arithmetic alone: this leg's `-sm layer` arms scored
41.20 and 41.39, which is exactly "Q4_K_XL at 448 GB/s effective", and the model reproduces
it (16.35 GiB / 448 GB/s = 39 ms/token = 25.5 tok/s raw, x1.62 accepted per MTP step ~ 41).

Rejected alternatives, and why:

| option | why not |
|---|---|
| 2x RTX 3060 12GB, ~1,500 PLN | Faster on both measured columns (42.9 / 477) and still fails: 12 GB per card caps context near 106k. A dead end -- it can never clear 120k. |
| 1x RTX 5070 Ti 16GB | 896 GB/s for about double the price. Same 16 GB, so the same context ladder; capacity, not bandwidth, is what binds. |
| IQ4_XS on one 16 GB card | ~33k context by extrapolation from two measured rungs. |
| IQ3_XXS on one 16 GB card | Reaches ~106k, but spends 1.66-2.02x the tokens per solution. |

Open and unverified until the hardware exists: **prefill**, estimated 350-450 against the
663 two Blackwell cards managed, because Turing's compute is far below its bandwidth and
prefill is the compute-bound phase. Also **NVFP4 is unusable** on this rig -- Turing has no
FP4 path -- so it forgoes the +13.5 percent prefill measured above, which stings exactly
where the rig is weakest. The build needs `sm_75;sm_120` multi-arch, and the layer split
needs an explicit `--tensor-split` because the 8 GB card cannot take its proportional share.

**Qwen3.8-27B is a fortunate fit, not a coincidence.** At 16.35 GiB it is the largest thing
that fits one 24 GB card with usable context left. Everything larger forces a split.

**The cheap 24 GB cards are architecturally dead**, which is why no budget path exists:

| card | why it fails |
|---|---|
| Tesla P40 24 GB | Pascal `sm_61`, dropped by CUDA 13; FP16 at 1/64 rate kills prefill |
| Tesla M40 24 GB | Maxwell, dropped earlier |
| Tesla V100 16 GB | Volta, dropped by CUDA 13, and not 24 GB |
| AMD MI50 32 GB | ROCm dropped `gfx906`; Vulkan only, weak FP16 |
| RTX 3080 10 GB | 8.2 GiB weights/card leaves ~1.6 GiB KV -> ~76k context |
| Tesla T4 16 GB | 70 W and efficient, but 16 GB and ~2000+ PLN each |

The only 12 GB SKUs in the 30 series are 3060 12GB, 3080 12GB and 3080 Ti 12GB. An
RTX 2060 Super is best used as the **display GPU** so both compute cards stay headless: at
8 GB it would cap a tensor split's KV and add no context, despite having more bandwidth
(448 GB/s) than a 3060.

**Apple unified memory, from Apple's own figures.** Bandwidth predicts decode better than
any other number on the spec sheet, and the tier matters far more than the capacity:

| chip | max memory | bandwidth | vs 3090 |
|---|---|---|---|
| M6 (Mac mini) | 32 GB | 170 GB/s | 0.18x |
| M5 Pro | — | ~339 GB/s *(approx)* | 0.36x |
| M5 Max (Mac Studio) | 128 GB | 614 GB/s | 0.66x |
| **M5 Ultra** (Mac Studio) | **512 GB** | **1.2 TB/s** | **1.31x** |

M6 is the *base* chip, not a newer tier -- 32 GB ceiling, below one 3060 in bandwidth. M5 Pro
sits below the 720 GB/s a 3060 pair aggregates. Only M5 Ultra is interesting: $5,499/96 GB,
$9,499/256 GB, 512 GB above $10,000 from late October. Apple claims 4.5x M3 Ultra peak GPU
compute for AI, which targets the prefill weakness -- a vendor peak figure, not measured
throughput.

Its real case is large MoE, where bandwidth demand scales with *active* parameters: 512 GB
holds a 400B-class MoE at Q4 and no consumer GPU rig reaches that at any price. For
Qwen3.8-27B it is 22k PLN to beat a 4k PLN card by ~30%, on a model using 16 of the 96 GB.

**Verdict: do not buy yet; rent.** `./serve` already does it at ~$0.20/hr with no capex, no
power bill, no heat and no obsolescence. Buying only makes sense once capability is proven,
and no amount of local hardware displaces the *orchestrator* tier -- the worker tier it could
displace already costs $0.018/task.

---

## The 2026-08-31 re-survey

Prompted by the 1,800 PLN listing turning out to be a scam. The physics did not change; the
prices did. Rows are arithmetic against the measured anchors above unless marked *meas.*

**Market conditions now bound every option.** Blended DRAM contract prices rose ~80%
quarter-over-quarter in Q1 2026, and 16 GB+ RTX 50 cards rose 15-20%, because wafer capacity
moved from GDDR to HBM for datacentre AI.

The RTX 50 SUPER refresh -- the only announced route to new 24 GB consumer cards -- is
**unresolved, and this file should not pretend otherwise.** NVIDIA has confirmed no model,
spec, MSRP or date. Leak reporting moved delayed -> CES 2027 (June 2026) -> cancelled with
development shifted to RTX 60 (Gamescom, August 2026). What is *not* contested is the
mechanism: the 24 GB configurations need 3 GB GDDR7 modules at $60-70 each against ~$20 for
the 2 GB parts, so even a SUPER that ships prices that memory in.

The decision-relevant consequence holds under either reading. **24 GB consumer cards are a
closed set today** -- 3090, 3090 Ti, 4090, 5090 -- so used 3090 prices should rise; and a
hypothetical 5070 Ti SUPER 24 GB would beat a 5060 Ti pair on slot count and power rather
than on throughput, at two to three times the marginal price. Waiting is therefore a
preference for one card over two, not a bet that pays.

**AMD, rejected twice for different reasons.**

| card | VRAM | bandwidth | why not |
|---|---|---|---|
| RX 9060 XT 16GB | 16 | 320 GB/s | Same 128-bit bus as a 5060 Ti but GDDR6, so 71% of the bandwidth for ~65% of the price. Bandwidth per zloty is a wash; the discount does not pay for the backend risk. |
| RX 7900 XTX 24GB | 24 | 960 GB/s | The one genuinely competitive AMD card -- 3090-class bandwidth at 3,850-4,500 PLN, new, with warranty. Rejected on MTP risk, not on silicon. |

The backend tax is specific, not a generic "CUDA is better". MTP/NextN on the Vulkan+RADV
path is reported to give low accept rates and garbled output on RDNA3, while HIP/ROCm is
correct -- and Vulkan is the *faster* backend on these cards, ROCm running 20-30% slower. So
AMD forces the slow path to get the feature that carries our entire decode story. On the
7900 XTX that bet is worth ~15 tok/s: with MTP working it beats a 3090, without it loses.
Multi-AMD-GPU is also reported held to layer split, forfeiting the 1.56x `-sm tensor` is
worth. Losing NVFP4 costs nothing, since it is rejected above on token cost.

**The mixed-pair ladder, 2060 Super as partner.** Decode scales the measured 5060 Ti
`-sm layer` arm -- 41.2 tok/s at 448 GB/s effective -- by per-card weight share over
bandwidth. The low end of each band derates the Turing card 15% for GDDR6 realising less of
its rating than GDDR7.

| pairing | total | effective BW | decode | prefill (est.) | max ctx | price |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| RTX 5060 8GB + 2060S | 16 | -- | -- | -- | **cannot load** | -- |
| 5060 Ti 16GB + 2060S | 24 | 448 GB/s | 39-41 | 350-450 | 131,072 | 2,800 |
| 5070 12GB + 2060S | 20 | 560 GB/s | 47-52 | 350-450 | **58,070** | 2,400-2,850 |
| 5070 Ti 16GB + 2060S | 24 | 674 GB/s | 57-62 | 430-550 | 131,072 | 4,600 |

**The 5070 fails on capacity, not speed.** It is cheaper than a 5060 Ti and 25% faster, and
still loses: 20 GB total leaves 3.15 GiB of KV. No 12+8 configuration reaches 120k.

**The 2060 Super is free in one pairing and costs half the throughput in the other.** At 448
GB/s it takes 33% of decode time for 33% of capacity beside a 5060 Ti -- the coincidence the
original plan rested on -- but 49% beside a 5070 Ti. Prefill barely moves across the ladder
because the Turing card's compute cost is fixed and dominates: 1.9x the Blackwell compute
buys ~22% more prefill.

**2x RTX 3090, projected.** Aggregate 1,872 GB/s under `-sm tensor` gives **~90-110 decode**
and **~1,300 prefill** at 48 GB, for ~8,000 PLN and 700 W. It is the only configuration in
the survey that clears the >=1000 prefill criterion. The band is wide because cross-GPU
reduction traffic is roughly fixed per token: two 3060s at 42.9 tok/s have 23 ms to hide it
in, two 3090s at 110 have 9 ms, so the 3% overhead measured on the slow pair cannot hold.
The 3090 is the last consumer card with NVLink, and a P2P driver patch landed April 2026 --
that is the lever which closes the spread. **`-sm layer` on two 3090s buys zero decode**:
one card is active per token, so it is one 3090 with more VRAM.

**Why the 5060 Ti still won.** At 2,000-2,500 per card it beats a used 3090 on both
decode-per-zloty and prefill-per-zloty, with the crossover near 2,150. Two of them at 2,000
each cost exactly what a used 3090 does and beat it outright -- measured 64.6 decode against
57.1, 32 GB against 24, new with warranty -- for 8% less prefill and one more slot. The
3090's remaining advantages are prefill (739 measured, 1.5-2x any mixed rig), a single slot,
and being the only row measured on our own harness; against no warranty, 350 W, and GDDR6X
memory junction temperatures of 100-110 C degrading thermal pads on mining-era cards.

## Running on one 16 GB card

What the purchased 5060 Ti serves with no second card. Context comes from the two measured
16 GB rungs (Q3_K_XL 57,344 and IQ3_XXS 106,496, a slope of 23,860 tokens per GiB); decode
scales the measured 5060 Ti `-sm layer` arm by weight ratio, MTP included.

| quant | weights GiB | max ctx | decode | token cost vs Q4 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| UD-Q4_K_XL | 16.35 | does not fit | -- | 1.00 |
| **UD-IQ4_XS** | 13.27 | **32,768** | ~51 | unmeasured; likely inside the 1.19x noise floor |
| UD-Q3_K_XL | 12.24 | 57,344 *(meas.)* | ~55 | 1.28-1.39 |
| UD-IQ3_S | 11.21 | 81,920 | ~60 | unmeasured |
| UD-IQ3_XXS | 10.18 | 106,496 *(meas.)* | ~66 | 1.66-2.02 |
| UD-Q2_K_XL | 9.15 | 131,072 *(cap, zero margin)* | ~74 | unmeasured |

Contexts assume `--cache-type-{k,v} q8_0`; f16 KV roughly halves every one. Decode ignores
KV read traffic, which grows with context, so expect sag at the top of each row.

**IQ4_XS at 32,768 was the pick and it did not survive contact.** Tried 2026-08-31; 32,768
binds hard enough to be unproductive. The working floor is **90,000-100,000**, which no
16 GB single-card configuration reaches at Q4-class quality: IQ3_S stops at 81,920, and only
IQ3_XXS clears it, at 1.66-2.02x the tokens. That is what forces the 2060 Super back in --
see below.

**Every quant carries the embedded MTP head.** Verified 2026-08-31 by range-reading the GGUF
headers on HuggingFace: `nextn` tensors are present in IQ4_XS, Q3_K_XL, IQ3_S, IQ3_XXS,
Q2_K_XL and Q4_K_XL, so `--spec-type draft-mtp` works on all of them and `MTP/mtp-*.gguf` is
never loaded. Confirm on the box from `/metrics`: `spec_decode_num_accepted_tokens_total`
over `spec_decode_num_draft_tokens_total` should land near the 60-64% measured on CUDA. Near
zero means the head is not being used and a third of decode is on the floor.

**32,768 is a task limit, not only a quality limit.** In the 78-task suite `rust/book-store`
filled a 131,072 window and timed out. At 32,768 more tasks truncate outright rather than
merely cost extra tokens. IQ3_S at 81,920 is the hedge if that binds.


## The quant: UD-Q4_K_XL, not IQ3_XXS

**IQ3_XXS decodes 24% faster and still loses.** 70.6–71.6 tok/s against Q4's 57.0 — and it
spends 1.66–2.02x as many tokens reaching a verified solution, so it loses on wall time by
roughly 1.3–1.6x. Speed per token is not the thing being bought; tokens to solution is.

| leg | n | geomean vs Q4 | 95% CI | sign test |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| UD-IQ3_XXS run 1 | 40 | 2.02 | [1.64, 2.50] | 34/40 |
| UD-IQ3_XXS run 2 | 39 | 1.66 | [1.36, 2.03] | 29/39 |
| UD-Q3_K_XL | 39 | 1.28 | — | 23/39 |
| UD-Q6_K_M | 40 | 1.14 | — | 21/40 |

**Only IQ3 separates.** Its sign test is decisive in both runs (binomial p < 0.005). Q3 and
Q6 are coin flips and sit inside the noise floor — at 40 paired tasks they are
indistinguishable from Q4 and from each other. So the honest statement is *not* "Q4 is the
smallest quant that does not lose tokens": **Q3 might do equally well, and is untested at
n large enough to tell.** Q4 is chosen because a 24 GB card has room for it and there is no
measured reason to go smaller.

**Overturned by:** a Q3-vs-Q4 leg at n >= 150, or any 2-bit quant clearing 120k with MTP at
acceptable quality. Ratio-of-totals runs 6–17% above the geometric mean on every degraded
leg; a buyer pays totals, so quote both.

---

## The drafter: MTP, not DFlash2

**On this card, DFlash2 drafts better and costs more than it returns.** It emits 3.80 tokens
per target forward pass against MTP's 2.79 (a real 1.36x win) but each pass costs 74.5 ms
against 46.5 ms (1.60x), so decode falls. Prefill falls too, 1,055 -> 890 tok/s, because the
1.1 GB drafter prefills alongside the target.

| | mtp-1 | mtp-2 | dflash2-1 | dflash2-2 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| median decode tok/s | 57.10 | 56.92 | 48.94 | 48.93 |

Six JavaScript prompts, one box, one binary, AB then BA so drift cannot pass as an effect.
DFlash2 loses on five of six in both orderings.

**What is and is not established.** The action is settled: per-task ratios span 0.723–1.095,
geomean 0.870, 95% CI **0.754–1.003** — no value in that interval makes switching to DFlash2
right, so keep MTP. The *effect size* is **not** settled: n=6 on one language, and the CI
